### [1] [Beyond PINNs: Boundary-Enriched Holomorphic Networks for Laplace Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29922)
*Andrei Vlad Nicula,Kalp Shah,Robert-Stefan Sofroni,Mihnea Andrei Bârsan,Agneya Bhardwaj,Jan Jelínek,Chris Hoes,Remco Hogerwerf,Finn Govers,Storm Koper,Gerrit Janssen,Stef de Wildt,Alejandro Valverde Mahou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对二维Laplace方程，比较混合调和-神经网络与"构造上即调和"的全纯神经网络（后者只需边界训练），并按边界段交点的内角与边界条件加入奇性函数做增强；在6个奇性基准上增强模型为所比较神经网络中最准确者，但结果显示当闭式解落在增强项张成空间内时，纯最小二乘拟合即可精确到舍入误差，且经典最小二乘（更多奇性项或调和多项式与有理极点）仍然更准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs通过惩罚PDE残差求解，但对具有奇异行为的解难以优化，因此需要利用问题特定解析结构来替代基于残差/基函数的PINN范式，检验这种思路在奇异问题上的真实增益。

Method: 聚焦二维Laplace方程，将控制方程直接嵌入神经表示：比较混合调和-神经网络模型与输出按构造即调和的全纯神经网络（仅需边界训练）；进一步依据各边界段交点的内角和边界条件选取奇性函数对全纯模型进行增强；在六个奇异基准上评测，并配合仅含奇性项的最小二乘拟合及经典最小二乘（更多奇性项、调和多项式+有理极点）作为对照，分析误差来源。

Result: 在六个奇异基准上，增强模型在所比较的神经网络中最准确；但在四个具闭式解的算例上，该解位于增强项张成空间内，仅用这些项做最小二乘即可精确到舍入误差。在Motz问题和裂纹梁上，神经核心将恢复的主奇异系数相对误差从约10^-2（同样四项的最小二乘）降至约10^-6；在四个解的光滑部分不在张成空间内的变体上，网络比仅拟合其奇性项精确4×10^4至2×10^7倍；而含更多奇性项、或含调和多项式与有理极点的经典最小二乘仍更准确。

Conclusion: 结果说明利用问题特定解析结构可作为基PINN与残差PINN的替代方案用于困难科学计算问题，但公平比较须计入构造上的优势（增强项已包含目标解），且经典谱/最小二乘方法在同等解析结构下往往仍具竞争力。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) solve partial differential equations by penalizing their residual, but this formulation can be difficult to optimize for solutions with singular behaviour. We focus specifically on the two-dimensional Laplace equation, whose strong analytical structure allows the governing equation to be enforced directly in the neural representation. We compare a hybrid harmonic-neural model with a holomorphic neural network whose output is harmonic by construction and therefore requires only boundary training. We further enrich the holomorphic model with singular functions chosen according to the interior angle and boundary conditions at each junction of boundary segments. On six singular benchmarks, the enriched model is the most accurate of the neural models we compare. On the four with a closed-form solution, however, that solution lies in the span of the enrichment terms, and a least-squares fit of these terms alone is exact to rounding error. On Motz's problem and the cracked beam, the neural core reduces the relative error of the recovered leading singular coefficient from about $10^{-2}$, for a least-squares fit with the same four terms, to about $10^{-6}$, and on four variants whose solution has a smooth part outside the span, the network is $4\times10^{4}$ to $2\times10^{7}$ times more accurate than a fit of its singular terms alone; classical least-squares fits with more singular terms, or with harmonic polynomials and rational poles, are more accurate still. Our results demonstrate how exploiting problem-specific analytical structure can provide an alternative to base and residual PINNs for challenging scientific computing problems.

</details>


### [2] [WISP: Composable Representations for Wrist-Worn Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28033)
*Zipeng Wu,Shiqiao Zhou,Fabian Spill,James Andrews*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 WristHARBench 腕部加速度人体活动识别基准（9个腕部数据集 + 2个多通道惯性数据集，统一采用参与者不相交评估），并提出可组合表示空间 WISP，在9个主数据集上平均 Macro-F1 达 70.20%，显著优于18个外部基线中最强者（59.62%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手表与手环是最常见的可穿戴设备，国家级健康研究亦在腕部记录运动数据；但现有人体活动识别基准混合了身体不同部位的传感器，无法单独衡量仅凭腕部信息可达到的识别性能，缺乏标准化、参与者不相交的腕部评估协议。

Method: 1) 构建 WristHARBench：在9个腕部加速度数据集与2个额外多通道惯性数据集上统一参与者不相交（participant-disjoint）评估协议；2) 基于"不同活动在腕部运动中留下互补模式（局部形状、节律、时序顺序、跨轴关系）"的假设，提出 WISP（Wrist-Informed Space of Predictive Representations）——一个可组合的表示空间，将对应不同模式的表示与兼容分类器组合，并以随机搜索进行配置选择；3) 通过受控消融与基线对比验证各表示成分的贡献来源。

Result: 在9个主数据集上，WISP + 随机搜索取得 70.20% 平均 Macro-F1，18个外部基线中最强者为 59.62%。消融与对比表明：识别性能的提升主要来自表示引入了其他表示所缺失的运动模式，而非以不同方式重复编码同一模式。

Conclusion: 腕部单点活动识别需要标准化、参与者不相交的基准来真实反映性能；互补性（而非表示形式的多样性）是构建腕部活动表示的核心原则，WISP 的可组合设计据此显著超越现有方法。

Abstract: Smartwatches and fitness bands are among the most common wearable devices, and large national health studies record movement at the wrist. Yet existing human activity recognition benchmarks mix sensors worn on different parts of the body, so they do not show how well activities can be recognised from the wrist alone. We introduce WristHARBench, a benchmark that standardises participant-disjoint evaluation across nine wrist-acceleration datasets and two additional multichannel inertial datasets. We hypothesise that different human activities leave complementary patterns in wrist motion, such as local shape, rhythm, temporal order and cross-axis relationships, and propose WISP (Wrist-Informed Space of Predictive Representations), a composable representation space that combines representations of these patterns with compatible classifiers. Across the nine primary datasets, WISP with random search achieves 70.20% mean Macro-F1, compared with 59.62% for the strongest of 18 external baselines. Controlled ablations and baseline comparisons indicate that recognition improves mainly when a representation adds motion patterns that the others lack, rather than when it represents the same pattern in a different way.

</details>


### [3] [IADR: Interface-Augmented Neural Operator for Phase-Field Mean-Curvature Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30153)
*Qinyi Zhang,Duanyu Feng,Yangshuai Wang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Interface-Augmented Diffusion-Reaction (IADR) 神经算子，用超网络将界面宽度 ε 映射为目标 DR 网络的权重，单个模型即可在 ε 范围内匹配逐 ε 专家模型，并保持相场结构与毫秒级推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Allen-Cahn 方程是平均曲率流( MCF )的标准相场模型，由扩散界面宽度 ε 参数化，实际仿真需覆盖多个 ε。PDE 专用神经算子在每个 ε 上均需重训练；通用神经算子虽单模型覆盖 ε 范围，却丢失目标方程的相场结构。作者希望同时解决这两个缺陷。

Method: 1) 目标网络采用扩散-反应(DR)结构以保留 Allen-Cahn 方程的相场结构；2) 超网络将 ε 映射为该目标网络的权重，从而摊销(amortise)对 ε 的依赖；3) 理论上在一致性(consistency)与稳定性(stability)假设下建立条件性 K 步 rollout 误差界。

Result: 在指定范围内，单个 IADR 在分布内数据上匹配逐 ε 专家模型；在 ε 与几何双重分布外偏移下仍保持其跨形状泛化能力；并保留紧凑的 DR 目标结构与毫秒级推理速度。

Conclusion: IADR 通过"DR 目标网络 + ε 超网络"的组合，兼顾 PDE 专用算子的结构保真度与通用算子的单模型多参数覆盖能力，并具备理论误差界与高效推理，为参数化相场模型提供统一神经算子方案。

Abstract: The Allen-Cahn equation is one of the standard phase-field models for mean-curvature flow (MCF), parameterised by the diffuse-interface width $\varepsilon$. Practical simulations span a range of $\varepsilon$, so a single model covering this range is needed. PDE-specific neural operators are accurate but must be retrained at every $\varepsilon$; general neural operators cover $\varepsilon$ in one model but lose the phase-field structure of the target. We introduce Interface-Augmented Diffusion-Reaction (IADR), a neural operator that addresses both shortcomings: a target network with a diffusion-and-reaction (DR) structure preserves the phase-field structure of the Allen-Cahn equation, while a hypernetwork that maps $\varepsilon$ to the weights of this target network amortises the dependence on $\varepsilon$ across the range. A single IADR matches the per-$\varepsilon$ specialists on in-distribution data within the prescribed range, retains their cross-shape generalisation under both $\varepsilon$- and geometry-out-of-distribution shifts, and retains their compact DR target structure with millisecond-scale inference. We further establish a conditional $K$-step rollout error bound under consistency and stability assumptions.

</details>


### [4] [Measuring and Teaching Language Model Agents to Act on Delayed Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28158)
*Shardul Bansal,Seth Schilbe,Jarrod Barnes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 SMB Bench——一个校准的90天批发经营模拟器（18个因果配对任务、859条前沿模型运行轨迹），用于考察智能体能否察觉并应对自身决策的延迟后果；在16个前沿模型中仅3个优于固定采购规则，且模型常能"诊断"却不"改行为"。作者进一步用归档决策及其后果构造可复用训练经验，训练开源模型后其更一致地履行客户保护承诺、减少严重亏损季度，但利润提升不确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型智能体评测多聚焦单轮孤立任务，而真实经营是跨多日的连续操作：今日决策会重塑后续观测与现金约束（缺货掩盖流失的客流需求、短交导致数周后订单萎缩、紧急采购消耗营运资金）。因此需要一个能暴露"决策延迟后果"的基准，并回答何种经验与学习信号能让智能体在未见过的业务中察觉、归因并应对这些后果。

Method: 1）构建 SMB Bench：校准的90天批发模拟器，含18个因果配对任务与859条前沿模型运行轨迹数据集；2）在16个前沿模型上评测，与固定采购规则基线对比，并分析模型笔记与实际采购行为的一致性；3）针对开源模型（Qwen3.8-27B），以培训业务记录提取运营规则，并将归档的前沿季度作为"起始状态"而非可模仿的对话记录，进行训练；4）在相同评估指令下的未见测试业务上检验策略迁移效果。

Result: 16个前沿模型中仅3个优于固定采购规则；模型常在笔记中记录正确诊断，却未及时改变后续采购，说明"诊断问题""改变行为""改善经营"是三件独立的事。训练后的策略在未见业务上更一致地遵守客户保护承诺、减少严重亏损季度，但额外利润收益仍不确定；同时暴露出履行承诺的成本——早期备货习惯使采购方依赖紧急供应并恶化供应商关系。

Conclusion: 研究区分了三个层次：识别延迟效应、持续一致地据此行动、以及协调周边决策使响应真正提升利润；SMB Bench 将归档决策及其后果转化为可复用的训练经验，把上述观察与学习方法连接起来，为长周期经营智能体的评测与训练提供统一框架。

Abstract: Language model agents are increasingly asked to run ongoing multi-day operations rather than finish isolated single-turn tasks. In daily operations, today's choices reshape later observations and cash constraints because empty shelves hide lost walk-in demand, short-shipped customers quietly shrink future orders weeks later, and emergency buying drains working capital. We introduce SMB Bench, a calibrated 90-day wholesale simulator with 18 causally paired tasks and an 859-run frontier trace dataset, to ask what experience and learning signal make an agent better at noticing, attributing, and acting on the delayed consequences of its own decisions in unseen businesses. Across 16 frontier models, only 3 outperform a fixed buying rule; models often record a relevant diagnosis in their notes without changing later purchases in time, showing that diagnosing a problem, changing behavior, and improving a business are separate achievements. To study how experience improves those failures in an open model (Qwen3.8-27B), we used training-business records to identify operational rules and archived frontier quarters as starting states to continue from rather than transcripts to copy. On an unseen test business under identical evaluation instructions, the trained policy followed customer-protection commitments more consistently and reduced severe-loss quarters, while the additional profit benefit remained uncertain. Carrying those commitments through the quarter exposed the cost of fulfilling them, as early-quarter stocking habits left the buyer relying on emergency supply and a deteriorating supplier. The results separate recognizing a delayed effect, acting on it consistently, and coordinating surrounding decisions so that the response improves profit. SMB Bench connects these observations to a learning method by turning archived decisions and their consequences into reusable training experience.

</details>


### [5] [Irreducible shallow tanh neural networks are a manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30553)
*Ivan Bioli,Isabella Carla Gonnella,Carlo Marcati,Federico Pichi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：以双曲正切为激活函数的浅层神经网络，在Sussmann意义下不可约（无冗余神经元）的网络所表示的函数集合，是Sobolev空间 W^{ℓ,p} 的平滑嵌入子流形；并刻画了该流形的连通分支数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络被广泛用作函数逼近器，因此有必要研究其函数空间的几何结构。已有研究多关注网络表示能力，而对"由网络表示的函数集合"本身的几何性质（是否为流形、参数有界性、连通性）缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 研究固定输入输出维数、n个隐层神经元的tanh浅层网络。关键是证明紧致性/有界性引理：若一列网络在 W^{ℓ,p} 中收敛到具有相同隐层神经元数的不可约网络，则其参数保持有界；由此借助正则值/嵌入子流形理论，证明不可约网络的函数集合是 W^{ℓ,p}（有界域上，任意 ℓ∈ℕ、p∈[1,+∞]）的光滑嵌入子流形。

Result: 不可约网络表示的函数集合构成 W^{ℓ,p} 的光滑嵌入子流形；当输入和输出均为标量时，该流形恰有 n+1 个连通分支，其余情形下流形连通。

Conclusion: 通过参数有界性这一核心引理，作者为浅层tanh网络的函数空间几何提供了严格的流形刻画，并完整给出其连通分支结构，为后续基于流形几何的网络分析与优化研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Neural networks (NNs) have been increasingly used as function approximators, motivating the study of the geometry of their function spaces. We study the set of functions represented by shallow neural networks with hyperbolic tangent activation, fixed input and output dimensions, and $n$ hidden neurons. In particular, we prove that the subset represented by irreducible networks in the sense of Sussmann (Neural Networks, 1992), i.e., networks with no redundant neurons, is a smooth embedded submanifold of the Sobolev spaces $\mathrm{W}^{\ell,p}$ on bounded sets for any $\ell\in\mathbb{N}$ and $p\in[1,+\infty]$. The key step is to show that if a sequence of networks converges in $\mathrm{W}^{\ell,p}$ to an irreducible network with the same number of hidden neurons, then the parameters of the approximating networks remain bounded. Finally, we show that this manifold has exactly $n+1$ connected components when both the input and output are scalar, and is connected otherwise.

</details>


### [6] [RefineKV: Query-Adaptive KV Cache Compression via Attention-Error Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28171)
*Kewei Lian,Jiayi Zhai,Anji Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出查询自适应的KV缓存压缩方法RefineKV，通过"重要性+失真"选择准则在簇级别决定用紧凑代表还是展开原始KV，实现近满注意力质量下注意力阶段最高7.6倍加速、解码1.66倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码中反复访问完整KV缓存是主要瓶颈；作者发现单个查询所需的KV子集很小且能准确近似全注意力，但不同查询所需子集的并集几乎覆盖整个缓存，因此需要查询自适应的KV访问方式。

Method: 将KV缓存划分为簇，对误差可忽略的簇使用紧凑代表、其余簇展开为原始KV条目；从注意力输出误差上界推导出选择准则，该准则包含两个互补因素——衡量簇影响的重要性与衡量簇可被忠实表示程度的失真；据此设计RefineKV这一解码期查询自适应KV压缩方法。

Result: 在两个模型、三个基准上实验，RefineKV保持接近满注意力质量，注意力阶段最高7.6倍加速、整体解码1.66倍加速，建立了新的质量-延迟Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: 查询自适应的簇级KV表示（按重要性/失真准则动态选择代表或展开）可在几乎不损失质量的前提下显著降低长上下文解码的KV访问开销。

Abstract: Repeatedly accessing the full key--value (KV) cache is a major bottleneck in long-context language model decoding. We find that small query-specific subsets can accurately approximate full attention, yet their union covers nearly the entire cache, motivating query-adaptive access for the KV cache. A natural approach is to split the KV cache into clusters, using compact representatives for clusters that incur negligible error while expanding the rest to their original KV entries. We derive a selection criterion from an upper bound on attention-output error, which reveals two complementary factors governing the error: importance measures the impact of a cluster, while distortion measures how faithfully the cluster can be represented. Based on this criterion, we propose RefineKV, a decoding-time query-adaptive KV compression method. Experiments on two models and three benchmarks demonstrate that RefineKV retains near-full-attention quality while achieving up to 7.6x speedup in the attention phase and 1.66x faster decoding, establishing a new quality-latency Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [7] [A world model for longitudinal health-trajectory prediction in older adults across multinational cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28175)
*Jingyi Wu,Chenghua Guo,Qianlin Zuo,Pengfei Li,Luxia Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究构建基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的"健康世界模型"，显式建模健康状态的时序演化，并在HRS训练、ELSA与CHARLS外部验证，实现多期死亡与ADL/IADL恶化的风险预测及动作条件化的反事实轨迹模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型多聚焦于孤立时间点的静态健康风险，未显式刻画健康轨迹随时间的展开过程，难以支持纵向风险预测与干预（动作）效果模拟。

Method: 采用Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture构建健康世界模型，组件包括状态编码器、动作与情境条件化的预测器、带stop-gradient的目标编码器及临床结局预测头。在美国HRS上训练，在ELSA与CHARLS上不重新拟合直接评估；采用开环评估检验死亡及ADL/IADL恶化的判别与校准，并进行指定动作情景下的轨迹模拟与反事实模拟。

Result: HRS留出测试集中，第1期的死亡、ADL恶化、IADL恶化AUROC分别为0.89、0.93、0.93，第5期分别为0.84、0.84、0.85；预测风险可在各时间跨度上对后续结局分层。外部验证中，死亡AUROC在ELSA为0.867–0.881、在CHARLS为0.796–0.801。模拟轨迹在预设动作机制下保持了预期的风险排序，反事实模拟中生活方式动作改变所引起的预测风险变化方向与观察到的关联一致。

Conclusion: 该模型为老年人群提供了纵向风险预测与动作条件化模拟的统一框架，具备跨队列泛化能力，并为干预情景下的健康轨迹推演提供了工具。

Abstract: Aging is characterized by continuously evolving health states, yet most existing prediction models focus on static health risk at isolated time points rather than explicitly modelling how health trajectories unfold over time. We developed a health world model based on the Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture to represent evolving health states and simulate future trajectories under specified actions. The model combines a state encoder, an action- and context-conditioned predictor, a stop-gradient target encoder, and clinical outcome heads. We trained the model on the US Health and Retirement Study (HRS) and evaluated it without refitting in the English Longitudinal Study of Ageing (ELSA) and the China Health and Retirement Longitudinal Study (CHARLS). Open-loop evaluation assessed discrimination and calibration for mortality and worsening of activities of daily living (ADL) and instrumental activities of daily living (IADL). In the held-out HRS test set, the areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROCs) for mortality, ADL worsening, and IADL worsening were 0.89, 0.93, and 0.93 at the first horizon and 0.84, 0.84, and 0.85 at the fifth horizon, respectively. Predicted risks stratified subsequent outcomes across horizons. Across the evaluated horizons, mortality AUROC ranged from 0.867 to 0.881 in ELSA and from 0.796 to 0.801 in CHARLS. Simulated trajectories preserved the expected risk ordering across predefined action regimes. In counterfactual simulations, changes in lifestyle actions shifted predicted risks in directions consistent with observed associations. The model provides a framework for longitudinal risk prediction and action-conditioned simulation in older adults.

</details>


### [8] [Feedback Shortcut: Speculative Decoding for Latent Feedback Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28227)
*Benhao Huang,Xuezhe Ma,Hector Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Feedback Shortcut，通过 state alignment 与 pipelined correction 两个手段，使带隐藏状态反馈的 Transformer 在保持精确解码性能的同时实现高效投机解码，解码加速 2.4–2.8 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将每个位置的顶层隐状态反馈到下一位置输入可提升 Transformer 性能，但这种反馈破坏了投机解码：验证草稿 token 需要其前一位置的顶层状态，迫使草稿依赖近似替代（stand-in）。让模型适配该替代会降低精确解码的性能上限，而推理时直接使用它又引入近似误差，两种瓶颈都需要解决。

Method: 提出 Feedback Shortcut：(1) state alignment——训练一个轻量投影，将多 token 预测头状态映射为 stand-in，不改动基座模型训练；(2) pipelined correction——用每个草稿前驱新计算出的状态重新评估该草稿，作为更接近真实反馈的代理；将该校正嵌入后续草稿的验证过程中，从而在零额外调用的情况下提升准确率。

Result: 在 135M 与 780M 规模上，Feedback Shortcut 在 Paloma 上与精确解码上限的差距在 10^-4 bits per byte 以内，解码加速 2.4–2.8 倍，训练算力仅比 latent feedback Transformer 多约 2%。在大 batch 下，跳过校正仍能保持 Transformer 速度并保留 latent feedback 80% 以上的收益。

Conclusion: Feedback Shortcut 有效兼顾了隐藏状态反馈带来的建模收益与投机解码的加速收益，几乎无损地逼近精确解码上限，并具备良好的大 batch 可扩展性。

Abstract: Feeding each position's top-layer hidden state back into the next position's input improves Transformer performance. However, this feedback breaks speculative decoding, since verifying a drafted token requires the top-layer state from its preceding position, which forces the draft to rely on an approximate stand-in. Training the model to adapt to this stand-in can lower the exact-decoding performance ceiling, while using it at inference time further introduces approximation error. We propose Feedback Shortcut to address both bottlenecks. First, state alignment trains a lightweight projection from multi-token prediction head states to stand-ins, leaving base model training untouched. Second, pipelined correction re-evaluates each draft using its predecessor's newly computed state, a much closer proxy to true feedback. Running this correction inside the verification pass of subsequent drafts improves accuracy with zero extra calls. Across 135M and 780M scales, Feedback Shortcut stays within $10^{-4}$ bits per byte of the exact-decoding ceiling on Paloma and accelerates decoding by 2.4-2.8x with about 2% more training compute than the latent feedback Transformer. At large batch sizes, skipping correction still preserves Transformer speed and over 80% of latent feedback's gains.

</details>


### [9] [Learning the Projection onto the Data Support: Distribution Learning that Avoids Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28234)
*Lin Chen,Dejan Slepčev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个通过估计最近点投影（nearest-point projection）来学习流形支撑分布的框架：定义一个目标泛函，其极小元即恢复该投影；方法自动适应内在维度、可处理不同维度流形之并；给出投影恢复的总体结论与有限样本 Wasserstein 泛化界，并在多组实验中验证，同时说明该目标天然排除记忆训练样本的行为，并提出直接记忆度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流生成模型（如扩散模型）的目标函数可被"记忆训练样本"所最小化，存在过拟合/复制训练集的隐患；同时真实数据常位于低维流形或其并集上，需要自动适应内在维度的方法。

Method: 在函数类上定义以最近点投影为极小元的目标泛函，用标准神经网络架构优化；理论上分析总体层面（population-level）的投影恢复，并推导有限样本 Wasserstein 泛化界；实验中引入直接记忆度量，检验生成样本是否为新样本而非复制训练集。

Result: 证明总体意义下可恢复流形投影，并得到有限样本 Wasserstein 泛化界；在多组设定下实证有效；与扩散模型不同，该表述排除记忆行为，记忆度量显示所学投影生成新样本。

Conclusion: 以最近点投影为学习目标为流形支撑分布建模提供了理论可证、实现简便且抗记忆的替代路线，并给出可验证的记忆评估手段。

Abstract: We introduce a framework for learning distributions supported on manifolds based on estimating the nearest-point projection onto the data support. Specifically, we define an objective functional over a class of functions whose minimizer recovers this projection. The proposed framework automatically adapts to the intrinsic dimension and allows for data supported on unions of manifolds with varying dimensions. The resulting objective can be optimized using standard neural network architectures. We present population-level results establishing recovery of the manifold projection and prove finite-sample Wasserstein generalization bounds. Furthermore, we validate the approach empirically across a range of settings. In contrast to widely used generative models such as diffusion models, whose objectives can be minimized by memorizing training samples, our formulation excludes such behavior. We also introduce a direct memorization metric to verify that the learned projection generates new samples rather than reproducing the training set.

</details>


### [10] [The Cross-Context Threshold Test: Detecting Discrimination Under Environmental Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28259)
*Jun Yuan,Xinyue Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究环境变化下歧视检测的阈值测试问题，提出跨情境阈值检验以保证分布不变性与阈值单调衰减，在纽约拦查数据上获得更可靠的阈值与偏差检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准阈值测试在"夜色面纱"（VoD）场景中将日光与黑暗数据分开处理，违反两项关键假设：风险分布随情境漂移、阈值任意波动，因此检测结果不可靠。

Method: 提出跨情境阈值检验（cross-context threshold test），对多情境联合建模，施加分布不变性约束与阈值单调衰减约束；使用纽约市stop-and-frisk数据并结合合成实验进行验证。

Result: 新模型给出更可靠的阈值估计，提升偏差检测性能，并与VoD测试的反事实逻辑保持一致；合成实验进一步验证其有效性。

Conclusion: 跨情境阈值框架可推广至任何环境情境影响决策的公平性审计场景。

Abstract: We study how threshold tests for detecting discrimination under environmental shifts, focusing on the Veil-of-Darkness (VoD) setting where visibility changes between daylight and darkness. We show that standard threshold tests, when applied separately to daylight and darkness data, violate key assumptions: risk distributions drift across contexts and thresholds fluctuate arbitrarily. We propose a cross-context threshold test that enforces distributional invariance and monotonic threshold decay. Using New York City stop-and-frisk data and synthetic experiments, we demonstrate that this model yields more reliable thresholds, improves bias detection, and aligns with the counterfactual logic of the VoD test. Our framework generalizes to fairness auditing whenever environmental context influences decisions.

</details>


### [11] [FoGConcept: Identifying Interpretable Movement Concepts for Freezing-of-Gait Detection from Multi-Scale IMU Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28262)
*Chuntian Chi,John Clapham,GinaMari Blackwell,Leslie Cloud,Ingrid Pretzer-Aboff,Yanfu Zhang,Huajie Shao,Gang Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出FoGConcept，一个基于机制的可解释框架，从多尺度IMU信号中学习九个人类可理解的运动概念（如步态启动、节律性双侧步态、原地颤抖、拖步、转身），这些概念与模型冻结步态（FoG）决策功能相关，专家识别率达88.9%，同时检测F1达94.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于IMU的FoG检测模型虽准确，但无法用人类可理解的运动概念解释其预测，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 识别并证实多尺度FoG模型编码的九个运动概念；验证其跨受试者复现性、与决策的功能关联及短/长时域上的涌现专门化；在新数据集、更宽时域和更深骨干网络上复现概念发现流程；提出FoGConcept机制型可解释框架。

Result: 领域专家独立识别出88.9%的概念；模型FoG检测F1为94.2%；更宽时域与更深骨干下仍可复现稳定的概念集合。

Conclusion: 准确的FoG检测可通过人类可识别的运动模式加以理解，揭示了检测模型区分FoG与非FoG运动所学到的内容。

Abstract: Freezing of gait (FoG) is a disabling motor symptom of Parkinson's disease, characterized by an episodic inability to initiate or sustain effective stepping. Current inertial measurement unit (IMU)-based models can detect FoG accurately, but they fail to explain their predictions in terms of human-understandable movement concepts. A major contribution of this work is to identify nine human-understandable movement concepts encoded by a multi-scale FoG model, including gait initiation, rhythmic bilateral gait, trembling in place, shuffling steps, and pivot turns. These concepts recur across subjects, are functionally linked to the model's FoG decisions, and exhibit emergent specialization across short and long temporal contexts. Repeating the concept-discovery procedure on a new FoG dataset, wider temporal contexts, and a deeper backbone recovers the same comparatively stable set of movement concepts. Accordingly, we propose FoGConcept, a mechanism-based interpretability framework for learning movement concepts from multi-scale IMU signals. Domain experts independently recognize 88.9% of the identified concepts, while the proposed model achieves an F1 score of 94.2% for FoG detection. Our findings show that accurate FoG detection can be understood through human-recognizable movement patterns, providing new insight into what a detection model learns to distinguish FoG from non-FoG movement.

</details>


### [12] [Failure Modes and Data-Efficient Remedies for Transformer-Based Prediction of Viscoplastic Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28267)
*Maedeh Saberi,Hugo Franca,Maziyar Jalaal,Safa Jamali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了基于Transformer的神经算子RheOFormer在稳态Bingham槽道流中的适用性，识别其失效模式，并通过对数速度表示、梯度感知损失与策略性数据增强加以改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黏塑性流动因屈服面、梯度间断及屈服/未屈服区强非线性转变而难以快速准确预测；RheOFormer等神经算子虽在非牛顿流代理建模中展现潜力，但其对黏塑性系统的适用性与恢复真实流动的能力尚不清楚。

Method: 将问题限定为简单几何与本构模型（稳态Bingham槽道流），考察数据集结构、数值范围和输入表示对参数空间内插与外推能力的影响；针对识别出的失效模式（栓塞主导流、超出训练速度尺度或流动结构的条件），引入对数速度表示与梯度感知损失，并通过少量跨越缺失物理区域的策略性样本进行训练集增强。

Result: 对数速度表示和梯度感知损失显著提升了基线失效工况下的精度；少量策略性样本显著降低外推误差；单个部分发展阶段的快照即可给出准确稳态预测，且模型优于简单复制输入的基线。

Conclusion: 合适的场表示与参数空间覆盖对数据高效地神经算子预测黏塑性流动至关重要；RheOFormer经针对性改进后可有效处理含低梯度栓塞区的黏塑性流动。

Abstract: Fast and accurate prediction of viscoplastic flows remains a challenge in computational rheology due to yield surfaces, discontinuous gradients, and strongly nonlinear transitions between yielded and unyielded regions. Transformer-based neural operators such as RheOFormer have recently shown strong potential for surrogate modeling of non-Newtonian flows. However, their applicability to viscoplastic systems and their efficacy in recovering realistic flows remain poorly understood. In this work, we present the first systematic investigation of RheOFormer applied to steady Bingham channel flow. By isolating the problem to a simple geometry and constitutive model, we examine how dataset structure, numerical value range, and input representation govern the model's ability to interpolate and extrapolate within the parameter space. The data regimes in which RheOFormer remains accurate and those in which errors emerge are identified, particularly for plug-dominated flows and for conditions outside the velocity scales or flow structures represented during training. Upon identifying these failure modes, targeted enhancements to the input representation and loss formulation are introduced to better predict low-gradient plug regions. Logarithmic velocity representations and gradient-aware loss formulations substantially improve accuracy in challenging regimes where the baseline formulation fails. In addition, extrapolation errors are reduced by augmenting the training set with only a few strategically selected cases spanning the missing physical regimes. Finally, a single partially developed snapshot suffices for an accurate steady-state prediction, and the model outperforms simply copying its input throughout flow development. These results highlight the importance of appropriate field representation and parameter-space coverage for data-efficient neural-operator prediction of viscoplastic flows.

</details>


### [13] [SONATI: Spatio-temporal ocean neighbourhood asymmetric transformer for imputation of sparse, tabular data with uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28270)
*Yvonne Jenniges,Boris Peter Koch,Maike Sonnewald,Sebastian Maneth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SONATI——一种基于局部时空邻域的掩码分解式时间-空间Transformer，用于稀疏表格型海洋观测数据的缺失值插补，RMSE较经典及Transformer方法至少降低12.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋观测数据多为表格形式、高度稀疏且具有时空结构，而KNN、MissForest等经典插补方法无法利用时空相关性；全局注意力Transformer计算开销过大。

Method: SONATI采用预计算的局部时间与空间邻域，替代全局注意力以降低复杂度；采用分解式时间-空间注意力与组合式（地理+逐样本）掩码策略；通过负对数似然损失提供偶然不确定性，通过多次前向传播的Monte Carlo Dropout提供认知不确定性；并通过注意力类型与掩码策略的系统消融确定最终架构。

Result: 在北大西洋大型观测数据集（位温、盐度、氧、硝酸盐、硅酸盐、磷酸盐）上，RMSE较经典与Transformer基线至少改善12.5%；组合掩码较仅逐样本掩码降低误差14.3%；不确定性校准中等（观测点误校准面积0.074±0.012），且与插补误差中等相关。

Conclusion: 局部时空邻域注意力可有效建模稀疏海洋表格数据，注意力类型与掩码策略显著影响精度与场平滑性；分解式时空注意力加组合掩码为最优配置。当前仅在规则网格数据上验证，非结构化数据适用性尚待检验。

Abstract: Missing value imputation is often a prerequisite for downstream analyses. In oceanography, observational data are frequently structured in a tabular manner, highly sparse and exhibit spatio-temporal structures that classical imputers such as k-nearest neighbours or MissForest are not suited to exploit. We present the Spatio-temporal Ocean Neighbourhood Asymmetric Transformer for Imputation (SONATI), a masked, factorised time-space transformer operating on pre-computed, local time and space neighbourhoods, which reduces computational complexity compared to global attention. SONATI is designed to work with tabular data and is thus in principle able to work on both gridded and unstructured data, though evaluation here was performed on gridded North Atlantic data only. It provides aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties through a negative log-likelihood loss and multiple forward passes with Monte Carlo dropout. Systematic ablations on attention type and masking strategies guided the final architecture design. The results showed that SONATI outperformed classical and transformer-based methods by at least 12.5% in terms of root mean squared error (RMSE) on a large observational oceanographic dataset with key biogeochemical and physical parameters in the North Atlantic (potential temperature, salinity, oxygen, nitrate, silicate and phosphate). Attention type and masking strategy strongly affected imputation accuracy and smoothness of the produced oceanographic fields. Best results were achieved by factorised time-space attention and combined geographic and per-sample masking. The combined masking strategy reduced the imputation error by 14.3% compared to per-sample masking only. Uncertainties were moderately well calibrated (miscalibration area at observed locations $0.074\pm0.012$) and moderately correlated with the imputation error.

</details>


### [14] [What Do Differentiable Rule Learners Need? FLARE: Fuzzy Rules with Additive Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28272)
*Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLARE：一种可微规则学习器，核心发现是规则分类器性能主要来自规则加权而非规则本身，据此学习简单规则+可学习变量编码，在表格数据上大幅超越同类规则学习器并接近随机森林。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则学习器因可读、可问责而适用于需要透明度的场景，但基于straight-through等松弛的可微规则学习器性能常落后于经典规则学习器与黑箱分类器。作者希望拆解规则分类器中哪些部分影响性能、梯度训练对每个部分学得如何。

Method: 分析性实验：用随机规则配学习到的权重对比已发表的可微规则学习器，定位性能来源（规则加权为主）。据此提出FLARE（Fuzzy Learned-Antecedent Rule Evidence）：学习简单规则（模糊学习前件），由规则组合形成最终决策；并为含数千类别的变量提出可学习编码。

Result: 在OpenML-CC18的42个数据集上，FLARE比对比的规则学习器高7.69–12.38个macro-F1点，与随机森林相当，低于调优梯度提升树1.00–1.11点；TabArena的38个分类任务上，与随机森林同级并领先其他规则学习器。作为概念瓶颈模型的概念到标签头，在6个设置中5个为最佳规则头。图数据上在PubMed和EMOGI有竞争力，但在Cora和Citeseer上落后于GNN。

Conclusion: 规则分类器性能主体来自规则加权；只要把加权学好，即使规则简单也能取得强性能。FLARE在表格分类上兼具可读性与竞争力、可作为概念瓶颈模型的高效规则头，但在部分图数据上仍不及专用图神经网络。

Abstract: Rule learners are used where transparency and accountability are required, because they give practitioners patterns they can read. Learning rules is a discrete problem, so neurosymbolic pipelines that use rules as explanations rely on relaxations such as the straight-through estimator. However, these differentiable rule learners often lag behind both classical rule learners and black-box classifiers in terms of performance. We study which parts of a rule-based classifier affect performance, and how well gradient-based training learns each of them. We found that most of the performance comes from how the rules are weighted: random rules with learned weights are already more accurate than every published differentiable rule learner we tested. Based on our results, we propose FLARE (Fuzzy Learned-Antecedent Rule Evidence), a differentiable rule learner that learns simple rules that combine to make the final decision. We also propose a learned encoding for variables with thousands of categories. We test FLARE on tabular data, using 42 datasets of OpenML-CC18 and the 38 classification tasks of TabArena. On CC18, FLARE exceeds the compared rule learners by 7.69 to 12.38 macro-F1 points and performs comparably to random forests, while remaining 1.00 to 1.11 points below tuned gradient-boosted trees. On TabArena it ranks at the same level as random forests and ahead of the other rule learners. As the concept-to-label head of a concept bottleneck model, FLARE is the best rule-based head in five of six settings. On graphs, it is competitive on PubMed and EMOGI but falls behind graph neural networks on Cora and Citeseer. Our code is available at https://github.com/Fuminides/FLARE

</details>


### [15] [Scalable Beckmann Maps Improve One-step Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28286)
*Ulrik Unneberg,Shiyi Wang,Lee Cheuk-Kit,Felix Berg,Boris Kozinsky,Yilun Du,Michael S. Albergo,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Scalable Beckmann Maps (SBM)，把无分类器引导（CFG）与可扩展训练配方引入 Beckmann Transport Models（BTM），在单次网络评估下实现高质量一步生成，ImageNet 256x256 一步 FID 1.6，GenEval 0.86。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BTM 无需教师、蒸馏阶段或预训练特征即可端到端学习一步生成映射，但缺少现代多步生成器质量背后的两个关键要素：分类器无关引导（CFG）与可扩展的训练配方。挑战在于 Beckmann map 本身不产生条件与无条件的速度/得分预测，CFG 无直接对象。

Method: 核心观察：BTM 的 current 是一个自治去噪场的正倍数，因而 CFG 可像作用于 velocity 一样作用于该去噪场。精确去噪器下得到 guided BTM；实际中用映射自身的条件预测与 null 预测替代去噪器，无需额外网络，并刻画了由此引入的偏差。证明引导更新与引导守恒方程一致，且引导可移动盆地边界，使更多噪声流向条件偏好的模态。在表示自编码器的潜空间中从零训练，不使用教师或特征损失。

Result: ImageNet 256x256 一步 FID 1.6，无教师、无特征损失，FLOPs 比 Improved-MeanFlow 基线少 3.2 倍；文生图单次网络评估 GenEval 0.86；作为世界模型（无引导训练）时，仅训练配方即在两个机器人操作基准的全部五项指标上超过 DriftWorld，训练算力少 1.9–2.8 倍，且无需预训练辅助特征编码器。

Conclusion: SBM 将 CFG 与可扩展训练配方成功移植到一步式 BTM，证明在单次网络评估下可同时获得强引导效果与可扩展性，并可推广到文生图与世界模型等任务。

Abstract: Beckmann Transport Models (BTM) learn one-step generative maps end-to-end from data, without a teacher, a distillation stage, or pretrained features. We introduce Scalable Beckmann Maps (SBM), which bring to BTM the two ingredients behind the quality of modern multistep generators: classifier-free guidance (CFG) and a training recipe that scales, while keeping sampling at a single network evaluation. CFG combines conditional and unconditional velocity or score predictions, which a Beckmann map does not have. Our guidance rests on one observation: the BTM current is a positive multiple of an autonomous denoising field, to which CFG applies as it does to a velocity. With exact denoisers this gives guided BTM. In practice we substitute the map's own conditional and null predictions for the denoisers, which requires no additional network and introduces a bias that we characterize. We show that the guided update is consistent with a guided conservation equation, and that guidance can move the boundaries between basins, sending more noise to the modes a condition favors. Trained from scratch in the latent of a representation autoencoder, SBM reaches a one-step FID of 1.6 on ImageNet 256x256 without a teacher or a feature loss, outperforming the Improved-MeanFlow baseline with 3.2x fewer FLOPs. In text-to-image generation it reaches a GenEval score of 0.86 with one network evaluation. As a world model, where we train without guidance, the recipe alone outperforms DriftWorld on all five metrics of two robot-manipulation benchmarks, with 1.9-2.8x less training compute and no pretrained auxiliary feature encoder.

</details>


### [16] [Benchmarking EEG feature families for ADHD research in children and adolescents: performance and explainability across attention tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28299)
*Javier Sanchis,Jorge García-Carrasco,Álvaro Navarro,Tahar Kechadi,Juan Trujillo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较了七类EEG特征族及四种融合方案在注意任务中的判别能力与解释稳定性。在最佳任务情境下，最高配置平均AUROC为0.773；连接性特征较频谱特征AUROC平均高0.2083（调整后p=0.0036），F4-AF4 beta频段相干性在五种选定流程中均居首并被每次拟合选中，但无通用最优分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADHD表现异质，诊断依赖临床与发育评估；EEG指标有望补充临床与行为刻画，但特征定义与评估方法不统一导致结果难以横向比较，因此需要统一框架下比较不同特征族并检验其解释的稳定性。

Method: 基于两款严肃游戏构建四个重叠的注意任务队列（各123–140人）；比较七类特征族与四种组合；采用重复嵌套的参与者留出评估判别招募组；以配对参与者簇自助法在任务内（72个对比）与融合（18个对比）两个族内做多重比较校正；对预测结果筛选出的8种配置，在未校准的留出原生概率上进行解释分析。

Result: 最强任务情境下最高配置三次重复的平均AUROC为0.773（固定预测95%区间0.692–0.854，未对取最大值校正），精确率0.740、敏感度0.556、特异度0.861；该任务中连接性减频谱特征的配对AUROC差在六种分类器与8 s/16 s时长上平均+0.2083（逐点95% CI 0.1076–0.3072，调整后p=0.0036）；F4-AF4 beta相干性在五种选定流程的重复级归因中均居首且每次拟合均被选中；其他情境判别力较弱、领先输入保留不一致；无普适最优分类器。

Conclusion: 连接性特征（尤其F4-AF4 beta相干性）值得独立重复验证，但不构成通用特征推荐；结果为探索性，不能确立因果性任务效应、疾病机制或诊断效度。

Abstract: Attention Deficit Hyperactivity Disorder varies in presentation. Diagnosis requires clinical and developmental assessment. Reproducible electroencephalography measures may complement clinical and behavioural characterisation, but differing feature definitions and evaluation methods hinder comparison. We compared seven feature families and four combinations across attention tasks and assessed the stability of held-out explanations. Two serious games yielded four overlapping attention-task cohorts of 123-140 participants each. Repeated nested participant-held-out evaluation assessed recruitment-group discrimination. Exploratory paired participant-cluster bootstrap tests corrected for multiplicity in two separate families: 72 individual-task contrasts and 18 fusion contrasts. For eight configurations selected after inspecting predictive results, we explained held-out native probabilities before calibration. In the strongest task context, the highest configuration mean AUROC over three repetitions was 0.773, with a fixed-prediction 95% interval of 0.692-0.854 unadjusted for selecting the maximum. Precision, sensitivity and specificity were 0.740, 0.556 and 0.861. In that task, the paired AUROC difference for connectivity minus spectral features averaged +0.2083 across six classifiers and the common 8 s and 16 s durations, with pointwise 95% CI 0.1076-0.3072 and adjusted p = 0.0036. F4-AF4 beta-band coherence led repetition-level attribution summaries in five selected procedures and was selected in every fit. Other contexts showed weaker discrimination and less consistent retention of leading scientific inputs. No general classifier winner emerged. Connectivity and its recurring coherence input warrant independent replication, but do not justify a universal feature recommendation. These exploratory findings do not establish causal task effects, disease mechanisms or diagnostic validity.

</details>


### [17] [AV-FLARE: Feature-Level Reliability Estimation for Multimodal Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28327)
*Ines Jemmali,Alaa Nfissi,Wassim Bouachir,Nizar Bouguila*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AV-FLARE 提出在多模态情感识别中显式估计时间与特征维度的细粒度可靠性分数，用于调制特征表示，并结合以文本为语义参考的分层跨模态注意力融合音视频，在 IEMOCAP 上相较无可靠性基线提升至 90.81% 准确率与 81.69% F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态情感识别依赖学习到的特征表示与注意力机制隐式加权信息，未显式纳入输入可靠性；而音频噪声、视觉遮挡以及情感线索稀疏、事件驱动的特性使可靠性建模成为必要。

Method: 在时间建模之前显式估计时间与特征两维度的细粒度可靠性分数并用于调制特征：(i) 与分类目标联合训练的监督网络提供任务驱动可靠性；(ii) 无监督高斯混合模型建模全局特征分布提供分布感知可靠性；二者结合。模态融合采用分层跨模态注意力，以文本特征作为语义参考引导音视频融合。

Result: 在 IEMOCAP 数据集上，AV-FLARE 相对无可靠性基线取得 90.81% 准确率与 81.69% F1，分别提升 +4.41% 与 +8.56%。

Conclusion: 在所评估设定下，显式引入输入可靠性估计可提升多模态情感识别的鲁棒性；但结论限于单一数据集与实验条件，泛化性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition (MMER) systems typically rely on learned feature representations and attention mechanisms that implicitly weight information across time and modalities. However, these mechanisms do not explicitly incorporate input reliability, despite the presence of noise in audio, occlusions in visual data, and the sparse and event-driven nature of affective cues. We propose AV-FLARE, a framework that explicitly estimates fine-grained reliability scores across both temporal and feature dimensions prior to temporal modeling and uses them to modulate feature representations. Reliability is computed using two complementary components: (i) a supervised network trained jointly with the classification objective, and (ii) an unsupervised Gaussian Mixture Model that models global feature distributions. These signals are combined to provide both task-driven and distribution-aware reliability estimates. To integrate modalities, we introduce a hierarchical cross-modal attention mechanism in which textual features are used to guide audio-visual fusion as a semantic reference modality. Experiments on the IEMOCAP dataset show that AV-FLARE improves over a no-reliability baseline, achieving 90.81\% accuracy and 81.69\% F1-score, corresponding to gains of +4.41% and +8.56%, respectively. These results suggest that incorporating explicit input reliability estimation may improve robustness in multimodal emotion recognition under the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [18] [TabK: Amortized Bayesian Estimation of the Number of Clusters in Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28335)
*Mohammadreza Bakhtyari,Bogdan Mazoure,Renato Cordeiro de Amorim,Guillaume Rabusseau,Vladimir Makarenkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TabK：一种零样本、置换不变的Transformer架构，将表格数据聚类数k的估计建模为摊销贝叶斯推断问题，通过分位数特征编码器（QFE）单次前向传播完成估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统k估计依赖计算昂贵的内部聚类有效性指标（CVI）网格搜索或自适应聚类算法，且受特定统计假设约束，难以通用且高效。

Method: TabK采用零样本、置换不变的Transformer架构；引入量化特征编码器（QFE），从经验边缘分布中提取与位置无关、由内容衍生的表示；在多样化合成先验上学习泛化结构偏置，以摊销贝叶斯推断方式实现单个集成成员一次前向传播估计k。

Result: 在50个真实世界基准上优于所有对比方法，MAE为1.04（最接近竞争者2.14）；平均推理时间低于1秒，比TabClustPFN快2倍，比Silhouette、Calinski-Harabasz等传统CVI快30倍。

Conclusion: TabK证明摊销贝叶斯推断结合置换不变Transformer可高效、准确地估计聚类数，兼具精度与计算效率优势。

Abstract: Estimating the number of clusters, $k$, is a fundamental prerequisite for most clustering algorithms. Traditionally, this estimation has relied on computationally expensive grid searches over internal cluster validity indices (CVIs) or adaptive clustering algorithms, both constrained by specific statistical assumptions. We introduce TabK, a zero-shot, permutation-invariant transformer architecture that frames the estimation of $k$ as an amortized Bayesian inference problem for tabular data. TabK leverages a novel Quantile Feature Encoder (QFE) to extract position-free, content-derived representations from empirical marginal distributions. By learning a generalized structural bias over a diverse synthetic prior, TabK estimates the number of clusters in a single forward pass per ensemble member. Evaluated across 50 diverse real-world benchmarks, TabK outperforms all competing methods, achieving a mean absolute error (MAE) value of 1.04 compared to 2.14 for the closest competitor. TabK also achieves the lowest average inference time, producing estimates in less than one second on average and running $2\times$ faster than TabClustPFN and $30\times$ faster than traditional CVIs such as Silhouette and Calinski-Harabasz. Code is available at https://github.com/mrbakhtyari/TabK.

</details>


### [19] [Which Loans, Not How Many: Exactly Level-Invariant, Anytime-Valid Monitoring of Deployed Risk Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28342)
*Xin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在再融资波动背景下，提出分离"截距（水平）漂移"与"斜率（排序关系）漂移"检验框架，用固定起点斜率乘积与重启水平累积和对Freddie Mac抵押贷款提前还款模型做持续监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 再融资浪潮会改变提前还款数量，却未必改变评分与提前还款之间的真实关系；传统整体AUC或校准指标把两类漂移混为一谈，无法判断模型排序能力是否真的失效。

Method: 逻辑模型中按月计数条件化以从似然中消去截距；构造固定起点斜率乘积检验标定斜率下界（阈值20时二次穿越概率≤5%）；重启水平累积和跟踪截距容差偏离；置信序列估计常数斜率，重启-相交规则侦测斜率变化；采用连续混合先验避免离散先验的离网宽度下限。

Result: Freddie Mac数据上，固定斜率重抽样解释开发集月AUC方差78%（95%区间57–100%）、留出集70%（48–100%）；新起点下重启水平累积和两条路径均穿越，固定起点斜率乘积均未超阈值；重启-相交规则对冻结模型于2018年4月示警向下变化，对维护模型补充运行于2019年8月示警向上变化。

Conclusion: 月AUC波动主要源自固定斜率下的样本/计数变动而非排序关系失效；所提监测规则可在不误报斜率漂移的前提下识别水平漂移，为抵押模型持续验证提供可操作的分层诊断工具。

Abstract: A refinancing wave can change the number of mortgage prepayments without changing the relationship between scores and prepayment. In a logistic model, conditioning on the monthly count removes the intercept from the likelihood of which loans prepay. Our fixed-start slope product tests a lower bound on the calibration slope. At threshold 20 its probability of ever crossing under the null is at most 5%. The restarted level sum tracks departures from intercept tolerance. A confidence sequence estimates a constant slope, and the restart-intersection rule detects changes in that slope. A continuous mixture avoids the off-grid width floor of a discrete prior. On Freddie Mac loans, fixed-slope resampling reproduces 78% of maintained-model monthly AUC variance in development and 70% in the holdout (95% intervals 57-100% and 48-100%). From fresh holdout starts, the restarted level sum crosses on both paths, and the fixed-start slope product stays below threshold on both. The restart-intersection rule signals a downward change for the frozen model in April 2018 and an upward change for the maintained model in a supplementary run in August 2019.

</details>


### [20] [Toward Adversarially Robust World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28345)
*Ethan Rathbun,Jonathan Nöther,Christopher Amato,Alina Oprea,Goran Radanović*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对具身AI中潜空间动态世界模型的脆弱性，提出两种攻击（状态幻觉攻击与动态偏移攻击）和一套新的理论分析视角，指出鲁棒的潜空间编码器是鲁棒世界模型的关键，并在四个世界模型家族上通过对抗性编码器训练验证了鲁棒性显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间动态世界模型兼具在线动作规划、安全策略评估与可扩展策略学习等多种用途，但正因如此，它成为一个脆弱且集中化的单点故障：一旦模型被攻破，会同时影响规划、评估与训练。因此在广泛部署于具身AI系统前，必须充分理解其安全性与鲁棒性。

Method: （1）提出两种直接作用于下游系统的新型攻击：状态幻觉攻击（state hallucination）与动态偏移攻击（dynamic shift）；（2）提出一种新的理论视角来分析潜空间动态世界模型的鲁棒性，并通过分析识别出鲁棒的潜空间编码器是鲁棒世界模型的关键组件；（3）在Dino World Model、Le World Model、r2Dreamer、Cosmos 3四个潜空间动态世界模型家族上进行实证验证，采用对抗性编码器模型训练提升鲁棒性。

Result: 理论分析表明鲁棒潜在编码器是鲁棒世界模型的核心；在Dino World Model、Le World Model、r2Dreamer和Cosmos 3四个模型家族上的实验显示，通过对抗性编码器训练可显著提升世界模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 世界模型的集中式脆弱性使其成为具身AI的关键安全风险；鲁棒的潜空间编码器是构建鲁棒世界模型的关键，对抗性编码器训练是一种有效的鲁棒性增强手段，为世界模型的安全部署提供了理论与实证基础。

Abstract: Latent dynamic world models have proven to be highly versatile tools for embodied artificial intelligence systems, enabling online action planning, safe policy evaluation, and scalable policy learning. However, this versatility makes world models a vulnerable and centralized point of failure as a compromised model can impact planning, evaluation, and training at once. Therefore it is critical that the security and robustness properties of world models are properly understood before they are universally deployed in embodied AI systems. In this work we take a unifying approach towards this understanding, developing two novel attacks against world models, named state hallucination and dynamic shift attacks, aimed directly at impacting downstream systems, and proposing a new theoretical lens through which to study the robustness of latent dynamic world models. Through our analysis we identify robust latent space encoders as the key component of robust world models. We empirically validate this finding on four separate latent dynamic world model families, studying Dino World Model, Le World Model, r2Dreamer, and Cosmos 3, demonstrating significant improvements in robustness through adversarial encoder model training.

</details>


### [21] [Coupling-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Co-Evolving Graph Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28351)
*Mina Kim,Guanghui Lan,Benoit Montreuil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将"需求侧设施图—供给侧网络图"双方各自运营、收益互相依赖的问题形式化为Co-Evolving Graph Game (CEGG)，提出CGA-PMD（跨图注意力+策略镜像下降）方法，在三种奖励域与五个真实城市网络上验证其可扩展性与稳定性优势，并从理论上给出KL正则更新的迭代一致累积漂移界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建氢站网络、充电网或疫苗配送体系需要协调两张分别运营的图（需求侧设施、供给侧网络），任一运营方收益都取决于对方选择。该问题介于设施选址、互依赖网络分析与图多智能体强化学习之间，但三者均未联合处理，因而需要独立形式化与专门算法。

Method: 形式化CEGG博弈；提出CGA-PMD：跨图注意力使每个智能体仅读取与其自身结构耦合的伙伴图节点（掩码式选择性信息交换），配合策略镜像下降更新以获得稳定训练；与own-only、无掩码注意力、PPO类变体对照，覆盖三种奖励域与多种实际网络规模；理论侧分析KL正则更新与clip更新的漂移性质。

Result: 方法优劣由耦合结构决定：小规模下多数方法相当；规模增大或耦合变得不规则时，仅CGA-PMD保持可靠，在五个真实城市网络上均取得最高成功次数，而own-only、无掩码注意力与PPO变体以各自不同的方式退化。理论上，KL正则更新具有迭代一致的累积漂移上界，而clip更新在裁剪不生效的迭代上不存在此类界。

Conclusion: CEGG是有独立价值的问题设定；结构化（掩码）跨图注意力加镜像下降更新在规模与耦合不规则性上升时具备鲁棒性优势；KL正则化的稳定训练性质可由累积漂移界刻画，clip机制缺乏对应保证。代码已开源。

Abstract: Building hydrogen station networks, electric vehicle charging grids, or vaccine distribution systems requires coordinating two separately operated graphs, demand-side facilities and the supply-side networks they depend on, where each operator's payoff depends on the other's choices. We formalize this as the Co-Evolving Graph Game (CEGG), a setting that sits between facility location, interdependent-network analysis, and graph-based multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) but is not jointly addressed by any of them. We propose Cross-Graph Attention with Policy Mirror Descent (CGA-PMD), pairing cross-graph attention, which lets each agent read only the partner-graph nodes structurally coupled to its own, with a policy mirror descent update for stable training. Across three reward domains and a range of practical network scales, we find that the structure of the coupling selects which method wins: at small scale most methods are competitive, but as scale grows or coupling becomes irregular, CGA-PMD is the one method that stays reliable: it attains the highest success count on each of five real city networks, while own-only, unmasked-attention, and Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)-based variants degrade in distinct ways. Our theoretical analysis addresses the optimizer side: the Kullback-Leibler (KL)-regularized update admits an iteration-uniform cumulative drift bound, whereas the clip update admits no such bound on the iterations where the clip does not bind. The implementation is available at https://github.com/Mina-Kim-Github/cegg-cga-pmd.

</details>


### [22] [HME-PFD: Heterogeneous Mixture-of-Experts Privileged Feature Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29094)
*Wen Xu,Xiaomin Zhang,Yucheng Yang,Danny Liu,Xunlong Gui,Fei Huang,Sha Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HME-PFD，将特权特征引入MoE学生模型的路由模块（硬编码+可学习路由），使学生在特权特征覆盖不全、分布异构时动态分配专家，在两项转化预测任务上稳定优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广告推荐中特权特征蒸馏（PFD）可让学生模型利用仅训练期可见的特权特征，但真实场景下特权特征覆盖率有限、分布异构，导致样本对蒸馏与学习的重要性不一，现有方法难以有效处理这种异构数据。

Method: 在基于MoE的学生模型中，将特权特征直接用于专家路由模块；同时设计硬编码路由（依据特权特征可用性与取值规则分配）与可学习路由，使学生依据特权特征的可用性和数值动态确定专家分配，从而区分高信号与低信号样本。

Result: 在两个后点击转化预测任务上的大量实验表明，HME-PFD均一致优于基线方法；即便特权特征不完整或稀疏，利用特权特征进行专家路由仍能带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 将特权特征用于学生模型的专家路由是应对特权特征部分可观测、分布异构的有效手段，能提升蒸馏与整体模型效果。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) is widely used to transfer knowledge from large teacher models to smaller student models, facilitating efficient serving of machine learning systems. In the context of ads recommendation, privileged features distillation (PFD) extends KD by allowing the teacher model to leverage privileged features, i.e., features available only during training, to enhance distillation performance. However, in real-world scenarios, privileged features often have limited coverage and exhibit heterogeneous distributions, resulting in training examples of varying importance for distillation and model learning. We propose Heterogeneous Mixture-of-Experts Privileged Feature Distillation (HME-PFD), a novel approach that incorporates privileged features into the routing module of a Mixture-of-Experts-based student model. Our method introduces both hard-coded and learnable routing modules, enabling the student to dynamically determine expert assignment based on the availability and values of privileged features. This design allows the student model to better handle the heterogeneous data scenario, where privileged features are partially observed, by differentiating between high- and low-signal samples. Extensive experiments on two post-click conversion prediction tasks demonstrate that HME-PFD consistently outperforms baseline methods. Our results show that leveraging privileged features for expert routing in the student model leads to improved model performance, even when privileged features are incomplete or sparse.

</details>


### [23] [Cross-Site Transformers Are Robust Heart Rate Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28366)
*Mason Kadem,Rong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Cross-Site Transformer，通过将多部位PPG融合限制为部位间交叉注意力来注入血流动力学先验，在WildPPG上取得新的单模型SOTA，并使融合路径可审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单点PPG心率估计在真实运动下严重退化；多部位PPG虽可缓解，但现有模型把各部位当作普通传感器通道融合，未利用"同一心搏沿动脉树传播、在不同部位表现为延迟且变换的副本"这一血流动力学结构。

Method: 设计Cross-Site Transformer：将多传感器融合约束为PPG部位之间的交叉注意力，形成血流动力学启发的归纳偏置；每个交叉注意力头对应一条有向部位通路，因此可借助因果重采样消融逐条隔离并检验信息流方向。

Result: 在WildPPG（目前最大真实场景基准）上取得新的单模型SOTA，误差较此前最佳方法降低21%，较最佳先前Transformer降低35%；消融胸到腕通路使误差翻倍，而反向通路影响远小。

Conclusion: 将血流动力学结构编码进注意力路由，既提升真实场景传感鲁棒性，又实现对多传感器融合的严格审计。

Abstract: Single-site heart-rate estimation from photoplethysmography (PPG) degrades sharply under real-world motion. Multi-site PPG provides a natural solution, but existing models fuse sites as generic sensor channels rather than exploiting their hemodynamic structure: each heartbeat propagates along the arterial tree, appearing across body sites as delayed and transformed copies of the same cardiac event. We propose the Cross-Site Transformer, which introduces a hemodynamically informed inductive bias by restricting multi-sensor fusion to cross-attention between PPG sites. On WildPPG, the largest in-the-wild benchmark available, our model establishes a new single-model state of the art, reducing error by 21% over the previous best and 35% over the best prior transformer. The same cross-site restriction makes the trained network directly auditable. Because each cross-attention head lies on one directed pathway between sites, causal resample ablation can isolate and test each direction of information flow. Ablating the chest-to-wrist pathway doubles error, whereas the reverse costs far less. Encoding hemodynamic structure into attention routing thus improves real-world sensing robustness and enables rigorous auditing of multi-sensor fusion.

</details>


### [24] [Data-Dependent Probability Estimation via Sample Amplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28399)
*Vitaly Feldman,Satyen Kale,Mahbod Majid,Kunal Talwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"在看到数据后才选定的事件/查询"的概率估计问题（data-dependent query），证明在已知选择规则的前提下，$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{d})$ 个样本即可达到常数均方误差，匹配已知下界（对数因子内），解决了 Feldman et al. (arXiv:2610.41244) 提出的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对事后（看到数据后）选定的事件，其经验概率可能严重误导；而若要以常数精度学习整个分布，在规模为 $d$ 的域上需要 $\Theta(d)$ 个样本。因此核心问题是：能否在不使用新样本的情况下，仅凭已有样本估计数据依赖查询的概率，并降低样本复杂度。

Method: 提出新的估计框架，将 sample amplification（arXiv:1904.12053、arXiv:2201.04315）与基于在线优化的分布学习相结合； estimator 支持任意 $[0,1]$ 值查询，且在给定规则的评估访问下可在多项式时间内运行。进一步将框架推广到均值未知、协方差为单位阵的高斯分布上的数据依赖查询，并分析了一个"平滑"变体问题。

Result: 样本复杂度 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{d})$ 达到常数 MSE，与下界在对数因子内匹配。具体界：$d\le n$ 时 MSE 为 $O(d^{3/4}/n)$，$d>n$ 时为 $\widetilde{O}(d^{1/4}/\sqrt{n})$；并给出下界 $\Omega(\min\{1,\sqrt{d}/n\})$。离散分布下，若样本量从 $\{n,\ldots,2n-1\}$ 均匀选取，则 $n=O(\log d)$ 即足以达到常数 MSE。

Conclusion: 数据依赖查询的概率估计可在显著少于全分布学习的样本量下完成，$\sqrt{d}$ 阶样本复杂度为本质最优；平滑（随机化样本量）变体使问题难度呈指数级下降。

Abstract: Can we estimate the probability of an event chosen after seeing the data, without using any fresh samples? Its empirical probability can be arbitrarily misleading, while learning the entire distribution to constant accuracy requires $Θ(d)$ samples on a domain of size $d$. We show that $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{d})$ samples suffice for constant mean squared error, provided the rule choosing the event is known. More generally, our estimator handles arbitrary $[0,1]$-valued queries and runs in polynomial time given evaluation access to the rule. This sample complexity matches the known lower bound up to logarithmic factors and resolves the open question posed by Feldman, Kale, Raman, Talwar, and Tewari (arXiv:2610.41244).
  Our estimation framework relies on a new combination of sample amplification (arXiv:1904.12053 and arXiv:2201.04315) with distribution learning via online optimization. Our estimator achieves mean squared error $O(d^{3/4}/n)$ when $d\leq n$ and $\widetilde{O}(d^{1/4}/\sqrt{n})$ when $d>n$. We complement this with a lower bound of $Ω(\min\{1,\sqrt{d}/n\})$ on the mean squared error. Using our framework, we also design an efficient estimator for data-dependent queries on Gaussian distributions with unknown mean and identity covariance. Finally, we show that a smoothed variant of the problem is much (exponentially) easier. For discrete distributions, if the sample size is chosen uniformly from $\{n,\ldots,2n-1\}$, then $n=O(\log d)$ suffices for constant mean squared error.

</details>


### [25] [WingBench: Benchmarking How AI Agents Search for Better Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28418)
*Yiming Yang,David Xia,Jisen Li,Yuqing Jian,Yunjia Yang,Koray Aydin,Zhaoran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WingBench多保真度基准，研究在CFD约束下用代理模型+有限CFD预算设计跨声速机翼，并比较5种基线优化器与3种AI agent方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 廉价学习预测（代理模型）常用于筛选候选设计以决定昂贵测试对象，但测试（如CFD）可能失败，尤其在优化偏好的非常规设计上，现有缺乏可衡量"有效性+质量"的基准。

Method: 构建WingBench基准：优化器/AI agent在搜索中设计跨声速机翼，可查询气动代理模型并消耗固定顺序CFD评估预算，每次返回分数或失败类型；评分对推荐设计分别报告是否获得有效分数及其质量。基于该基准测试5种基线优化器与3种AI agent方法，共产生6629条CFD评估记录。

Result: 反馈能帮助基线优化器在影响后续提案时产出有效设计，但差分进化变体以较低气动质量为代价；开放式研究型agent在32次CFD查询内达到与最强基线相近的质量（先验信息不同、总计算未匹配）。早期搜索中，反射与规划实现未稳定优于该agent。

Conclusion: CFD失败是设计搜索中的关键因素，需同时评估有效性与质量；反馈机制与agent设计策略各有权衡，简单开放式agent已具竞争力；公开数据、代码、提示词与分析脚本以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Design search often uses cheap learned predictions to choose which candidates deserve expensive tests, but these tests can fail, especially on the unusual designs that optimization favors. We introduce WingBench, a multifidelity benchmark in which optimizers and AI agents design transonic wings with computational fluid dynamics (CFD) inside the search. They can query a learned aerodynamic surrogate and spend a fixed budget of sequential CFD evaluations, each returning a score or a failure type. WingBench grades the design each method recommends, reporting separately whether it receives a valid score and how good that score is. Using WingBench, we contribute a study of five baseline optimizers and a comparison of three AI agent methods, which produced 6,629 CFD evaluation records. Feedback helps baseline optimizers deliver valid designs when it changes their later proposals, but the differential-evolution variant does so with lower aerodynamic quality. An open-ended research agent finds designs of similar quality to the strongest baselines over 32 CFD queries, with different prior information and unmatched total computation. In early search, our reflection and planning implementations do not consistently improve on it. We release the data, code, agent prompts, and analysis scripts for reproducibility.

</details>


### [26] [Lean Census: A Distributional Analysis of AI-Generated Lean Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28430)
*Justin Asher,Pietro Pellegrino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过代码简单统计量的分布，对比两个人工编写与四个AI生成的Lean库和Mathlib，发现AI生成库具有内联断言更多、证明更长、实例更少、私有声明更多的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏对AI生成形式化代码（Lean库）与人工编写代码在风格与结构上差异的量化刻画手段。

Method: 以Mathlib各子领域的自身变异为参照基准，对每个库测量四类指标：证明中的内联断言数量、证明长度、每个声明的被使用频率、设计选择（实例与私有声明）；并发布分析工具包lean-census。

Result: AI生成库相比人工编写库每定理内联断言更多、证明更长、实例更少、私有声明更多。

Conclusion: 简单代码统计量可有效区分AI生成与人工编写的Lean库，且分析代码以lean-census包形式开源，便于复现与扩展。

Abstract: We compare two human-written and four AI-generated Lean libraries with Mathlib through the distributions of simple statistics of their code. For each library we measure inline claims in proofs, proof length, how often each declaration is used, and design choices such as instances and private declarations, and we read each against the variation among Mathlib's own areas. The AI-generated libraries have more inline claims per theorem, longer proofs, fewer instances, and more private declarations than the human-written ones. We release the code for the analysis as a package, lean-census, at https://github.com/project-numina/lean-census.

</details>


### [27] [PFN-Syn: Generating Synthetic Tabular Data with Prior-Data Fitted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28440)
*Michael Zuo,Inwon Kang,Oshani Seneviratne,Stacy Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PFN-Syn 是一个免训练的表格数据合成框架，基于先验数据拟合网络（PFN），在八个二分类数据集上取得最高平均下游效用，生成吞吐量高于需训练基线，同时保持有竞争力的分布相似性与最近记录距离隐私表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私敏感领域需要合成表格数据以支持数据共享与模型开发，但现有生成器通常依赖数据集专属训练与大量计算。

Method: 提出 PFN-Syn：基于 prior-data fitted networks 的免训练表格数据生成框架；在八个二分类数据集上与基于训练的生成器对比评估。

Result: 在评估的合成生成器中平均下游效用最高；生成吞吐量高于基于训练的基线；分布相似性与"到最近记录距离"隐私行为保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型可作为实用合成表格数据生成的有效构建模块。

Abstract: Synthetic tabular data can help enable data sharing and model development in privacy-sensitive domains, but existing generators often require dataset-specific training and substantial computation. We introduce PFN-Syn, a training-free framework for synthetic tabular data generation based on prior-data fitted networks. We evaluate PFN-Syn against training-based generators on eight binary classification datasets. PFN-Syn achieves the highest average downstream utility among evaluated synthetic generators, with higher generation throughput than training-based baselines, while maintaining competitive distributional similarity and distance-to-closest-record privacy behavior. Our results suggest that tabular foundation models can serve as useful building blocks for practical synthetic tabular data generation.

</details>


### [28] [When a Permitted Action Completes a Forbidden Workflow: Designing Consequence-Oriented Approval for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28454)
*S M Asif Hossain,Ruksat Khan Shayoni,Md Kishor Morol,M F Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"累积授权失败"问题并提出累积同意账本(CCL)审批界面，通过追踪工件溯源的可信监控器重塑审批呈现方式，实验显示其降低不安全批准率、提升理解与速度，作为执行闸门可大幅降低不安全效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型语言模型代理常要求用户批准高后果操作，但审批提示只呈现下一步动作。例如向已批准收件人发送文件看似无害，而该文件是数步之前由"绝不可外流"的数据拼装而成。这类"累积授权失败"既是安全问题也是交互设计问题：评审者所需证据分散在整条轨迹上，按时序罗列会让评审者自行重建因果链。

Method: 提出累积同意账本(CCL)审批界面，基于四条设计原则：重述用户意图、将审查绑定到确切动作、组合累积后果、使每项主张可核验。由追踪工件谱系的可信监控器提供证据。开展80名被试、960次审批决策的组间实验（对比证据匹配的时序日志），并进行系统评估（120个违规检查点）。

Result: CCL的不安全批准率低于时序日志(24.2% vs 32.5%)，理解度更高、决策更快，但校正后不安全批准差异仍不确定(-7.9个百分点，95% CrI [-19.7, 3.8])。系统评估中监控器检出89.2%的违规检查点，作为执行闸门将不安全效应执行率从43.3%降至5.0%，良性任务完成率略有损失。

Conclusion: 应围绕"后果"而非"单次调用"来设计同意机制；CCL与可信溯源监控能在不显著拖累正常任务的前提下显著减少累积性不安全执行。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents increasingly ask people to approve consequential actions, yet an approval prompt shows only the next step. A request to send a file to an approved recipient can look harmless even when that file was assembled, several steps earlier, from data the user said must never leave. We call this a cumulative authorization failure and argue that it is an interaction design problem as much as a security problem. The evidence a reviewer needs is spread across the trajectory, and listing it in time order leaves the reviewer to reconstruct the causal chain. We present the Cumulative Consent Ledger (CCL), an approval interface built on four design principles that restate the user's intent, bind the review to the exact action, compose the cumulative consequence, and keep every claim checkable. A trusted monitor that tracks artifact lineage supplies the evidence. In a between-subjects study with 80 participants and 960 approval decisions, CCL was associated with fewer unsafe approvals than an evidence-matched chronological log (24.2% vs. 32.5%), higher comprehension, and faster decisions, although the adjusted difference in unsafe approvals remained uncertain (-7.9 points, 95% CrI [-19.7, 3.8]). In a system evaluation, the monitor detected 89.2% of 120 violating checkpoints and, used as an execution gate, reduced executed unsafe effects from 43.3% to 5.0% for one agent at a small cost to benign completion. We derive implications for designing consent around consequences rather than individual calls.

</details>


### [29] [Optimal User-Level Private Stochastic Convex Optimization and Mean Estimation with Heavy Tails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28460)
*Andrew Lowy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在用户级差分隐私（(ε,δ)-DP）约束下、重尾随机梯度（仅假设Lipschitz参数的二阶与k阶矩有界）的随机凸优化，刻画了minimax最优超额风险并给出达到最优率的算法，仅需Õ(1/ε)用户。副产品是用户级隐私均值估计的紧界，填补了近似DP下方向矩假设的采样复杂度最优性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常假设Lipschitz常数一致有界，无法刻画重尾、无界梯度分布；且用户级DP（保护每个用户的全部样本）下的重尾优化与均值估计在采样复杂度上尚非最优（如Agarwal et al. 2025遗留的近似DP缺口与额外d/ε用户需求）。

Method: 放弃一致Lipschitz假设，改用样本Lipschitz参数的二阶与k阶矩有界假设；设计用户级隐私的随机凸优化算法，并结合算法技术发展出在范数矩假设下的用户级隐私均值估计方法（含方向矩假设下的改进）。

Result: 刻画了至多对数因子的minimax最优超额总体风险；算法仅用Õ(1/ε)用户达到最优率；在范数矩假设下得到用户级隐私均值估计的紧界，优于Esfandiari et al. (2022)与Zhao et al. (2024)；在Agarwal et al. (2025)的方向矩假设下移除额外d/ε用户要求，达到至多对数因子的最优样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 在重尾、用户级DP框架下，基于矩假设可获得minimax最优的优化与均值估计保证，显著降低用户需求并超越已有结果，闭合约DP的复杂度缺口。

Abstract: We study stochastic convex optimization with heavy-tailed stochastic gradients under $(\varepsilon, δ)$-user-level differential privacy, which protects each user's entire collection of samples. Instead of assuming a uniformly bounded Lipschitz constant, we only assume bounds on the second- and $k$-th moments of the random sample Lipschitz parameters. This allows for unbounded, heavy-tailed stochastic gradient distributions and can lead to sharper excess risk bounds. We characterize the minimax optimal excess population risk up to logarithmic factors. Our algorithm achieves the optimal rate with only $\widetilde{O}(1/\varepsilon)$ users. As a byproduct of our algorithmic developments, we obtain tight bounds for user-level private mean estimation under norm-moment assumptions, improving over the guarantees of Esfandiari et al. (2022) and Zhao et al. (2024). Finally, for mean estimation under the directional-moment assumption of Agarwal et al. (2025), we close their remaining approximate-DP gap by removing the additional $d/\varepsilon$ user requirement and attaining the optimal sample complexity up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [30] [HiPPO Functional Memory: Coordinate-Queryable Attention for Irregular Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28465)
*Zhaodong Guo,Haoyun Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 HiPPO Functional Memory (HiPPO-FM)，一种将不规则历史写入带显式时间坐标的 rank-M 函数空间的注意力算子，使模型可在任意时刻连续读取记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则时间序列在非均匀时刻采样，却常在观测之间、末次观测之后或规则网格上被查询。连续时间模型依靠积分隐动态作答，时间感知注意力模型依靠重访观测 token 作答，二者各有局限。

Method: 掩码自注意力先在观测间交换内容；更新后的值再与核平滑的 HiPPO-Legendre 特征做收缩，并通过正则化 Gram 矩阵求解，所得系数定义了一个可由同一特征在任意时刻读取的函数。写入与读取呈域间稀疏高斯过程均值的形式，每次读出均落在 M 维再生核子空间内，并给出逐点界将子空间近似误差与学习记忆误差分离。

Result: 移除函数记忆使 Spiral 重建 RMSE 上升 18 倍；完整模型在 12 个 Spiral 设置中 11 个误差最低，在 20 个不规则 UEA 数据集上总体准确率最高、平均排名最优，在全部 5 个多类型事件基准上事件类型准确率最高，在 PhysioNet 上外推 MSE 比 CRU 低 21%。

Conclusion: 由于记忆秩固定，编码后保留的状态尺寸随历史增长保持不变，在 1,024 个观测时比 token 缓存小 102 倍。

Abstract: Irregular time series are recorded at nonuniform times and are often queried at other times: between observations, after the last one, or on a regular grid. Continuous-time models answer such queries by integrating latent dynamics, and time-aware attention models answer them by revisiting the observation tokens. We introduce HiPPO Functional Memory (HiPPO-FM), an attention operator that writes an irregular history into a rank-M function space with explicit time coordinates. Masked self-attention first exchanges content among the observations; the updated values are then contracted against kernel-smoothed HiPPO-Legendre features and solved through a regularized Gram matrix, so the retained coefficients define a function that is read at any time through the same features. The write and read have the form of an interdomain sparse Gaussian-process mean: every readout lies in an M-dimensional reproducing-kernel subspace, and a pointwise bound separates the approximation error of this subspace from the error of the learned memory. Removing the functional memory from the encoder raises Spiral reconstruction RMSE 18-fold. The full model has the lowest error in 11 of 12 Spiral settings, the highest overall accuracy and best average rank on 20 irregular UEA datasets, the highest event-type accuracy on all five multi-type event benchmarks, and a 21% lower extrapolation MSE than CRU on PhysioNet. Because the memory has fixed rank, the state retained after encoding keeps a fixed size as the history grows and is 102 times smaller than a token cache at 1,024 observations.

</details>


### [31] [PAMS: Preconditioned Activation-Metric Subspace for Zeroth-Order LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28476)
*Pradeep Kumar Ragu Chanthar,Saksham Sahai Srivastava,Jaewoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PAMS：用前向激活统计构造分层低秩预条件几何的零阶优化框架，实现无梯度的LLM微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规零阶（ZO）微调依赖各向同性扰动，忽视神经网络参数空间的各向异性结构，导致扰动效率与稳定性受限。

Method: 在线追踪主激活子空间的低秩近似及其谱，用谱对扰动方差做软重加权，并保留子空间外方向的探索；通过迹归一化解耦扰动几何与整体尺度；仅需 O(n_l·r) 内存，避免稠密激活协方差的 O(n_l^2)。

Result: 理论上证明所得扰动协方差等价于对高斯平滑目标梯度做右侧预条件，刻画了估计器的平滑偏差与二阶矩，并给出体现扰动几何与平滑半径影响的非凸收敛保证；GLUE 与 SuperGLUE 上基于 Transformer 的微调实验显示有效性与稳定性均有提升。

Conclusion: 结构化、层间自适应的扰动几何可在保持无梯度、仅依赖目标函数评估的前提下显著改善零阶优化的性能与稳定性。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization provides a computationally efficient approach to fine-tuning large language models (LLMs), but conventional methods typically rely on isotropic perturbations that ignore the anisotropic structure of neural-network parameter spaces. We introduce Preconditioned Activation-Metric Subspace (PAMS), a ZO optimization framework that uses forward-pass activation statistics to construct structured, layer-wise perturbation geometries without computing gradients or forming dense curvature matrices. PAMS tracks a low-rank approximation of the dominant activation subspace and its associated spectrum online, and uses the spectrum to softly reweight perturbation variance while preserving exploration in directions outside the retained subspace. A trace normalization separates the perturbation geometry from its overall scale, enabling consistent perturbation magnitudes across layers. We show theoretically that the resulting perturbation covariance induces right-side preconditioning of the gradient of the corresponding Gaussian-smoothed objective. We further characterize the estimator's smoothing bias and second moment and derive a nonconvex convergence guarantee that captures the effects of the perturbation geometry and smoothing radius. The proposed low-rank tracking procedure requires $\mathcal{O}(n_l r)$ persistent memory for a layer with input dimension nl and retained rank r, avoiding the $\mathcal{O}(n_l^2)$ memory required by a dense activation covariance. Experiments on transformer-based language-model fine-tuning tasks across GLUE and SuperGLUE demonstrate that PAMS improves the effectiveness and stability of zeroth-order optimization while retaining its gradient-free and objective-evaluation-based advantages.

</details>


### [32] [Don't Train It, Write It: An AI Agent Writes New Capabilities into Language Model Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28483)
*Santiago Rivadeneira Quintero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AI智能体在不使用反向传播、不针对任务答案拟合权重的前提下，通过在预训练模型内部层级插入门控注意力头与MLP神经元，将新能力写入模型，并在BBH六项子任务与20位精确乘法上取得显著提升，同时对未涉及任务几乎无副作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否能由AI智能体在极低预算（50次模型前向）且无验证/测试样本条件下，向预训练语言模型注入可组合的新能力，并严格验证其增益的真实性与副作用边界（预注册、密封测试、时间戳存证）。

Method: 在现有层内插入门控注意力头与MLP神经元进行模块化能力写入；不使用反向传播，不拟合任务示例答案；每模块预算50次模型pass。六项BBH子任务模块组合进单一模型；预注册假设H5/H6/H8/H9/H10/H12于密封测试上由冻结评估器一次性执行；以KL散度、跨任务答案一致性度量副作用；对照同预算LoRA。方案与报告句在首次会话前封存于git并以比特币时间戳存证，附录D列明带日期修订。

Result: Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct归一化BBH由19.95升至41.05（高于Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的上限35.01）；Gemma-3-1B-it由10.25升至32.86。同精度下对1,023,000个WikiText token的下一词分布KL恰为0，五个Leaderboard测试的15,845项答案与其他18个BBH子任务得分完全一致。20位精确乘法：仅在≤6位长度上运行即在缺席长度上达100.0%（同预算LoRA≤2.1%）。Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct上H5、H6、H8(web_of_lies)、H9、H12成功，H10(i)–(iii)成功、(iv)确认；Gemma-3-1B-it上H5、H6、H8(web_of_lies)、H8(tracking-3)成功，H9部分成功。失败项：45个单模块中4个出现小幅副作用，50次会话中5次触及12小时上限且未产出模块。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，低预算AI智能体可通过层级插入的神经元模块实现可组合、可验证的能力写入，并获得精确无损的副作用控制；方法在跨模型上具一定可迁移性，但模块级稳定性与时间预算仍存局限，且部分假设（如H9）仅部分成立。

Abstract: We show that an AI agent can write new capabilities into a pretrained language model by adding gated attention heads and MLP neurons inside its existing layers, without backpropagation and without fitting any weight to the answers of task examples. It has a budget of 50 model passes and sees no validation or test instance. Composed in one model, its modules for six BIG-Bench Hard (BBH) subtasks raise the normalized BBH score of Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct from 19.95 to 41.05 (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct: at most 35.01) and that of Gemma-3-1B-it from 10.25 to 32.86. Against the unmodified model at the same precision, both composites leave the next-token distribution on 1,023,000 WikiText tokens unchanged (KL exactly 0), give identical answers on 15,845 items of five other Leaderboard tests and identical scores on the other 18 BBH subtasks. The agent also writes exact multiplication up to 20 digits, running the model only up to 6 (100.0% on the absent lengths of the sealed test in both models; a LoRA with the same budget: at most 2.1%). Sets, rules and report sentences were sealed in git before the first session (manifests timestamped in Bitcoin), with dated amendments listed in Appendix D; a frozen evaluator ran the sealed test once. Verdicts (H5: tracking; H6: composite; H8: one pass vs. chain of thought; H9: fresh and rewritten instances; H10: side effects; H12: multiplication), on the preregistered sealed test, Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct: H5 success, H6 success, H8 (web_of_lies) success, H9 success, H10 (composite) success on (i) to (iii), (iv) confirmed, H12 success; Gemma-3-1B-it (H7: H5, H6, H8 and H9 in this model): H5 success, H6 success, H8 (web_of_lies) success, H8 (tracking 3) success, H9 partial, H10 (composite) success on (i) to (iii), (iv) confirmed, H12 success. Not everything worked: 4 of 45 single modules show small side effects and 5 of 50 sessions hit the 12-hour limit without a module.

</details>


### [33] [AMP-OD: Active Model Probing for Unsupervised Outlier Model Selection--Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28488)
*Hong-Phuc Phan,Tuan-Anh Vu,Tung Kieu,Son Ha Xuan,Sean Bin Yang,Bin Yang,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AMP-OD是一个"执行条件化"的探针-提交式无监督离群检测器选择框架：先运行少量廉价探针检测器，用其打分模式作为目标数据集证据，再在更大候选池中选定最终检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全无监督场景下无标签、无直接性能反馈，现有无监督模型选择方法仅依赖静态元特征与元数据集上的历史性能，在任何检测器于目标数据集上执行之前即做出选择，缺乏目标数据集上的实际行为证据。

Method: 提出探针-提交框架AMP-OD：运行少量低成本探针检测器获取打分模式；引入Leave-One-Out Family Probe Posterior (LOO-FPP)，将探针打分模式转化为门控的检测器族证据，并降低伪标签构建中的自验证偏差；结合历史可靠性（warm-start先验）、序贯探针/停止策略与下置信界（LCB）仲裁完成最终选择。

Result: 大量实验与消融表明，检测器响应能提供超越静态元特征的有用证据；LOO-FPP与保守式仲裁是主要贡献因素。

Conclusion: 执行条件化的探针证据可有效改进无监督离群检测器选择，为无标签场景下的模型选择提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Outlier detection is often deployed in fully unsupervised settings, where labels are unavailable and practitioners must choose a detector without direct performance feedback. Existing unsupervised outlier model selection methods predict detector quality from static meta-features and historical performance on meta-datasets, but they commit before observing how any detector behaves on the target dataset. We propose AMP-OD, an execution-conditioned probe-then-commit framework. AMP-OD runs a small number of inexpensive probe detectors, uses their score patterns as target-dataset evidence, and then selects a final detector from a larger candidate pool. To interpret unlabeled probe responses, we introduce a Leave-One-Out Family Probe Posterior (LOO-FPP), which converts probe score patterns into gated detector-family evidence while reducing self-validation in pseudo-label construction. AMP-OD further combines this evidence with historical reliability through a warm-start prior, a sequential probe/stop policy, and lower-confidence-bound arbitration. Extensive experiments and ablation studies show that detector responses provide useful evidence beyond static meta-features, with the LOO-FPP and conservative arbitration as key contributors.

</details>


### [34] [State of Mind: Learning and Evaluating World Models of Naturalistic Brain Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28508)
*Yuxiang Wei,Zundong Wu,Weiyu Sun,Qizhen Lan,Yanteng Zhang,Vince D. Calhoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BrainStateBench 基准与 Spectator 线性潜状态世界模型，直接检验脑模型的隐状态推断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自然情境下的脑活动不能只看刺激，需建模外部输入与个体演化脑状态的交互。现有脑世界模型无法维持持久潜状态，且标准神经预测精度无法区分模型是跟踪个体状态还是仅依赖刺激。

Method: 构建 BrainStateBench，用全皮层电影驱动 fMRI 上的冻结线性探针评估潜状态，设 State（重复刺激下的逐试次变异）、Forecast（无新观测预测未来）、Updating（有新观测后更新）三项任务。提出 Spectator：以联合嵌入预测目标训练的线性潜世界模型，将短/长时程观测历史编码为双时间尺度潜状态，并由当前状态与刺激线性预测状态转移。

Result: 仅刺激编码器 fMRI 预测相关 0.320 但 State 为 0%；先前深度脑世界模型 0.239、State ≤0.4%。Spectator 达 0.349 相关，State 5.5%、Forecast 16.0%、Updating 20.3%，全面超越基线，且预测速度比深度模型快 20 倍以上。

Conclusion: 频谱（线性）世界模型能在保持高预测精度的同时真正跟踪个体脑状态，且效率显著优于深度方法；BrainStateBench 为状态推断提供了可区分性的评测手段。

Abstract: Understanding naturalistic brain activity requires going beyond the stimulus alone, as external inputs continuously interact with an individual's evolving brain state. A world model captures such dynamics in a latent state by inferring it from neural observations, predicting stimulus-driven evolution, and updating with new observations. However, existing brain world models fail to maintain a persistent latent state over time, and standard metrics of neural prediction accuracy cannot tell whether a model tracks an individual's brain state or relies solely on the stimulus. To directly test state inference, prediction, and updating, we introduce BrainStateBench, which evaluates a model's latent state through a frozen linear probe on whole-cortex, movie-driven fMRI. Its State task tests whether a model captures trial-to-trial variability in an individual's responses to repeated stimuli, while Forecast and Updating evaluate future activity predictions without and with new observations, respectively. To track individual brain state across time, we propose Spectator, a linear latent world model trained with a joint-embedding predictive objective. Spectator encodes short- and long-horizon observation histories into a two-timescale latent state and predicts state transitions linearly from the current state and stimulus. Stimulus-only encoders reach 0.320 in fMRI prediction correlation without observing the brain (0% on State), while prior deep brain world models reach 0.239 yet score at most 0.4% on State. In contrast, Spectator achieves 0.349 correlation alongside 5.5% on State, 16.0% on Forecast, and 20.3% on Updating, outperforming all baselines while forecasting over 20x faster than deep forecasting models.

</details>


### [35] [DisSparse: Pipelined Top-$p$ Sparse Attention for Long-Context LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28522)
*Nurlan Nazaraliyev,Elaheh Sadredini,Nael Abu-Ghazaleh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DisSparse 是集成 top-p（nucleus）稀疏注意力到 vLLM 的服务系统，通过将块选择从调度关键路径解耦、双流流水线、单遍直方图 top-p 内核及 token 重定位内核，解决变长块分配与选择开销问题，块选择获得 1.17x–1.80x 加速，并支持最高 8x 更大批量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 推理受 KV cache 显存线性增长制约。动态稀疏注意力仅对关键 token 子集进行取用与注意力计算以缓解瓶颈。主流做法是固定预算的 top-k 选择，因其块数确定、契合 vLLM 等系统的静态显存分配；但固定预算无法适应注意力质量在头、层、解码步间的分布差异，导致注意力集中的头浪费带宽、注意力弥散的头丢失上下文。top-p 选择能自适应，但受两点系统性问题限制难以落地：块数可变破坏确定性块分配；选择内核开销甚至超过注意力本身。

Method: 提出 DisSparse：端到端将 top-p 稀疏注意力集成进 vLLM。核心设计包括(1) 将块选择与调度器关键路径解耦；(2) 采用双流流水线把选择延迟隐藏在层计算之后；(3) 单遍基于直方图的 top-p 内核降低选择开销；(4) 观察到块内稀疏性问题（定长 KV 块内高低重要度 token 混排导致选择预算膨胀），并用由累积 prefill 注意力分数引导的 token 重定位内核加以解决。

Result: 在多模型的長/中上下文基准上，稀疏化分析（块选择）相对 SOTA top-p 方法取得 1.17x–1.80x 加速，精度损失可忽略；在同等显存预算下，最大批量可达稠密 vLLM 的 8x。

Conclusion: top-p 稀疏注意力可通过系统级协同设计（关键路径解耦、流水线隐藏、高效选择内核、块内重定位）在生产服务中实用化，兼顾自适应预算与确定性调度约束，显著提升长上下文推理的吞吐与批处理能力。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference is bottlenecked by the memory demands of the key-value (KV) cache, whose size scales linearly with context length. Dynamic sparse attention reduces this bottleneck by fetching and attending only to a critical subset of tokens. The dominant primitive in dynamic sparse attention research and deployment is fixed-budget top-$k$ selection, which is favored because its deterministic block count aligns with the static memory allocation that systems like vLLM rely on.
