Top-$k$ uses a fixed budget regardless of how attention mass is distributed across heads, layers, and decode steps, leading to either wasted bandwidth on heads with peaky attention or dropped context on heads with diffuse attention. Top-$p$ (nucleus) selection adapts naturally to this variation but has seen limited adoption in production serving systems due to two system-level challenges: variable block counts that break deterministic block allocation and computationally expensive selection kernels that exceed the cost of attention itself. We present DisSparse, a serving system that integrates top-$p$ sparse attention into vLLM end-to-end. DisSparse decouples block selection from the scheduler's critical path, hides selection latency behind layer computation via a two-stream pipeline, and introduces a single-pass histogram-based top-$p$ kernel. We further observe intra-block sparsity, the inflation of selection budgets caused by mixing high- and low-importance tokens within fixed-size KV cache blocks, and address it with a token relocation kernel guided by accumulated prefill attention scores. Across long- and medium-context benchmarks on multiple models, DisSparse achieves 1.17x to 1.80x speedup in sparsity analysis (block selection) over state-of-the-art top-$p$ methods with negligible accuracy loss and supports up to 8x larger maximum batch sizes than dense vLLM under the same memory budget.

</details>


### [36] [SAFE-DRIFT: Data Selection for Supervised Fine-tuning with Controllable Off-Target Drifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28560)
*Yeo Jin Jung,Yating Liu,Lalchand Pandia,Claire Donnat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAFE-DRIFT数据选择框架，在监督微调中通过带约束优化（以Fisher信息量化的参考分布off-target漂移为预算），实现目标能力提升并抑制其他行为的漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法侧重提升目标能力，但容易引起模型在其他行为上的非预期漂移，缺乏对off-target行为退化的显式控制机制。

Method: 将数据选择形式化为约束优化：最大化与目标梯度的对齐，同时以参考分布的Fisher信息量约束off-target漂移预算；推导出闭式最优解为阻尼自然梯度步，对参考敏感方向降权；并设计在共享低维子空间中近似Fisher信息与目标梯度的可扩展算法，分析目标与参考任务纠缠对更新的影响。

Result: 在编码、医学问答等多个领域与SOTA数据选择方法对比，取得有竞争力的目标性能，同时减少指定off-target行为的退化。

Conclusion: 以Fisher信息约束漂移的数据选择可在提升目标能力与保持其他行为之间取得更优权衡，为可控微调提供了理论支撑与可扩展实现。

Abstract: We propose SAFE-DRIFT, a data-selection framework for supervised fine-tuning that improves a target capability while controlling unwanted drift in the model's other behaviors. We formalize this as a constrained optimization problem that maximizes alignment with the target gradient, subject to a budget on off-target drift which we quantify using the Fisher information of a reference distribution. Under this objective, the optimal, closed-form update is a damped natural-gradient step that downweights reference-sensitive directions. To estimate these high-dimensional gradients and work within the right geometry, we further derive a scalable algorithm that approximates the Fisher information and the target gradient in a shared low-dimensional subspace and analyze how entanglement of the target and reference task can affect the update. We evaluate SAFE-DRIFT against state-of-the-art data selection methods across diverse domains including coding and medical question answering, yielding competitive target performance while reducing degradation on specified off-target behaviors.

</details>


### [37] [Tight Mistake-Abstention Tradeoffs for Sequential Prediction under Adversarial Injections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28579)
*Moïse Blanchard,Ezra Edelman,Surbhi Goel,Shyam Narayanan,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究含对抗注入的序贯预测问题，提出"加权粉碎势"及其变体，给出在各种设定下基本刻画错误-弃权权衡的算法，弥补Yu与Blanchard (2026)遗留的上下界间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goel等(2023)提出该序贯预测模型：对手可在来自干净分布μ的i.i.d.流中注入任意多污染样本，学习器若对注入样本弃权则无代价。当μ未知时，Yu与Blanchard(2026)对有限VC维、遗忘型对手给出多项式权衡，但其上界与下界之间仍存在显著间隙，本文旨在填补该间隙并完整刻画权衡景观。

Method: 提出加权粉碎势（weighted shattering potential），并基于该势的多种变体设计算法，分别处理μ已知/未知、遗忘型对手/自适应对手、以及截断反馈（弃权后标签隐藏）等设定。

Result: μ已知时改进Goel等(2023)的结果，达到其猜想的O(d log T)错误界；μ未知且对手遗忘时，界在T与VC维d上均紧，完全刻画该设定的弃权-错误权衡，并给出O(√(dT))的平衡速率；自适应对手下，对具有有限约化维数D的结构化类，结果在poly(dD log T)因子内紧；结果可推广至截断反馈。

Conclusion: 加权粉碎势是刻画带注入污染序贯预测权衡的有效工具，所提算法在多种设定下达到（近乎）最优，闭合了现有上下界间隙，并拓展至截断反馈场景。

Abstract: We study the sequential prediction problem introduced by Goel et al. (2023), where an adversary can inject arbitrarily many corrupted instances in an i.i.d. stream from a clean distribution $μ$, while the learner is allowed to abstain from making a prediction at no cost if the current instance was indeed injected. When $μ$ is unknown, Yu and Blanchard (2026) show a polynomial tradeoff between mistakes and false abstentions for classes with finite VC dimension and oblivious adversaries, but still leave open a significant gap between upper and lower bounds in their tradeoff landscape. In this paper, we propose the weighted shattering potential, and based on variants of this potential, provide gap-closing algorithms that essentially characterize the tradeoffs under various settings of interest: When $μ$ is known, our algorithm improves over Goel et al. (2023) and achieves their conjectured $O(d\log T)$ mistake bound; When $μ$ is unknown, for oblivious adversary, our guarantees are tight both in $T$ and in terms of the VC dimension $d$, which completely characterizes the abstention-mistake tradeoff in this setting and gives an $O(\sqrt{dT})$ balanced rate. For adaptive adversary, our results are tight up to $\operatorname{poly}(dD\log T)$ factors for structured classes with finite so-called reduction dimension $D$. Our guarantees also extend to censored feedback, where the label is hidden after an abstention.

</details>


### [38] [Understanding and Mitigating the Fragility of Self-Rewarding Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28582)
*Rongyi Zhu,Tanqiu Jiang,Fenglong Ma,Ting Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文揭示自奖励优化（SRO）本质脆弱：训练提示的微小扰动即可污染自生成偏好信号并引发迭代崩塌；作者提出自奖励保真度（SRF）作为可诊断指标，并给出投影解耦防御以提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SRO 能让语言模型生成自身奖励信号以自我改进，但其成功与失败的条件尚不清楚。作者希望系统性地刻画 SRO 的脆弱性来源、给出可量化的诊断信号，并据此设计防御手段。

Method: 通过两类提示级攻击作为受控压力测试：Re-Ranking 攻击（将训练集中于自奖励与 oracle 奖励背离的提示）与 Preamble 攻击（学习短前缀以抬高自奖励但无 oracle 质量增益）。提出自奖励保真度（SRF）度量，量化在策略下自评估奖励与 oracle 奖励的一致程度，并对其施加受控扰动以观察 SRO 行为；进而提出 Projection Decoupling Defense，抑制 SRO 更新中沿奖励生成器局部敏感方向的分量。

Result: SRF 可作为自监督可靠性的诊断信号；对其受控扰动会呈现相变区域——SRF 足够高时 SRO 改善，低于模型与任务相关的阈值后崩塌。两类攻击均能触发该崩塌，而 Projection Decoupling Defense 在对抗条件下提升了 SRO 的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SRO 的失效可归因于自奖励保真度的下降，并具有阈值型相变特征；SRF 提供了实用诊断工具，投影解耦防御是提升 SRO 鲁棒性的有效方向，为后续稳健自奖励优化研究铺路。

Abstract: Self-rewarding optimization (SRO) promises to improve language models by enabling them to generate their own reward signals. Yet when and how SRO succeeds or fails remains poorly understood. We reveal that SRO is fundamentally fragile: even seemingly benign perturbations to training prompts can corrupt the self-generated preference signal and trigger iterative collapse. We demonstrate this through two prompt-level attacks that serve as controlled stress tests: a Re-Ranking attack that concentrates training on prompts where self-reward diverges from an oracle, and a Preamble attack that learns short prefixes inflating self-reward without any gain in oracle quality. To explain why these attacks succeed, we introduce self-reward fidelity (SRF), a metric quantifying on-policy agreement between self-evaluated and oracle rewards. We show that SRF can serve as a diagnostic signal of self-supervision reliability, and that controlled SRF perturbations induce a phase-transition region: SRO improves when SRF remains sufficiently high, but collapses once SRF drops below a model- and task-dependent threshold. This analysis motivates Projection Decoupling Defense, which suppresses SRO update components along directions to which the reward generator is locally sensitive, improving SRO robustness under adversarial conditions. This work sheds light on the fragility of SRO and charts promising directions toward making it more robust.

</details>


### [39] [DRAM: Memory-Efficient Muon via Rank Reallocation in Principal-Random Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28595)
*Haomin Bai,Dingzhi Yu,Xuling Zhao,Sifan Yang,Yibo Wang,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DRAM，一种内存高效的 Muon 优化器，通过在全训练过程中动态重新分配主子空间与随机子空间的秩（早期偏主成分、后期偏随机覆盖），在保持动量与子空间内矩阵符号归一化的同时，兼顾收敛保证与实际效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内存高效 Muon 方法在降低存储开销时，要么牺牲收敛保证，要么牺牲实际效率。子空间优化中子空间的选择决定了保留的梯度信息，需在保留主导信息与提供随机收敛所需覆盖之间取得平衡。

Method: 构造"主–随机子空间"：结合随机梯度的前导奇异方向与从其正交补中采样的随机方向。在固定秩预算下，主成分利用与互补覆盖存在权衡，故引入秩分配。关键观察是：训练早期宜偏主成分、后期宜偏随机方向，据此提出 DRAM，按阶段重新分配主/随机秩，并将动量维护与矩阵符号归一化完全在子空间内完成；采用简单的线性重分配调度。

Result: 理论上，对具有非递减覆盖因子的通用秩分配调度给出收敛保证：随机设置下速率 O(T^{-1/4})，确定性设置下 O(T^{-1/2})，固定分配为特例。实验上，在三个预训练规模和九个微调设置中取得最佳低内存性能。

Conclusion: 子空间秩的动态分配（由主导向随机覆盖过渡）是内存高效 Muon 的有效设计原则，DRAM 在理论收敛性与多种任务上的低内存性能上同时占优。

Abstract: Memory-efficient Muon methods aim to reduce storage overhead while preserving strong performance, yet existing approaches compromise either convergence guarantees or practical efficiency. In this paper, we focus on subspace optimization for Muon, where the choice of subspace determines the gradient information retained for updating. To preserve dominant information while providing the coverage needed for stochastic convergence guarantees, we construct a principal-random subspace that combines leading singular directions of the stochastic gradient with random directions sampled from their orthogonal complement. Under a fixed rank budget, there is a trade-off between principal exploitation and complementary coverage, making rank allocation important for practical optimization. Our key observation is that the preferred balance shifts from principal-heavy early in training to random-heavy later. Motivated by this shift, we propose \dram, a memory-efficient Muon optimizer that reallocates principal and random ranks across stages while maintaining momentum and performing matrix-sign normalization entirely in the subspace. Theoretically, we establish convergence guarantees for general rank-allocation schedules with nondecreasing coverage factors, obtaining rates of $O(T^{-1/4})$ in the stochastic setting and $O(T^{-1/2})$ in the deterministic setting, with fixed allocation as a special case. Using a simple linear reallocation schedule, \dram achieves the best low-memory performance across three pre-training scales and nine fine-tuning settings.

</details>


### [40] [Bridging Teacher-Student Capability Gaps with Recurrent Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28603)
*Zeyi Huang,Jiuxiang Gu,Eli Shechtman,Yong Jae Lee,Zongze Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文探究"更强教师未必教出更好学生"的成因，提出以循环/递归计算在不增加骨干参数的前提下扩展学生的有效容量；实验表明跨token递归能避免教师规模饱和与反转，并可将架构转换与在线蒸馏联合微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究发现知识蒸馏中教师规模扩大带来收益递减甚至负收益（teacher scaling saturation/reversal）。作者假设部分原因在于学生容量不足以吸收教师知识，因此希望在不增加参数量的情况下提升学生的有效容量。

Method: 通过修改计算图而非骨干参数量来扩展有效容量：(1) 权重共享的looping（权重共享循环）；(2) 跨token递归（cross-token recurrence）。在预训练阶段的teacher-forced KD，以及数学后训练阶段的teacher-forced KD与on-policy distillation (OPD) 下分别评估；并验证预训练LLM可jointly与OPD联合微调转换为looped/recursive架构，无需重做预训练。

Result: teacher-forced预训练KD下，权重共享looping提升性能但仍存在教师规模饱和；cross-token recurrence则不出现更大教师导致的性能反转。数学后训练中，教师规模增大时递归学生的扩展性优于looped与vanilla LLM。递归Qwen模型随教师变大而持续提升，并稳定超越looped与vanilla LLM；推理延迟接近vanilla。

Conclusion: 学生容量不足是教师规模收益饱和/反转的重要成因，递归计算可作为扩展有效容量的有效手段。提出一种新的后训练范式：将架构转换整合进蒸馏过程，省去独立转换阶段，在保持近vanilla推理延迟的同时显著提升学生性能。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation transfers capabilities from large language models to smaller students, yet stronger teachers do not always yield better students. Recent studies report diminishing or even negative returns from teacher scaling. We hypothesize that these limitations arise in part from insufficient student capacity to absorb the teacher's knowledge. We therefore investigate loop and recurrent computation as a way to expand the student's effective capacity by modifying its computation graph while keeping the backbone parameter count fixed. Under teacher-forced KD during pretraining, weight-shared looping improves performance but retains teacher-size saturation, whereas cross-token recurrence avoids the observed reversal with larger teachers. In mathematical post-training under both teacher-forced KD and on-policy distillation (OPD), recurrent students scale better with increasing teacher size than looped and vanilla LLMs do. Crucially, we show that a pretrained LLM can be readily fine-tuned into a looped or recurrent architecture jointly with OPD, without repeating pretraining. Our recurrent Qwen model improves with larger teachers and consistently outperforms looped and vanilla LLMs. This suggests a new post-training paradigm that integrates architectural conversion into distillation, avoiding a separate conversion stage while substantially improving student performance and maintaining near-vanilla inference latency.

</details>


### [41] [BitShare: Sensitivity-Aware Binning and Bit Sharing for Compacting LLM Weight Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28607)
*Qiuyu Ren,Sudipta Paria,Aritra Dasgupta,Swarup Bhunia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BITSHARE是一种无码本的组内变精度权重量化格式，在保持组量化整数网格与反量化器的前提下，通过敏感度分箱与静态比特共享实现组内非均匀精度，在2–3比特下取得更低困惑度并降低DRAM权重流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小批量解码受内存带宽限制，每权重的存储位数几乎决定推理成本；而组内整数量化对整组权重使用同一精度，忽略了组内输入通道敏感度差异，存在精度分配冗余。

Method: 敏感性感知分箱将每组通道按固定位置分入三个箱；静态比特共享结合最优比特值分配，让低敏感权重共享低位，每个值在每组或每对权重中仅存一次；采用共享感知的GPTQ选择所有存储值，无需微调、无需码本。提供两种模式：BITSHARE-U各组布局相同（固定连线解码），BITSHARE-N在相同比特预算内为每组自适应选择布局。在Pile上校准，并完成SkyWater 130 nm综合的硬件解码器验证。

Result: 相同存储量下，BITSHARE-U/N在WikiText-2与C4上的3比特与2比特困惑度均低于GPTQ；2比特下、Llama-2与Llama-3上，BITSHARE-N相较SliM-LLM最多降低WikiText-2困惑度70%，相较GPTAQ最多降低35%；在SliM-LLM同等2比特困惑度下，BITSHARE-U每权重少存13.6–19.4%比特，DRAM权重流量同比例下降；硬件解码器吞吐与INT-n及SliM-LLM解码器相当。

Conclusion: 组内变精度比特共享可在不引入码本与微调的条件下，同时改善极低比特量化的精度-存储权衡，并具备可综合、低开销的硬件实现可行性。

Abstract: Decoding with large language models at small batch sizes is limited by memory bandwidth, so the bits stored per weight largely set its cost. Group-wise integer quantization stores every weight of a group at the same precision, although the input channels of a group differ in sensitivity. We present BITSHARE, a codebook-free weight format that keeps the integer grid and the dequantizer of group-wise quantization but varies precision within each group. Sensitivity-aware binning places the channels of each group into three bins at fixed positions, and static bit sharing with optimal bit-value allocation lets the less sensitive weights share their lower bits, each stored once per group or once per pair of weights. A sharing-aware version of GPTQ chooses every stored value, with no fine-tuning and no codebook. BITSHARE has two modes. BITSHARE-U gives every group the same layout, so its decoder routes bits by fixed wiring, while BITSHARE-N chooses a layout per group within the same bit budget. With calibration on the Pile and at the same storage, both modes have lower perplexity than GPTQ at 3 and 2 bits on WikiText-2 and C4. At 2 bits across Llama-2 and Llama-3 models, BITSHARE-N lowers WikiText-2 perplexity by up to 70% against SliM-LLM and up to 35% against GPTAQ. At the 2-bit perplexity of SliM-LLM, BITSHARE-U stores 13.6-19.4% fewer bits per weight, cutting DRAM weight traffic by the same fraction. A verified hardware decoder implementation synthesized in SkyWater 130 nm reconstructs BITSHARE weights at the throughput of INT-n and SliM-LLM decoders.

</details>


### [42] [Action-Conditioned Transition Irreversibility for Out-of-Distribution Detection in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28611)
*Emil Mittag,Richard Dazeley,Peter Vamplew*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ACTI：基于动作条件化前/后向转移似然比、逐时刻校准与短窗平滑的强化学习OOD状态检测器；在OOD-RL-Bench六个环境中取得最高匹配时刻AUROC，速度比PEDM快约两个数量级，最终推荐轻量的ACTI-Forward。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL智能体自身行为会塑造其所见观测，状态并非独立同分布样本，OOD检测须基于动作条件化的轨迹建模，而非把单步输入当作独立样本处理。同时需兼顾检测性能与推理效率、并避免使用未来信息以保证在线可用性。

Method: ACTI计算前向与后向转移似然并作比较（不可逆性度量），用每个时刻的分布内画像校准该比较，再在短时窗内平滑得分，且不使用未来信息。基线含仅状态变体、容量匹配的仅前向变体ACTI-Forward与动力学模型基线PEDM。评测覆盖六环境、十五个检测器、每环境四种可通过的异常机制、每环境十个策略重复；主指标为匹配时刻AUROC/AUPRC，即异常出现后各时刻得分与干净孪生rollout同刻得分对比。

Result: ACTI取得最高宏观匹配时刻AUROC，优于PEDM，且打分速度快约两个数量级。相较仅状态变体，AUROC有小幅但一致的提升，检测延迟更短。容量匹配的ACTI-Forward在AUPRC上具统计显著优势且延迟更低，AUROC差异未达统计显著。消融显示性能增益主要来自动作条件化、逐步校准与似然得分平滑。

Conclusion: 动作条件化的转移似然对比配合逐时刻校准与短窗平滑是性能提升关键；后向项带来的收益有限，故实际推荐计算更省、延迟更低的ACTI-Forward。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning agents shape the observations they encounter; consequently, detecting out-of-distribution (OOD) states requires analysing action-conditioned trajectories rather than treating inputs as independent samples. We propose Action-Conditioned Transition Irreversibility (ACTI), a detector that evaluates forward and backward transition likelihoods, calibrates this comparison against the in-distribution profile at each timestep, and smooths the score over a short window, using no future information. We tested ACTI across six environments and fifteen detectors within OOD-RL-Bench, using the four anomaly mechanisms that qualified in every environment, with ten policy replicates per environment. All primary metrics are matched-time: each score after anomaly onset is compared with a clean twin rollout's score at the same timestep. Our method secured the top macro matched-time AUROC, outperforming the dynamics-model baseline (PEDM), while scoring transitions roughly two orders of magnitude faster. Compared to the state-only variant, ACTI achieved a small but consistent AUROC gain and a shorter detection delay. The capacity-matched forward-only baseline, ACTI-Forward, secured a statistically significant advantage in AUPRC alongside a reduced detection delay. The discrepancy in AUROC did not reach statistical significance. Ablation studies suggest instead that performance improvements stem from action conditioning, calibration at each timestep, and smoothing of the transition-likelihood scores. Based on these results, we recommend using ACTI-Forward.

</details>


### [43] [Interpreting Diffusion Model Activations in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28621)
*Yukinori Yamamoto,Kotaro Yoshida,Ryotaro Shimizu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 LatentQA 扩展到扩散模型激活，提出 Diffusion-specific Activation Oracle (DAO)，把扩散模型的内部激活翻译为对开放式自然语言问题的回答，从而实现可解释性分析、生成引导与组件归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解读扩散模型（DMs）的中间激活是理解其图像生成机制的关键。现有 DM 可解释性方法对开放式提问（open-ended interrogation）支持有限，难以提取人类可理解的洞见。

Method: 将原本面向大语言模型激活的 LatentQA 任务（用自然语言提问激活）迁移到扩散模型激活上，提出 DAO（Diffusion-specific Activation Oracle）。DAO 以 DM 激活为输入，输出对当前生成过程相关开放式问题的自然语言回答；并利用归因修补（attribution patching）经由 DAO 定位贡献特定视觉属性的 DM 组件。

Result: 实验表明：DAO 可仅凭早期去噪步骤的激活有效描述最终图像内容；其对激活空间结构的刻画足以用于将生成引导至目标属性；通过 DAO 的归因修补能够识别对自然语言指定视觉属性有贡献的 DM 组件。

Conclusion: DAO 为扩散模型提供了开放式、自然语言化的激活解读接口，兼具描述、控制（steering）与归因能力，扩展了激活问答范式的适用范围。

Abstract: Interpreting the intermediate activations of diffusion models (DMs) is key to understanding how they generate images. Existing interpretability methods for DMs provide limited support for open-ended interrogation, making it difficult to extract human-interpretable insights. We extend LatentQA, the task of answering natural-language questions about Large Language Model activations, to DM activations. We introduce the Diffusion-specific Activation Oracle (DAO), which translates DM activations into natural-language answers to open-ended questions about the ongoing generation. Our experiments show that DAO can effectively describe the content of the final image from activations at early denoising steps. Additionally, we demonstrate that DAO captures the structure of the DM's activation space well enough to steer image generation toward target attributes. Furthermore, we show that attribution patching through DAO identifies DM components that contribute to visual attributes specified in natural language. Our code is available \href{https://github.com/yamanoko/dao/tree/main}{here}.

</details>


### [44] [Nearly Minimax-Optimal Sample Complexity of Natural Policy Gradient with a Matching Information-Theoretic Lower Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28628)
*Asha Barua,Sajad Khodadadian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于生成模型的表格型有限时域MDP中自然策略梯度（NPG）的样本复杂度，证明其样本复杂度为Õ(SAH⁴/ε²)，并给出Ω(SAH⁴/ε²)的信息论下界，首次证明NPG的last-iterate保证在忽略对数因子下达到统计极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习的核心问题之一是学习近最优策略需要多少采样转移样本，以及直接的策略优化能否达到这一统计极限。本文旨在回答该问题，针对广泛使用的NPG方法进行分析。

Method: 考虑奖励已知、转移核未知且依赖时域、通过生成模型访问的表格型有限时域MDP。提出基于采样的NPG算法，通过反向扫描更新策略且不估计转移概率；在三种不同的批大小与步长调度下建立last-iterate保证，并构造信息论下界。

Result: 在三种调度下均得到样本复杂度Õ(SAH⁴/ε²)的last-iterate保证；下界Ω(SAH⁴/ε²)表明H⁴依赖在最坏情形下不可避免（即使查询可自适应）。此外，对已知转移核情形，证明步长η=1/H的精确NPG在最坏情形需Ω(H²/ε)次迭代，与已知上界匹配。

Conclusion: 结果表明通过NPG进行直接的策略空间优化可以达到强化学习的基本统计极限，同时保证最终策略的性能；并刻画了已知核下精确NPG在常数步长下的迭代复杂度紧界。

Abstract: A fundamental question in reinforcement learning is how many sampled transitions are needed to learn a near-optimal policy, and whether direct policy optimization can attain this statistical limit. To answer this question, we consider natural policy gradient (NPG), a widely used policy optimization method. We study tabular finite-horizon Markov decision processes (MDPs) with known rewards and unknown horizon-dependent transition kernels, accessed through a generative model. Our sample-based NPG algorithms update the policy by backward sweeps and do not estimate transition probabilities. Under three different batch-size and step size schedules, we establish last-iterate guarantees with sample complexity $\widetilde O(SAH^4/\varepsilon^2)$, where $S$, $A$, and $H$ denote the number of states, the number of actions, and the horizon length, respectively. In this setting, horizon-dependent transition kernels contribute a factor of $H$ to our transition-query bound. Our information-theoretic lower bound of $Ω(SAH^4/\varepsilon^2)$ shows that the resulting $H^4$ dependence is unavoidable in the worst case, even for agents that select their queries adaptively. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first last-iterate guarantees for NPG whose sample complexity matches an information-theoretic lower bound up to logarithmic factors. Consequently, our results demonstrate that direct policy-space optimization through NPG can achieve the fundamental statistical limits of reinforcement learning while guaranteeing the performance of the final policy. Furthermore, for known kernels, we prove that exact NPG with a constant step size $η=1/H$ requires $Ω(H^2/\varepsilon)$ iterations in the worst case, matching the known upper bound for that step size.

</details>


### [45] [Aegis: A Conversational Prototype for Lung Cancer Risk Information and Follow-up](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28642)
*Bowen Han,Chengwei Cao,Runxin Zhang,Yuyang Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Aegis是一个基于浏览器的肺癌筛查研究原型，整合对话式问卷、规则化风险分级、可下载报告、检索增强健康信息与医患沟通功能，采用多智能体架构分离信息采集、验证、评估与报告生成；但仅有45项功能测试规格与定性工作流描述，缺乏临床队列、预测性能与对比实验，其临床有效性与智能体设计收益尚未确立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌筛查工作流中，自报风险信息的采集以及"评估—解释—随访"各环节之间的连续性维护是实际痛点。作者希望构建一个原型系统来组织风险信息、支撑医患沟通与后续随访，并明确区分已实现流程与未完成扩展、界定可复现与具临床意义评估所需的证据。

Method: 浏览器端研究原型Aegis：会话导向控制器管理问题推进与澄清；语言模型辅助答案校验；结构化回答输入独立的规则化风险分析模块；报告通过Firebase与用户、会话记录关联；提供检索增强的健康信息问答与医生—用户沟通模块；整体采用多智能体设计，分离信息采集、验证、评估与报告生成。

Result: 验证范围为45项功能测试规格，覆盖账户管理、问卷交互、报告生成、医生端审核与咨询；评估仅定性描述工作流行为，未提供逐案例执行结果、临床队列、预测性能指标或对比实验数据。

Conclusion: Aegis被定位为用于组织风险信息与随访的工程原型，其临床有效性与智能体架构带来的收益仍未被证实；论文阐述了实现细节，并指出实现可复现、具临床意义的评估所需补齐的证据。

Abstract: Collecting self-reported risk information and maintaining continuity between assessment, explanation, and follow-up are practical components of a lung cancer screening workflow. We present Aegis, a browser-based research prototype that combines a conversational questionnaire, rule-based risk categorization, downloadable reports, retrieval-augmented health information, and doctor-user communication. A session-oriented controller manages question progression and clarification, while a language model assists with answer validation. Structured responses are passed to a separate risk-analysis module, and reports are linked to user and session records through Firebase services. The project adopts a multi-agent design that separates information collection, validation, assessment, and report generation. Its documented verification scope comprises 45 functional test specifications covering account management, questionnaire interaction, reporting, doctor-side review, and consultation. The available evaluation describes workflow behavior qualitatively; it provides no per-case execution results, clinical cohort, predictive-performance measurements, or comparative experiments. We therefore present Aegis as an engineering prototype for organizing risk information and follow-up, with clinical validity and the benefits of its agent-based design remaining unestablished. The paper describes the implementation, distinguishes implemented workflows from incomplete extensions, and identifies the evidence required for reproducible and clinically meaningful evaluation.

</details>


### [46] [Stein Guidance without Gradients: Discretization Uniform Diffusion Inference Time Scaling beyond Particle SMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28654)
*Youheng Zhu,Chenyang Wang,Minshuo Chen,Yiping Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Stein Guidance 方法：将无导数引导与整路径独立性 Metropolis-Hastings 校正相结合，利用每步仅两次查询的高斯候选值加权选择来近似期望意义下的梯度引导，并给出误差随 MH 更新次数超多项式衰减且常数不随引导深度增长的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励引导扩散需要有效引导并纠正近似值函数引入的偏差；布朗运动例子表明，若每步都做完整多项式重采样，SMC 需粒子数随引导深度线性增长才能保持精度，代价随深度不可接受。

Method: 用中心化高斯候选的值加权选择，在期望意义上近似梯度引导（每步最少两次查询）；中心化稳定局部归一化估计，同一批查询同时给出显式路径权重，用于整路径独立性 MH 校正。

Result: 在值函数均匀界与空间正则性条件下，证明有限网格全变差误差随 MH 更新次数呈超多项式衰减，且衰减常数不随引导深度增长；误差前因子在值函数趋近精确 Doob 值及时间网格加密时趋于零。

Conclusion: Stein Guidance 以极低查询成本实现稳定引导与严格校正，避免粒子数随深度线性膨胀，为奖励引导扩散提供具深度无关误差常数的可证明方案。注意：摘要未给出具体实验数值，上述均为理论结论。

Abstract: Reward-guided diffusion requires effective steering and correction of the bias introduced by approximate value functions. A Brownian example shows that SMC with full multinomial resampling at every step can require particle counts to grow linearly with guidance depth to maintain accuracy. We propose Stein Guidance, which combines derivative-free guidance with whole-path independence Metropolis-Hastings correction. Value-weighted selection from centered Gaussian candidates approximates gradient guidance in expectation, using as few as two queries per step. Centering stabilizes the local normalizer estimates, and the same queries yield an explicit path weight for correction. Under uniform value and spatial regularity bounds, we prove superpolynomial decay of finite-grid total variation error in the number of MH updates, with constants that do not grow with guidance depth. The error prefactor tends to zero as the value functions approach exact Doob values and the time grid is refined.

</details>


### [47] [Physical regime changes expose formula misuse in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28669)
*Maohao Ran,Yanting Zhang,Chendong Ma,Jun Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大语言模型在地球与环境科学计算中常跨物理区域误用公式，导致"貌似合理但物理错误"的结果；该文系统量化了这类失败，并验证了后训练（SFT+RL、教师蒸馏）可显著提升区域感知推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球与环境科学问题涉及多种物理区域（regime），公式各有适用域；LLM缺乏对区域依赖约束的识别能力，会把公式用在其有效域之外，产生高可信度的错误结果。作者希望系统刻画这一失败模式，并检验后训练能否缓解。

Method: 在72种物理机制上开展受控实验，评估前沿LLM跨区域误用公式的程度；随后比较三种后训练策略：(1) 可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）；(2) 少量示例监督微调（SFT）与RL结合；(3) 由被提供正确公式的教师模型进行蒸馏。

Result: 前沿LLM普遍在有效域外误用公式，准确率下降13–57个百分点。RLVR使Qwen3.5-9B在区域依赖任务上追平284B的DeepSeek V4 Flash，但效果受基座模型已有公式知识限制；SFT+RL比单独RL提升61个百分点；教师蒸馏使准确率从0%提升至71%。

Conclusion: 区域感知推理是LLM科学计算的关键短板；RL可对齐小模型性能但受基座知识上限制约，而少量示例监督与正确公式蒸馏能带来大幅增益，说明知识注入与奖励优化具有互补性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly employed for scientific computing across the Earth and environmental sciences, but problems in these fields often involve distinct physical regimes. LLMs may fail to recognize these regime-dependent constraints, applying formulas outside their domains of validity and producing plausible but physically incorrect results. We systematically investigate these failures through controlled experiments and assess whether post-training can improve regime-aware reasoning. Across 72 physical mechanisms, we find frontier LLMs frequently misapply formulas outside their regimes of validity, reducing accuracy by 13-57 percentage points. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards enables Qwen3.5-9B to match DeepSeek V4 Flash (284B) on regime-dependent tasks, but its effectiveness is limited by the formulas available in the base model. Combining supervised fine-tuning on a few worked examples with reinforcement learning improves accuracy by 61 percentage points, while distillation from a teacher supplied with the correct formula raises accuracy from 0% to 71%.

</details>


### [48] [Peel, Don't Whack: Partial Coverage, Imperfect Validators, and Intervention Based Shortcut Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28701)
*Ali Alavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析"标签保持编辑"（背景/水印替换等）式捷径缓解方法：提出加性决策规则理论刻画覆盖度与验证器误差的作用，揭示Whac-a-Mole（此消彼长）效应机制，给出假接受率容忍度闭式条件与模型无关认证，并据此提出Peel流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无组标签下捷径缓解依赖自动提出并由验证器接受的干预式标签保持编辑。完全编辑族不变性才是稳健性，但实际覆盖部分且验证器会出错，因此需要理解覆盖度与验证器误差如何影响各捷径组表现。

Method: 在风格化线性模型中分析总体逻辑解与梯度下降最大间隔极限：每个捷径翻转组决策遵循同一加性规则；据此推导worst-group准确率随覆盖度单调性、假接受率闭式容忍度（随覆盖度增长）、无噪声下单次假接受使最大间隔解忽略核心、基于敏感性的发现按显著性递减移除捷径，以及内容无关验证下的模型无关认证（验证器误差项来自审计）。实证含模拟、合成与真实图像深度网络，并提出Peel（Propose-Edit-Evaluate-Learn），可从模型自身错误提出候选编辑。

Result: 模拟验证理论；深度网络显示预测的Whac-a-Mole符号、单调worst-group准确率与验证器脆弱性。给定oracle干预算子库时Peel选出正确算子并在Waterbirds、UrbanCars上优于组标签方法；全生成编辑下从三个发现种子端到端运行时，在6个基准中5个优于ERM但不及精确编辑。

Conclusion: 干预式捷径缓解中覆盖度提升不损worst-group而单体组可退化，验证器假接受率存在随覆盖度放宽的容忍阈值；无噪声时假接受极为致命。内容无关验证下可用审计误差认证，Peel为可行的自动发现—编辑—学习流程，但生成编辑质量仍是瓶颈。

Abstract: Without group labels, shortcut mitigation increasingly relies on interventions : label-preserving edits, such as background or watermark replacement, that are proposed automatically and accepted by a validator. Invariance to a complete family of valid edits yields robustness, but in practice coverage is partial and validators err.
  We analyze both in a stylized linear model. For the population logistic solution and the max-margin limit of gradient descent, the decision on every shortcut-flip group follows one additive rule. Hence worst-group accuracy never decreases with coverage, while individual groups can provably degrade, a mechanism for the Whac-a-Mole effect; full coverage is not always needed. In the logistic regime the validator's false-accept rate must stay below a closed-form tolerance that grows with coverage. With noiseless training data, one false accept can make the max-margin solution ignore the core. In this model, sensitivity-based discovery removes shortcuts in decreasing salience. We also give a model-agnostic certificate, valid under content-independent validation, whose validator-error term comes from an audit.
  Simulations match the theory, and deep networks on synthetic and real images show the predicted Whac-a-Mole signs, monotone worst-group accuracy and validator fragility. The resulting procedure, Peel (Propose-Edit-Evaluate-Learn), can propose candidate edits from the model's own mistakes. Given an oracle library of the benchmarks' intervention operators, it selects the right ones and beats group-label methods on Waterbirds and UrbanCars. With fully generated edits, run end to end from three discovery seeds, it improves on empirical risk minimization (ERM) on five of six benchmarks but trails exact edits.

</details>


### [49] [CLAIR: Consistent LLM Adaptation via Index-free Reprogramming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28721)
*Dongkuk Kim,Jungkyu Shin,Yoonhyuk Choi,Jong Wook Kim,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CLAIR 框架，通过教师引导的 patch 级最优传输对齐（PTA）与预测级一致性（FC）联合约束，解决预训练 LLM 时序预测中 patch 表示如何对齐的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 时序预测依赖上下文化的 patch 表示，但 patch 对齐方式存在两个问题：(1) 同一位置的硬对齐过于受限，自注意力使 patch 状态融合了窗口内其他位置的信息，有用对应关系可能跨位置；(2) 位置无关的灵活匹配又难以保证预测一致——相似 patch 状态在原始时间顺序中可能承担不同角色。

Method: CLAIR（Consistent LLM Adaptation via Index-free Reprogramming）：教师-学生框架。干净视图 EMA 教师指导学生（输入轻度扰动），联合两类损失：(1) PTA，基于表示依赖的软对应关系做 patch 级传输对齐；(2) FC，在对应未来步上施加预测级一致性。预测头保留原始 patch 顺序，教师与传输模块仅训练时使用。

Result: 控制实验显示：无 FC 时 PTA 与逐索引对齐相近；在 EMA 监督下 PTA+FC 相比 Index+FC 将平均 MSE 降低 2.1%（ETTh1）与 2.6%（ETTm1），在 Weather、ETTh2 上同样占优。六个长期基准中五个取得最低 horizon 平均 MSE，M4 上取得最低 OWA，少样本表现具竞争力。

Conclusion: PTA 与 FC 具有互补性，二者结合优于纯索引对齐；该设计在推理阶段零额外开销（仅保留学生），在长期与短期基准上均取得领先或具竞争力的效果。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting with pretrained large language models (LLMs) uses contextualized patch representations, raising the question of how to align them. Matching patches at the same temporal position is natural, but self-attention allows each patch state to incorporate information from elsewhere in the input window, suggesting that useful correspondences can extend across positions. This flexibility, however, does not necessarily translate into consistent forecasts: similar patch states may play different roles when used in their original temporal order. To address both issues, we propose CLAIR (Consistent LLM Adaptation via Index-free Reprogramming), a teacher-guided framework that combines patch-level transport alignment (PTA) and forecast-level consistency (FC). A clean-view exponential moving average (EMA) teacher guides a mildly perturbed student, with PTA establishing soft, representation-dependent correspondences and FC encouraging agreement at corresponding future steps. The forecasting head retains the original patch order. We test this combination through controlled comparisons: while PTA and index-wise alignment perform similarly without FC, PTA+FC under EMA supervision reduces horizon-averaged mean squared error (MSE) by 2.1% on ETTh1 and 2.6% on ETTm1 relative to Index+FC. The same preference for PTA+FC emerges in comparisons with alternative matching strategies on Weather and ETTh2. Beyond these controls, CLAIR achieves the lowest horizon-averaged MSE on five of six long-term benchmarks and the lowest aggregate overall weighted average (OWA) on M4 among evaluated methods, with competitive few-shot performance. As the teacher and transport are used only during training, inference requires only the student.

</details>


### [50] [How many mistakes does it take to learn an interval? Exact Littlestone dimensions of intervals and their unions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28727)
*Miit Daga,Swarna Priya Ramu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文精确确定了离散区间及 j 个区间并集（在 m 个点上）的 Littlestone 维数，给出了一个三区间规律的闭式公式，并给出显式最优学习器及随机化下的精确/近似结果，所有公式经 Lean 4 机器验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对离散区间及 j 个区间并集，仅知道 Littlestone 维数的增长阶，缺乏精确值；同时"等待正例+二分端点"的自然学习器是否最优亦未知。

Method: 以切换预算 k 参数化问题（固定两端标签当 k 为奇数）；用 gap potential 与 block-pairing 学习器给出上界，用对手策略与 refinement step 给出下界，导出显式最优学习器（每轮 O(log m)）；对随机化学习器精确计算阈值类的随机化 Littlestone 维数，并分析贪心对手的期望错误数闭式。

Result: 为使 d 次错误可被强制的最小规模满足三段律：d≤k 时为 d+1；随后为 2d−k+1；当 d>⌈3k/2⌉+k−1 后每翻倍规模恰增 k 次错误。j 个区间对应 k=2j、规模 m+1；单区间阈值为 1,2,3,5,7,10,14,20,…。自然学习器非最优（m<10^5 中有 50,847 个规模可被多逼出 1 次错误），且随机流上平均错误更多。随机化贪心对手的界与最优相差 0.64 以内，猜想为精确（m≤70 已验证）。

Conclusion: 区间类的 Littlestone 维数被完全刻画为三区间闭式规律；自然学习器被证明次优，存在 O(log m)/轮的显式最优学习器（但随机流上更差）；随机化情形给出近最优且疑为精确的界；全部结果经精确计算与 Lean 4 形式化验证。

Abstract: The Littlestone dimension of a hypothesis class equals the smallest number of mistakes an online learner can guarantee against an adaptive adversary. For discrete intervals and unions of $j$ intervals on $m$ points, only its order of growth was known. We determine it exactly. For every switch budget $k$ (with both end labels fixed when $k$ is odd), the smallest size on which $d$ mistakes can be forced follows a three-regime law: $d+1$ while $d\le k$, then $2d-k+1$, and beyond $d=\lceil 3k/2\rceil+k-1$ doubling the size adds exactly $k$ mistakes. Unions of $j$ intervals on $m$ points correspond to $k=2j$ and size $m+1$, and a single interval gives the thresholds $1,2,3,5,7,10,14,20,\dots$. The natural learner that waits for a positive example and binary-searches both endpoints is not optimal: it can be forced into one extra mistake for 50,847 of the domain sizes below $10^5$. Upper bounds come from a gap potential and a block-pairing learner, and lower bounds from adversary strategies and a refinement step. These tools give an explicit optimal learner with $O(\log m)$ time per round, which on random streams makes more mistakes on average than the natural learner. For randomised learners, we compute the randomised Littlestone dimension of thresholds exactly and prove that a greedy adversary forces an explicit closed-form number of expected mistakes on intervals; this bound is within $0.64$ of optimal and, we conjecture, exact (confirmed for $m\le70$). Exact computation tests every result, and the formulas for every switch budget are machine-checked in Lean 4.

</details>


### [51] [The Complexity of Oracle-Efficient Realizable Transductive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28757)
*Changlong Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究可实现的直推式在线学习中，仅用多项式次ERM查询能否达到与halving算法相当的错误数保证；答案是负面的，即使允许加权ERM oracle也存在Ω_β(√(T log N))的错误下界，与技术核心的组合性引理共同给出信息论与oracle效率之间的尖锐分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可实现直推式在线学习中，若 learner 能显式访问假设类，halving算法仅犯 log₂N 次错误。一个自然的问题是：只用多项式次经验风险最小化（ERM）查询，能否获得可比的保证？论文旨在回答这一 oracle 效率与信息论最优之间的差距问题。

Method: 构造性下界证明：对任意固定 β∈(0,1) 与任意多项式查询次数的随机化 learner，构造一个固定的可实现实例（含 N=2^{⌊T^β⌋} 个假设），证明其期望错误为 Ω_β(√(T log N))。核心技术是组合引理：将两个可实现预测问题组合，使错误下界近似相加、而假设类规模只加法式增长；迭代该引理得到覆盖目标参数区间的困难实例族。

Result: 即使授予加权ERM oracle（可优化任意实值线性目标并返回完整最大化假设），learner 仍需 Ω_β(√(T log N)) 次期望错误，与随机playout用 O(T) 次查询达到的 agnostic 速率上界相匹配。对小类同样成立：当 log N=Θ((log T)²) 时，错误下界为 exp(Ω(log T/log log T))。

Conclusion: 显式访问假设类与仅通过多项式次ERM查询之间存在本质性的错误率分离，加权ERM oracle 也无法弥补；这确立了信息论保证与 oracle 高效保证之间的尖锐区分。

Abstract: In realizable transductive online learning, the learner knows the entire sequence of $T$ instances in advance, and the labels are consistent with one of $N$ hypotheses. With explicit access to the hypothesis class, the halving algorithm makes at most $\log_2 N$ mistakes. Can a comparable guarantee be achieved using only polynomially many empirical risk minimization (ERM) queries? We answer this question negatively, even for a weighted-ERM oracle that optimizes arbitrary real-valued linear objectives and returns a complete maximizing hypothesis. For every fixed $β\in(0,1)$ and every randomized learner making polynomially many oracle queries, there exists a fixed realizable instance with exactly $N=2^{\lfloor T^β\rfloor}$ hypotheses on which the learner makes $Ω_β(\sqrt{T\log N})$ mistakes in expectation. This matches the agnostic-rate upper bound achieved by random playout using $O(T)$ oracle calls. The separation persists for much smaller classes as well: along a sequence with $\log N=Θ((\log T)^2)$, we establish a mistake lower bound of $\exp(Ω(\log T/\log\log T))$. Our main technical contribution is a composition lemma that combines two realizable prediction problems such that their mistake lower bounds add, up to a small loss, while their hypothesis class sizes grow only additively. Iterating this lemma yields a family of hard instances spanning the desired parameter regimes, establishing a sharp separation between information-theoretic and oracle-efficient mistake guarantees.

</details>


### [52] [Operating-Condition Normalization in C-MAPSS: Matched Historical Baselines, Multi-Seed Robustness, and Prognostic-Score Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28759)
*Mohammad Adeel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对C-MAPSS FD002/FD004进行受控回顾性审计，采用发动机不重叠折、原始工况标识与仅训练引擎拟合规则，检验"工况标准化降低RUL误差"这一结论是否为比较特征的弱点所致。结果显示原文报告的XGBoost终端RMSE改善被原始传感器+工况匹配历史(28.09 vs 29.08)逆转，且FD002标准化虽降低RMSE却使NASA评分变差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工况变化会掩盖涡扇发动机退化信号，而基于条件标准化后RUL误差的下降常被解读为归一化的独特收益；作者认为这可能仅反映比较特征本身的缺陷，因此需要受控审计来检验该结论的稳健性与可复现性。

Method: 在NASA C-MAPSS FD002与FD004上构建发动机不重叠(engine-disjoint)折，使用原始工况标识、两种因果历史构造、早期寿命基线与连续工况修正；在仅用训练引擎拟合的显式规则下比较Ridge回归、随机森林与XGBoost；并做模型种子、工况簇数、RUL上限、分组验证与解释性检查的敏感性分析。

Result: FD004上XGBoost经六工况标准化后终端RMSE由30.38降至29.08周期，但原始传感器+工况匹配历史+工况标识达到28.09周期，逆转了朴素点估计排名，不过配对发动机区间跨越零。FD002上标准化XGBoost降低终端RMSE，却使非对称NASA评分由12,616升至13,289。六簇复现了已知模拟器运行工况。

Conclusion: 标准化带来的RUL误差下降并不能证明归一化具有独特优势，可能源于比较特征的弱点；仅做敏感性检验而不宣称存在普适最优表征。由于官方测试端点已在早期探索性比较中使用，所有新检验的替代方案均作为回顾性基准证据报告，而非来自无污染的确认性留出集；不宣称提出了新工况发现算法、因果诊断方法或可部署的预测系统。

Abstract: Operating-state changes can obscure turbofan degradation signals, but a reduction in remaining-useful-life (RUL) error after condition-wise standardization may reflect weaknesses of the comparison features instead of a unique benefit of normalization. A controlled retrospective audit examines NASA C-MAPSS FD002 and FD004 with engine-disjoint folds, raw condition identifiers, two constructions of causal history, early-life baselines, and continuous operating-setting correction. Ridge regression, random forests and XGBoost are compared under explicit training-engine-only fitting rules. The previously reported XGBoost FD004 terminal RMSE decreased from 30.38 to 29.08 cycles following six-condition standardization; however, raw sensor values accompanied by condition-matched histories and condition identifiers achieved 28.09 cycles, reversing the naive point estimate ranking, although the paired-engine interval spans zero. For FD002, normalized XGBoost reduced terminal RMSE yet increased the asymmetric NASA score (12,616 to 13,289). Additional model-seed, condition count, RUL-cap, grouped-validation and explanation checks test sensitivity rather than postulating a universally optimal representation. The six clusters reproduce known simulator operating regimes; no new condition discovery algorithm, causal diagnostic method, or field ready prognostic system is claimed. Since the official test endpoints were also used in earlier exploratory comparisons, all newly examined alternatives are reported as retrospective benchmark evidence, not as results from a pristine confirmatory holdout.

</details>


### [53] [Pretraining Representation Lottery Determines Fine-tuning Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28777)
*Fuming Yang,Core Francisco Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 预训练(PT)相当于在表征空间上的一次"彩票"抽签：不同随机种子可得到几乎相同的PT损失，却形成差异巨大的表征几何，这种表征几何决定了微调(FT)的泛化能力；向"不幸种子"蒸馏"幸运种子"的表征可解锁泛化，而仅蒸馏行为则无效。该结论在合成可控数据及Pythia-160M/410M多随机种子复现，且可迁移表征位于深层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PT+FT 是现代深度学习的核心范式，但PT如何塑造FT泛化仍理解不足（仅有PT损失与下游行为，缺乏对表征几何的因果解释）。作者希望建立一个真值概念几何已知的合成受控实验平台，直接研究表征几何对模型行为与FT泛化的因果作用。

Method: 1) 构造实体真值概念几何已知的合成任务，作为可控试验台；2) 发现仅种子不同、PT损失相近的模型在FT泛化上差异显著；3) 因果验证：注入真值概念几何；将"幸运种子"的表征蒸馏到"不幸种子"（对比只蒸馏行为）；表征蒸馏合并PT期间从未交互的实体；4) 在FT中正则化表征漂移；5) 在Pythia-160M/410M上以多个随机种子重复PT，复现蒸馏结果并做逐层定位。

Result: 注入真值几何可达近乎上限（ceiling）的FT泛化；表征蒸馏可显著提升不幸种子的FT泛化，而行为蒸馏无效；表征蒸馏可实现FT本身无法完成的实体合并；正则化表征漂移后，只有好表征模型能拟合并泛化任务，差表征模型在普通FT下靠重塑PT表征"迎合"训练目标；Pythia-160M/410M上复现蒸馏收益，且可迁移表征位于深层。

Conclusion: PT本质是对表征的一次随机抽签，抽到的表征决定FT期间模型如何适应，其作用无法用行为（损失/输出）层面的指标解释。表征而非行为才是PT→FT泛化的关键因果因子，提示应关注表征质量与FT中的表征漂移。

Abstract: Large scale pretraining (PT) followed by fine-tuning (FT) is the central paradigm of modern deep learning, yet how PT shapes FT generalization remains poorly understood. We use a synthetic setup in which the ground-truth concept geometry of entities is known, as a controlled testbed to study how representational geometry affects model behavior and how PT representations determine FT generalization. We first show that models differing only in training seed can closely match in PT loss yet vary substantially in FT generalization. We hypothesize that a representational lottery underlies this phenomenon: the random seed dependent geometry formed during PT determines what FT can reach. We confirm this causally. First, injecting the ground-truth concept geometry into the model yields near-ceiling FT generalization, showing that good representations directly drive generalization. Then, distilling a lucky seed's learned representation into an unlucky seed unlocks FT generalization, while distilling the same model's behavior does not. Furthermore, representation distillation can merge entities that never interacted during PT, which FT alone cannot. Finally, we show that regularizing representational drift during FT enables naturalistic learning: only models with good representations learn the task, and they generalize, while models with poor representations fail to fit it. Under plain FT, by contrast, models with poor representations fit the training objective by reshaping their pretrained representations. We reproduce the distillation result in Pythia-160M and Pythia-410M, each pretrained multiple times with different random seeds, and show that the transferable representation lives in the deep layers at both scales. Overall, PT acts as a lottery over representations, and the representation a model draws determines how it adapts during FT in a way that behavior alone does not explain.

</details>


### [54] [Joint Client Scheduling and Resource Allocation for Optimal Federated Learning over Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28782)
*Ahmad Dabaja,Rachid El-Azouzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出资源感知最优客户端采样（RAOCS），在无线上行资源块、轮次截止时间、更新信息与能量约束下统一最小化采样方差，并给出带上限的注水（capped water-filling）闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在跨设备无线系统中因上行资源有限、信道异构、能量受限与上传截止时间而常采用部分客户端参与，但部分参与引入采样方差，使采样更新偏离全参与更新。现有最优客户端采样（OCS）方法虽降低方差，却只考虑简化的客户端计数预算；无线感知调度方法考虑了通信约束但不直接优化采样方差。作者希望弥合这两类方法的鸿沟。

Method: 将客户端调度建模为在四类约束（上行资源块、轮次截止时间、更新信息、能量）下最小化采样方差的问题；通过分析该优化问题的结构，推导出带上限的注水（capped water-filling）解，该解在客户端更新重要度、无线资源代价与能量可行性三者之间进行平衡。

Result: 实验表明，RAOCS 达到与 OCS 相当的精度，同时将每次迭代的平均上传时间降低超过 252 倍。

Conclusion: RAOCS 将方差最优采样与无线资源约束统一在同一框架内，兼得精度与通信效率，说明在真实无线条件下可将最优采样思想推向可部署的调度方案。

Abstract: Federated learning often relies on partial client participation because full participation is costly in cross-device wireless systems with limited uplink resources, heterogeneous channel conditions, energy constraints, and strict upload deadlines. However, partial participation introduces sampling variance, causing the sampled update to deviate from the full-participation update. Existing optimal client sampling methods reduce this variance but usually rely on simplified client-count budgets, while wireless-aware scheduling methods account for communication constraints but do not directly optimize sampling variance. This paper proposes resource-aware optimal client sampling (RAOCS), a unified variance-minimizing client scheduling framework under uplink resource-block, round deadline, update-information, and energy constraints. The resulting problem admits a capped water-filling solution that balances client update importance, wireless resource cost, and energy feasibility. Experimental results demonstrate that RAOCS achieves accuracy comparable to that of OCS while reducing the average upload time per iteration by more than a factor of 252.

</details>


### [55] [ArrythML-II: An Optimized TinyML Framework for ECG Arrhythmia Detection and Multi-Class Beat Classification on Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28791)
*Nagarajan S,Kurian Polachan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出ArrythML-II——一个面向可穿戴平台、用于端侧心电心律失常检测与多类心拍分类的优化TinyML框架：以系统性超参数搜索与自定义评分机制筛选紧凑自编码器，扩展为六类心拍分类器，经INT8量化后用TFLite Micro部署于PSoC 6单导联可穿戴设备并完成实测验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备上实时心电分析需要模型体积小、延迟低、功耗低，但如何在检测性能、重构误差阈值与模型规模之间取得平衡并落地于资源受限MCU，现有工作缺乏系统化的架构选择方法与端到端硬件验证。

Method: 1) 构建系统化超参数优化框架，搜索紧凑自编码器架构用于二分类心律失常检测；2) 设计自定义评分机制，综合检测性能、重构误差阈值与模型体积来指导架构选择；3) 基于优化后的自编码器结构派生轻量分类器，扩展至六类心拍分类；4) 两模型均INT8量化，采用TensorFlow Lite Micro部署于自研电池供电、基于PSoC 6 MCU的单导联心电设备；5) 验证方式包括预载ECG片段、实时ECG信号，以及基于ESP32-S3的回放MIT-BIH注释记录为模拟波形的ECG仿真器。

Result: 自编码器与分类器模型规模约44 KB与22 KB，单段端到端处理时间分别为5.1 ms与4.7 ms，其中推理占3.95 ms与1.38 ms。在MIT-BIH心律失常数据库上的精细评估中，自编码器准确率84.76%、精确率82.68%、召回率77.99%；六类分类器准确率82.29%、加权精确率90.18%、加权召回率82.29%。平台级验证在预载片段、实时信号与ESP32-S3仿真器三种条件下均完成。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将经系统优化与INT8量化的TinyML模型集成到MCU级可穿戴平台以实现实时心电分析具备可行性；所提出的评分驱动架构选择流程与多通道验证（含硬件在环仿真）为资源受限设备上的心电监测部署提供了可复用范式。

Abstract: This paper presents ArrythML-II, an optimized TinyML framework for on-device ECG arrhythmia detection and multi-class beat classification on wearable platforms. A systematic hyperparameter optimization framework is developed to identify compact autoencoder architectures for binary ECG arrhythmia detection. A custom scoring mechanism jointly considers detection performance, reconstruction-error thresholds, and model size to guide architecture selection. The framework is further extended to six-class ECG beat classification using a lightweight classifier derived from the optimized autoencoder architecture. Both models are INT8 quantized and deployed using TensorFlow Lite Micro on a custom-developed, battery-operated, single-lead ECG device based on the PSoC~6 microcontroller. The deployed autoencoder and classifier have model sizes of approximately 44~KB and 22~KB, respectively, with end-to-end processing times of 5.1~ms and 4.7~ms per ECG segment. Model inference accounts for 3.95~ms and 1.38~ms of these processing times, respectively. In the refined arrhythmia detection evaluation using the MIT-BIH Arrhythmia Database, the autoencoder achieved an accuracy of 84.76\%, precision of 82.68\%, and recall of 77.99\%. The multi-class classifier achieved 82.29\% accuracy, weighted precision of 90.18\%, and weighted recall of 82.29\%. Beyond offline model evaluation, the wearable platform is validated using preloaded ECG segments, live ECG signals, and an ESP32-S3-based ECG emulator that replays annotated MIT-BIH recordings as analog waveforms. The results demonstrate the feasibility of integrating optimized TinyML models into wearable platforms for real-time ECG analysis.

</details>


### [56] [Synthetic EHR Generators Should Be Audited Like LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28804)
*Inigo Stibbe,Padmanabhan Ramnarayan,A. Aldo Faisal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文主张对合成EHR生成模型应开展记录级别的LLM式记忆审计，并在过拟合GPT-2模型上以患者级canary与轨迹重建验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成EHR生成日益采用大型自回归模型，但隐私评估滞后，过度依赖相似度启发式与聚合指标，掩盖了患者个体层面的记忆化风险；同时现有成员推断评估在患者表示与攻击流程上做法不一致。

Method: 横向比较四个主流自回归EHR生成器的成员推断评估实践；提出基于部分上下文的轨迹重建，并引入随机分配的患者级canary以区分记忆关联与临床泛化；在刻意过拟合1000条MIMIC-IV患者轨迹的GPT-2风格模型中做受控实验。

Result: 模型系统性恢复训练患者的canary，轨迹重建准确率随上下文增加趋近完美；留出患者无系统性canary恢复，重建率低且随上下文仅有边际提升。

Conclusion: 患者在个体记录层面存在可检测记忆化，LLM式审计可行；该实验确立了阳性对照基线，为在真实训练模型上进一步评估这些方法提供参照。

Abstract: Synthetic electronic health record (EHR) generation is increasingly adopting large autoregressive models, yet privacy evaluation has not kept pace, relying heavily on similarity-based heuristics and aggregate metrics that can obscure patient-specific memorisation. We argue that these models should additionally undergo LLM-style memorisation audits at the level of individual patient records. We support this position by comparing membership-inference evaluations across four prominent autoregressive EHR generators, highlighting inconsistent evaluation practices in patient representations and attack procedures. We evaluate trajectory reconstruction from partial context and introduce randomly assigned patient-level canaries to distinguish memorised associations from clinical generalisation. We examine these methods in a GPT-2-style model deliberately overfit to 1,000 MIMIC-IV patient trajectories, creating a controlled setting in which memorisation should be readily detectable. The model systematically recovers training patients' canaries, and their trajectory reconstruction approaches perfect accuracy with increasing context. Held-out patients show no systematic canary recovery, while reconstruction remains low and improves only marginally with additional context. These experiments establish a positive-control baseline for further evaluation of these methods in realistically trained models.

</details>


### [57] [RadAG: Reward-Adversarial Attention Guidance for Efficient Inference-Time Alignment of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28809)
*Hyeongchan Jeon,Kwanyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Reward-Adversarial Attention Guidance (RadAG)，通过对抗性地扰动单个注意力块的 logits 并外推，实现仅需局部反向传播的奖励引导采样，在提升奖励与文图对齐的同时显著节省时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流模型生成质量仍不理想。现有推理期方法存在权衡：外推类引导（如 CFG）仅需前向、开销低，但参考方向由与奖励无关的启发式规则构造，无法朝指定奖励引导；梯度类奖励引导可直接提升奖励，却需在每步对整个生成器反向传播，代价高昂。因此作者追问：外推式引导能否在不做全模型反向的情况下提升奖励。

Method: RadAG 将参考估计构造为：对单个注意力块的 attention logits 沿其奖励梯度做对抗性扰动，再由此外推远离该扰动参考。理论上证明该更新对干净估计的奖励提升具有一阶保证，且反向传播止于被扰动的块，而非遍历整个生成器。

Result: 在 FLUX.2-klein-4B 与 9B 上，配合 PickScore 与 aesthetic 奖励，RadAG 在所有设置下取得最高的目标奖励，且相较梯度式与候选采样式基线节省 30–65% 时间。

Conclusion: RadAG 表明外推式引导可在仅局部反向的条件下有效实现奖励导向采样，兼顾奖励最大化与推理效率，为推理期引导提供了一种低成本、可证明有效的折中方案。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based models have achieved remarkable success in generation, yet their samples often remain suboptimal in fidelity and text alignment. Two lines of inference-time methods address this: extrapolation guidance, such as classifier-free guidance (CFG) and its variants, and reward guidance, which aligns samples with reward models of human preference. Extrapolation guidance needs only forward passes, but it builds its reference by heuristic rules unrelated to the reward and therefore cannot steer sampling toward a given reward. Gradient-based reward guidance steers sampling directly toward a higher reward, but backpropagates through the entire generator at every step. This trade-off raises the question of whether extrapolation guidance can steer sampling toward a higher reward without a full backward pass. To answer this, we introduce Reward-Adversarial Attention Guidance (RadAG), which constructs the reference by perturbing the attention logits of a single block against their reward gradient and extrapolates away from it. We prove that this update increases the reward of the clean estimate to first order, while its backward pass stops at the perturbed block instead of traversing the entire generator. On FLUX.2-klein-4B and 9B with PickScore and aesthetic rewards, RadAG achieves the highest target reward in every setting with 30-65% less time than gradient-based and candidate-based baselines.

</details>


### [58] [Pixel Auto-Encoder: Reconstructing Language Model Activations Through Pixel Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28822)
*Haru Furuichi,Phan Xuan Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Pixel Auto-Encoder，将LLM中间激活转换为32×32、32色像素画并可重建回模型继续计算，探索以图像作为解释LLM激活的替代模态，揭示视觉对齐与语言能力之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM高维内部表征难以解释，现有工作多将其翻译为自然语言，作者希望探索图像作为另一种人类可理解的目标模态，检验激活能否被视觉化表达且不损害模型语言能力。

Method: 设计Pixel Auto-Encoder：把LLM中间激活编码为32×32、32色像素画，再解码重建回模型以支持后续计算；通过让中间表征趋近预定义概念图像进行约束，评估概念对齐程度、替换像素表征对下一词预测的影响，以及视觉对齐与语言模型性能的关系。

Result: 对部分概念模型能生成与目标对齐的图像；用概念图像替换像素表征可影响下一词预测；但存在视觉对齐与语言模型性能之间的权衡，且模型对难以视觉解读的像素级改动较为敏感。

Conclusion: 基于图像的LLM激活表征具有可行性，但受限于视觉—语言性能权衡和像素级敏感性，说明该方法作为解释工具既有潜力也有明显局限。

Abstract: Interpreting the high-dimensional internal representations of large language models (LLMs) remains a challenging problem. Recent approaches have explored translating these representations into human-understandable forms, particularly natural language. In this work, we explore the use of images as an alternative target modality for interpreting LLM activations. As a first step toward this goal, we introduce Pixel Auto-Encoder, which converts intermediate LLM activations into $32 \times 32$, 32-color pixel art and reconstructs them for subsequent computation within the model. By encouraging the intermediate representations to resemble predefined concept images, we investigate whether LLM activations can be expressed visually while preserving the model's language capabilities. We find that the model can produce target-aligned images for some concepts, and that replacing pixel representations with concept images can influence next-token predictions. However, we also observe a trade-off between visual alignment and language-model performance, as well as sensitivity to pixel-level changes that are difficult to interpret visually. These findings demonstrate the feasibility and limitations of image-based representations of LLM activations.

</details>


### [59] [Hidden Sensitivity in LLM Pre-training: A Dynamic System View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28830)
*Bo Liu,Yuan Xie,Junan Chen,Fujun Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将LLM预训练建模为权重与Adam状态共同演化的非自治动力系统，提出优化器相对坐标下的归一化敏感度分数以识别敏感检查点；发现仅10^-5的权重扰动即可造成35.7%的top-1预测分歧，并据此进行定向稳定化，将分歧降至0.83%、生成完全匹配提升至88.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练loss曲线会掩盖显著不稳定性：微小扰动可能导致预测急剧变化而loss几乎不变，传统基于loss的评估无法察觉这种隐藏的、依赖优化器状态的敏感性，需要揭示其机制并加以诊断与缓解。

Method: 将LLM预训练形式化为联合演化模型权重与Adam优化器状态的**非自治动力系统**；在该表述下引入**优化器相对坐标中的归一化敏感度分数**，用于定位敏感训练检查点；并在该检查点上分别对权重、Adam一阶与二阶矩施加扰动，观察top-1预测分歧、基准答案分歧与贪婪生成一致率；在OLMo-1B上验证敏感模式的普适性；最后依据诊断结果实施定向稳定化。

Result: 在敏感检查点上，仅10^-5的权重扰动经200步训练后导致35.7%的top-1预测分歧、16.2%的基准答案分歧，贪婪生成仅0.8%保持一致，而验证loss相近；对Adam一阶、二阶矩的扰动产生相当量级的影响，OLMo-1B呈现类似敏感模式；定向稳定化将top-1分歧降至0.83%、生成完全匹配升至88.3%，代价是验证loss上升0.11 nat。

Conclusion: LLM预训练中存在一种隐藏的、依赖优化器状态的敏感性，基于loss的常规评估可能完全忽略它；本文的诊断方法既能识别该现象，也具备缓解潜力，说明预训练稳定性评估应纳入优化器相对坐标下的敏感度指标。

Abstract: In Large Language Model (LLM) pre-training, loss curves can conceal substantial instability, i.e., even small perturbations may lead to dramatically different predictions while leaving loss nearly unchanged. To investigate this phenomenon and reveal the underlying mechanisms, we first formulate LLM pre-training as a non-autonomous dynamical system that jointly evolves model weights and Adam optimizer states. Based on this formulation, we introduce a normalized sensitivity score in optimizer-relative coordinates to identify sensitive training checkpoints. At one such checkpoint, a weight perturbation of only 10^(-5) leads to 35.7% disagreement in top-1 predictions and 16.2% disagreement in benchmark answers after 200 training steps, with only 0.8% of greedy generations remaining identical despite similar validation losses. Furthermore, we find that perturbations to Adam's first and second moments induce comparably large effects, with similar sensitivity patterns observed in OLMo-1B. Finally, guided by our diagnostic, targeted stabilization reduces top-1 disagreement to 0.83% and increases generation exact match to 88.3%, at the cost of a 0.11-nat increase in validation loss. Our findings reveal a hidden form of optimizer-state-dependent sensitivity in LLM pre-training that conventional loss-based evaluations may overlook, while demonstrating its potential for diagnosis and mitigation.

</details>


### [60] [AgriManager: A Framework and Benchmark for LLM-RL Generalization in Agricultural Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28844)
*Chi Gui,Junwen Zheng,Ritwik Nigam,Qianlan Yang,Meagan Lang,Vardhan Dongre,Jeremiah Barr,Yu-Xiong Wang,Vikram S. Adve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AgriManager 框架，首次用强化学习跨多个作物过程模拟器（DSSAT、WOFOST、Cycles）训练 LLM 策略，并按"schema shift"形式化定义三级泛化阶梯，系统评估 LLM-RL 在农业管理中的分布内与跨界面泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业管理异质性体现在两个维度：一是固定任务界面内的分布偏移（天气、作物、价格随年份和地点变化），二是跨界面的结构偏移（不同农场观测变量、可用动作与管理目标不同）。作者将后者形式化为 schema shift，即策略所面对的 schema Σ=(Σ_O, Σ_A, Σ_R) 在训练与部署间发生变化，并追问 LLM-RL 能否同时跨这两个维度泛化。

Method: 构建 AgriManager 框架，统一 DSSAT、WOFOST、Cycles 三个基于过程的作物模拟器，用 RL 训练 LLM 策略。提出三级泛化阶梯：Tier 1 同 schema 内的分布偏移；Tier 2 沿 Σ_O/Σ_A/Σ_R 的单轴 schema 偏移；Tier 3 跨模拟器的组合式 schema 偏移。全部在冻结的留出测试集上评估，每种方法使用三个训练种子。

Result: 固定 schema 下，带显式推理、经 RL 训练的 Qwen3-4B 在相同交互预算下优于神经网络 PPO；缺少推理时训练停滞在接近"无动作"处（因奖励惩罚投入）。单轴 schema 偏移下：Σ_O 迁移依赖决策关键状态；Σ_A 结果与联合动作菜单覆盖度一致；带推理时仅用产量目标训练的策略可迁移到未见目标。组合式 schema 偏移下：单一来源训练大多损害跨模拟器零样本迁移；仅在相同组件其他组合上训练的通用策略，在 DSSAT 上恢复约 86% 的专家策略增益，但在 WOFOST 与 Cycles 的利润目标上仍低于专家。

Conclusion: LLM-RL 在固定 schema 内可超越同等预算的神经网络 PPO，显式推理是其有效学习的关键；单轴 schema 迁移可行且有明确条件（决策关键状态、动作菜单覆盖、推理能力），但组合式 schema 偏移仍是主要瓶颈，跨模拟器零样本迁移能力有限，统一策略尚不能完全替代针对性专家策略。

Abstract: Agricultural management is heterogeneous along two axes. The first is distributional shift within a fixed task interface: weather, crops, and prices vary across years and locations while sensors, actions, and objective stay fixed. The second is structural shift across interfaces: different farms expose different observed variables, available actions, and management objectives. We formalize the second axis as schema shift: variation in the policy-facing schema $Σ= (Σ_O, Σ_A, Σ_R)$ between training and deployment. We test whether LLM-RL generalizes across both axes with AgriManager, to our knowledge the first framework that trains LLM policies with RL across multiple process-based crop simulators, unifying DSSAT, WOFOST, and Cycles. We design a three-tier generalization ladder: Tier 1 within-schema distributional shift, Tier 2 single-axis schema shift along $Σ_O / Σ_A / Σ_R$, Tier 3 compositional schema shift across simulators, all scored on a frozen held-out test suite with three training seeds per method. Within fixed schemas, Qwen3-4B trained by RL with explicit reasoning exceeds neural-network PPO trained with the same interaction budget; without reasoning, training stalls near no-action where the reward penalizes input use. Under single-axis schema shift, $Σ_O$ transfer needs decision-critical state, $Σ_A$ results are consistent with joint action-menu coverage, and with reasoning a policy trained on yield alone transfers to unseen objectives. Under compositional schema shift, single-source training mostly hurts zero-shot transfer across simulators, and a unified policy trained only on other combinations of the same components recovers about 86% of the gain of a specialist trained on the target combination on DSSAT and stays below the specialists on the WOFOST and Cycles profit targets. Project page: https://agrimanager-framework.github.io

</details>


### [61] [Dynamic Model Merging with Multi-Layer Aggregated Routing Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28855)
*Yuxi Li,Jiacong Hu,Jie Song,Mingli Song,Yuanyu Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MATE（多层聚合路由证据），通过量化并选取高可分性层、聚合其路由分数，改进动态模型合并中的任务路由与头路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态模型合并（如任务路由对路由层选择高度敏感、头路由仅靠简单 max-logit 竞争）的路由组件存在局限，需更鲁棒的路由信号。

Method: 引入任务路由可分性度量，跨层经验分析发现不同层提供互补证据；据此按可分性选取多个信息量大的层，聚合成层路由分数得到稳健信号用于任务选择；再将该信号与 max-logit 分数结合以指导分类头选择。

Result: 在视觉基准上的大量实验表明 MATE 持续达到 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: 多层聚合路由证据可统一改进任务级与头级路由，缓解层选择敏感性与简单竞争机制的不足。

Abstract: Dynamic model merging aims to retain the efficiency of a unified model while better preserving task-specific expertise through input-dependent routing at both the task and head levels. However, even the routing components of the state-of-the-art approach remain limited: the task routing is highly sensitive to the choice of routing layer, whereas the head routing relies on simple max-logit competition. To address these limitations, we propose multi-layer aggregated routing evidence (MATE) as a unified mechanism for improving both task and head routing. Specifically, we first introduce a metric to quantify task routing separability across different layers. Empirical analysis of this metric reveals that different layers provide complementary evidence for identifying the tasks relevant to an input. Motivated by this observation, MATE selects multiple informative layers according to their routing separability and aggregates their layer-wise routing scores to produce a more robust signal that can be directly used for task selection. Moreover, we observe that this signal also encodes useful information about the suitability of the corresponding classification heads. This motivates us to further combine the MATE signal with the max-logit score to guide classification head selection. Extensive experiments on vision benchmarks demonstrate that MATE consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [62] [PhyMind: Physics-Informed Masked Autoencoding for EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28856)
*A Adith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PhyMind 用可生成式 EEG 前向物理模型替代自由解码器，在掩码 Transformer 编码器上显著提升 token 频谱可线性解码性，并在冻结特征下以 7.2M 参数逼近 92.8M 的 MANAS-2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EEG 基础模型几乎只用自由形式解码器的掩码重建（如 MANAS-2 外加手工频谱目标），解码器缺乏对 EEG 生成机制的约束。作者希望考察把解码器换成 EEG 生成式前向模型后，编码器表征会发生什么变化。

Method: 保留标准掩码 Transformer 编码器；解码器须经 68 个皮层区域解释被掩码信号，每区由阻尼随机振子与非周期 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程驱动，并用固定边界元导联场按导联组合投影到记录蒙太奇。训练损失为掩码重建、Whittle 谱似然、振子动力学残差、最小范数源先验与带惩罚神经残差之和；在同一编码器、数据、步数与随机种子下与 MAE 解码器及 MANAS-2 目标做受控对比。

Result: token 级频谱探针（幅度归一化目标）平均 R^2 在三数据集为 0.67/0.77/0.77，MAE 为 0.24/0.50/0.50，MANAS-2 目标为 0.48/0.67/0.66，接近已发布 92.8M 参数的 MANAS-2（0.71/0.81/0.80）。受控设置下下游增益较小：四任务平均平衡准确率 56.6（MAE 冻结 53.8），微调 63.8 vs. 62.7。物理解码器的区域级特征使冻结四类运动想象准确率提升 7–14 点；7.2M 参数、3k 小时公开 EEG 预训练模型冻结特征 63.0，对比 MANAS-2 的 61.6；全微调后 MANAS-2 更优（66.8 vs. 65.4）。推断源未复现经典逆解在运动想象中的偏侧化，故不主张解剖可解释性。

Conclusion: 以生成式物理前向模型作解码器，能在小参数规模下显著改善编码器表征的频谱线性可解码性与冻结特征的下游表现，但受控下游增益有限、全微调仍不及大规模 MANAS-2，且源推断缺乏解剖可解释性；代码与权重已公开。

Abstract: EEG foundation models are pre-trained almost exclusively with masked reconstruction through a free-form decoder, sometimes with hand-crafted spectral targets added, as in MANAS-2. We study what happens when that decoder is replaced by a generative forward model of EEG. PhyMind keeps a standard masked transformer encoder, but its decoder must explain the masked signal through 68 cortical regions, each driven by damped stochastic oscillators and an aperiodic Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process, projected to the recording's own montage by a fixed boundary-element lead field evaluated per derivation. Training combines masked reconstruction with a Whittle spectral likelihood, an oscillator-dynamics residual, a minimum-norm source prior and a penalised neural residual. In a controlled comparison (same encoder, data, steps and seed), the physics decoder produces encoder tokens whose spectral content is far more linearly decodable than with a masked-autoencoder decoder or with the MANAS-2 objective: mean R^2 on a token-level spectral probe with amplitude-normalised targets is 0.67/0.77/0.77 on three datasets, against 0.24/0.50/0.50 (MAE) and 0.48/0.67/0.66 (MANAS-2 objective), approaching the released 92.8M-parameter MANAS-2 (0.71/0.81/0.80). Downstream gains in the controlled setting are small (four-task mean balanced accuracy 56.6 vs. 53.8 for MAE frozen, 63.8 vs. 62.7 fine-tuned). Region-level features from the physics decoder raise frozen four-class motor-imagery accuracy by 7-14 points, so a 7.2M-parameter model pre-trained on 3k hours of open EEG is comparable to the released MANAS-2 with frozen features (63.0 vs. 61.6). After full fine-tuning, MANAS-2 remains better (66.8 vs. 65.4 for our best model). The inferred sources do not reproduce the lateralisation that a classical inverse solution finds in motor imagery, so we do not claim anatomical interpretability. Code and weights are released.

</details>


### [63] [Interaction Retention and Output-Likelihood Training in Parallel Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28858)
*Da Li,Bo Jin,Xiaopeng Wei,Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文给出并行采样器（如掩码扩散语言模型）分布失真的精确局部刻画：在满支撑乘积律附近，采样器响应的Gram算子在对偶分解中可对角化，其特征值为“保留概率”谱，据此确定输出精度与条件校准的精确边界，并导出训练与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行采样器每步从条件边缘分布同时生成多个坐标并随机化同批坐标，即使使用精确条件分布，输出分布仍被扭曲；现有工作缺乏对该失真的精确刻画，因而无法界定输出精度与条件校准之间的取舍。

Method: 在满支撑乘积律邻域做一阶/局部展开；证明采样器响应的Gram算子（协方差型）在对偶分解（interaction decomposition）基下对角，特征值为“某交互具有唯一最晚被揭示坐标”的保留概率（retention probabilities）；由此计算该保留谱对输出精度、条件校准边界及正则化路径的决定作用，并分析有限混合似然训练的总体响应；对成对交互给出二分类结论：同质保留下输出似然训练等价于单一上下文增益（context gain），异质保留下出现超出标量的校正方向，增益受显式异质性间隙上界约束。实验：二值化数字与MNIST，异质调度库内输出似然训练；以及冻结的1.696亿参数掩码扩散语言模型上测量串行归一化上下文增益。

Result: 理论上得到保留谱决定精度—校准前沿的精确关系及标量增益的充要条件；实验上十二组配对比较中，异质调度库内的输出似然训练全部降低校准后测试负对数似然，且该增益在对每对分别重校准及更强路径优化器下仍保持；在1.696亿参数掩码扩散模型上，8轮与16轮的串行归一化上下文增益为1.070与1.056，与预测值1.134和1.058相符。

Conclusion: 并行采样的分布失真是可精确刻画、由保留概率谱控制的局部线性效应；这一谱既给出输出精度与条件校准的边界与训练响应理论，也指导在异质调度下进行输出似然训练以获得可测量的校准改进。

Abstract: Parallel samplers such as masked diffusion language models generate several coordinates per step from conditional marginals and randomize which coordinates are generated together, so even exact conditionals return a distorted distribution. We give an exact local account of this distortion. Around any full-support product law, the Gram operator of the sampler's response is diagonal in the interaction decomposition, and its eigenvalues are retention probabilities: the probability that an interaction has a unique latest-revealed coordinate. This retention spectrum determines the exact frontier between output accuracy and conditional calibration, its regularization path, and the population response of finite-mixture likelihood training. For pairwise interactions it yields a sharp dichotomy. Under homogeneous retention, output-likelihood training is equivalent to a single context gain; heterogeneous retention opens correction directions beyond any scalar, with an advantage bounded by an explicit heterogeneity gap. On binarized digits and MNIST, output-likelihood training within a heterogeneous schedule library lowers calibrated test negative log-likelihood in all twelve paired comparisons, and the gain survives pair-specific recalibration and a stronger path optimizer. For a frozen 169.6-million-parameter masked diffusion language model, serially normalized context gains are 1.070 and 1.056 at 8 and 16 rounds, against the predicted values 1.134 and 1.058.

</details>


### [64] [MolReJev: Decision Models Are In-Context Molecular Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28965)
*Kaiyuan Lu,Jiatong Li,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MolReJev，将Jev式候选打分与语言模型上下文学习结合，一次前向联合评分候选标签，实现跨任务的分子预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分子预测器针对固定任务集训练，缺乏跨任务泛化；逐token生成答案推理成本高，且通用语言模型缺少任务特定分子知识。

Method: 从现有任务数据中检索相似带标签示例，基于语言模型进行上下文学习（ICL），以Jev式候选打分方式在一次前向传播中联合为候选标签打分。

Result: ICL提升了通用Kev检查点与单独训练的MolReJev的预测；增益覆盖所有评估组（含留出任务），并扩展到训练未见的化合物；在匹配评估和同示例最近邻预测上总体优于MolecularGPT；四示例设置下与训练文本基线ROC-AUC相近但吞吐更高。

Conclusion: 决策模型可作为上下文分子预测器，适用于化合物、药物对与反应预测。

Abstract: Molecular prediction helps prioritize compounds for screening. Specialized predictors are trained for a fixed set of tasks, limiting cross-task generalization. Language models can condition on task descriptions and have recently shown promise in molecular prediction. However, generating answers token by token adds inference cost, and general-purpose models often lack task-specific molecular knowledge. We thus propose MolReJev, which combines Jev-style candidate scoring with in-context learning (ICL) on a language model. MolReJev retrieves similar labeled examples from existing task data and scores candidate labels jointly in one forward pass. ICL improves predictions from the released general-purpose Kev checkpoint and the separately trained MolReJev. For the trained model, gains span all evaluation groups, including held-out tasks, and extend to compounds absent from training. MolReJev generally outperforms MolecularGPT on matched evaluations and nearest-neighbor prediction with the same examples. With four examples, MolReJev and a trained text baseline achieve similar ROC-AUC, with higher measured throughput for MolReJev. These results show that decision models can serve as in-context molecular predictors for compounds, drug pairs, and reactions.

</details>


### [65] [Global Stability and Loss Convergence in Matrix Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28975)
*Eric Gan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究矩阵分解目标 L(X,Y)=f(XY) 上的梯度下降收敛性，证明了存在正的学习率阈值，使得从任意有限初始化出发，参数有界、平方梯度可和、损失收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵分解（如深度线性网络、低秩/过参数化模型）上的梯度下降虽在实践中有效，但缺乏在较弱假设下的收敛保证；作者希望在凸、二阶连续可微、梯度有界且Lipschitz的输出损失（含交叉熵损失）这一较一般设定下，给出可证明的收敛条件。

Method: 针对 L(X,Y)=f(XY) 的梯度下降轨迹，构造由两参数矩阵之间的不平衡量（imbalance）导出的一个标量量，并证明该量在每一步迭代中单调下降，从而结合输出损失的凸性得到全局收敛性分析；极小集在输入/输出不变性意义下紧致。

Result: 证明了存在一个正学习率阈值：只要学习率不超过该阈值，从每一个有限初始化出发都保证参数有界、梯度平方可和（summability of squared gradients）以及损失函数收敛。

Conclusion: 在较弱的正则性假设下，矩阵分解模型上的梯度下降具有严格的可证明收敛性；基于矩阵不平衡量的单调递减构造是分析此类非凸问题的有效工具，为交叉熵等常见损失提供了收敛保证。

Abstract: We study the matrix factorization objective $\mathcal L(X,Y)=f(XY)$ for convex, twice continuously differentiable output losses with bounded and Lipschitz gradient and a minimizing set that is compact modulo specified input and output invariances. This includes the well-known cross entropy loss. We prove that a positive learning-rate threshold guarantees bounded parameters, summability of squared gradients, and convergence of the loss from every finite initialization. The proof constructs a quantity from the imbalance between parameter matrices that decreases over every step of the gradient descent trajectory.

</details>


### [66] [Stochastic non-metric MDS for noisy high-dimensional data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28985)
*Sebastian Damrich,Dmitry Kobak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将度量MDS在高维无信息数据上的理论推广到有限维、高维且距离集中的数据，证明其嵌入仍接近与数据无关的极限情形；同时指出非度量MDS对高维噪声更稳健，并给出基于SGD的PyTorch快速实现，实验表明其在全局结构保持上显著优于其他方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度量MDS在高维情形下受"维数灾难"影响，已有理论结果仅针对完全无信息数据；而高维真实数据中距离趋于集中，需要更一般的理论刻画，同时非度量MDS因计算慢而缺乏实用实现。

Method: 理论层面：将度量MDS在无信息数据上的分析扩展至有限高维、距离集中的数据，证明其嵌入在分布上接近数据无关极限。算法层面：利用PyTorch以随机梯度下降实现快速非度量MDS；实验层面：在多个模拟与真实数据集（群体基因组学、scRNA-seq）上评估全局结构保持性能。

Result: 度量MDS在距离集中的高维数据上仍退化为近似数据无关的嵌入；非度量MDS对高维噪声更稳健，可产生有信息量的嵌入；所提快速实现使非度量MDS在全局结构保持上大幅优于其他方法。

Conclusion: 度量MDS在高维数据下受集中现象制约，而非度量MDS是处理高维噪声数据的有效且可行的选择；快速SGD实现使其具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Multidimensional scaling (MDS) constructs low-dimensional embeddings that approximate pairwise distances in a high-dimensional dataset. MDS exists in several flavors, with metric MDS approximating distances directly and non-metric MDS additionally optimizing for an arbitrary monotonic transformation of the high-dimensional distances. Empirically, metric MDS is known to suffer from the curse of dimensionality. Here we extend prior theoretical results for metric MDS on fully uninformative data to a more general case of finite, high-dimensional data with concentrated distances. We show that the metric MDS embeddings of such datasets stay distributionally close to the data-independent limit case. We then show that non-metric MDS is more robust to high-dimensional noise and can yield informative embeddings of such data. While existing implementations of non-metric MDS are slow, we develop a fast implementation using stochastic gradient descent in PyTorch. Using our implementation, we conduct an empirical study using multiple simulated and real-world datasets, including population-genomic and scRNA-seq data, and show that non-metric MDS can strongly outperform other methods in terms of global structure preservation.

</details>


### [67] [S$^2$LMR: Supervised Sequential Long-Context Multi-Step Retriever](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28995)
*Qiang Hu,Kaiyi Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 S²LMR，一个简单高效的监督式序列检索器，将多步检索建模为显式文本状态的演化，由单个共享 Transformer 编码，用约2小时单卡训练即可在最长10M token上下文上达到与SOTA相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文多步检索是RAG的关键能力，但现有强方法训练成本高、流程复杂；监督式检索器简单便宜却在多步检索场景明显落后。作者指出这一差距并非源自监督训练本身，而是训练与推理之间存在三处错配：训练负例过于简单、检索前缀含噪、打分与位置无关。

Method: 基于上述诊断，将多步检索形式化为"显式文本状态"的逐步演化，每一步状态由同一个共享 Transformer 编码并输出，从而替代复杂的多模块或多轮独立检索流程；针对三类错配相应调整训练负例、前缀处理和位置感知打分。

Result: 在长上下文推理、多跳QA与needle-in-a-haystack基准（上下文4K至10M token）上，性能与SOTA方法相当，显著优于此前的监督式检索器；训练仅需单张A6000约2小时（覆盖最长10M token上下文），且对超参数范围具备鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 监督式检索器在多步长上下文检索中的落后主要源于训练-推错配而非范式本身；通过显式文本状态演化与针对性对齐，可用极低训练成本获得接近SOTA的效果，为高效RAG检索提供了简洁可行的方案。

Abstract: Multi-step retrieval over long contexts is a key capability for retrieval-augmented generation, but strong existing approaches are costly and complex to train. Supervised retrievers are far simpler and cheaper, yet they fall well behind in this setting. We show that this gap stems not from supervised training itself, but from three misalignments between training and inference: easy training negatives, noisy retrieval prefixes, and position-agnostic scoring. Guided by this analysis, we introduce S$^2$LMR, a simple yet effective supervised sequential retriever that models multi-step retrieval as the evolution of an explicit textual state encoded by a single shared Transformer. On long-context reasoning, multi-hop QA, and needle-in-a-haystack benchmarks with context lengths ranging from 4K to 10M tokens, S$^2$LMR achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art methods, substantially outperforming prior supervised retrievers. Notably, it requires only $\sim$2 hours of training on a single A6000 for contexts of up to 10M tokens, while remaining robust across a wide range of hyperparameter settings.

</details>


### [68] [DoC: Learning to Defer or Commit for Timely, Accurate, and Stable Conversational Diagnosis Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29003)
*Xueren Ge,Anthony Cortez,Homa Alemzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架DoC，让模型在临床对话中自主学习"何时下诊断"（Defer）与"何时承诺诊断"，在EMSDialog/MediTOD上实现最早提交下的最高首次诊断准确率与最低提交后错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话级诊断预测要求从持续进行的临床对话中给出准确、早期且稳定的预测，但现有数据只有对话级标签，无法指示模型应在对话的哪一时刻做出承诺（commitment），即缺乏承诺时间监督，导致"何时下判断"这一问题未被建模。

Method: 两阶段框架：（1）迭代式承诺边界估计（iterative commitment-boundary estimation），在无承诺时间标注的情况下自动构造Defer/诊断的监督信号；（2）Defer感知的监督微调（Defer-aware SFT）初始化策略，再以强化学习精调，采用轨迹级奖励，联合优化及时性（timeliness）、首次承诺准确率与承诺后稳定性。

Result: 在EMSDialog与MediTOD两个数据集上，DoC能够更早地做出承诺；在经后训练（post-trained）的方法中取得最高首次承诺准确率（76.7%/51.2%），并具有最低的承诺后错误率（16.4%/48.3%）。

Conclusion: 将"推迟（Defer）"作为显式决策目标，并通过两阶段监督构造与RL优化，可在无承诺时间标注条件下实现早期且稳定的对话诊断承诺，优于现有后训练方法。

Abstract: Conversational diagnosis prediction requires accurate, early, and stable predictions from ongoing clinical dialogue, yet dialogue-level labels do not specify when to commit. We propose a two-phase framework for learning deferral and diagnosis decisions. First, iterative commitment-boundary estimation constructs Defer/diagnosis supervision without annotated commitment times. Second, Defer-aware supervised fine-tuning initializes a policy, refined through reinforcement learning with trajectory-level rewards for timeliness, first-commit accuracy, and post-commit stability. On EMSDialog and MediTOD, DoC commits early, achieving the highest first-commit accuracy among post-trained methods (76.7%/51.2%) and the lowest post-commit error overall (16.4%/48.3%).

</details>


### [69] [Mirrored Min-of-K Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29008)
*Jiaru Zhang,Zeyun Deng,Hyunsoo Yun,Yupeng Zhou,Bingyao Du,Juntong Peng,Zichong Yang,Can Cui,Chuheng Wei,Ruqi Zhang,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析 Min-of-K (MoK) 训练的分布偏差，指出仅基于目标-候选距离的损失无法恢复部分数据分布，并提出镜像 MoK (MMoK) 通过额外采样与距离相减提供候选间相对位置信息，理论与实验验证其可恢复 MoK 无法恢复的分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoK 广泛用于训练从随机噪声产生多样输出的模型，但其生成分布通常不匹配数据分布。作者希望系统刻画 MoK 偏差随 K 变化的规律，并揭示其根本局限。

Method: 1) 分析所有 K 值下 MoK 偏差，证明 K 的调节在失效模式间切换：小 K 导致模式丢弃，大 K 导致分布平坦化；2) 证明任何仅依赖目标-候选距离的非平凡损失都无法恢复某些数据分布；3) 提出 MMoK：额外从模型采样一个样本，以其为参照执行最近候选选择规则，并减去相应距离，从而引入候选间相对位置信息。

Result: 理论证明 MMoK 原则上可恢复 MoK 无法恢复的数据分布。在五个场景（合成混合分布、行人与车辆轨迹预测、热带气旋预报、机器人策略学习、模糊医学图像分割）上实验验证，MMoK 在分布学习与任务性能上均有效且具通用性。

Conclusion: MoK 的偏差源于损失仅利用目标-候选距离，忽略候选间相对关系；MMoK 以极小改动（一次额外采样与距离相减）补充该信息，兼具理论保证与跨任务实证效果，可作为 MoK 的直接替代方案。

Abstract: Min-of-K (MoK) training is widely used to train models that produce diverse outputs from random noise. For each target, it draws K outputs from the model and backpropagates only through the one closest to the target. However, MoK generally does not make the generated distribution match the data distribution. In this paper, we analyze the MoK bias across all values of K and show that tuning K shifts between failure modes, from mode dropping at small K to distribution flattening at large K. We further prove that every nontrivial loss based only on target-candidate distances fails to recover some data distribution, since these distances do not reveal how the candidates are placed relative to each other. Based on these analyses, we propose mirrored MoK (MMoK), a drop-in modification of MoK training that draws one extra sample from the model, applies the closest-candidate selection rule against it, and subtracts the resulting distance.
