Since this distance is measured between samples of the model, it provides additional information about how the candidates are placed relative to each other. We prove that MMoK can in principle recover data distributions that MoK cannot. We empirically validate MMoK on five scenarios, namely synthetic mixtures, pedestrian and vehicle trajectory prediction, tropical cyclone forecasting, robotic policy learning, and ambiguous medical image segmentation. The results confirm the effectiveness and generality of MMoK in both distribution learning and task performance.

</details>


### [70] [Do Larger Fully Connected Neural Networks Generalize Better?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29039)
*Mohammad Jalili Torkamani,Suhib Hamid,Moaz N. Ishag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在Fashion-MNIST上比较深浅两种全连接网络架构（参数量相差约5.9倍），在2种学习率×2种L2正则共8种配置下以验证损失选型并测试，发现更大容量并不必然带来更好泛化（四组匹配比较中仅一组更优，测试精度差仅0.25个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络容量更大并不必然在未见数据上表现更好，但"更深/更大架构是否带来更好泛化"缺乏在受控条件下的系统检验。作者希望在同一数据集与同族超参数下，考察架构容量与优化/正则的交互对泛化能力的影响。

Method: 在Fashion-MNIST上对比两种全连接架构：可训练参数分别为95,410与561,470（约5.9倍差异）。每种架构搭配2种学习率与2种L2正则设置，共8种配置。以验证损失选择检查点与最终配置，并在独立留出测试集上评估所有配置；另进行类别级误差分析与像素移除的定性分析。

Result: 所选较大配置取得89.82%验证精度与88.77%测试精度；按各自最低验证损失选型后，两种架构测试精度为88.77%与88.52%，相差0.25个百分点。在四组匹配训练设置的比较中，较大架构仅在1组取得更高测试精度。类别级误差集中在视觉相似的服装类别，像素移除分析显示对形状与边界的敏感性。

Conclusion: 仅增加架构容量并不能保证更好的泛化性能；架构选择必须同时考虑优化与正则化设置。研究提示应以验证损失驱动的模型选择与受控消融来评估容量收益，而非默认"更大即更好"。

Abstract: Greater neural-network capacity does not necessarily yield better performance on unseen data. This study investigates whether a deeper, fully connected neural-network architecture generalizes better than a shallower architecture on the Fashion-MNIST dataset. We compare two architectures under two learning rates and two L2-regularization settings, producing eight configurations. The architectures contain 95,410 and 561,470 trainable parameters, respectively, an approximately 5.9-fold difference. Checkpoints and the final configuration are selected by validation loss, and all configurations are evaluated on a held-out test set. The selected larger configuration achieves 89.82% validation accuracy and 88.77% test accuracy. Selecting the lowest-validation-loss configuration within each architecture yields test accuracies of 88.77% and 88.52%, respectively, a difference of 0.25 percentage points. Across matched training settings, the larger architecture achieves higher test accuracy in only one of four comparisons. Class-level errors are concentrated among visually similar garment categories, and qualitative pixel-removal analyses suggest sensitivity to shape and boundaries. Overall, the findings suggest that increasing architectural capacity alone does not guarantee better generalization, highlighting the importance of considering optimization and regularization when selecting neural-network architectures.

</details>


### [71] [A Finite-Rate Selection Law for Annealed Gibbs Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29041)
*Izhar Ali,Muhammad Haris Khan,Saeed Ahmad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了退火Gibbs采样在有限降温速率下的模式选择偏差，引入保留对数几率比例c作为误差度量，在Curie–Weiss模型与Hopfield模型中推导了c的解析规律并做了数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退火Gibbs采样每次运行最终落入单一模式，无偏Monte Carlo估计要求各模式终结频率正比于其模型概率；但有限降温速率下这一性质可能失效，需要定量刻画该误差。

Method: 定义误差指标c（两个模式间模型对数几率中被样本保留的比例）；在带弱外场的Curie–Weiss模型中借鉴Kondepudi等人的选择概率，推导c的解析规律，并用九条线性降温曲线做精确计算对比；在Hopfield模型中推导模式间竞争对c的放大因子，并在随机模式集上测量c。

Result: Curie–Weiss模型中解析规律与精确计算在九条线性降温ramp上误差在2%以内；Hopfield模型中随机模式集上的实测c与预测相差3–6%，但单个模式集偏离预测，与其推导不符。

Conclusion: 有限降温速率的退火Gibbs采样的模式选择偏差可被解析刻画：弱场导致的偏置在转变附近被Gibbs更新的随机涨落压制，仅轻微影响模式选择；Hopfield中模式竞争可定量放大c，但单样本波动提示该推导存在局限。

Abstract: Annealed Gibbs sampling samples a multimodal model by redrawing one variable at a time from its conditional distribution as the temperature falls from a high value, where the distribution is broad, to the target temperature. Each run ends in a single mode, and unbiased Monte Carlo estimates require the runs to end in each mode in proportion to its probability under the model. At a finite cooling rate this can fail. We measure the error by $c$, the fraction of the model's log-odds between two modes that the samples retain. In the Curie--Weiss model, where a weak external field favors one of two modes, each run selects its mode near the transition at which the modes form, and rarely switches once they have separated. Near the transition, the random fluctuations of the Gibbs updates still outweigh the weak field, which therefore biases the choice of mode only slightly. Building on the selection probability of Kondepudi and coworkers, we derive a law for $c$ that agrees with an exact computation to within $2\%$ on nine linear cooling ramps. For the Hopfield model, whose modes are stored patterns and their negatives, we derive a factor by which competition among patterns raises $c$. The $c$ measured over random pattern sets lies within $3$--$6\%$ of its prediction, but single pattern sets depart from it, contrary to its derivation.

</details>


### [72] [Mirror Descent at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29043)
*Vincent X. Wang,Vikas Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"稳定边缘"(edge of stability)现象从梯度下降推广到镜像下降，提出mirror sharpness度量，发现其稳定在略高于2/η的水平，并通过对偶中心流ODE刻画了该现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降中已观察到损失Hessian最大特征值徘徊于2/η的稳定边缘现象，但镜像下降是非欧氏的，已有的基于欧氏几何的解释无法直接迁移，缺乏对该现象的刻画。

Method: 将sharpness概念推广为mirror sharpness，以镜像势函数度量损失的曲率；经验性地测量其在训练中相对2/η阈值的动态；并为镜像下降推导对偶中心流ODE，在连续时间中建模稳定边缘起始阶段的迭代轨迹。

Result: 实验中mirror sharpness稳定在略高于2/η的阈值之上；所推导的对偶中心流ODE能刻画稳定边缘起始阶段的迭代行为，并揭示出将梯度下降中"sharpness稳定于2/η"的论证推广到一般镜像下降时存在的困难。

Conclusion: 稳定边缘现象可在镜像下降中以mirror sharpness形式重现，但其理论解释需要新的非欧氏工具；现有针对梯度下降的证明机制无法一般性地推广。

Abstract: In many deep learning settings, gradient descent has been shown to operate at the edge of stability -- a regime where the largest eigenvalue of the loss Hessian hovers around $2/η$. We study this phenomenon in mirror descent, a generalization of gradient descent. As mirror descent is non-Euclidean, existing explanations of the edge of stability phase do not extend to this setting. We generalize the existing notion of sharpness to introduce mirror sharpness, which measures the curvature of the loss with respect to the mirror potential. Empirically, we observe that the mirror sharpness hovers slightly above the $2/η$ threshold. Furthermore, we derive a dual central flow ODE for mirror descent that models iterates in continuous time at the onset of edge of stability. Our ODE provides insights into the difficulties of extending existing arguments for why sharpness stabilizes at $2/η$ from gradient descent to mirror descent in full generality.

</details>


### [73] [Certified Comparative Statics for Conservation-Law Equilibrium Layers, with an Application to RNA Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29050)
*Shadi Khodagholi,Sara Karimi,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究隐式/平衡神经层中不动点对输入的比较静态，证明当残差雅可比为非奇异M-矩阵时，所有输入-输出比较静态的符号可被认证，且认证符号仅依赖占据率导数的符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论仅证明隐式层不动点的存在性，未刻画其如何随输入变化；而符号可靠性（可解释、可信的单调性）对下游决策至关重要，故需要一个与具体占据形式无关的可移植保证。

Method: 针对保序耦合的守恒律层，用M-矩阵理论给出形式无关的判据：残差雅可比为非奇异M-矩阵 ⇔ 所有比较静态符号可认证；认证符号只取决于占据率导数符号。由此给出严格强于M-矩阵条件的相变边界，并推导竞争性去阻遏（独立打分器导数为恒零，此处可证非零）与资源耦合抑制的非子模性。实验上在siRNA脱靶竞争的单一池实例上，用公开真实数据（17个miRNA、两项滴定研究、配对化学修饰实验）验证，另有非生物物理数据实例；并作为可训练层端到端学习。

Result: 给出该类层首个认证符号敏感性结果；认证保证同时覆盖Langmuir、Hill与饱和指数占据形式。训练后的层在构造上保持认证单调，幅度与参数量匹配的前馈基线持平，并能表达基线任意权重都无法表示的竞争效应；非生物数据上复现相同认证符号。

Conclusion: 形式无关性是可移植的不变量，M-矩阵判据为隐式层提供可解释的单调动性保证；但单池实例仅验证机制，不能对脱靶幅度排序或给出位置特异的序列设计。

Abstract: Implicit and equilibrium neural layers define their output as the fixed point of a map. Existing theory certifies that this fixed point exists; it does not certify how it responds to its inputs. For a conservation-law layer with order-preserving coupling we give a form-independent answer: every input-output comparative static is sign-certified if and only if the residual Jacobian is a nonsingular M-matrix, and the certified sign depends only on the sign of the occupancy derivative, so one guarantee covers Langmuir, Hill, and saturating-exponential occupancies. The M-matrix machinery is classical; the contribution is form-independence as the portable invariant, the first certified-sign sensitivity result for a layer of this class, and a sharp phase boundary at which certification is lost while the equilibrium remains well posed. Three consequences follow: the phase boundary, which is strictly stronger than the M-matrix condition; competitive derepression, whose derivative is identically zero for any independent per-target scorer and provably nonzero here; and non-submodularity of resource-coupled suppression. We instantiate on siRNA off-target competition and validate on real public data only, across seventeen microRNAs, two titration studies, and a paired chemical-modification experiment; a second, non-biological instantiation shows the same certified sign on measured physical data. As a trainable layer the operator learns end-to-end while remaining certified-monotone by construction, reaching magnitude parity with a parameter-matched feedforward baseline while expressing a competition effect that baseline cannot represent for any weights. The single-pool instantiation validates the mechanism but does not rank off-target magnitude or resolve position-specific design. Code: https://github.com/shadi97kh/certified-equilibrium-layers

</details>


### [74] [Hypothesis Generation and Semantic Attribution in Inverse Perturbation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29055)
*Pascal Passigan,Manolis Kellis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究训练稀疏类型化图神经网络，从LINCS L1000配对表达谱中逆向恢复被扰动基因，并用于在指定癌细胞背景下提名可引导恶性转录程序的干预靶点，再通过归因分析使提名背后的生物学解释可被检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模理解基因调控关系有助于识别干预点并预判非预期效应，但将黑箱模型学到的计算与生物学知识对应仍很困难；作者希望检验稀疏、类型化的基因网络能否生成可审视（inspectable）的假说，用于把恶性转录程序导向与良好预后相关的表达模式。

Method: 使用LINCS L1000 CRISPR/Cas9扰动谱与匹配的板内对照，训练图神经网络从基线与终点表达逆向恢复扰动基因；节点融合ProTrek蛋白表示与表达特征，边编码带符号的调控关系及其他基因互作。对指定癌细胞背景下的多基因目标表达变化进行干预提名，并用积分梯度与路径电导（path conductance），沿从细胞基线到请求终点的路径，将提名归因到基因状态、类型化消息和残差连接。

Result: 在A549中，模型提名TTK（其自身表达在请求中未变），并经由检查点相关的MAD1L1关系给予支持性贡献、经由E2F4转录调控通道给予反向贡献。在九个留出细胞系的333个筛选提名中，请求路径归因在每一次状态转移上都与有限消息删除效应呈正的系均对应；既有TTK敲除测量显示，在其他标志物上请求方向一致率达第98.2百分位（10孔），示例筛选的17个实测案例中有10个满足预设支持标准。

Conclusion: 该分析框架使针对某一提名的相互竞争的生物学解释变得可审视、并可用于实验检验，为将黑箱模型的内部计算转化为可验证的生物学假说提供了途径。

Abstract: Understanding gene regulatory relationships at scale can help identify intervention points and anticipate unintended effects, but connecting a black-box model's learned computation to biological knowledge remains difficult. We investigate whether a sparse, typed gene network can generate inspectable hypotheses for steering malignant transcriptional programs toward expression patterns associated with favorable prognosis. Using LINCS L1000 CRISPR/Cas9 profiles paired with matched plate controls, we train a graph neural network to recover the perturbed gene from baseline and endpoint expression. Nodes combine ProTrek-derived protein representations with expression features; edges encode signed regulatory relationships and other gene interactions. We use this inverse formulation to nominate interventions for a desired multigene expression change in a specified cancer-cell context, and use integrated gradients and conductance along a path from the cell baseline toward the requested endpoint to attribute each nomination to gene states, typed messages, and residual connections. In A549, the model nominates TTK although its expression is unchanged in the request; the account assigns supporting credit through the checkpoint-associated MAD1L1 relationship and opposing credit through the E2F4 transcriptional-regulatory channel. Across 333 screened nominations in nine held-out cell lines, request-path attribution shows positive line-averaged correspondence with finite message-deletion effects at every transition. Existing TTK knockout measurements show requested-direction agreement across other markers at the 98.2nd percentile (10 wells), and ten of seventeen measured cases in the illustrative screen meet the predefined support criterion. These analyses make competing biological accounts of a nomination examinable and open to experimental test.

</details>


### [75] [DiRGeo: Learning Directional Geometry of Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29057)
*Muhammad Ahtesham,Xin Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiRGeo框架，在冻结视觉表征上学习基于Randers几何的方向性几何，通过自监督对比从有序图像轨迹中学习状态依赖方向场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉表征的几何度量是对称的，对同一路径的正反向运动赋予相同代价，无法刻画表征空间中的方向性结构。

Method: 基于Randers几何，利用有序图像轨迹与自监督对比学习状态依赖的方向场，无需语义标签；引入球面切向校正与吸引子相对分解，以区分真实方向结构与归一化伪影及共性收敛。

Result: 在17种视觉过程、3类编码器家族及多种表征深度上实验，展现一致的方向判别性与超越吸引子解释的整路径不对称性；深层表征中方向场更具过程特异性；自然视频实验显示事件依赖的方向偏好；DiRGeo可指导复原选择。

Conclusion: 方向性几何是有益补充，可弥补对称表征分析的不足。

Abstract: Conventional geometric measures of visual representations are symmetric, assigning equal costs to forward and reverse motion along the same path. We propose DiRGeo, a framework for learning directional geometry over frozen visual representations. Grounded in Randers geometry, DiRGeo learns a state-dependent directional field from ordered image trajectories through self-supervised contrast, without semantic labels. Sphere-tangent correction and attractor-relative decomposition distinguish learned directional structure from normalization artifacts and common convergence. Experiments across 17 visual processes, three encoder families, and multiple representation depths demonstrate consistent directional discrimination and whole-path asymmetry beyond simple attractor-based explanations. The learned fields become increasingly process-specific in deeper representations, while natural-video experiments reveal event-dependent directional preferences. As functional validation, DiRGeo guides restoration selection. These results establish directional geometry as a useful complement to symmetric representation analysis.

</details>


### [76] [When Can a Rewiring Control Test LLM Weight Readers? C2 and an Eligibility Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29120)
*Vaishak Girish Kumar,Sanika Vilas Najan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实对照 C2（置换网络末端读出层的列，保持其余参数及读出层的奇异值、范数与元素多重集不变，并可按可精确重算的方式改变函数），并给出“资格协议”三项条件，用以区分大模型从原始权重预测小网络输出究竟是“指纹识别”还是“真实计算”。结论：所测自然场景均不满足资格，仅在人造可控种群中成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明大模型能从原始权重预测小网络输出，但该成功可能只是识别了权重空间中的规律（指纹/fingerprint），而非真正执行计算；而保持函数不变的控制实验无法将二者区分开。因此需要一个能分离“指纹识别”与“计算”的因果性对照设计。

Method: 提出 C2 反事实：对网络最后一个线性读出层做列置换，使其余所有参数、读出层的奇异值、范数及元素多重集保持不变，函数变化可精确重算，并逐一验证每个置换确实改变了函数。提出“资格协议”（eligibility protocol）三项条件：(1) 仅使用 C2 不变统计量的策略本身须已能预测目标；(2) C2 必须改变与重连特异相关的性质；(3) 读取者（reader）的身份须可被证实。据此在小型 MLP、两个公开 CNN zoo、以及植入指纹规则的人造种群上做检验，并用自托管 Qwen3-27B、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B 进行复现实验。

Result: (a) 小型 MLP 上，前沿 reader 在全部 24 个网络上跟随重连后的函数，但不变基线接近随机水平，且其后端身份无法证实；(b) 两个公开 CNN zoo 中条件相互冲突：权重统计量可在分布内预测 CIFAR-10 准确率，但 C2 对准确率的影响不特异于置换；逐图像目标具有特异性却无法由不变摘要预测；(c) 仅在人为构造、植入指纹规则的种群中三项条件全部满足，C2 成功区分“规则遵循”与“执行计算”的 reader；(d) 自托管 Qwen3-27B 跟随重连函数而非捷径，且在关闭思维链时无使用权重的证据；(e) 新网络上的注册式重复验证两结论（tracking 减 stuck = 0.875，95% 区间 [0.750, 1.000]），DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B 亦跟随重连（0.729 [0.552, 0.885]）。

Conclusion: 在所测试的所有自然设置中，资格协议均不成立，故现有证据不足以证明大模型从权重到输出的预测属于真实计算而非指纹识别；C2 的分离能力仅在刻意构造的、指纹规则可用的种群中得到验证。作者公开变换、审计与原始记录，强调需要满足资格协议方可宣称“计算”解释。

Abstract: Large language models can predict the outputs of small neural networks from raw weights, but success could reflect recognition of weight-space regularities (fingerprints) rather than computation, and function-preserving controls cannot tell these apart. We propose C2, a counterfactual that permutes the columns of a network's final linear readout: every other parameter, and the readout's singular values, norms and entry multiset, are unchanged, and the function can change in an exactly recomputable way; we verify that each evaluated permutation does. C2 separates fingerprinting from computation only under three conditions, which we state as an eligibility protocol: a strategy restricted to C2-invariant summaries must already predict the target; C2 must change a property specific to the rewiring; and the reader's identity must be attested. No natural setting we tested is eligible. On tiny MLPs a frontier reader route followed the rewired function on all 24 networks, but invariant baselines were near chance and its backend could not be attested. In two public CNN zoos the conditions traded off: weight statistics predicted CIFAR-10 accuracy in-distribution, but C2's effect on accuracy was not specific to the swap, while per-image targets were specific but not predictable from invariant summaries. On a constructed population where a planted fingerprint rule works, all conditions hold and C2 separates a rule-following reader from an executor. A self-hosted Qwen3.8-27B followed the rewired function rather than the shortcut, and showed no evidence of using the weights with thinking disabled. A registered replication on fresh networks confirmed both findings (tracking minus stuck 0.875, 95% interval [0.750, 1.000]), and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B also followed the rewiring (tracking minus stuck 0.729 [0.552, 0.885]). The transforms, audits and raw records are available from the authors.

</details>


### [77] [Pain Reassessment from Pulse Waveforms with Language Model Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29128)
*Cheng Ding,Jiaming Liu,Daoqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究基于52,082条与自评报告关联的PPG评估（MIMIC-III 21,514名重症患者及MC-MED急诊患者），提出将纵向PPG表征与本地证据链接的语言解释相结合的工作流，用于两次疼痛自报之间的个体化疼痛再评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常PPG可提供连续生理信号，用于自报疼痛之间的再评估；但既往记录能否在护理流程时间信息之外提供个体化生理参照，尚不明确。

Method: 在27种方法比较中，采用源端选择的全历史模型，将纵向PPG表征与本地证据链接的语言解释配对；并通过消融（去除历史脉搏段表征、保留再评估可得性与经过时间）检验历史表征的贡献；另用本地Qwen模型对冻结预测进行解释，并做自动化证据核验。

Result: 全历史模型平均精度达0.824（重症→急诊）与0.822（反向）；去除历史脉搏段表征后，重症→急诊4小时内患者内一致性由0.718降至0.534；2,905名测试患者中，本地Qwen模型在至多一次修订后于自动化证据检查上达到94.7%患者等权通过率。

Conclusion: 纵向脉搏上下文与证据链接的语言解释可支撑以患者为中心的疼痛再评估工作流；历史脉搏段表征在时间线索之外对患者内排序具有独立贡献。

Abstract: Routine photoplethysmography (PPG) offers a continuous physiological signal for pain reassessment between self-reports. Whether prior recordings provide an individualized physiological reference beyond care-process timing remains unresolved. Across 52,082 report-linked assessments from 21,514 patients in MIMIC-III critical care and MC-MED emergency care, we evaluate a workflow that pairs longitudinal PPG representations with local evidence-linked language interpretation. Among 27 methods, source-selected full-history models achieved average precision of 0.824 from critical to emergency care and 0.822 in reverse. Removing historical pulse-run representations while retaining reassessment availability and elapsed time lowered critical-to-emergency four-hour within-patient concordance from 0.718 to 0.534. This comparison supports the contribution of prior pulse-run representations to within-patient ordering beyond timing cues. Across 2,905 test patients, a local Qwen model interpreting frozen predictions achieved a 94.7% patient-equal pass rate on automated evidence checks after at most one revision. Longitudinal pulse context and evidence-linked language interpretation support a patient-centred workflow for pain reassessment.

</details>


### [78] [Towards Edge Artificial Intelligence for Predicting Antimicrobial Resistance in Mycobacterium tuberculosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29143)
*Nishat Tasnim,Faisal Ahmed,Rokan Uddin Faruqui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级机器学习方法，将全基因组序列的4-mer频率特征与随机森林分类器结合，用于预测结核分枝杆菌的抗生素耐药性，可在边缘设备上运行，在20种抗生素上平均AUC为0.82。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结核分枝杆菌耐药性（尤其多重耐药）严重威胁全球结核病防控，基于基因组数据快速准确预测耐药性有助于及时治疗决策与公共卫生干预。但现有计算方法的流程计算密集，难以部署在资源受限的边缘设备上，这在中低收入国家医院尤为突出，其算力与高性能计算资源有限。

Method: 从全基因组序列中提取4-mer频率特征，结合随机森林（Random Forest）分类器构建轻量级机器学习模型，用于预测MTB的抗生素耐药性，设计上适合边缘设备部署。

Result: 在独立测试集上，对20种抗生素的平均ROC曲线下面积（AUC）达到0.82。

Conclusion: 该轻量级方法在保持较高预测性能的同时降低了计算开销，具备在资源受限环境中部署的潜力，可为结核病耐药性的快速预测提供可行方案。

Abstract: Antimicrobial resistance (AMR) in Mycobacterium tuberculosis (MTB) poses a major challenge to global tuberculosis control, as multidrug-resistant strains limit available treatment options. Rapid and accurate prediction of drug resistance from genomic data could support timely treatment decisions and public health interventions. However, many existing computational approaches for genomic AMR prediction rely on computationally intensive pipelines, limiting their suitability for deployment on resource-constrained edge devices. This limitation is particularly relevant in hospital settings in low- and middle-income countries, where computing infrastructure and access to high-performance computational resources may be limited. To address this challenge, we present a lightweight, novel machine learning (ML) approach suitable for running on edge devices that combines 4-mer frequency features derived from whole-genome sequences with a Random Forest classifier to predict antibiotic resistance in MTB. The proposed ML approach achieved an average area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.82 across 20 antibiotics on an independent test set.

</details>


### [79] [VeriLogic: Reinforcement Learning over Verifiable Logical Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29147)
*ZeLiang Chen,Yunyao Zhang,Xinglang Zhang,Wenbing Li,Junqing Yu,Zikai Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出VeriLogic，将逻辑推理的内部推理轨迹转化为显式可验证的学习对象，通过"Grounding-Optimization-Refinement"流水线实现过程级强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖回答级结果信号，忽略推理轨迹内部的异质正确性：正确答案可能包含无效推理，错误回答中也可能出现有效中间步骤。

Method: 三阶段统一流水线：(1) Verification-Grounded Reasoning Environment Construction，将逻辑推理落地为显式中间状态与形式可检验的证明义务；(2) Logical Structured Policy Optimization，通过全局与局部互补奖励从验证器反馈中学习，同时优化整体结果并给不同中间推理路径提供定向信号；(3) Verifier-Guided Iterative Refinement，利用证明状态与错误信息迭代诊断并修复失败推理。

Result: 在六个逻辑推理基准上相对强基线取得一致提升，Qwen3与Llama-3.1上平均分别提升6.39%和5.00%。

Conclusion: 使逻辑推理轨迹可验证、可学习、可精炼（超越仅依赖最终答案的监督）具有显著价值。

Abstract: Logical reasoning derives conclusions from premises through explicit intermediate inference steps, providing a natural basis for process-level verification and supervision. However, existing learning methods often rely on response-level outcome signals, which overlook heterogeneous correctness within the reasoning trace: a correct answer may contain invalid reasoning, while valid intermediate steps may also appear in an incorrect response. To address this issue, we introduce Reinforcement Learning on Verifiable Logical Reasoning (VeriLogic), which turns internal reasoning traces into explicit and verifiable learning objects. Rather than treating a response as an indivisible learning unit, VeriLogic decomposes logical reasoning into explicit intermediate judgments, proofs, and final decisions, and organizes them into a unified "Grounding - Optimization - Refinement" pipeline: (1) Verification-Grounded Reasoning Environment Construction grounds logical reasoning into explicit intermediate states and formally checkable proof obligations, making the internal reasoning process accessible to verification and learning; (2) Logical Structured Policy Optimization learns from verifier feedback through complementary global and local rewards, jointly optimizing the overall reasoning outcome while providing targeted optimization signals to different intermediate reasoning paths; and (3) Verifier-Guided Iterative Refinement acts on verifier feedback by leveraging proof states and error messages to iteratively diagnose and repair failed reasoning. Experiments on six logical reasoning benchmarks show consistent gains over strong baselines, with average improvements of 6.39% and 5.00% on Qwen3 and Llama-3.1. These results highlight the value of making logical reasoning traces verifiable, learnable, and refinable beyond final-answer supervision.

</details>


### [80] [ARC: Adaptive Rollout Control for Efficient Synchronous LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29156)
*Yihui Zhang,Tianyu Wo,Jinghao Wang,Xiao Zhou,Huitian Xue,Xiaoyang Sun,Zeying Wang,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARC 是一种面向大语言模型同步强化学习的闭环 rollout 架构，在 group 粒度上调度 rollout，通过流式回填、延迟组完成、自适应准入与流水线，减少空闲与无效计算，实现 37.4% rollout 时间下降（1.60× 加速）与 50.1% 有效 GRPO 吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步 RL 采用 rollout—barrier—update 循环，每次更新须等待固定数量的完整 group-relative 训练样本；但单条回复长度与奖励结果差异大且仅在生成过程中显现，静态准入与无条件完成导致部分步骤加速器空闲，并浪费计算于永不参与本次更新的样本。

Method: 1）Calibrated Streaming Backfill：基于部分观测判断某组是否仍值得完成，若不值得则立即回填新候选；2）Deferred Group Completion：将不确定的提前终止暂存并在之后恢复，而非直接丢弃，并以恢复周期上限保证终止；3）Adaptive Admission 与 Pipelining：按早期测得时长排序已准入样本，且仅在预期供给仍低于目标接受组数时继续准入候选。

Result: 在线评测下，ARC 相比现有基线将 rollout 时间减少 37.4%（1.60× 加速），有效 GRPO 吞吐提升 50.1%，并直接回收 98.9% 最终本会被保留的训练组。

Conclusion: 以组为粒度对同步 RL rollout 进行闭环调度，可有效缓解同步屏障带来的空闲与算力浪费，在几乎不损失有效训练样本的前提下显著提升 rollout 效率与训练吞吐。

Abstract: Synchronous reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models proceeds through a repeated rollout--barrier--update loop. Each update must wait for a fixed number of complete, group-relative training samples, yet individual response lengths and reward outcomes vary widely and only emerge as generation progresses. Static admission and unconditional completion therefore leave accelerators idle in some steps, and waste computation generating samples that will never contribute to the current update. We present ARC, a closed-loop rollout architecture that governs synchronous RL rollout at group granularity. ARC uses \textit{Calibrated Streaming Backfill} to judge from partial observations whether a group is still worth completing, backfilling a fresh candidate immediately when it is not; \textit{Deferred Group Completion} to park an uncertain early stop and resume it later instead of discarding it outright, bounded by a resume-cycle limit that guarantees termination; and \textit{Adaptive Admission} and \textit{Pipelining} to order admitted samples by early-measured duration and to admit further candidates only while expected supply still falls short of the target number of accepted groups. Under online evaluation, ARC reduces rollout time by $37.4\%$, a $1.60\times$ speedup, and improves effective GRPO throughput by $50.1\%$ over existing baselines, while directly recovering 98.9\% of the training groups that would ultimately have been kept.

</details>


### [81] [What Makes Recurrence Generalize Across Lengths?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29171)
*Yoonsung Jung,Sunghun Jung,Louise Nayeong Koh,Yongjun Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究循环Transformer在状态跟踪任务中的长度外推能力，发现仅靠循环结构不足，原子操作表示与扩大循环容量是提升长度泛化的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步推理要求模型在计算过程中保持并更新中间信息，而模型在训练范围内的状态转移上表现良好，未必能在需要更多更新时泛化，因此需探究循环计算何时能超越训练长度范围。

Method: 在受控的状态跟踪任务中，通过改变操作表示（多token模板 vs 原子操作）、位置编码（绝对/周期等）和循环容量（循环算子缩放、循环深度），系统性地研究循环Transformer的外推行为，并测试推理时增加循环计算的效果。

Result: 多token模板表示与绝对位置编码严重限制外推，原子操作表示显著增强长度泛化；位置结构的效果随循环深度变化，周期位置编码在更大深度下平均性能最佳；扩大循环算子可将外推范围延伸至仅用2-10步训练后、20-40步序列上达到35.1%精确匹配，而更大的固定深度Transformer仍为0%；测试时增加循环计算可扩展泛化范围但无法消除超长序列上的失败。

Conclusion: 循环计算可支撑长度可泛化的状态跟踪，但其有效性关键在于所学计算能否在不同序列长度间保持可复用性。

Abstract: Multi-step reasoning requires models to preserve and update intermediate information as computation unfolds. State tracking captures this fundamental requirement, but models that succeed within a fixed range of state transitions may not generalize when substantially more updates are required. Recurrent Transformers offer a natural mechanism for addressing this challenge by repeatedly applying shared Transformer blocks, allowing computation to scale with problem length. We study when such recurrent computation generalizes beyond the training range in a controlled state-tracking task, varying operation representation, positional encoding, and recurrent capacity. We find that recurrence alone is insufficient: multi-token template representations and absolute positional encoding can severely limit extrapolation, whereas atomic operation representations enable substantially stronger length generalization. The effect of positional structure also varies with recurrent depth, with periodic positional encoding achieving the strongest mean performance at greater recurrent depth. Most importantly, scaling the recurrent operator extends the extrapolation horizon, reaching 35.1% exact-match accuracy on sequences of 20-40 operations after training only on 2-10, while substantially larger fixed-depth Transformers remain at 0%. Additional recurrent computation at test time can extend the range over which the model generalizes, but does not eliminate failure on much longer sequences. These results suggest that recurrent computation can support length-generalizable state tracking, but that its effectiveness depends critically on whether the learned computation remains reusable across sequence lengths.

</details>


### [82] [On Adaptive Step Sizes for Eligibility Traces in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29178)
*Esraa Elelimy,Martha White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究深度强化学习中资格迹与向量步长的结合问题：证明自适应向量步长必须纳入资格迹以保持前向-后向等价，指出现有优化器（如Adam）缺乏在线后向视角，并提出 Function-Space Distance (FSD) 优化器，在流式设置下优于SGD与直接套用Adam，并与PPO相当或更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验回放缓冲存在内存效率低、学习延迟的问题（只有被采样到时才能学习），且一步或短多步TD更新在信用分配上较慢。资格迹算法可支持无缓冲的增量更新并实现多步信用分配，但此前多用于线性函数逼近，近期才引入深度RL；其中如何在深度RL下将向量步长与资格迹结合等基本问题尚未解决。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明为保持前向视角与后向视角的等价性，自适应向量步长本身必须作为资格迹的一部分；并证明Adam等常用优化器的步长不存在在线后向视角。2) 提出新优化器FSD（Function-Space Distance），其设计可维持前向-后向等价。3) 实验：测试四种资格迹算法，覆盖半梯度与梯度TD更新，以及基于价值与Actor-Critic两类方法，与SGD、朴素应用Adam及其他流式方法对比，并以使用缓冲的PPO为参照。

Result: FSD优于SGD和朴素应用的Adam；由FSD得到的资格迹算法在多数环境中优于或匹配其他流式方法，并与PPO相当或更优。

Conclusion: 自适应向量步长应被纳入资格迹以保持前向-后向等价，而Adam类优化器不具备在线后向视角；FSD作为满足等价性的优化器，使无缓冲的流式资格迹深度RL算法在性能上具有竞争力，可作为回放缓冲方法（如PPO）的可行替代。

Abstract: Many deep reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms store agent-environment interactions in a replay buffer and later learn by sampling transitions from it. However, this approach can be memory-inefficient and can delay learning because the agent can learn from a transition only when the buffer samples it. Additionally, most of these methods use one-step or short multi-step temporal-difference (TD) updates, which are slow at credit assignment. An alternative approach is to learn from the stream of observations as they arrive without relying on a buffer. Eligibility-trace algorithms allow these incremental updates and can also assign credit over many steps. These algorithms were common with linear function approximation but have only recently been used in deep RL. Basic questions, such as how to use vector step sizes with eligibility traces in the deep RL setting, remain open. In this work, we first show that adaptive vector step sizes must be part of the eligibility trace to keep the equivalence between the forward and backward views. We then show that the step sizes of common optimizers, such as Adam, do not have an online backward view. We introduce a new optimizer, Function-Space Distance (FSD), that maintains the forward-backward equivalence. We test four eligibility-trace algorithms covering semi-gradient and gradient TD updates, as well as value-based and actor-critic methods. We show that FSD performs better than SGD or naively applied Adam. The resulting algorithms outperform or match other streaming methods and are comparable to or better than PPO, which uses a buffer, in most environments. Code available at https://github.com/esraaelelimy/ada_traces

</details>


### [83] [Ziv Meets Sumcheck: Zero-Knowledge Proofs of Floating-Point LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29188)
*Hassan Khodaiemehr,Khadijeh Bagheri,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出针对浮点矩阵乘法的零知识证明方案，用"正确舍入乘积"（每个点积精确计算后仅舍入一次）避免逐次加法舍入带来的顺序依赖，成本接近整数矩阵乘法，并在两核CPU上19分钟证明、21秒验证GPT-2 small完整前向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型零知识证明系统只能证明模型的整数近似，而非实际部署的浮点运算；直接证明浮点矩阵乘法困难，因为每次加法后的舍入使结果依赖加法顺序。

Method: 不逐操作证明浮点电路，而是证明"正确舍入的乘积"：每个点积精确计算、仅舍入一次，结果与硬件无关且精度不低于标准内核。为此构造交互式证明，成本接近整数矩阵乘法，所需承诺值比逐操作浮点电路约束少约千倍；将方案扩展到transformer每一层，并给出完整论证的隐私构造，在明确假设下证明其零知识性。

Result: 实现系统并完成GPT-2 small全前向传播证明（含全部12个block及全词表输出），双核CPU耗时19分钟，验证21秒；验证者拒绝全部236个测试攻击；模型输出与高精度推理的吻合度至少与标准16位推理相当。

Conclusion: 以"正确舍入乘积"为核心的零知识证明可行且高效，使语言模型的浮点推理可被实际证明，兼具精度与安全（抗攻击）优势。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge proofs can show that a neural network produced a given output without revealing the network, but current systems for language models prove integer approximations of the model rather than the floating-point arithmetic that is actually deployed. Proving floating-point matrix multiplication has been regarded as hard, because rounding after every addition makes the result depend on the order of the additions. We avoid this obstacle by proving the correctly rounded product, in which every dot product is computed exactly and rounded once. This result is independent of the hardware and at least as accurate as standard kernels. We give an interactive proof for this relation whose cost is close to that of integer matrix multiplication, with roughly a thousand times fewer committed values than per-operation floating-point circuits need constraints, extend it to every layer of a transformer, and give a privacy construction for the complete argument whose zero knowledge we prove under explicit hypotheses. We implement the system and prove the full forward pass of GPT-2 small, including all twelve blocks and the output over the whole vocabulary, in 19 minutes on two processor cores; verification takes 21 seconds. The verifier rejects all 236 attacks we tested, and the model's outputs agree with high-precision inference at least as closely as standard 16-bit inference does.

</details>


### [84] [Direct Feedback Policy Optimization for AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29193)
*Fei Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出单轨迹强化学习算法DFPO，从单条轨迹直接训练AI编程智能体，在同等基座与执行框架下任务解决率高于GSPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程智能体通过多轮交互生成、修改与验证代码，现有强化学习方法依赖稀疏的终端奖励，难以评估中间决策对任务结果的影响；组相对方法（如GSPO）还需对每个任务重复采样，样本成本高。

Method: 提出Direct Feedback Policy Optimization（DFPO），一种单轨迹强化学习算法，直接从单条轨迹进行训练，无需组内多次采样与组相对基线估计。

Result: 在匹配基座模型与执行框架（execution harness）的条件下，DFPO的任务解决率高于GSPO。

Conclusion: DFPO为多轮编程智能体提供了一种样本高效的单轨迹训练范式，缓解稀疏终端奖励与重复采样问题。摘要仅给出相对GSPO的初步对比结果，尚缺绝对指标、基线完整性与消融分析。

Abstract: AI coding agents generate, modify, and validate code through multi-turn interactions. Existing reinforcement learning methods often rely on sparse terminal rewards, making it difficult to assess how intermediate decisions affect task outcomes, and group-relative methods also require repeated sampling for each task. We propose Direct Feedback Policy Optimization (DFPO), a single-trajectory reinforcement learning algorithm that trains directly from a single trajectory. DFPO achieves higher task resolution rates than GSPO under matched base models and execution harnesses.

</details>


### [85] [THETA: Heat Diffusion as a Structural Prior for Cross-Domain Transfer in Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29210)
*Haonan Liu,Shuo Wang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: THETA 提出以热核签名（HKS）作为拓扑结构先验的图基础模型，在预训练与少样本自适应阶段同时注入热扩散结构信息，实现跨域图表示迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型依赖从节点属性推断的关系，而这种关系受限于领域特有的特征语义，难以迁移到未见图，尤其在低标注（low-label）场景下迁移性差。作者希望找到一种不依赖属性对齐的通用结构描述子来补充基于特征的关系。

Method: 采用 Heat Kernel Signatures（HKS），通过刻画节点在不同扩散尺度下的热回返行为，得到无需属性语义对齐的通用结构描述符，反映局部与多尺度连通性。预训练阶段：将 HKS 与节点属性结合构造精细化图视图，以对比学习训练共享编码器。自适应阶段：用 HKS 指导边权调整与原型精化，冻结编码器，并保留有标注支持节点的贡献，从而利用目标域无标注数据进行少样本迁移。

Result: 在多个不同图域的广泛实验中，THETA 在下游任务上持续优于现有基线模型。

Conclusion: 热扩散可作为可迁移图表示的有效结构先验，以拓扑信息弥补属性语义的领域偏置，提升跨域与低标注条件下的图迁移性能。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) aim to learn transferable representations across domains with heterogeneous node attributes and graph structures. However, relations inferred from node attributes can rely on domain-specific feature semantics, limiting their transferability to unseen graphs, particularly in low-label regimes. We propose THETA (Topology-aware HEat-based TrAnsfer), a graph foundation model that leverages Heat Kernel Signatures (HKS) as a topology-based structural prior for cross-domain transfer. By characterizing nodes' heat-return behavior across diffusion scales, HKS provides a common structural descriptor across graphs without requiring aligned attribute semantics, complementing feature-based relations with information about local and multiscale connectivity. THETA incorporates this prior into both pre-training and few-shot adaptation. During pre-training, it combines HKS with node attributes to construct refined graph views and trains a shared encoder via contrastive learning. During adaptation, it uses HKS to guide edge-weight adjustment and prototype refinement while keeping the encoder frozen and retaining the contribution of labeled support nodes. Through HKS, THETA uses structural information from heat diffusion to guide both representation learning and few-shot adaptation with unlabeled target-domain data. Extensive experiments across diverse graph domains demonstrate that THETA consistently outperforms existing baselines on downstream tasks, highlighting the value of heat diffusion as a structural prior for transferable graph representations.

</details>


### [86] [NANO: Unifying Missingness and Input Uncertainty in Tabular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29214)
*Kuangdai Leng,Maria Tsekhmistrenko,Matteo Bagagli,Simon Jeffery,Tarje Nissen-Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一输入编码插件NANO，用贝叶斯证据加权处理缺失值的不确定性，并显式表示"N/A（不适用）"语义状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格缺失值处理方法忽视两点：一是观测值可能不确定、未观测值可能有合理估计，输入应是从"无证据"到"完全可信观测"的连续谱；二是Not-Applicable（无意义值存在）这一语义状态缺乏显式表示。

Method: NANO作为插件式输入编码模块，适配多数token类架构。基于贝叶斯证据加权，按置信度调节所提供值的影响，所有零置信度估计映射到统一的N.O.表示；训练/推理时置信度信息可选而非必需。对N.A.采用单独表示，使模型能将不适用性及其schema定义的原因编码进上下文。

Result: 在11个数据集、9种骨干网络上验证：混合置信度训练使单一NANO模型跨推理置信度水平泛化；无估计时仍具竞争力，随估计可靠性与置信度提升而稳步改善。在3个增强数据集上进一步验证了N.A.处理的有效性。

Conclusion: NANO以证据加权与独立N.A.表示统一处理缺失值的不确定性与不适用性，具备即插即用、置信度可选、跨置信度泛化的特性。

Abstract: Missing values are common in tabular data, yet current approaches to handling them often overlook two dimensions. First, an observed value may be uncertain, while a Not-Observed (N.O.) value may still have a reasonable estimate. Inputs should therefore span a continuous spectrum from no evidence to a fully trusted observation. Second, Not-Applicable (N.A.) means that no meaningful value exists for a record, yet this semantic state often lacks explicit representation. To address both dimensions, we introduce NANO, a unified plug-in for input encoding that fits most token-based architectures. Guided by Bayesian evidence weighting, NANO adjusts the influence of a supplied value according to confidence, with all zero-confidence estimates mapped to the same N.O. representation. NANO accepts, but does not require, per-example confidence information during training or inference. For N.A., a separate representation enables models to encode inapplicability and its schema-defined reasons into context. Experiments across eleven datasets and nine backbones show that mixed-confidence training enables a single NANO model to generalize across inference confidence levels, remaining competitive without estimates and improving steadily as estimate reliability and confidence increase. The effectiveness of NANO's handling of N.A. is further demonstrated on three augmented datasets.

</details>


### [87] [Curvature Mismatch and Local Errors in Galaxy Property Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29224)
*Austin Lutterbach,Angus Hoy,Jasper Chong,Aman Kumar,Michael J. Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 线性探针的误差部分源于表征流形的内禀几何：探针法向分量与第二基本形式的缩并（"探针面向曲率"）非零时会产生系统性偏差，对该曲率项干预可将星系任务held-out局部R²提升0.006–0.010。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探针被广泛用于评估学习表征并从无标签数据推断物理量，但当目标信息以线性探针无法访问的形式编码时，即便表征包含完整信息也会产生系统性探针误差；作者希望揭示这类误差中源于流形内在几何的部分。

Method: 将仿射探针限制在底层表征流形上，定义内禀Hessian即"探针面向曲率"⟨w_N, II⟩（w_N为探针法向分量，II为流形第二基本形式）；在五个预训练编码器的星系嵌入上拟合线性探针，并干预该曲率项以评估其对探针性能的贡献。

Result: 保留继承曲率可在其与标签曲率一致处改善局部拟合；在二者不一致处去除曲率分量同样带来改善；在每个邻域校正曲率使星系数据的held-out局部R²提升0.006至0.010，且两种曲率分歧越大收益越明显。

Conclusion: 探针误差可归因于表征流形的几何结构而非最小二乘训练的固有保证，说明线性探针评估需考虑流形曲率的影响。

Abstract: Linear probes are widely used to evaluate learned representations and infer physical quantities from unlabelled data. The probe's predictions are from an affine function in ambient space restricted to the underlying representation, and consequently, even a representation that has complete information needed to predict a target can produce systematic probe errors when that information is encoded in a form inaccessible to the linear probe. Here we share that some of this probing error is due to the innate geometry of the manifold. Linear probes, when evaluated on a curved manifold, generally have a nonzero intrinsic Hessian (what we colloquially call "probe-facing curvature") given by $\langle w_N,\mathrm{II}\rangle$, the manifold's second fundamental form dotted with the probe's normal component. We fit linear probes to galaxy embeddings from five pretrained encoders and intervene on this curvature term to evaluate its contribution to the probe performance. We find that keeping this inherited curvature improves the local fit where it matches the label's curvature. Removing the curvature component of the probe where it does not match the label curvature also provides improvements, and correcting it in every neighbourhood raises held-out local $R^2$ by 0.006 to 0.010 on galaxies, gaining more where the two curvatures disagree. We isolated these gains to be from the underlying geometry and not an automatic guarantee from least squares training.

</details>


### [88] [Fuzzy e-Means: Fuzzy c-Means on Large Data with Moving-Average Mini-Batch Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29241)
*Paritosh Tiwari,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 e-FCM：先以硬分配的 e-means 单遍初始化，再执行不带重定位的模糊移动平均遍，从而在大规模数据上以 2–4 遍达到 25 次 AO 迭代的目标函数值，并在高维嵌入集上比 AO 快 11–20 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCM 的标准求解器交替优化（AO）每次迭代需完整遍历数据且迭代次数多；面向大数据的方法要么目标函数更差，要么仍需数十遍遍历。作者希望复用 e-means（一种基于缩放律设定批大小与步长的 mini-batch k-means，维护簇内点数与点和的移动平均）的高效优化器来加速 FCM。

Method: 将 e-means 优化器迁移到 FCM：由于 FCM 质心同样是加权和除以总权重，优化器可直接沿用。但直接迁移有两处失效：e-means 中重定位近空簇的规则会移除小而远簇的质心；且从随机点启动的 FCM 易陷入较差局部极小。故 e-FCM 先用一遍硬分配的 e-means 初始化，再运行不带重定位的模糊移动平均遍；并可通过匹配已知重叠度、约每个候选值两遍的方式选择模糊因子。

Result: 在多达 1.095 亿点、16,384 个簇的 28 种配置上，当簇内点数达数百以上时，e-FCM 用 2–4 遍即达到 25 次 AO 迭代的目标函数值；在 100 万点、1,024 簇的嵌入集上比 AO 快 11–20 倍，最终与收敛 AO 相差在 0.16% 以内；在随机启动 AO 陷入劣质极小的情况下甚至优于收敛 AO。但在 AO 单遍仅需毫秒级的小规模低维问题上慢于 AO。此外，常用模糊因子 m=2 在多数高维数据集上导致质心塌缩或合并。

Conclusion: e-FCM 通过硬分配 e-means 初始化与无重定位的模糊移动平均遍，显著加速大规模 FCM 并改善局部极小问题，但不适用于小规模低维问题；同时揭示了 m=2 在高维数据上的失效，并提供基于重叠度匹配的模糊因子选择方法。

Abstract: Fuzzy c-means (FCM) gives every point a degree of membership in every cluster. Its standard solver, alternating optimization (AO), needs a full pass over the data per iteration and often many iterations, while methods built for large data stop at a worse objective or still need tens of passes. We run FCM with the optimizer of e-means, a recent mini-batch k-means that keeps moving averages of the number and the sum of the points in each cluster and sets its batch size and step size by scaling laws. The optimizer carries over because an FCM centroid is also a weighted sum divided by a total weight, but this direct transfer fails in two ways: the e-means rule that relocates nearly empty clusters also removes the centroids of small, distant groups, and FCM started from random points can settle in poor local minima. Fuzzy e-means (e-FCM) therefore starts with one pass of e-means with hard assignments and then runs fuzzy moving-average passes without relocation. On 28 configurations with up to 109.5 million points and 16,384 clusters, e-FCM reaches the objective of 25 AO iterations in two to four passes when clusters hold several hundred points or more, 11 to 20 times faster than AO on one-million-point embedding sets with 1,024 clusters. It ends within 0.16% of converged AO on these sets and below converged AO where AO from random points lands in poor minima, but it is slower than AO on small low-dimensional problems, where an AO pass takes milliseconds. Finally, the common fuzzifier m=2 makes the centroids collapse or merge on most high-dimensional sets, and e-FCM can choose the fuzzifier by matching a known overlap at about two passes per candidate value.

</details>


### [89] [Tissot indicatrix for location encoders: Characterizing local distortion in learned geographic embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29249)
*Guofeng Cao,Gengchen Mai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将制图学中的Tissot指示图推广为eTissot，用以量化位置编码器对地理空间的形变（各向异性与方向），并揭示该形变随空间变化、部分继承输入几何且影响近邻一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理AI中位置编码器把经纬度映射为嵌入，可能像地图投影一样扭曲地理空间，即等距离位移在不同方向引起不等的嵌入变化；这种扭曲尚未被系统考察，并会传播到下游应用。

Method: 在每一位置，比较编码器在32个等距方向上的输出，拟合以椭圆表示的张量，用其伸长率（各向异性）和朝向刻画局部形变；仅需编码器输出、无需模型内部信息，并在几何已知的参照上验证。

Result: 在5个编码器家族共13个已发布checkpoint上，形变随空间变化并以不同程度上继承输入几何（GeoCLIP配Equal Earth输入时各向异性相关性0.88，其余弱至中等）；形变影响近邻判断：20个查询点1km内地理与嵌入空间各自40近邻的中位重叠仅57.5–87.5%，而拟合椭圆在299次比较中有265次选出完全相同的嵌入近邻。

Conclusion: 为位置编码器提供了基于地理的评价依据，并发布eTissot包以支持用户自定义编码器的形变分析。

Abstract: With the increasing adoption of geographic artificial intelligence, location encoders are widely used to map geographic coordinates to learned embeddings for tasks in Earth observation and geospatial modeling. Similar to map projections, location encoders can distort geographic space, that is, equal-distance displacements in different directions can result in unequal embedding changes. However, such distortion remains largely unexamined, and it can propagate into downstream applications. We adapt Tissot's indicatrix, a cartographic tool for map projection distortion, to characterize this distortion. At each location, we compare encoder outputs at equal distances in 32 directions and fit a tensor, represented by an ellipse, whose elongation (anisotropy) and orientation describe the local distortion. The fit requires only encoder outputs without access to model internals and is validated against references with known geometry. By applying the method to thirteen released checkpoints of five encoder families, we find that distortions vary across space and inherit the input geometry to varying degrees, closely in GeoCLIP with its Equal Earth input (anisotropy correlation of 0.88) and weakly or moderately in the other encoders. The distortion also affects the locations an encoder treats as neighbors. Within 1km of 20 query sites, the 40 nearest neighbors in geographic and embedding space overlap by a median of only 57.5--87.5% across the checkpoints, while the fitted ellipses select exactly the same embedding neighbors in 265 of 299 comparisons. These findings provide a geographic basis for assessing location encoders, and the accompanying eTissot package implements the methods for user-specified encoders.

</details>


### [90] [OAT3D: A Generative Model for 3D Shape- and Resolution-Free Structural Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29253)
*Hongrui Chen,Kittipong Thiamchaiboonthawee,Zhen Wei,Amin Heyrani Nobari,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OAT3D生成模型用于高分辨率3D结构拓扑优化，通过稀疏问题编码与稀疏体积VAE+条件扩散Transformer，构建迄今最大3D拓扑优化数据集（501,865个结构），在512³分辨率下生成结构，原始生成加速44.9×，达到同等柔度加速3.65×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑优化问题高度非凸，需反复有限元分析，高分辨率3D优化计算代价极高；数据驱动方法又受制于训练数据生成需大量求解昂贵问题，导致现有工作集中于2D，大规模3D数据集稀缺，模型在问题规模与边界条件多样性上受限。

Method: 提出OAT3D生成模型：引入新颖的稀疏问题编码表示载荷与固定约束条件；结合稀疏体积变分自编码器（sparse volumetric VAE）与条件扩散Transformer（conditional diffusion transformer）；并构建了含501,865个优化拓扑的数据集（耗费51,800 GPU小时，结构最多达百万单元，载荷与支撑配置更复杂）。

Result: 数据集为目前最大的3D拓扑优化结构数据集；OAT3D可在512³分辨率生成结构，原始候选结构生成加速44.9×，达到相近柔度时相对传统拓扑优化加速3.65×。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了数据驱动拓扑优化在三维结构设计中的规模与多样性，缓解了高分辨率3D拓扑优化的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Topology optimization determines how material should be distributed within a design domain to optimize structural performance under prescribed loads, supports, and constraints. The problems are highly nonconvex, and conventional solvers require repeated finite element analyses, making high-resolution 3D topology optimization computationally expensive. Data-driven approaches face an additional bottleneck: generating training datasets requires solving large numbers of these costly problems, so existing work in data-driven topology optimization primarily focuses on 2D problems. Large-scale 3D datasets remain scarce, and existing models remain limited in problem scale and boundary-condition diversity. Here, we introduce Optimize Any Topology-3D (OAT3D), a generative model for high-resolution 3D structural topology optimization. OAT3D introduces a novel sparse problem encoding to represent load and fixity conditions and combines a sparse volumetric variational autoencoder with a conditional diffusion transformer. We construct a dataset of 501,865 optimized topologies, generated using 51,800 GPU-hours and encompassing structures with up to one million elements and substantially more complex loading and support configurations than any existing structural topology optimization dataset. To our knowledge, this is the largest dataset for 3D topology optimization structures. Trained on this dataset, OAT3D generates structures at 512$^3$ resolution and achieves a $44.9\times$ speedup for raw candidate structure generation or $3.65\times$ speedup to reach similar compliance over conventional topology optimization. These contributions expand the scale and diversity of data-driven topology optimization for three-dimensional structural design.

</details>


### [91] [Denoised but Not Improved: What EEG Denoising Does to a Downstream Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29256)
*Xianglong Bao,Zhe Wang,Kewen Wang,Ming Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文审计了头皮EEG去噪的评测目标，发现以波形保真度为导向的五种标准去噪方法均无法提升癫痫检测任务；并提出一种label-only、无分类器的探针，将去噪效果分解为类间距离与类内散度，揭示下游增益实际来自任务相关特征的类内方差抑制（约四分之一），而非波形保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG去噪文献以"与干净参考波形匹配程度"为评分标准，但真实头皮记录不存在该参考：它由人为加入伪迹模板的"假定干净"信号合成，且即便完美分离也只能得到经容积传导模糊的皮层活动，而非活动本身。因此波形保真度是不可验证的目标，且其是否能预测下游收益基本未被检验。

Method: 固定癫痫检测流水线，仅改变预处理阶段进行审计实验；比较解析类、跨通道类与监督深度类共五种标准去噪方法；并形式化一个label-only、无需分类器的探针，用其诱导的任务相关特征可分性几何来评价预处理阶段，将变化分解为类间距离项与类内散度项。

Result: 五种标准去噪方法均未能优于未处理信号，多数反而使性能下降。在一个确实提升任务的变换上，探针将全部增益归因于类内散度约下降四分之一，而类间分离在统计上无显著变化。

Conclusion: 下游模型的收益来源于任务相关结构上的方差抑制，而非波形保真；波形保真度指标既不测量也不奖励该性质。因此EEG去噪应以任务相关可分性（尤其类内散度）作为评价与优化目标。

Abstract: Scalp electroencephalography (EEG) is routinely denoised before it reaches a downstream model, and the denoising literature scores a method by how closely its output matches a clean reference waveform. That reference does not exist for real scalp recordings: it is synthesized by adding artifact templates to signals merely presumed clean, and even an exact separation would return cortical activity blurred by volume conduction rather than the activity itself. Fidelity to it is therefore an unvalidatable objective, and whether it predicts any downstream benefit has gone largely unexamined. We report an audit that examines it. Holding a seizure-detection pipeline fixed and varying only the preprocessing stage, we find that five standard denoising methods, spanning analytic, cross-channel and supervised deep families, all fail to improve on the unprocessed signal, and most degrade it. To say what such a stage must instead do, we formalize a label-only, classifier-free probe that reads it by the separability geometry it induces on task-relevant features, decomposing any change into a between-class distance and a within-class scatter term. On a transform that does improve the task, the probe attributes the entire gain to a roughly one-quarter reduction of within-class scatter, with class separation statistically unchanged. What a downstream model benefits from is therefore variance suppression on task-relevant structure, which waveform-fidelity metrics neither measure nor reward.

</details>


### [92] [Look Back: Reinforcement Learning with Counterfactual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29259)
*Jinwoo Kim,Toghrul Abbasov,Lucius E. J. Bynum,Aahlad Puli,Stephanie Milani,Hyunwoo Ryu,Seunghoon Hong,Kyunghyun Cho,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出“反事实世界模型”（条件于已观测轨迹，预测在相同环境随机性下采取替代动作的结果），并据此给出策略梯度估计器REINFORCE Look Back (RLB)，用反事实回报作为基线区分动作效应与环境运气，证明了无偏性与方差缩减条件；实验表明在环境演化比动作导致的变化更难预测时，反事实世界模型更易学习、并支持更好的策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂随机环境中，世界模型的预测能力有限，环境随机性又会混淆动作后果。作者假设：与其凭空想象全新轨迹，不如对已观测轨迹做“替代动作”的变换，即以经验为参照预测动作相关变化，从而规避对环境整体动力学的建模难度。

Method: 提出反事实世界模型：以观测为条件，在相同潜在环境随机性下预测替代动作的结果。基于此构造策略梯度估计器RLB，将反事实回报作为基线，并理论证明其无偏性条件与方差缩减条件；与普通世界模型在可学习性和策略学习效果上进行对比。

Result: 理论给出RLB的无偏性与方差缩减成立条件；在环境演化难以预测、而动作诱导变化相对可预测的设定下，反事实世界模型比普通世界模型更易训练，并能支撑更有效的策略学习。

Conclusion: 反事实思维使智能体无需独立复现环境的全部复杂性即可学习有效行为，为复杂随机环境中的基于模型的强化学习提供了更易学习的建模目标与更优的策略梯度估计途径。

Abstract: Agents in complex, stochastic environments must contend with dynamics that can exceed their world models' predictive capacity and randomness that obscure the consequences of their actions. We hypothesize that, compared to imagining entirely new trajectories in such complex environments, it can be easier to transform observed trajectories with alternative actions. Counterfactual world models condition on observations to predict alternative outcomes under the same underlying environmental randomness, using experience as a reference for predicting action-dependent changes. We present a policy gradient estimator, REINFORCE Look Back (RLB), that uses counterfactual returns as baselines to distinguish action effects from environmental luck, and prove conditions for unbiasedness and variance reduction. In settings where environmental evolution is harder to predict than action-induced changes, we show that counterfactual world models are easier to learn and support better policy learning than ordinary world models. Our findings suggest that counterfactual thinking can help agents learn effective behavior without independently reproducing the full complexity of their worlds.

</details>


### [93] [On the Interplay between Backtracking and Latent Confidence Directions in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29265)
*Stephen Cheng,Zahra Sodagar,Sandeep Soni,Dinesh Manocha,Sarah Wiegreffe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出模型内部存在一个表征不确定性的线性"置信方向"，该方向不仅能调控模型言语化的置信度，还能同等有效地调控答案回退（backtracking）行为；对置信方向进行激活引导可在几乎不损失准确率的前提下大幅调节推理轨迹长度与答案提案数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在多次提案答案时因回退产生过长思维链，即使已得到唯一正确答案仍平均做约8次答案提案（32B模型约87%的rollout存在此"装饰性"CoT）。作者据此追问：模型内部是否编码了不确定性表征，该表征能否预测/控制模型是回退还是commit某个答案。

Method: 基于模型内部维护不确定性表征的假设，在响应轨迹的每个答案提案后查询模型言语化的置信度，据此提取区分高/低置信度的线性潜变量方向（latent confidence direction）；并检验该方向对回退行为的因果作用（next-token回退概率），再与直接从回退样例中提取的线性方向对比。随后在逐步更真实的干预设置下进行激活引导：从轨迹中部答案提案处连续引导，以及从生成开始时（提案位置未知）引导，观察轨迹长度、提案数量与准确率变化。

Result: 该置信方向可按预期引导言语化置信度，也意外地能引导回退的next-token概率，效果与从回退样例直接提取的方向相当。从中途答案提案处连续引导，可将响应长度调节约−1.3k至+2.5k token、答案提案数调节−4.18至+4.75，准确率损失极小。从生成开始（位置未知）施加该方向仍能缩短轨迹且准确率相当，其效应体现在提案的语言置信度上。

Conclusion: 模型内部的潜置信度表征与其回退或commit的倾向存在紧密耦合，可被线性提取并因果性地用于控制推理轨迹的冗长度与答案提案行为，揭示了内部置信度与回退行为之间错综复杂的相互作用。

Abstract: Reasoning models deliberate over multiple answer proposals, often resulting in excessively long traces due to backtracking, even when the correct answer has already been reached (Across three 32B models, ~87% of rollouts contain 1 unique answer yet still make ~8 answer proposals on average). Given this potentially "decorative" chain of thought (CoT) reasoning, can we look to the model internals to determine if the model will backtrack or commit to an answer proposal? Operating under the hypothesis that models maintain an internal representation of their uncertainty, we seek to extract this representation and characterize its relationship with backtracking behavior at answer proposals. By querying the model's verbalized confidence following answer proposals throughout a response trace, we extract a linear direction for latent confidence that separates high and low verbalized confidence. This direction can steer verbalized confidence as expected, but it surprisingly can also steer the next token probability of backtracking, performing on par with a linear direction extracted directly from backtracking examples. We then test the direction under progressively more realistic interventions. Steering continuously from a mid-trace answer proposal modulates response length (~-1.3k - ~+2.5k tokens) and the number of answer proposals (-4.18 - +4.75) in both directions, with minimal cost to accuracy. Applying the confidence direction from the start of generation, where proposal positions are unknown, still shortens traces at comparable accuracy, and its effect surfaces in the linguistic confidence of proposals. Overall, our study reveals the intricate interplay between a model's internal confidence and its proclivity to backtrack or commit to an answer.

</details>


### [94] [Dually Constrained Parameter Projection for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29269)
*Bowyn Tan,Zhilong Zheng,Yang Guan,Liming Xiao,Liye Tang,Xiao Li,Naizheng Wang,Keqiang Li,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DUET，一种双向投影的持续学习后训练算法，同时施加旧任务与新任务的Jacobian零空间约束，并在秩亏情形下改进步长选择准则，实现多项数据集上的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Jacobian零空间投影的持续学习方法采用单向投影，仅最小化参数到新任务最优解的距离，忽略了新任务损失曲面，导致约束不充分。

Method: 将新任务训练后的参数调整建模为双重约束优化问题：最小化训练前后参数距离，同时对新旧两任务施加损失保持约束；提出交替双向投影算法求解，用压缩映射定理保证收敛；针对秩亏Jacobian情形，以有效秩感知的方式修正步长选择准则以支持可靠的多步投影。

Result: 大量实验表明DUET持续优于现有单向投影方法，在多个数据集上取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 双向投影优于单向投影，是缓解持续学习中灾难性遗忘的有效范式；步长准则的有效秩修正对多步投影的可靠性至关重要。

Abstract: Continual learning requires a model to learn multiple new tasks sequentially while retaining performance on previous tasks. Projecting model parameters onto the Jacobian null space of previous tasks after unconstrained training on a new task has emerged as an effective paradigm. However, unidirectional projection merely minimizes the parameter distance to the new-task optimum, overlooking the new-task loss landscape. To address this limitation, we propose DUET, a bidirectional projection algorithm that imposes Jacobian null-space constraints from both previous and new tasks during post-training projection. Specifically, DUET formulates the post-training adjustment as a dually constrained optimization problem. It minimizes the distance between parameters before and after training on a new task, subject to loss preserving constraints on both tasks. To solve the optimization problem, we develop an alternating bidirectional projection algorithm, whose convergence is guaranteed by the contraction mapping theorem. For reliable multi-step projection, we rectify existing step size selection criteria via an effective-rank aware formulation, overcoming their failure under rank-deficient Jacobians. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DUET consistently outperforms existing unidirectional projection methods and achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple datasets.

</details>


### [95] [QASparse: Query-Adaptive Sparse Attention with Tailored Budgets and Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29280)
*Zhiyuan Chen,Zihao Yang,Quanjiang Li,Haijiang Li,Jiancheng Pan,Qichao Zhao,Zihan Dong,Qiushi Shi,Jinshi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QASparse：一种查询自适应稀疏注意力机制，跨头共享重要性估计，但按查询区域与注意力头自适应分配预算与选择token，并配专用kernel实现硬件高效执行，在预填充阶段取得更优基准分数与更快计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列推理中prefilling代价高，稠密注意力随上下文长度二次增长。利用注意力头间相似性可减少冗余计算，但已有方法仅依赖局部查询观测来决定头级复用或预算分配；头间相似并不意味着各查询的算力需求一致，如何适配不同查询区域的预算需求与选择偏好仍是关键挑战。

Method: 1）保留跨头共享重要性估计的高效性，同时让计算预算与token选择随查询需求自适应；2）局部预算回归（Local Budget Regression）：建模查询位置上计算需求的局部变化，为不同查询区域和各注意力头估计预算；3）边界投票（Boundary Voting）：头内各查询基于真实query/key分数对选择截断附近的候选进行投票，在保持初始选择规模下精化保留集合；4）长度特化选择kernel：降低动态预算的执行开销，在多种上下文长度下高效支持查询自适应稀疏模式。

Result: 实验表明，相比现有稀疏注意力基线，基准分数提升，prefill注意力计算更快。

Conclusion: QASparse将共享估计与局部决策有效结合，同时自适应"计算多少注意力"和"保留哪些交互"，为长序列高效推理提供可行方案。

Abstract: Long-sequence inference in large language models is constrained by the high cost of prefilling, where dense attention scales quadratically with context length. Exploiting similarity across attention heads offers a way to reduce redundant computation. Prior approaches in this direction have relied on localized query observations to determine head-level computation reuse or budget allocation. However, inter-head similarity does not imply uniform query requirements, leaving adaptation to varying budget needs and selection preferences across query regions a key challenge. We introduce QASparse, a Query-Adaptive Sparse attention mechanism with hardware-efficient execution. QASparse preserves the efficiency of shared cross-head importance estimation while adapting computational budgets and token selections to query requirements, and it executes these dynamic sparse decisions through dedicated kernels. Its query adaptivity stems from two complementary innovations: Local Budget Regression models local variations in computational demand along query positions to estimate budgets for different query regions and attention heads; Boundary Voting lets queries within each head vote over candidates near the selection cutoff using actual query/key scores, refining the retained set while preserving the initial selection size. To reduce the execution overhead of dynamic budgets, we further design length-specialized selection kernels that efficiently support query-adaptive sparse patterns across context lengths. Together, these designs efficiently combine shared estimation with local decisions while adapting both how much attention is computed and which interactions are retained. Experimental results demonstrate improved benchmark scores and faster prefill attention computation compared with existing sparse attention baselines.

</details>


### [96] [Compared to What? Calibrating Machine-Unlearning Diagnostics with Present, Absent and Suppressed Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29285)
*Arsalan Kaleem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出行为层面无法区分"遗忘"与"抑制",构建已知答案的锚点(存在/缺失/抑制)检验现行内部诊断,发现探针、logit lens与重学习速度能在实体级AUROC上区分,但它们在NPO/RMU上相互矛盾;同时所有模型对遗忘答案的log-probability显著偏低,可作为黑盒信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型遗忘(unlearning)评估主要依赖行为(不再输出答案),但模型可能只是抑制而非真正删除。已有内部诊断(线性探针、logit lens、重学习速度)缺乏"已知答案"的真值校验,因此无法确认其可靠性。

Method: 构建三类锚点:present anchor(学习合成事实)、absent anchor(从未教过的oracle,重训时去掉该事实)、suppressed anchor(冻结低层后做遗忘,事实按构造仍编码于冻结块之下)。在GPT-2 Small与Qwen3-0.6B-Base上,以匹配工作点对比NPO、RMU、gradient difference,对每个实体用AUROC(以oracle为参照)评估各诊断。

Result: 行为无法区分抑制锚点与oracle(AUROC 0.51–0.58);探针、logit lens、重学习增益可区分(0.65–1.00),oracle种子接近0.5。用于NPO(两模型)与RMU(Qwen3)时,经验证的诊断相互矛盾:探针显示事实已达oracle水平,但重学习仍远快于从未教过的模型。所有被遗忘模型(无论锚点或方法)给遗忘答案的log-probability都远低于oracle,该黑盒信号标记出全部模型;drift-ratio特异性规则在已知案例上失效。

Conclusion: 单一诊断不足以保证遗忘真实性:探针与重学习速度可给出相反结论,需结合多重内部与黑盒信号;现有的drift-ratio特异性判据不可靠。作者开源代码、数据与全部运行记录。

Abstract: Unlearning methods for language models are judged mainly by behaviour, but a model can stop producing an answer while still encoding it. Internal diagnostics such as linear probes, the logit lens and relearning speed are used to tell removal from suppression, yet none has been checked against cases where the answer is known. We build such cases. A present anchor learned a set of synthetic facts; an absent anchor is a never-taught oracle retrained without them; and suppressed anchors are unlearned with their lower layers frozen, so the facts stay encoded below the frozen block by construction. We compare these anchors with NPO, RMU and gradient difference at a matched operating point on GPT-2 Small and Qwen3-0.6B-Base, scoring every diagnostic per entity with AUROC against the oracle. Behaviour cannot separate the suppressed anchors from the oracle (AUROC 0.51 to 0.58), while probes, the logit lens and relearning gain can (0.65 to 1.00), and the oracle seeds stay near 0.5. Applied to NPO on both models and RMU on Qwen3, the validated diagnostics disagree: probes put the facts at the oracle's level, while the models still relearn them much faster than a never-taught model does. Every unlearned model, anchor or method, also assigns the forgotten answer a far lower log-probability than the oracle does, a black-box signal that flagged all of them. A drift-ratio specificity rule fails on known cases. Code, data and every run are at https://github.com/ArsalanKaleem/unlearning-audit.

</details>


### [97] [Which Failure Does an Intervention Repair? When Agent Interventions Help, and Where Self-Evolving Skills Stall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29290)
*Zihan Jin,Gaoyuan Du,Jiate Li,Dongze Ye,Shawn Li,Yue Zhao,Defu Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过近90万条轨迹的匹配因子实验，论文主张智能体干预（技能、harness、推理预算）不能以单一平均增益评价，而应按"修复哪种失败、在何种配置下"来评判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常只在一个设定下给干预记一个平均增益，掩盖了干预可能改善某一智能体却损害另一智能体、叠加更强harness未必能修复损伤的事实。作者希望回答：每种干预何时有效、修复了哪类失败。

Method: 在9个基准、4个模型、4种技能条件、4个harness层级、3种预算下做匹配因子研究，逐次只改变一个轴，共约90万条轨迹；并引入"匹配恢复"实验与20任务标注试点来选择模型。

Result: 无任何配置在所有场景最优；干预间存在交互而非可加；harness可吸收技能增益、挽救技能造成的失败或保持增益不变。缺失知识需更强模型、执行错误需更丰富harness；匹配恢复主要修复带可见错误信号的失败。自演化技能极少超过简短人工技能，会学到数据集捷径、跨模型迁移失效，且无法提供缺失事实。

Conclusion: 智能体干预应以"修复何种失败、在何种配置下"为评判标准；基于20任务标注试点的基准级模型切换可同时取得更高性能与更低成本，优于始终使用更强模型。

Abstract: An intervention that improves one agent can break another, and adding a stronger harness on top need not repair the damage. Agent interventions such as skills, harnesses, and extra inference budget are nonetheless usually credited with one average gain, measured under one setup. We ask instead when each intervention helps and which failure it repairs. A matched factorial study over nine benchmarks, four models, four skill conditions, four harness levels, and three budgets, with nearly 0.9 million trajectories, changes one axis at a time. No configuration is best everywhere, interventions interact rather than add, and a harness can absorb a skill's gain, rescue a failure the skill causes, or leave the gain intact. The failure left by the current system points to the fix: missing knowledge to a stronger model, execution errors to a richer harness. Yet feedback is not recovery: a matched recovery repairs mainly failures that leave a visible error signal. Skills that evolve from the agent's own experience hit a bottleneck: they rarely beat a short human-written skill, can learn dataset shortcuts, break when moved to another model, and cannot supply missing facts, which outside information does. A benchmark-level switch to the model favored by a 20-task labeled pilot achieves both higher performance and lower cost than always using the stronger model. Agent interventions should be judged by which failure they repair and under which configuration.

</details>


### [98] [Reward Tilting via Semantic Noising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29318)
*Rafał Karczewski,Peter Blohm,Markus Heinonen,Vikas Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 semantic noising：以数据的含噪语义表示作为条件生成，使后验对应语义空间中的线性-二次奖励指数倾斜，单一模型可在训练后支持任意目标与强度选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将生成模型导向期望属性通常需针对每个奖励单独微调，或在采样时增加额外计算，成本高且不灵活。

Method: 对语义表示加噪并以此条件化生成；训练条件生成器直接逼近相应后验；对以扩散生成语义表示的模型，可解析推出同一奖励族的倾斜去噪器。在自回归分子生成器、ImageNet 上图像-CLIP 联合扩散模型、以及 Kandinsky 2.2 等预训练因子化模型三个场景中验证。

Result: 分子生成中可单独或联合控制性质，reward–KL 权衡优于性质条件模型；ImageNet 上联合扩散模型 FID 与 REG 相当，向更高美学分数倾斜时以单次采样达到16选1的最佳水平；Kandinsky 2.2 无需重训即可支持同样的倾斜。

Conclusion: 语义噪声是一种免逐奖励微调、训练后可灵活指定目标与强度的通用属性控制框架，并具备解析可解释性与跨模型架构的迁移性。

Abstract: Steering a generative model toward a desired property typically requires separate fine-tuning for each reward or additional computation during sampling. We propose semantic noising, which conditions generation on a noisy semantic representation of the data. The resulting posteriors correspond to exponential tilts by linear-quadratic rewards in semantic space, allowing a single model to support objectives and strengths chosen after training. A conditional generator can be trained to approximate these posteriors directly, while models that generate semantic representations by diffusion provide analytic tilted denoisers for the same family of rewards. We illustrate semantic noising in three settings. An autoregressive molecular generator trained with semantic noising controls properties individually and jointly, with a better reward--KL trade-off than property-conditioned models. On ImageNet, a joint diffusion model of images and their CLIP embeddings achieves FID comparable to REG, and tilting it toward a higher aesthetic score matches the best of 16 samples at the cost of a single sample. Finally, pretrained factorized models such as Kandinsky 2.2 support the same tilts without retraining.

</details>


### [99] [Hybrid state-space and attention neural operators for nonlinear structural dynamics under environmental loading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29334)
*Anastasios Stamou,Rui Teixeira,Maria Nogal,Michalis Fragiadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合神经算子DeepOJamba，融合选择性状态空间递推与全注意力，用于预测动力荷载下结构响应时程，在15 MW单桩式海上风机上取得最低窗口误差与闭环疲劳/极限荷载误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复的非线性时程分析计算负担大，限制了其在结构评估与设计中的应用，需要高效且能长时程预测的代理模型。

Method: DeepOJamba为混合神经算子：结合选择性状态空间递推（SSM）与全注意力；引入物理参数条件化（physical-parameter conditioning）与序列间预测状态迁移，使单一训练算子可表征不同系统条件并生成超出训练序列长度的响应时程。以15 MW单桩式海上风机在风浪联合荷载下评测，对比纯注意力、纯状态空间、Fourier与DeepONet算子。

Result: 在所有通道上窗口误差最低；闭环疲劳与极限荷载误差分别为9.4%与6.0%，次优纯注意力算子为14.6%与8.7%；训练波浪频带外的海况下误差仅增加约0.4个百分点；纯状态空间算子需采用与塔架自振周期匹配的更大卷积核才能接近同等低误差，训练成本增加40%。

Conclusion: 结果支持采用混合神经算子实现稳健的长时程结构响应与需求预测。

Abstract: The computational burden of repeated nonlinear time-history analyses limits their application for structural assessment and design. We therefore propose DeepOJamba, a hybrid neural operator that combines selective state-space recurrence with full attention to predict the structural response history due to dynamic loading. Physical-parameter conditioning and predicted-state transfer between sequences enable a single trained operator to represent varying system conditions and generate response histories beyond its training sequence length. DeepOJamba is evaluated on a 15~MW monopile offshore wind turbine under wind and wave loading. The proposed operator is compared to pure attention, pure state-space, Fourier, and DeepONet operators and is found to give the lowest window error on every channel and the lowest closed-loop fatigue and ultimate-load errors of $9.4\%$ and $6.0\%$, against $14.6\%$ and $8.7\%$ for the next-best pure attention operator examined. Moreover, on sea states outside the training wave band, the error slightly increases by approximately $0.4$ percentage points. Pure state-space operators can approach similar low errors only with a much wider convolution kernel tuned to the tower eigenperiod, which increases the training cost by $40\%$. In all, the results obtained support hybrid neural operators for robust long-duration structural response and demand prediction.

</details>


### [100] [DeciForm: Incorporating Task Structure into Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29340)
*Qiuyu Tian,Haojie Yin,Yingce Xia,Youyong Kong,Zequn Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Deciform 将候选选择、布尔判定与数值评分统一在同一模型中，并针对"无序集合"与"有序数值"两类答案空间结构分别设计编码与监督方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策模型虽能统一候选选择、布尔判定和数值评分，但其答案空间结构差异被忽略：分类候选是无序集合，数值评分具有内在顺序与距离，需把这种结构差异显式引入计算与监督。

Method: 提出 Deciform：以无候选查询与孤立候选分支保证重排序下分类一致性；用对数球面（log-spherical）目标训练候选概率；用十进制前缀读出结合单值监督与区间加权累积监督进行数值评分；通过共享适配器、任务私有残差与输出通道增益实现联合适配。

Result: 在共享的 15,000 条请求测试集上，Deciform 在 Qwen 与 ModernBERT 上三项决策指标均提升；源归一化误差支持 Score 增益可跨数据混合泛化；规范化联合编码消除了测试中的顺序翻转，隔离进一步改善预测指标；单轮 MIND-small 适配后，两骨干上的多正例新闻排序均有提升。

Conclusion: 结果支持在统一决策接口内，将编码与监督方式与答案空间结构相匹配的理论主张。

Abstract: Decision models unify candidate selection, Boolean judgment, and numerical scoring within a single model. Their answer spaces nevertheless carry different structures: classification candidates form an unordered set, while numerical scores have an intrinsic order and distance. We introduce Deciform, which incorporates these distinctions into computation and supervision. Candidate-free queries and isolated candidate branches support consistent classification under reordering. A log--spherical objective trains candidate probabilities, while a decimal-prefix readout combines individual-value and interval-weighted cumulative supervision for numerical scoring. Shared adapters, task-private residuals, and output-channel gains support joint adaptation. On a shared 15,000-request test set, Deciform improves all three decision metrics on Qwen and ModernBERT; source-normalized errors support the Score gains across the data mixture. Canonicalized joint encoding also eliminates tested order flips, while isolation further improves prediction metrics. Separate objective studies and main-configuration ablations examine the classification and numerical components. After one epoch of MIND-small adaptation, Deciform improves multi-positive news ranking on both backbones. These results support matching encoding and supervision to answer-space structure within a unified decision interface.

</details>


### [101] [SleepXA: Composing Pretrained Neural and Cardiac Foundation Models for Sleep Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29364)
*Xiaoyu Chen,Xiao Gu,Chao Zhang,Wen Wu,Shuaicong Hu,Qinfan Xiao,Jie Yang,Chen Chen,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SleepXA框架，利用同步多导睡眠图（PSG）组合独立预训练的EEG与ECG基础模型，学习共享与模态特异表征；在27个下游任务中22个进入前二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠中记录的生理信号可同步反映神经、心脏、呼吸等多系统，并保留模态特异信息。已有EEG与ECG基础模型各自捕获丰富但不同的生理结构；相比从零训练多模态睡眠模型，组合现有预训练模型更具吸引力，但缺乏有效组合范式。

Method: SleepXA以同步PSG为"接口"，将独立预训练的EEG编码器与ECG编码器与额外的轻量PSG模态编码器组合，通过睡眠引导的对齐（sleep-guided alignment）同时学习共享表征与模态特异表征。在三个大型队列共9,230例整夜PSG记录上预训练。

Result: 在覆盖睡眠表征、临床与疾病预测、日间生理迁移的27个下游任务中，22个任务排名前二；控制实验显示在不同传感器配置与EEG-ECG骨干选择下收益一致，预训练初始化优于架构匹配的从头训练，且优于其他多模态组合策略。

Conclusion: 同步多模态生理信号可作为组合现有生理基础模型的天然接口，产生可复用表征，并可扩展至睡眠以外的临床与日间生理场景。

Abstract: Physiological signals recorded during sleep provide synchronized views of neural, cardiac, respiratory, and other bodily systems, while preserving modality-specific information. Existing EEG and ECG foundation models already capture rich but distinct physiological structure, motivating an alternative to training a multimodal sleep model from scratch: composing existing pretrained models. We introduce SleepXA, a framework that uses synchronized polysomnography (PSG) to compose independently pretrained neural and cardiac foundation models. SleepXA combines pretrained EEG and ECG encoders with lightweight encoders for additional PSG modalities, and learns shared and modality-specific representations through sleep-guided alignment. We pretrain SleepXA on 9,230 overnight PSG recordings from three large cohorts and evaluate it on 27 downstream tasks spanning sleep representation, clinical and disease prediction, and daytime physiological transfer. SleepXA ranks among the top two methods on 22 of 27 tasks. Controlled experiments further show consistent benefits across sensor configurations and EEG-ECG backbone choices, while pretrained initialization outperforms architecture-matched training from scratch and SleepXA surpasses alternative multimodal composition strategies. These results suggest that synchronized multimodal physiology can serve as a natural interface for composing existing physiological foundation models, yielding reusable representations that extend beyond sleep to clinical and daytime physiological settings.

</details>


### [102] [BenchMint: From Real-World Tasks to Executable Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29367)
*Zining Huang,Zuo Wang,Mengyao Zhang,Yilan Piao,Zhe Wang,Shuohan Wang,Ruiying Wang,Huicong Yi,Qianyu Yang,Zilong Pan,Wenlong Wu,Jiajun Shi,Shen Yan,Xingyu Pan,Houxuan Chen,Yuan Xu,Jinzhao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自动化基准构建流程 BenchMint 及 100 项日常任务集 Daily-Life，用同一源文档联合生成任务与评分标准，由模型评审结合工具轨迹与私有证据判分，并报告 26 种模型/框架配置的描述性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测缺乏对日常推理与工具使用（如约束条件下的行程规划、将数值答案落到证据上）的覆盖，需要可自动化构建、评分标准与任务同源的基准。

Method: BenchMint 从同一源文档联合构建任务与评分标准；语言模型评审依据评分标准、工具调用轨迹和私有证据对候选答案评分；构建 11 个真实领域共 100 项任务（Daily-Life）。

Result: 共报告 26 种模型/框架配置（含 GPT、Claude、Gemini 家族），其中 17 种具备完整领域画像，属描述性配置结果而非受控排名；在某一旅行任务中将参考总时长由 320 修正为 380 分钟后，错误答案的算术判定由通过变为不通过，正确答案由不通过变为通过。

Conclusion: 单次投票案例可暴露参考标注错误，但不足以确立可重复性或全集效度；现有构建方式的对比尚不完整，无法证明联合创作或反馈引导修订具有普遍优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) need evaluations of everyday reasoning and tool use, such as planning itineraries under user constraints and grounding numerical answers in evidence. We introduce BenchMint, an automated benchmark-construction workflow, and Daily-Life, its collection of 100 selected tasks across 11 real-world domains. BenchMint jointly constructs tasks and scoring criteria from the same source documents. Language-model judges assess possible answers against these criteria using tool traces and private evidence. We report 26 model/harness configurations, including GPT, Claude and Gemini families; 17 have complete domain profiles. These are descriptive configuration results, not a controlled model ranking. In one selected travel task, we corrected the reference total from 320 to 380 minutes while keeping both answers unchanged. The incorrect answer's arithmetic verdict switched from pass to fail; the correct answer's switched from fail to pass. With one ballot per cell, this exposes a reference error without establishing repeatability or collection-wide validity. Incomplete construction comparisons establish no general advantage of joint authoring or feedback-guided revision.

</details>


### [103] [BQDNA: Multi-Head Binary Spherical Quantization for Genomic Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29395)
*Xudong Wang,Siwei Cheng,Haozhe Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出上下文感知的BQVAE（DNA标量量化器）与支持多头码本的自回归基因组语言模型BQDNA，以学习式向量量化替代手工分词器，在极少训练下取得SOTA且具跨物种泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长重复DNA序列的生物学信息解码困难；gLM沿用通用"预训练-适配"范式，但DNA缺乏天然语义切分与先验规则，k-mer、BPE等手工分词器并非最优。

Method: 引入上下文感知的BQVAE对DNA进行标量化量化，并构建多头码本的自回归基因组语言模型BQDNA；系统分析DNA空间中向量量化的训练动力学。

Result: (1) 揭示DNA空间向量量化的独特训练动力学，强调训练语料与下游数据对齐的重要性；(2) BQVAE优于手工分词器与vanilla VQVAE；(3) BQDNA具显著跨物种泛化能力，仅用0.1B多物种训练BQVAE、hg38上1个epoch训练BQDNA，即超越预训练token量多10~100倍的gLM，达SOTA。

Conclusion: 学习式向量量化在基因组序列表示中潜力巨大，为gLM的分词与预训练范式提供了高效替代方案。

Abstract: Decoding biological information from long, repetitive DNA remains challenging. Genomic language models (gLMs) often follow the conventional language-model pretrain-and-adapt paradigm. However, the lack of inherent semantic segmentation and prior rules makes hand-crafted tokenizers like k-mer and BPE suboptimal. We introduce context-aware \textbf{BQVAE} for scalar quantization of DNA and \textbf{BQDNA}, an autoregressive gLM supporting multi-head codebooks. Comprehensive experiments reveal that: (1) unique training dynamics of vector quantization in DNA space, including the importance of alignment between training corpus and downstream data. (2) superior performance of BQVAE over hand-crafted tokenizers and vanilla VQVAE. (3) remarkable cross-species generalization capability of BQDNA, which achieves state-of-the-art performance with merely 0.1B multi-species training for BQVAE and 1 epoch training on hg38 for BQDNA, outperforming gLMs pretrained with 10$\sim$100x more tokens. Our findings underscore the potential of learned vector quantization for genomic sequence representation. Code is available at https://github.com/Sophie10001b/BQDNA

</details>


### [104] [HKD-ACPA: Heterogeneous Knowledge Distillation Adversarial Cyber-Physical Attacks in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29418)
*Junhao Ren,Honglin Gao,Shuo Feng,Kun Cao,Qiyu Kang,Xiaofeng Zong,Shuping He,Gaoxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究零查询（zero-query）场景下的输电网攻击定位规避问题，提出HKD-ACPA框架：通过异构知识蒸馏构建代理检测器集成并校正视图偏差，再用双层PGPPO求解稀疏幅度受限的FDI合成问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
