Motivation: 现有针对电网攻击定位学习算法的对抗攻击依赖白盒访问或反复在线查询，实用性受限；而本地测量与中心（受DoS遮蔽）测量之间存在视图不对称，难以直接迁移攻击。

Method: 协同物理攻击、DoS攻击与FDI攻击的零查询攻击框架HKD-ACPA：将历史检测器预测蒸馏为异构图表征代理集成，训练残差校正模块修正视图导致的logit偏差；在校正后的代理模型上，构建带组平衡目标的稀疏、幅度受限FDI优化问题，并采用双层投影梯度近端策略优化（PGPPO）求解离散-连续混合问题。

Result: 在IEEE 30节点与118节点系统上，HKD-ACPA在所有稀疏度预算下均优于所有基于代理的基线；在10种系统-预算组合中有8种超越在线查询攻击，且无需查询检测器模型。

Conclusion: 零查询、基于异构知识蒸馏与残差校正的代理建模可有效弥合视图不对称，配合PGPPO求解使攻击在稀疏约束下具备强迁移性与实用性。

Abstract: Learning-based algorithms assist power-grid control centers in localizing transmission-line attacks, yet existing adversarial attacks typically require white-box access or repeated online queries. We study a practical zero-query setting in which an adversary coordinates physical, denial-of-service (DoS), and false data injection (FDI) attacks using only local measurements and historical detector outputs. To bridge the input-view asymmetry between local adversarial observations and DoS-masked control-center measurements, we propose Heterogeneous Knowledge Distillation Adversarial Cyber-Physical Attacks (HKD-ACPA). HKD-ACPA distills historical detector predictions into a heterogeneous graph surrogate ensemble and trains a residual calibration module to correct the view-induced logit discrepancy. Over this calibrated surrogate, we formulate sparse, magnitude-bounded FDI synthesis with a group-balanced objective that jointly induces missed detections and false alarms, and solve the resulting hybrid discrete--continuous problem via bi-level projected gradient-based proximal policy optimization (PGPPO). Experiments on the IEEE 30-bus and 118-bus systems show that HKD-ACPA outperforms all surrogate-based baselines across every sparsity budget and surpasses online-query attacks in eight of ten system--budget settings without querying the detector model.

</details>


### [105] [Optimal Bandits with Switching Costs: $(T/\log(T))^{2/3}$ Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29438)
*Chenyu Gan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究带切换成本的对抗性赌博机问题，证明了带单位切换成本的极小极大遗憾为 Θ(min{T, K^{1/3}T^{2/3}/log^{2/3}(2+T/K)})，并提出了优于简单分块策略的新算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有简单分块（blocking）策略在每个块内只玩一个臂，被证明是次优的；作者希望刻画带切换成本下对抗性赌博机的真实极小极大遗憾界，并设计更优算法。

Method: 算法在每个块内以随机时间进行常数次切换；核心思想是在切换时刻附近赋予观测更高权重以从每次切换中提取更多信息；同时引入“绕行”（detour）机制——临时玩一个新采样的臂，观测损失越大则绕行概率越高，用以抵消分块带来的时间相关性项，同时保留估计所需的观测。下界则通过对改进的多尺度随机游走构造进行精细分析得到。

Result: 证明了极小极大遗憾为 Θ(min{T, K^{1/3}T^{2/3}/log^{2/3}(2+T/K)})，并给出匹配的下界；结果表明简单分块策略是次优的。

Conclusion: 在带单位切换成本的对抗性赌博机中，通过在块内随机切换、加权切换时刻附近观测以及引入绕行机制，可以突破简单分块策略的局限，达到最优的极小极大遗憾阶。

Abstract: We study adversarial bandits with switching costs. For $K$ arms, $T$ rounds, unit switching costs, and oblivious losses in $[0,1]$, we prove that the minimax regret is \begin{equation*}
  Θ\!\left(\min\left\{T,
  \frac{K^{1/3}T^{2/3}}{\log^{2/3}(2+T/K)}\right\}\right)\,. \end{equation*} This shows that simple blocking, which plays one arm throughout each block, is suboptimal. Our algorithm makes at most a constant number of switches at random times within each block. The key idea for the improvement is to extract more information per switch by giving greater weight to observations near switching times. We also introduce detours, during which the learner temporarily plays a newly sampled arm. Larger observed losses make a detour more likely. These detours cancel temporal correlation terms induced by blocking while preserving the observations used in the estimates. A matching lower bound follows from a refined analysis of a modified multiscale random walk construction.

</details>


### [106] [RumiFold: Teaching an LLM to Natively Fold Proteins and Reason About Their Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29444)
*Kevin Lu,David Bau,Wengong Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RumiFold，一种预训练聊天模型，直接从自身隐藏状态预测蛋白质结构，并通过折叠表征以自然语言回答蛋白质功能问题，实现折叠与语言推理的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究通用语言模型能否学会折叠蛋白质，以及学习折叠除结构预测外还能带来什么（即折叠对表征与生物分子推理的作用）。

Method: RumiFold通过多轮重复折叠pass处理蛋白质序列，从模型自身隐藏状态预测结构，并利用折叠后的表征以自然语言回答功能问题；辅以因果干预实验分析其内部机制。

Result: 折叠精度与SimpleFold等专用单一表征模型相当；在Gene Ontology蛋白质功能预测和自由文本功能描述上达到最先进性能；折叠预训练使隐藏状态围绕蛋白质功能组织，形成具有生物学依据的逐残基内部工作区（workspace）。

Conclusion: 因果干预表明该折叠工作区在功能预测时连接结构与语言，为生物分子推理提供丰富表征；RumiFold是迈向"原生生物分子智能"的一步，使基于蛋白质的计算与基于语言的推理成为通用语言模型的集成能力。

Abstract: Can we teach a general-purpose language model to fold proteins? And if so, what does learning to fold enable beyond structure prediction itself? We introduce RumiFold, a pretrained chat model that learns to predict protein structures directly from its own hidden states. RumiFold processes proteins through repeated folding passes, then uses these folded representations to answer questions in natural language. It achieves folding accuracy comparable to dedicated single-representation models such as SimpleFold, alongside state-of-the-art performance on Gene Ontology protein function prediction and free-text function description. We find that folding pretraining organizes the model's hidden states around protein function, creating an internal per-residue workspace that is biologically grounded. Causal interventions show that this folded workspace links structure and language when RumiFold predicts function, providing rich representations for biomolecular reasoning. We see RumiFold as a step toward native biomolecular intelligence: where protein-based computations and language-based reasoning become integrated capabilities of general-purpose language models. Code, data, and models are available at: https://rumifold.baulab.info

</details>


### [107] [Beyond Accuracy: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Hypothesis-Driven Experiment Planning in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29467)
*Ryo Kuroki,Amin Mansouri,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出超越最终准确率的LLM科学智能体诊断评测框架，在动力学机理辨识的闭环系统辨识任务中，将推理过程分解为信念更新、假设判别、判别性实验设计、干预下预测推理四个步骤并分别评分，同时量化探索行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM智能体科学任务的评测通常仅归结为最终任务准确率，掩盖了推理在哪里成功、在哪里失效，无法定位能力瓶颈。

Method: 构建面向"假设驱动的自适应实验规划"的基准与诊断评测框架，实例化于闭环世界中的动力学机理辨识。诊断维度有二：(1) 在实验设计空间中量化智能体的空间填充（space-filling）或趋角（corner-seeking）探索模式；(2) 将推理轨迹拆分为信念更新、假设判别、判别性实验设计、干预下预测推理四步，分别打分。对比对象包括10个LLM智能体与自适应基线（贝叶斯优化）、非自适应基线（拉丁超立方），并辅以独立专家标注。

Result: 较强智能体优于贝叶斯优化与拉丁超立方两类基线，且探索模式不符合朴素启发式。分步分解显示：强智能体四步均稳定表现良好；弱智能体在信念更新与假设判别上常能成功，但将二者转化为可执行实验设计与尖锐对比性预测的能力不可靠。专家标注支持所设验证标准下的预测推理，判别性实验设计的证据为正但一致性较弱。

Conclusion: 存在"识别合理假设"与"转化为对比性预测"之间的能力缺口，实验设计瓶颈的证据较为初步。基准网站：https://schwallergroup.github.io/beyond-accuracy/

Abstract: Evaluating large language model (LLM) agents on scientific tasks typically reduces to final-task accuracy, which obscures where reasoning succeeds or breaks down. We introduce a benchmark and diagnostic evaluation framework for hypothesis-driven adaptive experimental planning in closed-world system identification, instantiated in kinetic mechanism identification. The framework goes beyond final accuracy in two ways. First, we evaluate exploration behavior by quantifying the extent to which agents exhibit space-filling or corner-seeking patterns in the experimental design space. Second, we decompose the reasoning process into four interpretable steps: belief update, hypothesis discrimination, discriminative experiment design, and predictive reasoning under interventions, each scored separately on reasoning traces. Across ten LLM agents, we find that stronger agents outperform both adaptive (Bayesian optimization) and non-adaptive (Latin hypercube) baselines and exhibit exploration patterns inconsistent with naive heuristics. Decomposing reasoning reveals that the stronger agents score consistently well across all four steps, whereas weaker agents often succeed at belief update and hypothesis discrimination but are less reliable at translating them into actionable designs and sharp contrastive predictions. Independent expert annotations support predictive reasoning under our stated validation criteria, while evidence for discriminative experiment design is positive but less consistent. The results suggest a gap between recognizing plausible hypotheses and translating them into contrastive predictions, with more tentative evidence for a corresponding experiment-design bottleneck. The benchmark website is available at https://schwallergroup.github.io/beyond-accuracy/.

</details>


### [108] [OnCache: Learning Dynamic Caching Schedules for Efficient Diffusion Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29480)
*Zhikang Xie,Xichen Ye,Yifan Wu,Yuxuan Liu,Qianlong Wang,Haoshen Yu,Peizhu Gong,Weizhong Zhang,Cheng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OnCache动态缓存框架，通过提示自适应的在线计算分配与特征刷新策略，并配以神经校正器补偿缓存误差，在扩散/视频生成中实现约3倍加速且保持高质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型因迭代去噪导致推理延迟高；现有缓存加速策略多采用静态、与样本无关的调度，忽视了(i)不同提示下去噪动态与计算需求各异、(ii)不同时间步对缓存误差的敏感度不同，导致在敏感步复用特征或在非敏感步浪费计算。

Method: OnCache框架：训练一个离线策略，在相同去噪状态下的配对"计算续算"与"缓存续算"上学习，以终端保真度差异和NFE作为决策的下游代价信号，在线决定计算量分配与缓存刷新时机；另设轻量神经校正器补偿缓存引入的误差并抑制其沿去噪轨迹累积。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev上，1024²分辨率实现近3倍生成加速，LPIPS 0.156、SSIM 0.860；扩展至Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B视频生成，在两种模态上均取得SOTA的效率—质量权衡。

Conclusion: 动态、提示自适应的缓存调度结合误差校正，可在几乎不增加推理延迟的前提下显著加速扩散与视频生成并保持保真度，验证了自适应计算分配的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have revolutionized generative tasks but incur high inference latency due to iterative denoising. Cache-based strategies accelerate inference by reusing intermediate features, yet they largely rely on static, sample-agnostic schedules. Our empirical analysis reveals two factors that these schedules overlook. (i) denoising dynamics and computational requirements vary across prompts, motivating adaptive computation allocation. (ii) sensitivity to caching errors varies across timesteps, so static schedules may reuse features at error-sensitive steps or waste computation at less sensitive ones. We therefore propose OnCache, a dynamic caching framework that makes prompt-adaptive decisions online about how much computation to allocate and when to refresh cached features. A policy is trained offline on paired compute and cache continuations from identical denoising states, using differences in terminal fidelity and the number of function evaluations (NFE) to account for the downstream consequences of each decision. To further improve the generation quality, a neural corrector compensates for caching-induced errors and mitigates their accumulation throughout the denoising trajectory. Both components are lightweight and introduce negligible additional inference latency. On FLUX.1-dev, OnCache achieves a nearly $3\times$ generation speedup at $1024^2$ resolution, with LPIPS of $0.156$ and SSIM of $0.860$. We further extend OnCache to video generation on Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B, achieving state-of-the-art efficiency--quality trade-offs across both modalities.

</details>


### [109] [Vanishing Initialization Turns Diagonal Tensor Gradient Flow into Greedy Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29484)
*Dmitry Yarotsky,Eugene Golikov,Yaroslav Gusev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究CP分解张量在零初始化下的梯度流学习动力学：证明极限动力学等价于贪心二部匹配过程，并由此导出模加法任务的无分布大系统损失曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CP分解模型在零初始化下训练时被认为存在"惰性/激活"式的分阶段学习现象，缺乏严格刻画；模加法任务训练中的"顿悟"（grokking）现象也需理论解释，故需给出可解析的学习动力学理论。

Method: 针对模式方向正交基下对角化的ν≥3阶目标张量，将每个原子的每个"标量扇区"赋予初始化依赖的激活时钟，按时钟升序做贪心二部匹配（原子与扇区各至多被选一次）以确定实际学习事件序列；对复数权重的模加任务，经傅里叶对角化后激活时钟独立同分布，结合随机匹配渐近理论分析。

Result: 证明GF轨迹收敛到与匹配序对应的目标分量阶梯；复数权重模加任务得到显式、无分布假设的大系统损失曲线（时间重参数化后），并给出该曲线与原GF训练时间的渐近关系式；实数权重情形下共轭傅里叶模构成二维块，刻画其秩与边界秩级联，并猜想其GF动力学为交错多阶段学习过程。

Conclusion: 零初始化下CP张量回归的梯度流可被精确约化为贪心匹配过程，为分阶段学习和模加法中的顿悟现象提供严格理论框架。

Abstract: We study gradient-flow (GF) learning of tensors from vanishing initialization in canonical polyadic (CP) factorization models. For tensors of order $ν\geq3$ whose targets are diagonal in mode-wise orthonormal bases, we prove that the limiting learning dynamics follows a simple greedy bipartite matching process. Each rank-one component of the model, called an atom, can potentially learn each rank-one component of the target, called a scalar sector. Every atom--sector pair has an initialization-dependent activation clock, representing the time at which the atom would grow to macroscopic size in that sector if the pair evolved in isolation. Processing these clocks in increasing order and selecting a pair whenever neither its atom nor its sector has been selected before determines the sequence of actual learning events. We prove that the GF trajectory converges to the corresponding staircase of learned target components. The task of learning modular addition by a CP model with complex weights falls exactly into this framework after Fourier diagonalization. Its activation clocks are independent and identically distributed, allowing us to combine the GF reduction with known random matching asymptotics and derive an explicit, distribution-free large-system loss curve after time reparametrization. We also obtain an asymptotic formula relating this curve to the original GF training time. For models with real weights, conjugate Fourier modes instead form two-dimensional blocks with richer internal structure. We characterize their rank and border-rank cascade and conjecture that the corresponding GF dynamics follows interleaved multi-stage learning processes.

</details>


### [110] [Learning Global Probabilistic Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29493)
*Frédéric Koriche,Louenas Bounia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于谱方法的全局解释器，用傅里叶分析与 junta 学习在成员查询下高效找到满足基数约束的最大相关特征子集，并给出 PAC 保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部解释只说明单个预测，缺乏刻画模型在输入分布上整体行为的全局概率解释；需在基数约束下识别最大化相关性的特征子集。

Method: 定义全局解释为特征子集 K，其相关性为随机输入 x 与在 K 上一致的另一随机输入被赋予同一标签的期望概率；在离散特征空间上具全支撑的乘积分布下，利用对黑盒分类器的成员查询，结合傅里叶分析与 junta 学习构造谱解释器，输出 PAC 解释。

Result: 算法关于解释规模上限、最大特征基数与目标精度为固定参数可解（FPT）；合成与真实数据实验验证理论保证，且随解释规模增大，谱方法优于贪心基线。

Conclusion: 谱解释器为全局概率解释提供了带 PAC 保证且计算可行的方案，在较大解释规模下优于贪心策略。

Abstract: Interpreting the predictions of complex black-box classifiers remains a central challenge in explainable artificial intelligence.
  While local explanations clarify individual predictions, there is a significant need for global probabilistic explanations that capture a model's overall behavior across the input distribution.
  Such an explanation is a small subset $K$ of features, whose relevance is measured by the expected probability, over a random input $\mathbf x$, that the classifier assigns the same label to $\mathbf x$ and to a random input agreeing with $\mathbf x$ on $K$.
  In this study, we investigate the task of identifying maximally relevant global explanations under a cardinality constraint, focusing on product distributions with full support on discrete feature spaces.
  We introduce a spectral explainer that leverages membership queries to the black-box classifier and employs Fourier-analytic, junta-learning methods to produce probably approximately correct (PAC) explanations.
  Our algorithm is fixed-parameter tractable with respect to the explanation size limit, maximum feature cardinality, and desired accuracy.
  Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets confirm the theoretical guarantees and show that the spectral explainer outperforms a greedy baseline as the explanation size increases.

</details>


### [111] [CoHABIT: Complementary Evaluation of Furniture Layouts through Spatial Rules and Household Routines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29494)
*Ewsey Obzherin,Samira Atlangirieva,Anastasia Antsiferova,Valentin Khrulkov,Art Prosvetov,Andrey Kuznetsov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoHABIT框架，通过静态空间规则与基于学习型住户策略的动态模拟互补评估家具布局质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有家具布局评估多依赖静态几何或设计偏好指标，缺乏对真实住户日常活动、多智能体协同与个体负担的考量，难以判断布局是否真正服务于不同家庭需求。

Method: 框架分两层：静态评估检验几何有效性、人机工程可达性、功能组织及可配置设计偏好（含风水）；动态评估以学习得到的住户策略模拟日常活动、资源共享、协同调度与多人家庭中的个体负担，并结合住户画像与日程进行受控对比，同时以独立核验的住户需求为基准。

Result: 受控实验表明，静态空间准则与家庭行为指标揭示布局质量的不同侧面；住户画像与日程安排会改变家具配置的排序结果。

Conclusion: 以观测需求为依据的动态比较使评估更具可解释性，可刻画家具环境对不同家庭的服务效果，为布局评价提供静态与动态结合的互补视角。

Abstract: We introduce CoHABIT, a framework for evaluating furniture layouts through complementary spatial rules and household routines. Static assessment captures geometric validity, ergonomic access, functional organization, and configurable design preferences, including Feng Shui. Dynamic assessment uses learned resident policies to simulate everyday activities, resource sharing, coordination, and individual burdens in multi-agent households. Controlled comparisons show how spatial criteria and household behavior reveal distinct aspects of layout quality, while resident profiles and scheduling influence furnishing rankings. Independent verification of household needs grounds dynamic comparisons in observed outcomes, enabling interpretable assessments of how furnished environments serve different households.

</details>


### [112] [GeoFusion: An Uncertain Machine Learning Framework for Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29510)
*Basant Ahmed Mounir Abdelhamid,Amr T. Abdel-Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoFusion：三阶段不确定性机器学习框架，通过协同多部手机的不确定GPS观测提升定位精度，在保留测试集上MAE达2.90±0.15米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本智能手机GPS传感器实现高定位精度仍是难题。现有方法将每次GPS观测视为绝对点，丢弃了测量条件固有的不确定性，也未利用多部手机对同一位置观测的协同潜力。

Method: 三阶段框架：（1）将每次GPS观测建模为不确定对象，提出两种模型——基于卫星几何的volcano函数、基于真实手机数据统计结构经验推导的mountain函数；（2）用划分式与图算法按位置事件对不确定对象聚类；（3）用基于滤波与基于学习的估计器精化聚类输出。数据为Google Smartphone Decimeter Challenge。

Result: 不确定性建模是精度提升的主要驱动因素：mountain函数显著优于仅依赖几何的volcano函数；不确定性感知的UK-medoids显著优于其确定性对应版本k-medoids。聚类后深度神经网络进一步精化估计，推荐配置在保留测试行车数据上MAE为2.90±0.15米，与约3米的最佳已报道单机精度相当且平均更优。

Conclusion: 协同式不确定性感知融合可显著提升低成本GPS传感器定位精度，验证了不确定性建模与多机协同的潜力，为智能手机高精度定位提供了可行路径。

Abstract: High positioning precision is a critical requirement in many Global Positioning System (GPS) applications, yet achieving it with affordable smartphone sensors remains an open challenge. Existing approaches treat each GPS observation as an absolute point, discarding the uncertainty inherent in the measurement conditions and leaving the cooperative potential of multiple phones observing the same location untapped. In this work, we propose GeoFusion, a three-stage uncertain machine learning framework for cooperative sensor fusion. In the first stage, each GPS observation is modeled as an uncertain object using one of two proposed models: the volcano function, grounded in satellite geometry, and the empirically derived mountain function, built from the statistical structure of real smartphone data. In the second stage, uncertain objects are clustered by location event using partitional and graph-based algorithms. In the third stage, the cluster output is refined using filter-based and learning-based estimators. Experiments on the Google Smartphone Decimeter Challenge dataset show that uncertainty modeling is a primary driver of precision gains: the mountain function significantly outperforms the geometry-only volcano function, and uncertainty-aware UK-medoids significantly outperforms its certain counterpart, k-medoids. A post-clustering deep neural network further refines the cluster estimates, and the recommended configuration achieves a mean absolute error of 2.90 +/- 0.15 meters on held-out test drives, comparable to and on average below the best reported single-phone precision of approximately 3 meters, demonstrating the potential of cooperative uncertainty-aware fusion for affordable GPS sensors.

</details>


### [113] [Recursive Self-Editing: LLMs Program Their Own Weight Edits for Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29513)
*Linghua Zhang,Yunhe Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SELFWRITE 让语言模型在持久 Python 会话中自省权重与激活，用白盒原语写程序构造低秩权重编辑，经隐藏门集打分后合并最优改进编辑，无需任何梯度即实现递归自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型的自我改进依赖数据、训练配方或脚手架，权重更新由梯度下降写入而非模型自身。作者希望让模型直接"写"自己的权重，验证模型能否在无梯度、无训练工具的条件下自主产生有益的权重修改，从而实现真正的递归自我改进。

Method: SELFWRITE 采用代际迭代：每一代当前模型在全新训练题上研究自身推理，在持久 Python 会话中检视自身权重与激活，并编写程序从白盒原语（奇异方向、正确/错误答案间的激活对比、秩一投影、张量计算）构造低秩权重编辑，不使用训练或梯度工具。每个候选编辑在隐藏门集上对当前模型评分一次，严格改进且最优的编辑被合并进权重，编辑后的模型负责撰写下一次编辑。

Result: 在 Qwen3.8-27B 与 DAPO-Math 数学题上，五代 SELFWRITE 将门集准确率从 73.95% 提升至 76.45%；在循环从未见过的留出测试集上，avg@4 从 76.30% 升至 77.75%，pass@4 从 82.60% 升至 84.80%，全程无一次梯度步。

Conclusion: 语言模型能够在不使用梯度的情况下对自身权重作出有效修改，且被编辑后的模型仍能持续写出有益的编辑，表明基于白盒自省的权重自写作是实现递归自我改进的可行路径。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement asks a system to improve the very system that improves it. Today's language models improve themselves through data, training recipes or scaffolds. Whenever their weights change, gradient descent writes the update, not the model. We let the model write its own weights. In SELFWRITE, each generation the current model studies its own reasoning on fresh training problems, inspects its own weights and activations in a persistent Python session, and writes a program that constructs a low-rank weight edit for itself from white-box primitives (singular directions, activation contrasts between its right and wrong answers, rank-one projections, tensor computations), with no training or gradient tool. Every candidate is graded once on a hidden gate set against the current model; the best strictly improving edit is merged into the weights, and the edited model writes the next edit. On Qwen3.8-27B and math problems from DAPO-Math, five generations of SELFWRITE raise gate accuracy from 73.95% to 76.45% and accuracy on a held-out test split that the loop never sees from 76.30% to 77.75% (avg@4) and from 82.60% to 84.80% (pass@4), without a single gradient step. A language model can author useful changes to its own weights without gradients, and the edited model keeps writing useful edits.

</details>


### [114] [Adaptation or Poisoning? Security Risks of Conversational Test-Time Learning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29532)
*Honghao Feng,Shanhan Qu,Kai Tong,Shuquan Man,Huiping Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出P-TAP攻击：仅通过多轮对抗查询，利用测试时学习(TTL)会话适配器的参数更新进行投毒，从而越狱LLM，使攻击成功率显著上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTL在部署时用未标注输入更新轻量适配器，且更新跨轮次持久化，使每个用户查询既是请求又是适应数据；攻击者可借此通过普通交互影响参数更新、削弱安全对齐，这一风险尚未被系统研究。

Method: P-TAP：仅提问（query-only）的多轮攻击。用一系列逐级升级的对抗查询逐轮塑造会话适配器；最终有害请求在新累加状态下被回答，且其本身不参与适配，从而完成越狱。在四个开源指令微调模型上，与"同样查询但不更新"的对照会话比较ASR，并做适应数据替换消融与现有防御检测评估。

Result: 启用更新后，攻击成功率平均提升13.00个百分点（贪心解码）、22.25个百分点（best-of-10采样）。若仅回答对抗查询而在无关文本或同主题良性文本上适应，则大部分提升消失。查询筛查可在最终请求前标记所有攻击流，而基于困惑度的不安全适应检测器未标记任何成功攻击。

Conclusion: 持久化的测试时适配引入了新的投毒攻击面，可通过纯查询交互越狱；危害主要来自"在对抗输入上适应"而非"回答对抗输入"，故应限制/隔离会话更新，并以输入筛查等防御优先应对。

Abstract: Test-time learning (TTL) enables large language models to adapt to unlabeled inputs during deployment. The model updates a lightweight adapter by minimizing the next-token prediction loss on the input it receives. When these updates persist across conversation turns, every user query serves both as the request answered in the current turn and as adaptation data for later turns. By choosing the queries used for adaptation, an attacker can influence parameter updates and weaken safety alignment through ordinary interactions with a TTL chat service. To demonstrate this risk, we introduce Progressive Test-Time Adaptation Poisoning (P-TAP), a query-only multi-turn attack that jailbreaks the model by poisoning its session adapter through the service's own updates. A sequence of escalating adversarial queries shapes the adapter turn by turn; the final harmful request is then answered under the accumulated state without itself being used for adaptation. Across four open-weight instruction-tuned models, enabling updates raises attack success rates on average by 13.00 percentage points under greedy decoding and by 22.25 points under best-of-10 sampling, relative to matched sessions receiving the same queries without updates. Answering adversarial queries but adapting on unrelated text or on benign text about the same topic removes most of this increase. Among existing safeguards, screening incoming queries flags every attack stream before the final request, while a perplexity-based detector of unsafe adaptation flags none of the successful attacks.

</details>


### [115] [Learning Acceptable Lotteries via Queries: Minimizing Aggregated Violation Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29555)
*Nicholas Teh,Paul W. Goldberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究"仅接收接受/拒绝二元反馈"下的随机化AI部署（候选人彩票选择）问题，将以往"求得全体接受彩票"的目标推广为最小化各利益相关者违反量的幂平均（以到接受边界的归一化距离度量），刻画了二元回答可唯一确定这些距离的充要条件，并给出紧致的查询复杂度与算法结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署AI系统时，开发者可对多个候选版本做随机化（彩票）以在多利益相关者之间折中。实际场景中每个利益相关者只反馈"是否接受"这一比特信息，且其可接受彩票集合位于某个未知超平面的一侧。但安全与有用性等要求冲突时，"全体接受的彩票"可能根本不存在，因此需要把原问题（求全体接受的彩票）松弛为可优化的违反度最小化问题；同时二元回答不含违反的幅度与跨主体的可比信息，需要恰当定义违反度并厘清可学习性。

Method: 1）建模：n个利益相关者、m个候选版本的彩票空间，每人仅返回接受/拒绝，其接受集为未知超平面一侧的半个空间；2）目标：最小化违反量的幂平均（power mean），每个违反量用到该主体接受边界的归一化距离度量，以解决二元反馈的幅度缺失与跨主体不可比问题；3）理论刻画：给出接受/拒绝答案能够唯一确定这些距离的充要条件；4）算法：可识别时给出在线学习算法，不可识别时给出自然的最坏情况（鲁棒）方法并证明其（含无限查询方法在内的）常数因子最优性；5）查询预算受限时给出采样算法，并证明确定性算法在不查询几乎全部主体时无法保证小损失；6）合成实验验证"多查询主体"与"对每个主体学得准"之间的权衡。

Result: 可识别情形下，O(nm log(1/ε)) 次查询即可学到距离（ε为效用精度），且该界是紧的；不可识别情形下，自然的最坏情况方法在常数因子内最优，甚至优于可无限查询的方法；有查询预算时，所给采样算法达到给定精度所需查询数与 n 无关，同时确定性算法若不查询几乎所有利益相关者则无法保证小损失；合成实验展示了查询广度与单主体学习精度之间的权衡曲线。

Conclusion: 该工作把"寻找全体接受彩票"推广为基于二元反馈的违反度最小化框架，完整刻画了归一化距离的可识别性，并给出紧致查询界、常数因子最优的鲁棒方法以及预算受限下的采样策略与确定性下界；其结论表明在多利益相关者随机化部署中，查询广度与个体学习精度存在本质权衡，采样（随机化）是突破确定性下界的必要手段。

Abstract: When deploying an AI system, a developer can randomize over several candidate versions, sending each request to a version chosen at random, to balance the requirements of multiple stakeholders. We study a setting in which each of $n$ stakeholders reveals only whether a proposed lottery over $m$ candidates is acceptable, and each stakeholder's acceptable lotteries lie on one side of an unknown hyperplane. While previous work sought a lottery that every stakeholder accepts, such a lottery need not exist when, for instance, safety and helpfulness requirements are in conflict. We therefore minimize a power mean of the stakeholders' violations; since binary answers reveal neither the size of a violation nor how to compare violations across stakeholders, we measure each violation by a normalized distance to the stakeholder's acceptance boundary. We characterize exactly when accept/reject answers determine these distances. When they do, $\mathcal{O}(nm\log(1/\varepsilon))$ queries suffice to learn them, where $\varepsilon$ is the precision of the utilities, and this bound is tight; when they do not, a natural worst-case approach is optimal up to a constant factor, even compared with methods that make unlimited queries. For limited query budgets, we give a sampling algorithm whose number of queries for a given accuracy does not depend on $n$, and show that a deterministic algorithm cannot guarantee a small loss without querying almost all stakeholders. Synthetic experiments illustrate the resulting tradeoff between querying many stakeholders and learning each of them accurately.

</details>


### [116] [Think Before You Forecast: Scaling Test-Time Compute for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29566)
*Daojun Liang,Xiao Hong,Pei Zhang,Yinglong Wang,Shuo Li,Jing Chen,Ying Guo,Hu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TTC4TS——一种架构无关的测试时计算框架，通过生成多个候选预测轨迹再进行筛选/融合，在冻结主干模型的前提下提升时序预测精度并支持不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序预测模型通常仅做单次前向推理，面对分布漂移与非平稳性时鲁棒性差；而测试时计算在视觉与语言建模中已被证明有效，时序领域尚缺失。

Method: 两阶段框架：生成器（generator）通过数据增强、检索、适配器等手段，从预训练冻结主干产生多个候选预测轨迹；合成器（synthesizer）通过聚合、分类、候选打分与排序等方法选择或组合候选，输出最终预测。通过增加候选数量可扩展测试时计算而无需重训主干。

Result: 在多种数据集与主干模型上取得一致的点预测精度提升与校准改善；候选预测可用于不确定性量化，提升区间预测；并给出测试时计算何时有效的理论分析。

Conclusion: TTC4TS 是首个面向时序的架构无关测试时计算范式，兼顾精度、校准与不确定性建模，可与现有冻结主干即插即用地结合。

Abstract: Test-time compute has become an important paradigm in computer vision and language modeling, where additional computation at inference time enables models to explore multiple candidate solutions and select or refine the most reliable one. However, time series forecasting models typically produce predictions through a single forward pass, leaving them vulnerable to distribution shifts and non-stationarity that frequently arise in real-world temporal data. We propose Time Test Compute for Time Series (TTC4TS), an architecture-agnostic framework that improves the predictive performance of frozen forecasting models at inference time. Given a pretrained backbone, the generator produces multiple candidate forecast trajectories using augmentation, retrieval, adapter. The synthesizer then selects or combines these candidates using aggregation, classification, or candidate-scoring and ranking methods to produce the final forecast. By increasing the number of candidates, TTC4TS scales test-time compute without retraining the forecasting backbone. Beyond improving point-forecast accuracy, TTC4TS can exploit candidate forecasts for uncertainty quantification, which supports improved interval forecasts. We also theoretically analyze when additional test-time computation improves forecasting. Experiments across diverse datasets and backbones demonstrate consistent gains in accuracy and calibration. Code is provided in https://github.com/Anoise/TTC4TS.

</details>


### [117] [From Learning Rates to Rotational Schedules: Finite-Horizon Theory on the Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29579)
*Jacopo Graldi,Antonio Orvieto,Anastasia Koloskova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从"旋转运动"视角重新形式化学习率调度设计，在测地凸球面设定下给出任意调度的一般化收敛界（尺度与时间分布可分离），推导出使该界最小的唯一调度（随训练视界增大趋近线性衰减），并据此预测与改进LLM预训练中的调度选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有cosine与WSD等调度在大规模预训练中被广泛使用，但其效果受参数范数影响；归一化（scale-invariant）参数组的损失只依赖方向，范数仅影响旋转速度。已有分析无法解释实践中观察到的调度排序（如不同模型规模下WSD与线性衰减的相对表现），缺少针对调度"分布形状"的针对性理论。

Method: 将调度设计表述在旋转运动空间中：在测地凸的球面流形上，把学习率视为参数方向旋转的速率，推导对任意旋转调度的last-iterate收敛保证，并将该界分解为总体尺度项与时间分布项；解析求解该界下的唯一最优调度。实验上，在Adam、Muon以及Hyperball风格的AdamH、MuonH上系统比较cosine、线性衰减、WSD等调度，跨模型规模检验理论预测。

Result: 理论最优调度随视界增大趋近线性衰减。Hyperball设定下的实验证实线性衰减LR表现强、WSD表现弱，与预测一致；纳入参数范数动态后，理论转而预测Adam与Muon实践中WSD的优异表现。所提出的新LR调度（用于Adam与Muon）优于既有的cosine与WSD调度。

Conclusion: 旋转动力学可作为理解、预测并指导LLM预训练学习率调度的有效框架；调度效应本质上取决于参数范数是否被归一化（尺度不变），这解释了vanilla与Hyperball类优化器间相反的调度排序。

Abstract: How should step sizes be scheduled across a finite training horizon? Cosine and warmup-stable-decay (WSD) schedules are widely adopted in large-scale pretraining, yet their effect on optimization also depends on parameter norms. Normalization can make parameter groups scale invariant, so that the loss depends only on their direction, while their norms still affect how fast the parameters rotate. In this work, we formulate schedule design directly in the space of rotational motion. Working within a geodesically convex spherical setting, we derive a last-iterate convergence guarantee for arbitrary rotational schedules that separates their overall scale from their distribution over time. We derive the unique schedule that minimizes this bound and prove that it approaches linear decay as the horizon grows. Empirically, our rotational interpretation and idealized spherical analysis provide useful predictions for LLM pretraining in practice, including schedule comparisons missed by existing analyses. Our analysis predicts schedule ordering across model scales for both vanilla (Adam, Muon) and Hyperball-style (AdamH, MuonH) optimizers. Our Hyperball experiments support the predicted strong performance of linear LR decay and confirm our prediction that WSD underperforms in this setting. Accounting for parameter norm dynamics, we predict instead the strong performance of WSD for Adam and Muon shown in practice. Guided by our theoretical insights, we also derive a new LR schedule for Adam and Muon, which improves over the established cosine and WSD schedules. All in all, these results support the use of rotational dynamics to understand, predict, and ultimately guide scheduling in LLM pretraining.

</details>


### [118] [How Multi-Timescale Memory Cools Optimization: Theory and Evidence from AdEMAMix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29588)
*Jacopo Graldi,Antonio Orvieto,Anastasia Koloskova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究多时间尺度动量（multi-timescale momentum）改善大模型预训练的内在机制：多尺度优化器记忆与归一化网络的几何之间存在交互作用，慢动量通过改变参数范数间接控制参数旋转速度，充当内生退火调度（endogenous annealing schedule）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdEMAMix 等方法在 AdamW 上叠加多时间尺度动量可提升 LLM 预训练效果，但其增益来源机制尚不清楚，缺乏理论解释与可验证的因果分析。

Method: 从 decoupled weight decay 下的精确范数动力学出发，推导参数范数与更新序列时间相关性之间的关系，并说明 weight decay 控制相关性的影响时长；分析独立加权的慢动量对参数范数的放大作用及其对瞬时更新的功率贡献可忽略；在 AdEMAMix 语言模型预训练上做受控干预实验。

Result: 慢动量可显著增大参数范数，但对瞬时更新的功率贡献可忽略，从而表现为内生退火调度；受控干预显示该调度机制具有明确的实践后果，是 AdEMAMix 相对 AdamW 优势的重要来源。

Conclusion: 多时间尺度动量的收益可由"范数—旋转"耦合机制解释：优化器记忆通过范数动态实现隐式退火；该机制为设计更优优化器提供了可解释的指导原理。

Abstract: Augmenting AdamW with multiple momentum timescales improves large language model pretraining, yet the mechanism driving these gains remains poorly understood. We identify an interaction between multi-timescale optimizer memory and the geometry of normalized networks: memory changes parameter norms and thereby controls how fast parameters rotate. Starting from exact norm dynamics under decoupled weight decay, we show that the parameter norm depends on the temporal correlation of the updates, with weight decay controlling how long these correlations influence optimization. We show how an independently weighted slow momentum can substantially increase the norm of parameters, while contributing negligible power to the instantaneous update, therefore acting as an endogenous annealing schedule. We study this mechanism in AdEMAMix for language-model pretraining. Controlled interventions show that this scheduling mechanism has clear practical consequences and is an important factor behind AdEMAMix's advantage over AdamW.

</details>


### [119] [Aggregation Dispersion: An Information-Geometric Diagnostic of Oversmoothing in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29600)
*Abdessalam Ed-dib,Amine Mohamed Aboussalah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Fisher-Rao几何的"聚合分散度" D_k 作为无需训练的过平滑诊断指标,理论上等价于传播矩阵秩一性并由谱隙控制衰减,实验上其与测试精度的相关性最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPNN堆叠过多层会导致节点表示趋于一致(过平滑),但缺乏一个几何上可解释、无需训练即可量化该现象的诊断指标。

Method: 将每层传播算子视为为每个节点分配一个随机游走分布 p_v^(k),这些分布在概率单纯形上构成点云;用Fisher-Rao度量(平方根嵌入下为Hellinger距离)度量点云收缩,以嵌入后单位球上点云的方差定义聚合分散度 D_k。

Result: 证明四条性质:(i) D_k=0 当且仅当第k步传播矩阵秩为一(完全混合的充要条件);(ii) D_k 随深度单调不增;(iii) 传播矩阵谱隙控制衰减速率;(iv) 对收缩型MPNN,D_k 上界表示分散度(相差图与初始化相关常数)。在14种模型配置、11个数据集上,D_k 与测试精度的平均绝对Pearson相关性在八种诊断中最高(|r|=0.721,对数变换),与训练后有效秩(|r|=0.688)相当,且无需训练。

Conclusion: D_k 是一个理论刻画严谨、计算廉价且无需训练的过平滑诊断量,可替代依赖训练的有效秩用于深度相关的性能预测。

Abstract: Message-passing neural networks (MPNNs) learn node representations by iteratively aggregating information from neighbors, but stacking too many layers causes all representations to converge, an effect known as oversmoothing. We propose a geometric perspective on this phenomenon. At each depth $k$, the propagation operator assigns to every node $v$ a walk distribution $p_v^{(k)}$, a probability distribution over source nodes. These walk distributions form a point cloud on the probability simplex, whose contraction to a single point characterizes complete mixing of the propagation operator, and thus oversmoothing. To measure this contraction, we use the Fisher-Rao metric, the unique Riemannian metric on the simplex invariant up to scale under sufficient statistics, whose scaled chordal distance under the square-root embedding $p \mapsto \sqrt{p}$ is the Hellinger distance. The resulting diagnostic, the aggregation dispersion $D_k$, is the variance of the embedded point cloud on the unit sphere. We prove that: (i) $D_k = 0$ if and only if the $k$-th step propagation matrix has rank one, a necessary and sufficient condition for complete operator mixing; (ii) $D_k$ is non-increasing with depth; (iii) the spectral gap of the propagation matrix controls the rate of this decay; and (iv) for contractive MPNNs, the aggregation dispersion upper-bounds the representation dispersion up to graph- and initialization-dependent constants. Across 14 model configurations and 11 datasets, $D_k$ attains the highest mean absolute Pearson correlation with test accuracy across depths among eight diagnostics ($|r| = 0.721$, using log-transformed diagnostic values), numerically comparable to the post-training effective rank ($|r| = 0.688$), while requiring no training.

</details>


### [120] [Attacking Tabular Foundation Models: An Experimental Taxonomy of Context Poisoning and Test-Time Evasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29627)
*Ramak Nassiri,Sujan Kumar Reddy Challa,Feroz Ahmed,Souhail Chakkour,Md Shohel Rana,Aditya Garg,Charan Gudla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表格基础模型在推理期上下文上的四类操纵攻击（标签翻转、后门上下文投毒、干净标签上下文选择、测试时逃逸），并在CIC-IDS2017与N-BaIoT上比较TabPFN-2.5、TabICL与XGBoost，发现攻击效果强依赖于操纵发生的位置与攻击者改动数据的自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型以推理时提供的带标签样本为条件进行预测，这一机制形成了区别于传统模型训练的独特攻击面，现有工作缺乏对该攻击面的系统刻画与比较。

Method: 将四种操纵族按攻击阶段、标签一致性和分布真实性三维度组织；在统一预处理与评估流程下，于CIC-IDS2017和N-BaIoT上评估TabPFN-2.5、TabICL与XGBoost；实现受CAPGD启发但不等价的简化代理迁移约束PGD攻击，与等预算随机噪声对照。

Result: 后门上下文投毒能在几乎不损失干净准确率的情况下对上下文模型实现强定向控制；选取真实且标签正确的边界拥挤样本在偏差比例达20%时也未造成系统性准确率下降；简化代理迁移约束PGD在多数设置下优于等预算随机噪声并实现测试时逃逸。

Conclusion: 攻击有效性同时取决于操纵发生的位置（上下文内/外）与攻击者对数据改动的自由度；上下文注入类攻击（尤其后门投毒）是表格基础模型的主要风险面，而干净标签选择式偏差危害有限。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models condition predictions on labeled examples supplied at inference time, creating an attack surface that differs from conventional model training. We study four manipulation families (label flipping, backdoor context poisoning, clean-label context selection, and test-time evasion) and organize them by attack stage, label consistency, and distributional realism. TabPFN-2.5, TabICL, and XGBoost are evaluated on CIC-IDS2017 and N-BaIoT using a common preprocessing and evaluation pipeline. Backdoor context poisoning provides strong targeted control over the in-context models with little change in clean accuracy, whereas selecting real, correctly labeled boundary-crowding rows causes no systematic accuracy degradation up to a 20% bias fraction. A simplified surrogate-transfer constrained PGD attack inspired by, but not equivalent to, CAPGD also produces test-time evasion, outperforming equal-budget random noise in most settings. The comparison shows that attack effectiveness depends strongly on both where manipulation occurs and how much freedom the attacker has to alter the data.

</details>


### [121] [How Seasonal Reference Choice Changes the Apparent Gains of Chronos Models in Electricity Load Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29653)
*ChandraMohan K*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文定量考察"基线选择"对零样本基础模型预测增益幅度与统计确信度的影响：在小时级电力负荷上，Chronos-Bolt 与 Chronos-2 相对周持久性基线的明确胜出次数多于相对"验证期选出的参考基线"，中位增益亦由 17.3% 降至 11.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声称"击败简单基线"的结论高度依赖所选基线，但基线选择对增益大小与显著性的影响缺少系统量化；同时零样本基础模型（Chronos 系列）在电力负荷上的表现需要一个多维度的基线对照来检验其实际价值与不确定性。

Method: 构建 60 个固定案例，取自 4 个公开数据集合中的 8 条序列；预测跨度 24 与 168 小时，历史长度至多 2,048 小时。基线集合含日持久性、周持久性、工作日混合、日-周加权混合，以及由验证期从上述基线中选出的参考基线。保留每个预测起点的逐点预测，用配对循环分块 bootstrap（块长 168 与 336 小时）估计区间。

Result: 一周跨度下，Bolt 相对周持久性在 30 个案例中的 21 个明确胜出，而相对验证期选定参考基线仅 14 个；中位增益由 17.3% 降至 11.9%。Chronos-2 的对应数字为 18 与 15。Chronos-2 在 24 个案例上周前 MAE 低于 Bolt，但仅 12 个差异明确。验证期选出的参考基线并不总是更难，部分测试窗口上其表现劣于周持久性。

Conclusion: 基线选择同时改变报告的增益幅度与结论的确信度，故"击败基线"的表述必须明确基线定义。结果为探索性：序列与窗口相互关联、曾检视早期试点、无法排除预训练数据重叠、案例级区间未做多重比较校正。

Abstract: When a forecasting model is said to beat a simple baseline, the size of that win depends on which baseline was used. We measure how much this matters for two zero-shot foundation models, Chronos-Bolt and Chronos-2, on hourly electricity load. The study uses 60 fixed cases drawn from eight series in four public collections, with forecasts 24 and 168 hours ahead and up to 2,048 hours of history. Each model is compared with daily persistence, weekly persistence, a weekday hybrid, a daily-weekly blend, and a reference chosen from these on a validation period. We keep every origin-level forecast and estimate uncertainty with paired circular block bootstrap intervals using 168- and 336-hour blocks. A week ahead, Bolt clearly beats weekly persistence in 21 of 30 cases but the validation-selected reference in only 14, and its median gain falls from 17.3% to 11.9%. For Chronos-2 the clear-win counts are 18 and 15. Chronos-2 has lower week-ahead MAE than Bolt in 24 cases, yet only 12 of those differences are clear. The selected reference is not always the harder baseline, since on some test windows it does worse than weekly persistence. The choice of baseline therefore changes both how large the reported gain is and how sure we can be of it. The results are exploratory: the series and windows are related, an earlier pilot was inspected, pretraining overlap cannot be ruled out, and the case-level intervals are not corrected for multiple comparisons.

</details>


### [122] [One Checkpoint, Four Converters: Per Input Disagreement After On Device Conversion and a Probe Set That Catches It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29661)
*Sharven Rane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 模型转换后仅核对top-1准确率会掩盖单样本决策翻转；本文系统比较34组FP32与INT8转换的逐样本决策，发现FP32完全一致而等价准确率的INT8仍有>1%翻转，并提出用源模型低margin探针集检查转换一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前"on-device/加速推理"转换的验收标准是top-1准确率与源模型一致，但准确率一致并不等于逐样本决策一致；转换器、INT8 recipe与校准抽样各自都会改变决策。因此需要量化"准确率等价但决策翻转"的程度并给出可操作的检查手段。

Method: 取5个公开checkpoint（4个ImageNet分类器+1个情感模型），用ExecuTorch、LiteRT、ONNX Runtime、TensorRT的官方FP32与INT8 recipe转换，在7,854张图像与1,938条句子上与PyTorch源模型逐样本比对top-1决策；对翻转样本分析源模型margin的AUROC；并预注册式检验"翻转集中于低margin样本"假设；另在转换前构造200个最小源margin训练样本作为探针集评估其检出能力。

Result: 34组严格FP32转换全部逐决策复现；4个保持准确率等价的视觉INT8 recipe均翻转>1%决策：ONNX Runtime static INT8在ResNet-50上翻转4.07%（95% CI [3.65,4.53]）而准确率仅变化-0.15点，同一recipe两次不同校准抽样之间不一致率3.44%（[3.06,3.88]）。在准确率保持的场景下，翻转样本确为源模型"勉强判定"的样本（ResNet-50上源margin的AUROC 0.90–0.97，各CI均落在[0.89,0.98]内），但"对所有recipe成立"的预注册假设在两个移动网络（INT8直接破坏准确率）上不成立。200样本探针集以95% CI [0.91,1.00]的召回标记出全部40个视觉栈与全部6个翻转≥0.5%的文本栈，且未误报任何精确FP32转换。

Conclusion: 转换验收不应只看top-1准确率，而应在预先选定的低源margin探针集上比对逐样本决策；该探针集能以高召回检出会翻转决策的INT8栈且不误伤FP32精确转换，为设备端部署提供更敏感、校准无关的回归检查。

Abstract: A model converted for on device or accelerated inference ships once its top 1 accuracy matches the source model. Accuracy can match while individual decisions change, and the converter, its INT8 recipe and its calibration draw each move them. We convert 5 public checkpoints (four ImageNet classifiers and a sentiment model) with the documented FP32 and INT8 recipes of ExecuTorch, LiteRT, ONNX Runtime and TensorRT and compare every top 1 decision with the PyTorch source on 7,854 images and 1,938 sentences. All 34 strict FP32 conversions reproduce every decision, yet each of the 4 vision INT8 recipes that keeps accuracy equivalent flips more than 1% of decisions: ONNX Runtime static INT8 flips 4.07% (95% CI [3.65, 4.53]) of ResNet 50 decisions while accuracy moves by -0.15 points, and two builds of that recipe from different calibration draws disagree on 3.44% [3.06, 3.88]. Where accuracy is kept, the flipped inputs are the ones the source model barely decides (AUROC of the source margin 0.90 to 0.97 on ResNet 50, every 95% CI inside [0.89, 0.98]), but our preregistered claim that this holds for every recipe fails on the two mobile networks, where INT8 breaks accuracy outright. A probe set of the 200 training inputs with the smallest source margin, chosen before any conversion, flags all 40 vision stacks (recall 95% CI [0.91, 1.00]) and all 6 text stacks that flip at least 0.5% of decisions and none of the exact FP32 conversions, so conversion checks should compare decisions on such a probe set, not accuracy.

</details>


### [123] [Exact on Paper: Which LLM Serving Shortcuts Flip Graded Answers, Measured Against the Noise of Reordering the Same Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29663)
*Sharven Rane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在vLLM上以9种单特性配置和5个参考副本跑3380条请求：默认kernel下"精确"特性翻转1.47%–1.78%的判定结果（不高于调度底噪1.72%），FP8 KV cache翻转3.36%–3.91%；batch invariant kernel下7个精确特性实现逐请求文本复现，建议以此作为认证条件或采用≥3副本的调度底噪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团队常以"聚合准确率不变"作为开启prefix caching、chunked prefill、投机解码等特性的依据，因为引擎将其文档化为"精确（exact）"。但准确率是聚合指标，可能在总体不变的情况下改变具体哪些请求被答对，从而掩盖服务变更带来的判定翻转。论文旨在暴露这种聚合指标的盲区，并给出可操作的认证方法。

Method: 单一4B指令模型、vLLM推理，设置9种单特性配置与参考配置的5个副本；每次运行对来自5个benchmark的3380条请求评分。将每个候选与两类底噪比较：重启底噪（新进程）与调度底噪（同一批请求不同顺序）。分别在默认kernel与batch invariant kernel两种内核条件下测量判定翻转率，并给出95%置信区间；同时评估FP8 KV cache与FP8权重的对照效果，并检验基于单副本对的验收门限的表现。

Result: 默认kernel下所有配置准确率差异在1个百分点以内；7个精确特性的判定翻转率为1.47%（95% CI 1.13–1.85）至1.78%（1.39–2.20），均不超过调度底噪1.72%；FP8 KV cache翻转3.36%（2.80–3.96），与FP8权重相当。batch invariant kernel下7个精确特性（含投机解码）对每条请求逐字复现参考输出，而FP8 KV cache仍翻转3.91%（3.28–4.53）。仅比较单一副本对的验收门限会错误拒绝一个未变更的配置。

Conclusion: 聚合准确率不足以证明服务变更的安全性。建议在batch invariant kernel下认证服务变更（此时底噪为零），或至少基于三个及以上副本测得的调度底噪进行比对；避免使用单副本对的判定门限。

Abstract: Teams enable serving features such as prefix caching, chunked prefill and speculative decoding after checking that benchmark accuracy is unchanged, because the engine documents them as exact. Accuracy is an aggregate, so a feature can hold it while flipping which requests are answered correctly. We serve one 4B instruction model in vLLM under nine single feature configurations and five replicas of a reference configuration, grade 3,380 requests from five benchmarks in every run, and compare each candidate with a restart floor (fresh process) and a scheduling floor (same requests in a different order). With default kernels every configuration keeps accuracy within one point, and the seven exact features flip the graded outcome of 1.47% (95% CI 1.13 to 1.85) to 1.78% (1.39 to 2.20) of requests, none above the scheduling floor of 1.72%, while the FP8 KV cache flips 3.36% (2.80 to 3.96), as many as FP8 weights. With batch invariant kernels all seven exact features, speculative decoding included, reproduce the reference text on every request, while the FP8 KV cache still flips 3.91% (3.28 to 4.53) of graded outcomes. A gate that compares against a single replica pair rejected an unchanged configuration, so we recommend certifying a serving change under batch invariant kernels, where the floor is zero, or against a scheduling floor measured from at least three replicas.

</details>


### [124] [CrysLDM: Latent Diffusion Model for Crystal Material Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29675)
*Subhojyoti Khastagir,Kishalay Das,Pawan Goyal,Seung-Cheol Lee,Satadeep Bhattacharjee,Niloy Ganguly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出潜空间扩散模型CrysLDM，结合VAE与扩散模型，在低维潜空间中生成3D晶体材料，显著提升训练与采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在特征空间操作，需用复杂架构联合建模原子类型、坐标与晶格结构的分布，扩散步数多、训练与采样成本高，难以在时间与资源受限条件下生成新晶体材料。

Method: 先由VAE编码器将3D晶体结构映射到低维、平滑的潜空间，再在该潜空间上运行扩散模型，从而简化生成过程并减少计算负担。

Result: 在基准数据集与任务上的大量实验表明，CrysLDM可生成稳定且有效的材料，质量与SOTA方法相当，同时在计算资源与时间上显著更高效。

Conclusion: 将扩散过程迁移至VAE潜空间是兼顾晶体材料生成质量与效率的有效途径，适合资源受限场景。

Abstract: Generating new crystal materials with desirable chemical properties has long been a challenging task. Existing diffusion models operate in feature space, requiring complex diffusion architectures to model the joint distribution of atom types, coordinates, and lattice structures. This complexity increases the number of diffusion steps, leading to higher training and sampling costs. In this work, we aim to generate novel crystal materials within a time- and resource-constrained setup, where existing models are not well-suited. To address this, we propose CrysLDM, a novel latent diffusion model for 3D crystal materials, which integrates a variational autoencoder and a diffusion model. The VAE encoder maps 3D crystal structures into a latent space, where the diffusion model operates. Since CrysLDM leverages a smooth, lower-dimensional latent space, it simplifies the generative process and accelerates both training and inference. Through extensive experiments on benchmark datasets and tasks, we show that CrysLDM generates stable and valid materials with quality comparable to state-of-the-art methods, while being significantly more efficient in terms of computational resources and time.

</details>


### [125] [Towards Global Counterfactual-Based Self-Explainable Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29681)
*Wendy Zheng,Yinhan He,Jundong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GCSGNN，将反事实生成融入GNN架构，实现自解释且具备全局（模型级）反事实解释能力的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图反事实解释(GCE)存在两大局限：(1)依赖事后(post-hoc)解释范式，单独训练解释器，无法利用GNN表征学习过程，解释质量较低；(2)多为局部解释，仅针对单样本生成反事实，难以捕获数据分布中的可泛化模式，限制其在药物发现等需要全局规律的应用中的价值。

Method: 提出GCSGNN统一框架：将反事实生成内嵌于GNN架构中，使解释忠实反映模型决策过程；通过发现跨图共享的潜在模式生成全局(模型级)反事实解释；并设计高效的隐空间反事实机制，规避离散图操作带来的组合复杂度，实现可扩展的泛化修改模式发现。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，GCSGNN在生成有效反事实解释方面优于现有方法；代码已开源。

Conclusion: GCSGNN通过自解释架构与隐空间全局反事实机制，同时解决事后性与局部性两大局限，为图模型提供忠实、可泛化且可扩展的解释方案。

Abstract: Graph Counterfactual Explanations (GCEs) have emerged as an effective approach for interpreting Graph Neural Network (GNN) predictions by identifying minimal graph modifications needed to change predictions. However, existing methods suffer from two fundamental limitations: (1) They rely on a post-hoc explanation paradigm, where a separate explainer is trained after the GNN. This sequential optimization prevents the explainer from leveraging how the GNN learned its representations, often resulting in lower-quality explanations. (2) They are largely local, generating counterfactual explanations only for individual samples. Consequently, they fail to capture generalizable patterns across the data distribution, a crucial gap for applications like drug discovery, where global principles enable systematic decision-making across thousands of cases. To address these limitations, we propose GCSGNN (Global Counterfactual-based Self-explainable GNN), a unified framework that integrates counterfactual generation into the GNN architecture, encouraging explanations that faithfully reflect the model's decision-making process. Beyond local explanations, GCSGNN discovers shared latent patterns influencing predictions across graphs, yielding global (i.e., model-level) counterfactual explanations. We further introduce an efficient latent-space counterfactual mechanism that avoids the combinatorial complexity of discrete graph manipulation, enabling scalable discovery of generalizable modification patterns. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that GCSGNN outperforms existing methods in generating effective counterfactual explanations. Our code is available at https://github.com/wendyzheng22/gcsgnn.

</details>


### [126] [Loop-Count Schedules in Looped Transformers: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29704)
*Konstantin Kolokolov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 循环迭代次数R的训练调度决定了每次额外前向是"整层"还是"微小修正"，其关键因素是采样区间的下界是否包含R=1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Looped transformer以共享权重重复计算换取参数节省，但训练时循环次数R的调度如何影响各次前向的作用强度与可外推深度尚不清楚。

Method: 在46M–110M参数、2B token的训练阶梯上，对每轮pass加可学习残差门，比较三种调度：R固定为2；R从{1,…,6}均匀采样；R从{2,3,4}采样；并考察复用首轮KV对后续pass的影响。

Result: {2,3,4}调度最优（2.909 nats，对比参数匹配普通模型2.951）；深度加倍仅退化0.038 nats；固定R下残差流方向在超出训练深度后几何收敛但损失发散；复用首轮KV在各类设定下损失0.01–0.03 nats，使逐token迭代一致收缩并将推理缓存减半。

Conclusion: 应使采样下界≥2以保持每次pass的整层强度；循环在等参数下仅恢复约三分之一的真实深度收益；固定R训练并不收敛的既有结论得到细化。

Abstract: Looped transformers apply one block of layers several times with shared weights, trading parameters for repeated computation. We study how the schedule of the loop iteration count R during training determines whether each extra pass acts as a full layer or as a small correction, using a controlled ladder of 46M-110M-parameter models trained on 2B tokens with learned residual gates on every loop pass and three schedules: R fixed to 2, R sampled uniformly from {1,...,6}, and R sampled from {2,3,4}. We find that the lower bound of the sampling range is the decisive choice. If the sampled range includes R=1, so that some training steps run the block only once and compute the loss on that single pass, the gates collapse to 0.1-0.5 and each additional pass becomes a small correction to the first; if the range starts at R=2, so that every training step runs the block at least twice, the gates stay at 0.6-0.9 and each pass keeps the strength of a full layer, while the model can still be run at any R from 2 up to twice the largest trained value with validation loss within a few hundredths of a nat of its best. The {2, 3, 4} schedule gives the best looped model, 2.909 nats against 2.951 for the parameter-matched plain model, and degrades by only 0.038 nats when run at twice its trained depth. We further show that under a fixed R the direction of the residual stream converges geometrically beyond the trained depth while the loss diverges, which refines a recent finding that static-depth training does not converge, and that reusing the first pass's keys and values on later passes costs 0.01-0.03 nats in every regime while making the per-token iteration uniformly contractive and halving the inference cache. The loop recovers about a third of what real depth provides at equal parameters. The code, trained weights, and per-token traces are released.

</details>


### [127] [Learning-Augmented QUBO for Time-Expanded Multi-Commodity Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29711)
*Aikaterini Nikolidaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将时间扩展图上的能量感知多商品流（NP难）建模为QUBO，给出可证保证可行性的显式更紧惩罚系数，并利用分层结构得到树宽界、低键维MPO表示与正能隙结论，此外给出学习增强路由的Hamming距离与一致性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间扩展图上能量感知多商品流刻画分层网络中的动态路由，问题一般NP难；需要将其转化为量子/Ising可求解形式并控制系统规模与可行性，同时为学习增强路由提供理论保证。

Method: 将问题表述为二次无约束二值优化（QUBO），推导可证保证可行性的显式惩罚系数；利用时间扩展图的分层结构证明树宽界（给出以包大小为参数的FPT精确算法）、小键维矩阵乘积算子（MPO）表示，并证明唯一最优实例下Ising哈密顿量退火能隙严格为正且在有界窗口上一致成立；对学习增强路由，用流误差界定预测流与最优流的Hamming距离，并分析“取预测与求解器输出较优者”的混合算法一致性。

Result: 所给惩罚系数约为标准界除以活跃链路数的因子，显著更小；得到树宽界与FPT精确算法、低键维MPO表示、唯一最优时正退火能隙（有界窗口一致）；学习增强方面，Hamming距离受流误差控制，混合算法代价超出最优的倍数随预测误差线性增长。

Conclusion: 结论是对QUBO及其Ising哈密顿量的精确陈述，独立于具体物理实现，因而适用于任意实现在分层结构驱动的算法设计与学习增强路由中提供可证保证。

Abstract: We study energy-aware multi-commodity flow on time-expanded graphs, a problem that captures dynamic routing in layered networks and is NP-hard in general. We formulate the problem as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization problem, give an explicit penalty coefficient that provably guarantees feasibility, and show that it is smaller than the standard bound by a factor of roughly the number of active links. The layered structure of the time-expanded graph yields three structural results: a treewidth bound that gives a fixed-parameter tractable exact algorithm in the bag size, a matrix-product-operator representation of small bond dimension, and a proof that the annealing gap of the associated Ising Hamiltonian is strictly positive for every instance with a unique optimum, uniformly over bounded windows. For learning-augmented routing, we bound the Hamming distance between a predicted and an optimal flow by the flow error, and prove a consistency guarantee: a hybrid algorithm that keeps the better of the prediction and the solver output returns a flow whose cost exceeds the optimum by a factor that grows linearly with the prediction error. The results are exact statements about the QUBO and its associated Ising Hamiltonian, and they hold for any physical implementation.

</details>


### [128] [From States to Dynamics: Detecting Rule Violations in OthelloGPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29715)
*David L. Cao,Sai Sumedh R. Hindupur,Demba Ba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探究OthelloGPT的激活空间是否编码游戏规则，发现线性探针能准确区分合法与非法落子并能区分三类规则违背，且基于激活步长的无监督检测器可高于随机水平识别非法落子，稀疏自编码器潜空间可进一步提升检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明序列模型在游戏走子数据上训练后可编码世界模型，但世界模型本质上是动态的——规则约束状态如何随时间演化。作者因此追问：仅用Othello走子序列训练的OthelloGPT，是否以及如何在激活空间中体现游戏规则。

Method: 将Othello规则及其对应违背划分为三类：在空格落子(R1)、夹住对手棋子(R2)、按轮次行棋(R3)。训练线性探针区分合法与非法走子及三类违背类型；提出基于相邻走子间激活"步长"的无监督规则违背检测器；并进一步在若干稀疏自编码器的潜空间中计算步长以改进检测指标。

Result: 线性探针能准确分离合法与非法走子，并能区分三种规则违背类型；基于激活步长的无监督检测器以高于随机的水平识别非法走子；在稀疏自编码器潜空间中计算步长可进一步提升检测性能。

Conclusion: OthelloGPT在很大程度上遵循Othello游戏规则，且可通过作用于模型激活的结构化检测器识别规则违背，表明其激活空间以可解码的方式表征了游戏的动态规则。

Abstract: Sequence models trained on game moves have been shown to encode world models, with structured linear probes revealing the underlying game states. World models, however, are inherently dynamic: in deterministic board games, a set of rules constrain how states evolve over time. We therefore ask if and how OthelloGPT, a transformer trained only on Othello move sequences, manifests the rules of the game in its activation space. We divide the rules of Othello, and symmetrically their violations, into three classes: placing a disc on an empty square (R1), flanking an opponent's disc (R2), and moving in turn (R3). We find that linear probes accurately separate legal from illegal moves, and also distinguish between the three kinds of rule violations. We introduce an unsupervised rule-violation detector based on the step length of activations between consecutive moves, which identifies illegal moves above chance. We further improve our detection metric by computing step length in the latent space of select sparse autoencoders. Together, we find that OthelloGPT follows game rules of Othello to a large extent, and that we can detect rule violations using structured detectors on model activations.

</details>


### [129] [Randomized Weighted Distance Nearest Neighbor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29720)
*Shayan Shafaei,Naeem Shahabi Sani,Chao Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出随机化加权距离最近邻压缩算法 RWNN，以随机化球搜索替代 WNN 的贪心搜索，将压缩时间降至 WNN 的 0.1%–1%，且保持相近分类精度并继承一致性等理论性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近邻分类器内存效率低，常用解决方案是压缩训练数据。已有的加权距离最近邻（WNN）压缩算法虽理论优雅、分类有效，但其压缩过程需在数据空间中贪心搜索最稠密同质球，计算代价高昂。

Method: 对 WNN 的球搜索过程进行随机化，构造随机变体 RWNN：以随机采样方式替代确定性的贪心最稠密同质球搜索，从而完成训练数据压缩。

Result: 压缩时间大幅降低（常为 WNN 的 0.1%–1%），分类精度与之相近；理论上继承 WNN 的一致性与泛化界等性质；多数据集实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 随机化贪心搜索可在几乎不损失精度与理论保证的前提下显著提升 WNN 压缩效率，是一种高效的原型压缩方案。

Abstract: The nearest neighbor classifier is known to be memory-inefficient, and a popular solution is to compress its training data. Recently, a compression algorithm based on weighted distance nearest neighbor (WNN) was proposed, which has elegant theoretical properties and effective classification performance. However, its compression process is computationally expensive, for it performs a greedy search of the densest homogeneous balls in data space. In this paper, we propose a randomized variant RWNN. It simply randomizes the ball search process, and results in substantially reduced compression time (often 0.1%--1% of WNN's) and similar classification accuracy. RWNN also inherits several theoretical properties of WNN including consistency and a generalization bound. Experimental results on multiple datasets demonstrate its efficacy.

</details>


### [130] [LAF-ECG: Multi-Resolution Lead-Aware Fusion of Time-Series and Language Foundation Models for ECG Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29721)
*Kiana Taktehrani,Maryam Hosseini,Amir Keynia,Aram Montazami,Arash Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LAF-ECG双基础模型框架，通过导联感知接口将冻结TSFM与时序-语言重编程通路融合，提升ECG分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG自动判读多依赖特定任务且label-dependent；LLM与时间序列基础模型（TSFM）表征互补，但因特征空间不匹配及ECG信号导联/尺度依赖结构，二者融合困难。

Method: LAF-ECG在MedTsLLM基础上扩展：引入导联感知接口接入冻结TSFM骨干，保留原重编程通路；捕获多分辨率时序上下文；通过自适应残差集成融合时序表征与语言对齐表征；采用分支特定监督加跨分支对齐。

Result: PTB-XL上准确率71.10%、macro-F1 62.43%；Chapman-Shaoxing上准确率89.73%、macro-F1 65.94%，均优于MedTsLLM。

Conclusion: 融合时序与语言基础表征时保留导联特有时序结构具有显著价值；代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated electrocardiogram (ECG) interpretation has advanced substantially with deep learning, yet many methods remain task-specific and label-dependent. Large language models (LLMs) and time-series foundation models (TSFMs) offer complementary pretrained representations, but integrating them is challenging because of mismatched feature spaces and the lead- and scale-dependent structure of ECG signals. We propose LAF-ECG (Lead-Aware Foundation Fusion for ECG), a dual-foundation framework that extends MedTsLLM with a lead-aware interface to a frozen TSFM backbone while retaining its reprogramming pathway. The proposed framework captures multi-resolution temporal context, fuses temporal and language-aligned ECG representations through adaptive residual integration, and employs branch-specific supervision with cross-branch alignment. LAF-ECG achieves 71.10% accuracy and 62.43% macro-F1 on PTB-XL, and 89.73% accuracy and 65.94% macro-F1 on Chapman-Shaoxing, outperforming MedTsLLM on both datasets. These results highlight the value of preserving lead-specific temporal structure when combining temporal and language foundation representations for ECG classification. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/kkianamm/LAF-ECG.

</details>


### [131] [Deterministic Proper Positive-Only Learning Is Independent of ZFC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29737)
*Shai Ben-David,Farnam Mansouri,Anay Mehrotra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文证明：仅正样本二分类中确定性proper可学习性在ZFC下不可判定——存在VC维为2的类，其确定性proper可学习性独立于ZFC，尽管随机化学习器可学；且无有限特征维度可刻画该性质，可学习性取决于域基数阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅正样本的PAC变体中，非proper可学习性已有教科书式刻画，而proper可学习性数十年悬而未决；近期工作刻画了带随机性的情形，并显示随机性带来本质差别，从而遗留确定性情形的开放问题。

Method: 基于Erdős–Rado划分定理构造新工具，分析确定性proper可学习性与域基数、集合论公理的关系，并考察是否存在有限特征的维度刻画。

Result: 存在VC维为2的类，其确定性proper可学习性独立于ZFC（但可被随机化学习器学习）；无任何有限特征维度可刻画确定性proper可学习性；在基数低于某阈值的域（含所有可数域）上该性质在ZFC中有组合刻画，而在足够大基数域上失效；GCH下该阈值是紧的。

Conclusion: 确定性proper可学习性本质依赖域基数与集合论公理，不能由标准维度刻画；域大小是决定性因素，阈值的紧性在GCH下成立。

Abstract: Binary classification from positive-only samples is a variant of PAC learning in which the learner receives i.i.d. samples only from the positive region of an unknown target concept, but is evaluated on the full distribution. Improper learnability in this model has a textbook characterization, whereas proper learnability has remained open for decades. Recent work characterized it for learners with access to randomness, and showed that, unlike in PAC learning, some classes are properly learnable with randomness but not without it, leaving the deterministic case open. We show that this case cannot be resolved within the standard axioms of mathematics (ZFC): there is a class of VC dimension two whose deterministic proper learnability is independent of ZFC, even though a randomized learner provably learns it. Moreover, no dimension of finite character, a desirable property shared by most dimensions in learning theory, characterizes deterministic proper learnability. We also show that the size of the domain is decisive. In ZFC, deterministic proper learnability has a combinatorial characterization on every domain whose cardinality is below a certain threshold, including every countable domain. In contrast, this characterization fails on every domain of sufficiently large cardinality. Under the generalized continuum hypothesis, this threshold is sharp: it is the exact cardinality at which the characterization starts to fail. Finally, our proofs introduce new tools, based on the Erdos--Rado partition theorem, that may be of broader interest.

</details>


### [132] [Why Units Die in the Layer After a Frozen Random Encoder, and What the Number of Live Units Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29752)
*Scott M. Norton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现随机编码器后层神经元死亡源于输入"偏移量"（均值/变异比）过大；据此纠正了"活神经元数量可衡量游戏复杂度"的旧结论，并建议在固定前端后测量偏移量、若过大则减去输入均值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作用编码器后层"存活ReLU单元数"衡量Atari游戏复杂度，但该指标准确性存疑。作者欲探究单元死亡的真正原因，并修正这一结论。

Method: 定义"偏移量（offset）"为层输入相对零点的距离与其变异程度之比；在已训练智能体、图像分类器、储备池计算与GPT-2稀疏自编码器中检验该机制；用随机编码器与解析公式预测中位偏移量（44个案例）；在Pong与Breakout中做减去平均屏幕的干预实验。

Result: 44个案例中公式预测中位偏移量误差在25%以内；Pong中减均值使存活单元由3–6/64升至59–63/64；无干预时50M步内无一智能体达到18分，减均值后均在约5M步内达到，速度接近训练编码器；Breakout中存活单元增多但平均分数无明显提升，与训练编码器仍有较大差距。

Conclusion: 存活单元数不能反映游戏复杂度；偏移量是单元死亡的关键因素。建议在固定前端、训练层之前测量偏移量，若偏大则尝试减去输入均值；但证据仅来自小网络与三款游戏，且干预同时改变了输入幅度。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning agents can learn Atari games through a convolutional encoder whose random weights are never trained. They reach expert play in Pong and, without random action repeats, in Breakout, but training takes far longer and few ReLU units in the layer after the encoder stay alive. A dead unit outputs zero for every input and cannot learn. In earlier work we took the number of live units to measure the game's complexity. Here we ask why units die and correct that conclusion. We find that more units die as the layer's inputs lie farther from zero relative to how much they vary. We call this ratio the offset. Earlier work showed this in sparse autoencoders at the start of training. We saw it after training in agents, image classifiers, a reservoir computer and sparse autoencoders on GPT-2. A random encoder leaves the offset of its input roughly unchanged, and a formula predicted its median offset within 25 percent in all 44 cases. So a game with a bright, mostly unchanging screen gives the next layer a large offset. In Pong, subtracting the average screen raised the number of live units from between 3 and 6 of 64 to between 59 and 63, so the number alone cannot show a game's complexity. On unaltered screens none of the agents reached a training score of 18 in 50 million steps. With the subtraction all did within 5 million, about as fast as a trained encoder on unaltered screens. In Breakout the subtraction left more units alive, but average training scores did not clearly improve, and a large gap to a trained encoder remained. So we do not treat their number as something to raise for its own sake. The evidence is from small networks and three games, and in agents and image classifiers changing the offset also changed the size of the inputs. We suggest measuring the offset before training a layer on a fixed front end and, if it is large, trying to subtract the mean input.

</details>


### [133] [What Should World Models Plan Over? Learning Geometry-Aligned Representations of Body Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29759)
*Renhao Zhang,Abhranil Chandra,Bruno Castro da Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MotionPort 用低维身体运动潜表征替代动作块表征，并按运动相似性组织搜索空间，使预测改进可转化为可执行的成功轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型规划器通常在“为预测而学习的动作表征”上搜索，但作者指出预测精度本身并不能保证优化能找到可执行的成功轨迹，预测与规划之间存在不匹配。

Method: 学习身体轨迹（body motion）的低维潜表征而非动作块表征；以运动相似性组织规划搜索空间，使规划器在运动空间中优化，从而缓解预测精度与执行可行性之间的错位；无需微调状态编码器。

Result: 在四个领域、七种设置、三个骨干网络上，将平均目标达成成功率从 37.9% 提升至 87.8%；在 AntMaze-OGBench 任务上达到 72% 成功率，而最强基线仅 4%。

Conclusion: 以运动轨迹为中心的表征与相似性驱动的搜索空间组织，是实现准确动力学预测与有效长程规划的关键，且具备跨骨干、跨域的可迁移性。

Abstract: We introduce MotionPort, which learns low-dimensional latent representations of body motion for accurate dynamics prediction and effective long-horizon planning. Existing world-model planners often search over action representations learned for prediction, yet we show that predictive accuracy alone does not ensure that optimization can discover successful trajectories. MotionPort addresses this mismatch by representing body trajectories rather than action chunks and organizing the search space by motion similarity, helping predicted improvements translate into successful execution. Without fine-tuning state encoders, MotionPort raises average goal-reaching success from 37.9% to 87.8% across three backbones and seven settings in four domains. On the evaluated AntMaze-OGBench tasks, it achieves 72% success, compared with 4% for the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [134] [probly: Uncertainty-Aware Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29762)
*Paul Hofman,Clemens Damke,Timo Löhr,Santo M. A. R. Thies,Alireza Javanmardi,Valentin Margraf,Jakub Paplham,Yusuf Sale,Eyke Hüllermeier,Maximilian Muschalik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Python 包 probly，在一个模块化框架内统一了不确定性表示、量化与评估：提供将现有模型转化为不确定性感知模型的轻量变换（支持 PyTorch、scikit-learn、Flax），支持二阶分布、credal 集与共形预测等表示及相应度量与评估协议，并在选择性预测、OOD 检测、主动学习三个标准任务以及一阶数据任务上进行了基准研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习系统在真实场景中部署，不确定性的表示与量化已从方法学边角问题变为核心议题。但实践中让模型具备不确定性感知仍然困难：相关工具分散在不同库中，各自绑定特定框架与特定不确定性建模方式，而表示、量化与评估的选择通常完全交由用户，缺乏指导。

Method: 提出统一、模块化的 Python 包 probly，包含四个可组合组件：(i) 将已有模型转为不确定性感知模型的轻量变换，当前支持 PyTorch、scikit-learn 与 Flax；(ii) 多种不确定性表示，包括二阶分布、credal 集与共形预测；(iii) 对应的量化度量；(iv) 若干可近乎任意组合的评估协议。

Result: 基于 probly 在三个标准任务（选择性预测、OOD 检测、主动学习）上开展基准研究；与现有基准不同，额外在一阶数据（目标本身为结果上的分布）上评估适用方法；并通过大语言模型、图神经网络、数据流等非标准案例展示了包的灵活性。

Conclusion: probly 将分散的不确定性工具整合为单一模块化框架，实现了表示、量化与评估的自由组合，包已在 https://github.com/pwhofman/probly 公开可用。

Abstract: As machine learning systems are increasingly deployed in real-world applications, the question of how to represent and quantify uncertainty has moved from a methodological side issue to a central concern. In practice, however, making a model uncertainty-aware is still surprisingly difficult: relevant tools tend to be scattered across libraries, each tied to a particular framework and a particular approach to modeling uncertainty, and the choice of representation, quantification, and evaluation is typically left to the user without much guidance. In this paper, we present probly, a Python package that addresses these issues in a single, modular framework. probly offers (i) lightweight transformations that turn existing models into uncertainty-aware ones, currently supporting PyTorch, scikit-learn, and Flax, (ii) several representations, including second-order distributions, credal sets, and conformal prediction, (iii) the corresponding quantification measures, and (iv) a number of evaluation protocols, which can be combined more or less arbitrarily. Using probly, we conduct a benchmark study on three standard tasks --- selective prediction, out-of-distribution detection, and active learning. In addition, and unlike existing benchmarks, we evaluate suitable methods on first-order data, i.e., datasets for which the target itself is a distribution over outcomes. We further illustrate the flexibility of the package on a number of less standard case studies, including large language models, graph neural networks, and data streams. The package is publicly available at https://github.com/pwhofman/probly.

</details>


### [135] [Selecting Stable Sampling Dynamics for Compositional Diffusion: A Feynman-Kac Weight Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29764)
*Sunjin Kim,Sungsoo Ahn,Michael Chertkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多个单独训练的扩散模型，研究如何在Feynman–Kac加权扩散族中选择提议动力学，以稳定地从专家终端分布的β_k加权乘积中采样，并证明标量参数λ=1/B是结构性最优选择（Feynman–Kac reduction）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Feynman–Kac构造可给出具有正确目标边缘分布的多种加权SDE，但如何在其中选择有用的提议动力学尚不清楚；直接联合重训练专家代价高，需要无需重训的可组合采样方案与选择准则。

Method: 构造实现同一"中间专家乘积"轨迹的显式加权扩散族：标量λ控制提议漂移跟随组合专家分数的强度，Langevin分量可独立调节。记B=Σβ_k，分析瞬时Feynman–Kac对数权重率中聚合平方分数项与分数不一致项的行为；在界发散假设及紧支撑数据下的线性扩散协议中推导上界与双侧界。

Result: 在λ=1/B处，聚合平方分数项在瞬时Feynman–Kac对数权重率中精确抵消，剩余分数不一致项在正指数下非正，称为Feynman–Kac reduction；有界发散假设下对所有λ≤1/B给出瞬时未归一化对数权重率上界；线性扩散协议、紧支撑专家数据下λ=1/B对任意符号指数在每个非终端时刻给出双侧界，单专家情形亦有对应结果。

Conclusion: 在指定族内，λ=1/B在统一最坏情形意义下是唯一的最优选择；但高斯混合与分子采样实验显示，经验最优参数未必与解析预测一致，稳定性界与实际采样质量之间存在差距。

Abstract: Given $K$ separately trained diffusion models, how should one sample from a $β_k$-weighted product of their terminal laws without jointly retraining the experts? Existing Feynman--Kac constructions provide many weighted stochastic differential equations (SDEs) with the correct target marginal. The consequential question is how to select useful proposal dynamics among them. We study an explicit family of weighted diffusions that realizes the same product-of-intermediate-experts trajectory. A scalar parameter $λ$ controls how strongly the proposal drift follows the composed expert score, while an additional Langevin component can be varied independently. Writing $B=\sum_{k=1}^{K}β_k>0$, we identify the distinguished choice $λ=1/B$. At this value, the aggregate squared-score term in the instantaneous Feynman--Kac log-weight rate cancels exactly. For positive expert exponents, the remaining score-disagreement contribution is non-positive. We call this structural cancellation Feynman--Kac reduction. Under bounded-divergence assumptions, it yields an upper bound on the instantaneous unnormalized log-weight rate for all $λ\leq 1/B$. For linear diffusion protocols with compactly supported expert data, $λ=1/B$ yields two-sided bounds at every nonterminal time, for exponents of either sign. A corresponding result holds for a single expert under the same divergence assumption. In these two-sided settings, the selected value is unique within the specified family in a universal worst-case sense. Experiments on Gaussian mixtures and molecular sampling compare the analytically predicted stability with actual sampling quality, including instances in which parameter choices different from $λ=1/B$ perform better empirically.

</details>


### [136] [When Simpler Beats Learned: Dual-Weighted Sampling vs. Machine Learning for the Pricing Subproblem in Graph Coloring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29769)
*Nikolai Antonov,Přemysl Šůcha,Mikoláš Janota,Jan Hůla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Dual-Weighted Sampling，用限制主问题对偶解替代机器学习评分进行独立集采样，作为分支定价中最大权独立集定价的轻量替代方案，在统一框架下于运行时间与解质量上超过 ML 定价启发式、手工启发式与精确求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图着色可由分支定价（B&P）求最优，但性能取决于列生成（CG）中每次迭代求解最大权独立集（MWIS）定价问题。当前最强的机器学习定价启发式（MLPH）采用"预测-采样"范式，需特征工程与训练；作者观察到评分过程本身并不需要学习，因此质疑 ML 的必要性并寻求更轻量的替代。

Method: 在统一的 CG 与 B&P 框架中仅替换定价 oracle，将学习得到的顶点分数替换为受限主问题（RMP）的对偶解，据此构造分布并进行独立集采样，形成无需特征工程、无需训练的 Dual-Weighted Sampling。

Result: 在标准图着色基准上，Dual-Weighted Sampling 在运行时间与解质量两方面均优于 MLPH、手工启发式与精确求解器；在 MLPH 原评估中表现最强的 DIMACS 随机族上仍具竞争力，并在递归稀疏族与真实世界图族上实现 CG 运行时间两倍以上的加速。

Conclusion: 定价中的启发式评分不必依赖机器学习，RMP 对偶信息即可提供同等或更优的引导；简洁的对偶加权采样是 ML 定价启发式的有力替代，提示应审慎评估 ML 在该环节中的实际贡献。

Abstract: The graph coloring problem can be solved to optimality by branch-and-price (B&P), but its performance hinges on the column generation (CG) step, where each iteration requires solving a maximum weight independent set (MWIS) pricing problem. The state-of-the-art machine-learning (ML) pricing heuristic follows a prediction-sampling paradigm: a learned model scores each vertex of a graph, and independent sets are sampled from the induced distribution. We observe, however, that the scoring procedure does not need to be learned, and critically assess the role of ML by replacing the learned scores with the dual solution of the restricted master problem, resulting in Dual-Weighted Sampling, a proposed lightweight alternative that requires no feature engineering or training. Within a unified CG and B&P framework that varies only the pricing oracle, Dual-Weighted Sampling outperforms MLPH, hand-crafted heuristics, and exact solvers on the established graph coloring benchmark in both runtime and solution quality. In particular, it remains competitive on the DIMACS random family, where MLPH was shown strongest in the original evaluation, while achieving more than a two-fold speedup in CG runtime on recursive-sparse and real-world graph families.

</details>


### [137] [Constrained Diffusion from Binary Evaluations via Learned Doob h-Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29779)
*Ssaumya Kammal Jaiswal,Mahfuza Farooque*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于Doob h-变换的约束生成框架：仅用干净样本的二值可行性评估，通过前向加噪标签的平方误差回归学习"噪声样本最终落入约束集的概率"这一函数，冻结基座模型、每条约束训练一个轻量头，实现精确（非归一化）引导，在CIFAR-10、QM9分子属性过滤与Stable Diffusion 1.5上显著提升约束满足率且成本远低于拒绝采样/无导数选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多约束（如分子属性筛选）只能在完整样本上检验，无法提供可微测量算子。现有扩散模型引导方法依赖可微测量、成对条件数据或每步基座模型rollout，难以适用；且常见归一到采样的引导丢弃了概率因子与步长权重，导致约束满足受损。

Method: 以Doob h-变换为理论基础，将条件生成化归为学习单一函数h：噪声样本最终去噪落入约束集的概率。仅用干净样本的二值可行性标签，经前向加噪后在含噪状态上做平方误差回归；基座模型冻结，每条约束训练一个轻量预测头，噪声状态分类器引导作为特例出现。引导采用精确形式：在约束信号可证消失处衰减，并携带样本逆概率因子（而非归一化引导）。

Result: 固定噪声日程与平均步长、FID可比条件下，精确引导较归一化引导在CIFAR-10上约束满足率高17.0–18.5个百分点（多个独立训练的估计器一致）。在1.7%基率下，每满足样本成本比拒绝采样低50倍以上；QM9上Lipinski型属性过滤器通过率54.3%→96.8%；Stable Diffusion 1.5满足率7.2%→60.0%，成本仅为无导数选择（9.0%）的1/16。

Conclusion: 干净样本的二值评估即可作为约束生成的充分监督；该框架在像素空间、潜空间与分子扩散任务中通用有效，为不可微约束下的扩散条件生成提供了理论清晰、低成本且高满足率的方案。

Abstract: Conditional generation under constraints that can only be checked on a finished sample, such as a molecular property filter, remains challenging for diffusion models: most guidance methods require differentiable measurement operators, paired conditional data, or rollouts of the base model at every step. We present a framework grounded in the Doob $h$-transform that reduces conditional generation to learning a single function, the probability that a noisy sample denoises into the constraint set. We learn this function from binary clean-sample evaluations alone, by squared-error regression on forward-noised labels; the base model is frozen, one lightweight head is trained per constraint, and noisy-state classifier guidance arises as a special case. The exact guidance step fades where the constraint signal provably vanishes and carries a factor of the inverse probability across samples, which normalized guidance discards: with the schedule and mean step size held fixed, equalizing the step costs 17.0 to 18.5 points of constraint satisfaction on CIFAR-10 at comparable FID, across independently trained estimators. Guidance is more than 50$\times$ cheaper per satisfying sample than rejection sampling at a 1.7% base rate, raises a Lipinski-type property filter on QM9 from 54.3% to 96.8%, and lifts satisfaction on Stable Diffusion 1.5 from 7.2% to 60.0% at one sixteenth of the cost of derivative-free selection, which reaches 9.0%. These results demonstrate that binary evaluations of clean samples are sufficient supervision for constrained generation across pixel, latent, and molecular diffusion.

</details>


### [138] [Learning Gaussian Process Priors with Linear Semigroups and Kinetic Regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29796)
*Angela Lavinia Varazi,Felipe Tobar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于线性半群构造高斯过程先验的方法：以线性半群将 t=0 的源GP演化为 t=1 的先验，使回归计算可行；半群由生成元参数化，通过边际似然与动能正则两项竞争目标学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GP先验（如核函数选择）多为人工设定，缺乏从数据中系统性构造先验的可计算框架；作者希望用半群演化算子把"先验构造"转化为可学习、可计算的轨迹问题，并借用最优传输与卷积模型的视角。

Method: 线性半群作用于源GP，线性性保持高斯性，可解析刻画协方差演化并联系最优传输、卷积模型。半群由其生成元参数化，学习采用两个竞争目标：边际似然（拟合观测）与动能正则（偏好低能量轨迹、限制相对源GP的多余质量位移）。

Result: 在独立GP采样函数上的实验表明：训练函数足够多时，学到的先验对留出函数预测准确；动能正则显著降低生成轨迹的能量，且不损害预测精度。

Conclusion: 半群参数化的GP先验是可学习且计算可行的，边际似然决定预测能力而动能正则控制轨迹复杂度，二者可解耦；该框架为数据驱动的先验构造提供了与最优传输相通的统一视角。

Abstract: We introduce a semigroup-based construction of Gaussian process (GP) priors. A linear semigroup transforms a source GP at $t=0$ into a prior at $t=1$, enabling computationally tractable regression. Linearity preserves Gaussianity, allowing us to characterise the evolution of the covariance and draw connections with optimal transport and convolution models. The semigroup is parametrised by its generator, which is learned through two competing objectives: the marginal likelihood encourages agreement with observations, while a kinetic-energy regulariser favours lower-energy trajectories and limits unnecessary mass displacement from the source GP. Experiments on independent GP draws show that, with sufficiently many training functions, the learned prior provides accurate predictions on held-out functions, while kinetic regularisation substantially reduces the energy of the generated trajectories without compromising prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [139] [Information-Theoretic Analysis of Multi-Objective Bandits: Pareto Violations & Gap-Based Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29808)
*Muhammad Qasim Elahi,Murat Kocaoglu,Mahsa Ghasemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究贝叶斯多目标 bandit 的信息论问题：实现低遗憾需要"解决"环境的哪些方面。作者发现答案依赖于遗憾准则的信息需求层次：Pareto 违背遗憾可由集合级信息控制，而基于 gap 的 Pareto 遗憾需要更精细的奖励几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标 bandit 中"低遗憾需要对环境掌握何种信息"这一信息论问题尚未被系统刻画；不同遗憾准则（违背型 vs. gap 型）对信息的需求可能本质不同，需要建立分层的信息需求理论。

Method: 1) 针对 Pareto 违背遗憾，提出竞争候选 Pareto 集之间的预测分离度（predictive separation）度量，并将其与关于未知集合的信息增益相联系；据此分析 Thompson Sampling 并设计 Information-Directed Sampling（IDS）。2) 针对 gap 型 Pareto 遗憾，指出 Pareto 集信息过粗（同一 Pareto 集可对应不同奖励几何与 gap，且集内平均不保持 Pareto 支配），转而开展参数级信息论分析，为 TS 与 IDS 推导 gap 遗憾界。

Result: 在一致分离与多项式衰减分离下建立次线性贝叶斯遗憾；并证明弱分离会带来不可避免的统计代价的下界。在次线性信息预算下，Pareto Thompson Sampling 同时获得次线性 gap 遗憾并在同一次运行中恢复完整 Pareto 集，对基于 gap 的 IDS 固定混合亦有相应保证。

Conclusion: 结果揭示信息需求的分层结构：二元 Pareto 归属可通过集合级信息控制，而定量 Pareto-gap 次优性可能需要更精细的奖励几何知识。
