We initially pretrain the mapping by flow matching using precomputed optimal transport maps as targets and then continue its training using combined geometry and CFD objectives. We apply it to unseen geometries in a single forward pass. The learned flow deforms the surface points, and we project its endpoints onto the sphere. We then splat geometric features onto the spherical grid, apply a neural operator (we tested both Fourier and Spherical Fourier Neural Operators), and interpolate its output back to the surface. For a mesh with $N$ points, evaluating the map costs O(N), and the full prediction costs O(NlogN) with the FNO backbone. We evaluate surface pressure prediction on ShapeNet-Car, AhmedML, and DrivAerNet++, and drag coefficient prediction on DrivAerNet++. Across the three pressure benchmarks, our accuracy is on par with that of the most accurate baseline. On a surface with one million points, our method achieves a 354x end-to-end inference speedup over that baseline while keeping the same memory usage asymptotics of O(N).

</details>


### [172] [Toward a Theoretical Understanding of Transformer Attention: Bayesian Estimation and Predictive Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30349)
*Fouad B. Chedid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理已有的概率与信息论结果，给出Transformer注意力的理论解释：明确注意力计算后验均值所依赖的假设，以及准确预测下一词元所需的附加条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于注意力"后验均值最优性"的结论常被直接引申为预测能力保证，混淆了表示质量与预测头正确使用信息这两个环节，缺乏对二者界限的精确刻画。

Method: 理论分析/结果综述：整合概率与信息论已有结论，辨析平方误差损失下后验均值最优性成立的前提；引入超额对数损失（excess-log-loss）分解，将表示的信息损失与预测头误差分离。

Result: 指出后验均值最优性本身不足以确立预测充分性；即便表示是充分的，仍需能正确利用其信息的预测头，并用分解形式量化表示信息损失与预测头误差两部分。

Conclusion: 澄清了获得预测保证所需的条件；后续需确定训练后模型中这些条件何时成立，以及架构与训练选择如何促成这些条件，从而指导更有效保留并利用预测信息的AI系统设计。

Abstract: This paper develops a theoretical account of Transformer attention by organizing existing probabilistic and information-theoretic results. It identifies the assumptions under which attention computes a posterior mean and the additional conditions required for accurate next-token prediction. Posterior-mean optimality under squared-error loss does not by itself establish predictive sufficiency; even a sufficient representation requires a prediction head that uses its information correctly. An excess-log-loss decomposition makes this distinction precise by separating representation information loss from prediction-head error. The analysis clarifies the conditions required for predictive guarantees. Determining when these conditions hold in trained models, and how architectural or training choices can support them, could guide the development of AI systems that preserve and use predictive information more effectively.

</details>


### [173] [Low-Rank Tensor Representations for Efficient Multimarginal Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30376)
*Johann Cl{é}ment-Cottuz,Gilles Gasso,Maxime Berar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出低秩多边际最优传输（LRMOT），通过将传输方案限制为有界多线性秩的张量，获得紧凑结构表示，从而在低内存下高效求解多边际最优传输问题，并给出了近似界与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多边际最优传输（MOT）的传输方案以高维张量表示，其计算与存储代价随边际数量呈指数增长，制约了其可扩展性。

Method: 将传输方案约束为具有有界多线性秩（multilinear rank）的张量，即采用低秩张量分解形式的耦合表示；基于该结构设计低内存开销的高效算法，并对所得低秩形式建立近似误差界。

Result: 理论方面给出了低秩形式的近似界；实验方面表明所提算法显著缩短计算时间，同时所得解的传输代价保持在较低水平。

Conclusion: 将低秩张量结构引入MOT可有效缓解维度灾难，在计算效率与解质量之间取得良好平衡，为大规模多边际耦合问题提供了可行的近似求解框架。

Abstract: Multimarginal Optimal Transport (MOT) suffers from exponential computational and memory costs in the number of marginals, as transport plans are represented by high-dimensional tensors. We introduce Low-Rank Multimarginal Optimal Transport (LRMOT), which restricts transport plans to tensors with bounded multilinear rank, providing a compact and structured representation of multimarginal couplings. This structure enables efficient algorithms with a low memory footprint. We establish approximation bounds for the resulting low-rank formulations and demonstrate experimentally that the proposed algorithms substantially reduce computational time while achieving solutions with low transport cost.

</details>


### [174] [IP-GNO: An Inductive Physics-Informed Graph Neural Operator for Automated Distribution System State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30387)
*Sakshi Kumar,Chhavi Jhari,Ajay Singh,Subho Paul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出归纳式物理知情图神经算子（IP-GNO），将配电网状态估计（DSSE）建模为算子学习问题，实现跨网络规模与拓扑的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新型配电网节点注入波动导致电压波动与运行不确定性加剧，DSSE成为实时监测与控制的关键工具；但现有GNN方法在不同规模、拓扑网络间泛化性差，难以实现跨电网零样本迁移。

Method: 受节点导纳矩阵逆的Neumann级数展开启发，将母线电压估计构建为算子学习问题；设计导纳参数化核（零阶项含自导纳门控、层间含持续源注入跳连）刻画电气依赖；提出物理嵌入树编码（PETE），由路径阻抗与直流线性化相角先验生成五个规模不变的位置特征；输出采用正余弦形式以处理相角周期性并稳定训练。

Result: 在两个小型（IEEE 33、69节点）、两个中型（IEEE 123、136节点）及一个大型（2522节点）配电网评估；仅用IEEE 33节点训练、不重训不微调即在四个不同规模未见网络零样本评估，与现有SOTA DSSE工具对比显示优越性。

Conclusion: IP-GNO具备可扩展性与跨异质配电网的迁移能力，可支撑规模化DSSE应用。

Abstract: Distribution System State Estimation (DSSE) is a crucial tool for real-time monitoring, operation, and control of the new-age distribution systems (DSs) due to intensified voltage fluctuations and operational uncertainties introduced by volatile power injection at the nodes. Existing GNN-based approaches generally exhibit limited generalization across networks with different sizes and topologies, making zero-shot cross-grid transfer to unseen DSs a significant challenge. To overcome the aforementioned challenges, this article proposes an Inductive Physics-Informed Graph Neural Operator (IP-GNO) for scalable and transferable DSSE. The proposed framework frames complex bus voltage estimation as an operator-learning problem inspired by the Neumann series expansion of the inverse bus admittance matrix. An admittance-parameterized kernel, incorporating a self-admittance gate in the zeroth-order term and a persistent source-injection skip connection across network layers, captures the underlying electrical dependencies. Additionally, a Physics-Embedded Tree Encoding (PETE) scheme introduces five grid-size-invariant positional features derived from path impedance and a DC-linearized phase-angle prior. A sine-cosine output formulation is further employed to address phase-angle periodicity and ensure stable training. The proposed IP-GNO module is evaluated on two small (IEEE 33- and 69-bus), two medium (IEEE 123- and 136-bus), and one large (2522-bus) sized DSs. Particularly, its zero-shot cross-grid generalization capability is investigated by training the model exclusively on the IEEE 33-bus system and directly evaluating it on four unseen distribution networks of different sizes without retraining or fine-tuning. A detailed comparative analysis with the existing state-of-the-art DSSE tools proves the superiority of IP-GNO, enabling scalable DSSE across heterogeneous DSs.

</details>


### [175] [Time Series Foundation Models on the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30404)
*Marco Pichler,Patrick Podest,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文考察时间序列基础模型(TSFM)在边缘设备上做增量预测刷新时的可行性，提出将因果模型状态跨刷新持久化以避免全量重算，并在九种配置中验证仅TiRex-2同时满足快速刷新、恒定延迟/内存与长历史精度保持三项要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中一旦有新观测到达就必须刷新预测，而隐私与延迟约束常迫使刷新在资源受限的边缘设备上完成。作为边缘预测系统，TSFM须满足三点：快速刷新、随流增长仍保持延迟与峰值内存有界、随历史累积仍保持精度。既有做法缺乏对这三项要求的系统评估。

Method: 从时间因果性切入分析：非因果模型每次更新都需全量重算；因果注意力可用KV缓存，但随历史线性增长；因果循环模型以常数时间与内存更新固定大小状态。作者对所有因果架构使状态跨刷新持久化，并验证其与全量重算的等价性。在单变量预测任务上评估九种TSFM配置，测量精度随上下文长度的变化、单次刷新延迟，以及在服务器级GPU与三种边缘设备上长达一百万步流的延迟与内存。

Result: 没有模型能在平均精度上超越其训练上下文长度，多数在达到完整历史前精度即下降；KV缓存的延迟与内存随保留历史线性增长。九种配置中仅TiRex-2满足全部三项要求：GPU上刷新14 ms、树莓派5上120 ms，在一百万步上延迟、能耗与峰值内存保持恒定，且在未截断历史上保持精度。

Conclusion: 因果循环架构的状态持久化是边缘端流式增量预测的关键使能因素；KV缓存式因果注意力不适合长流场景。TiRex-2证明了在百万步量级流式刷新下同时达成低延迟、有界内存与精度保持的可行性。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) forecast zero-shot with accuracy exceeding that of task-specific models, and the smaller ones fit on edge devices. Operational deployments must refresh their forecast as new observations arrive, and privacy and latency requirements often force this refresh onto resource-constrained edge devices. To serve as a forecasting system there, a TSFM must refresh quickly, keep latency and peak memory bounded as the stream grows, and retain its accuracy as the observed history accumulates. Refreshing incrementally without altering the forecast requires temporal causality: non-causal models recompute at every update, causal attention admits a KV cache that grows linearly, and causal recurrent models update a fixed-size state in constant time and memory. We make these states persist across refreshes for every causal architecture and verify equivalence to full recomputation. We evaluate nine TSFM configurations on univariate forecasting against all three requirements, measuring accuracy as a function of context length, single-refresh latency, and latency and memory on streams of up to one million time steps on a server-grade GPU and three edge devices. No model gains on average accuracy beyond its trained context and most lose it well before the full history is reached, while KV caches leave latency and memory growing linearly with the retained history. TiRex-2 is the only one of the nine configurations meeting all three requirements: it refreshes in 14 ms on the GPU and 120 ms on a Raspberry Pi 5, keeps latency, energy and peak memory constant over a million steps, and retains its accuracy on the untruncated history.

</details>


### [176] [Does coverage explain core-set active learning? Mean, max, and the choice of distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30450)
*Ferdinand Equilbey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视core-set selection的泛化界，指出其证明仅需标注点的零条件风险假设，并给出不依赖类别数C的更紧界；尺度分析与实验表明半径无法一致地对选择进行排序，且不使用池标签的界在图像池上从不具有信息性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Core-set selection将批量主动学习视为覆盖问题，以池的覆盖半径控制池损失与标注样本损失之差的泛化界。作者希望厘清该界实际依赖哪些假设、在实践中究竟能说明什么，尤其是其常数与尺度是否具有信息性。

Method: 首先以证明实际所需的假设（标注点处零条件风险）重新陈述该界，证明其适用于任意相异度，以及任何不使用样本外标签的选择规则。证明逐点进行，涉及两个平均量：池到标注样本的平均距离，以及标注点所附单元格的纯度。随后在确定性标签及全池Lipschitz回归函数的设定下做尺度比较。最后提出错位准则（misalignment criterion）衡量某量是否与gap或测试误差的排序一致，并在高斯池、digits与MNIST（八种距离、每轮重训网络）上实验。

Result: 保留两个平均量后，在标注点标签确定时可得到不设零损失假设、也不含原常数中类别数C因子的池损失界。尺度比较显示：含原常数时，界只有在半径小于类间最小距离除以C时才可能具信息性；去掉C后，半径形式的界只有在基于样本的最近邻规则在池上完全正确时才具信息性。实验表明半径对选择的排序与gap不一致；在图像池上，不使用池标签的界从不具信息性；以均值而非最大值覆盖（如greedy k-medoids）相比随机采样获得更高纯度与准确率。

Conclusion: Core-set的覆盖半径界在实践中条件苛刻、常数导致其鲜具信息性，且半径并不能可靠指示选择质量；基于均值覆盖的贪心k-medoids是更可取的选择策略。

Abstract: Core-set selection casts batch active learning as a covering problem, in which the points to label minimise the covering radius of the pool. Its main argument is a generalization bound in which this radius controls the gap between the loss on the pool and on the labelled sample. We examine what this bound requires and what it says in practice. We first state it with the assumption that its proof uses at the labelled points, a zero conditional risk, and show that it then holds for any dissimilarity and any selection rule that uses no label outside the sample. The proof works point by point and involves two averages, the mean distance of the pool to the labelled sample and the purity of the cells attached to the labelled points. Keeping them gives, when labels are deterministic at the labelled points, a bound on the pool loss with no zero-loss assumption and without the factor C, the number of classes, of the original constant. We then compare scales, for deterministic labels and a regression function Lipschitz on the whole pool. With the original constant, the bound can be informative only if the radius is below the smallest distance between two classes divided by C. Without C, its radius form can be informative only once the nearest-neighbour rule built on the sample is exact on the pool. Finally, a misalignment criterion measures whether a quantity ranks selections as the gap or the test error do. Experiments on a Gaussian pool and on two image pools, digits and MNIST, the latter under eight distances, and with a network retrained at each cycle, show that the radius does not rank selections consistently with the gap and, on the image pools, that the bounds that do not use the labels of the pool are never informative and that covering in mean rather than in maximum, as greedy k-medoids does, gives a higher purity and accuracy than random sampling.

</details>


### [177] [SGD Selects Initialization-Dependent Representation in Overparameterized ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30467)
*Nikos Tsikouras,Yorgos Pantis,Ahmed Mehdi Inane,Mehrab Hamidi,Ioannis Mitliagkas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究有限宽度、无偏置两层ReLU网络在高斯输入下学习线性教师时，联合训练两层、批大小为1的SGD从足够小初始化出发，可将每个神经元驱动到接近两个教师方向之一，并在有限时间内达到任意小的总体误差，且无需显式正则化或逐层训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化网络可用多种隐藏配置表示同一函数，需弄清训练算法偏好哪种表示。宽度m≥4时存在无穷多个非对齐的精确拟合，作者要考察SGD在这种模糊性下会选择何种内部表示。

Method: 理论分析两层、无偏置、有限宽度ReLU网络，输入为高斯分布，目标是线性教师；采用批大小为1的联合SGD，从足够小的初始化出发，证明神经元向教师方向对齐、总体误差趋于零、以及由初始化决定的函数在神经元间的分配选择；并用数值实验验证。

Result: 证明在任意宽度m≥4下，SGD使每个神经元任意接近两个教师方向之一，并在有限时间达到任意小的总体误差；该过程不需要显式正则化、逐层训练、梯度裁剪或学习率调度。动态还选择了依赖初始化的函数分配；数值实验复现了对齐、拟合与初始化相关分配。

Conclusion: 给出了标准SGD在过参数化造成无穷多定性不同精确表示时，仍表现出强烈隐式偏置、趋向高度结构化内部表示的充分条件；并细化了SGD所选择的表示的刻画。

Abstract: Overparameterized neural networks can represent the same function through many different hidden configurations, raising the question of which representation is favored by a given training algorithm. We study how stochastic gradient descent (SGD), jointly training both layers, resolves this ambiguity in finite-width, bias-free, two-layer ReLU networks trained to learn a linear teacher under Gaussian inputs. At width two, exact fitting leaves no choice: the neurons must align with the teacher directions. At every width $m \ge 4$, however, infinitely many non-aligned exact fits are possible. Despite this ambiguity, we prove that joint batch-size-one SGD from sufficiently small initialization drives every neuron arbitrarily close to one of the two teacher directions while reaching arbitrarily small population error in finite time. This occurs without explicit regularization, layerwise training, gradient clipping, or a learning-rate schedule. The dynamics additionally select an initialization-dependent allocation of the fitted function across neurons, providing a finer characterization of the representation selected by SGD. Numerical experiments reproduce the predicted alignment, fitting, and initialization-dependent allocation. Our results identify sufficient conditions under which standard SGD exhibits a strong implicit bias toward a highly structured internal representation, even when overparameterization creates infinitely many qualitatively different exact representations.

</details>


### [178] [Policy Invariance Is Not Learning Invariance: Potential-Based Reward Shaping for Navigation with a Limited View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30492)
*Nodirjon Khabibullaev,Rajat Dandekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析基于势函数的奖励塑形(PBRS)在不改变最优策略的前提下如何改变单步奖励，并在PPO智能体上通过预注册实验测量其影响。BFS测地势势函数在LavaCrossing上显著加速学习，而欧氏势函数无效果；非势函数奖励项导致全部失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PBRS 理论上不改变最优策略，但其加速学习的效果取决于势函数的选择以及该保证之外的因素（终端状态规则、势函数常数平移、非势函数奖励的正循环等），这些机制缺乏系统分析。

Method: 从理论上分解 PBRS 对单步奖励的改变：(γ-1)Φ 每步项、终端状态规则、势函数常数平移、非势函数奖励中的正循环。在 MiniGrid 两个导航任务(LavaCrossing、SimpleCrossing)上使用有限自我中心视野的 PPO 智能体进行预注册实验：160 次运行、每条件 10 个种子；比较 BFS 测地势、欧氏势与稀疏基线；视野为 7x7 与 5x5；另设 20 次运行的非势函数奖励条件。

Result: LavaCrossing: BFS 势显著快于稀疏基线与欧氏势(AUC 差 +0.27~+0.39, Holm p 0.007~0.0499)；欧氏势与基线无差异；欧氏势在训练早期导致的熔岩死亡次数超过两倍。20 次非势函数奖励运行中无一学会任务。视野大小未检测到对塑形收益的影响。SimpleCrossing 5x5 视野下部分 BFS 运行陷入循环且从未学习，平移势函数无法消除，说明单独 (γ-1)Φ 项不足以解释。

Conclusion: PBRS 的实际加速效果高度依赖势函数设计：几何测地势有效而欧氏距离无效；非势函数奖励会破坏学习；循环行为等问题不能仅由每步 (γ-1)Φ 项解释，需关注策略-势函数交互等保证之外的因素。

Abstract: Potential-based reward shaping (PBRS) does not change the optimal policy, but how much it speeds up learning depends on the potential and on effects outside this guarantee. We analyse how PBRS changes the one-step reward while it preserves the discounted return: a per-step $(γ-1)Φ$ term, the terminal-state rule, a constant shift of the potential, and positive cycles in a bonus that is not potential-based. We then measure these consequences in pre-registered experiments with a PPO agent. The agent has a limited egocentric view and is tested on two MiniGrid navigation tasks (160 runs, 10 seeds per condition). On LavaCrossing, a geodesic (BFS) potential learned faster than both the sparse baseline and a Euclidean potential with a 7x7 and a 5x5 view (AUC difference +0.27 to +0.39, Holm p between 0.007 and 0.0499). The Euclidean potential did not differ from the baseline. In 20 runs with a non-potential bonus, none of the agents learned the task. The Euclidean potential caused more than twice as many deaths in lava during early training. View size did not detectably change the benefit of shaping. With a 5x5 view on SimpleCrossing, some BFS runs got stuck in loops and never learned; shifting the potential did not remove this, so the per-step term alone does not explain it.

</details>


### [179] [Why Calibrated BM25 Filtering Barely Reduced Context on LongMemEval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30500)
*John Knight,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在长对话检索问答中，将BM25过滤置于"整段装入、按固定顺序跳过放不下的段落"的打包器之前，几乎不节省输入token（75题仅省0.096%，仅改变1个prompt），且不改变模型答对的问题集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要型助手在后续问答中可能需回溯原始对话细节；但只有能装入prompt的段落才能被答题模型看到，因此检索增益受打包上限约束。作者此前已区分"被检索到的证据"与"被送达的证据"，本文检验在计入打包上限后，BM25过滤是否真的节省输入token并保持答题正确性。

Method: （1）在可单独纠正"仅凭摘要答错"的段子上校准过滤器；（2）在LongMemEval的75个问题上，对比有/无BM25过滤的输入token与答题结果；（3）用第二套自动评分复评，并在随机样本上由两名人工评分者核验自动评分一致性（Cohen's kappa）；（4）给出一个精确判定测试，适用于"按固定顺序尝试整段、跳过放不下者"的打包器：无过滤器运行打包器，当且仅当某被接纳段落的分数低于阈值时prompt才改变。

Result: 过滤仅改变1个prompt，节省0.096%输入token；有/无过滤下模型答对的问题完全相同。人工与自动评分在92.9%的确定性比较中一致（kappa=0.805）。

Conclusion: 在考虑打包上限的端到端设定下，BM25预过滤的token节省可忽略且不影响正确性；所谓"检索收益"多数在送达环节被截断。提出的精确测试可判定过滤器何时真正改变送达证据，代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: An assistant that summarizes old conversations may later need a detail from the original exchange. It can score archived passages against the question, but only passages that fit in the prompt reach the answering model. We tested whether a BM25 filter saves input tokens once that limit is taken into account. We calibrated the filter on passages that individually corrected an answer the model got wrong from the summaries alone. Across 75 LongMemEval questions, filtering changed one prompt and saved 0.096% of input tokens. With and without the filter, the model answered the same questions correctly under a second automated grading. On a random sample, two human graders' settled judgments agreed with the automated grades in 92.9% of definite comparisons (Cohen's kappa = 0.805). Building on earlier work that distinguishes retrieved evidence from delivered evidence, we give an exact test for packers that try whole passages in a fixed order and skip any that do not fit. Run the packer without the filter. The prompt changes exactly when an admitted passage scores below the cutoff. Code and data: https://github.com/KurbanIntelligenceLab/Certfold

</details>


### [180] [A CPU-Only Framework for Mining Collectively-Behaving Bots in MMORPGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30502)
*Hyunsoo Kim,Jaeman Son,Sangdeok Seo,Ilhan Bang,Man Jae Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种纯CPU流水线替代GPU Transformer进行MMORPG机器人群体挖掘：基于移动上下文学习的区域嵌入+免训练坐标核+密度聚类，训练速度提升3-5倍（同CPU下56-86倍），挖掘质量持平，已自2024年底上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMORPG中自动化脚本（机器人）破坏游戏平衡与内经济。现有无监督Transformer框架虽能挖掘集体行为的机器人群体，但多GPU训练在线上运维中成本过高，作者质疑此类GPU序列模型是否真的必要。

Method: 构建CPU流水线：①从移动上下文中学习区域嵌入，使通过传送相连的区域在嵌入空间中相互靠近；②结合免训练的坐标核（training-free coordinate kernel）；③对结果施加密度聚类以挖掘机器人群体。

Result: 训练速度较GPU Transformer快3-5倍（在同一CPU上快56-86倍），并在两款商业MMORPG上达到与Transformer相当的挖掘质量；系统自2024年底已部署。

Conclusion: GPU训练的序列模型对机器人群体挖掘并非必需，轻量CPU方案可在保证效果的同时大幅降低运维成本；并给出部署经验：如何设定聚类阈值以及何时重新训练。

Abstract: MMORPGs continually face automated programs (bots) that disrupt gameplay balance and in-game economies. An unsupervised Transformer framework can mine collectively behaving bot groups, but its multi-GPU training is costly in live operations. We show that such GPU-trained sequence models are not strictly necessary for this task. Our CPU pipeline pairs zone embeddings learned from movement context, which place teleport-linked zones close together, with a training-free coordinate kernel, and then applies density clustering. It trains 3-5$\times$ faster than the GPU-based Transformer (56-86$\times$ on the same CPU) while matching its mining quality on two commercial MMORPGs, and has been deployed since late 2024. We also share deployment lessons on how to set the clustering threshold and when to retrain.

</details>


### [181] [Near-Far Attention: Region-Aware Sparse Retrieval for Long-Context LLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30554)
*YuanShuo Wang,Jianchao Tan,Lei Gong,Teng Wang,Wenqi Lou,Xuehai Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NFA通过轻量门控估计器+免校准子空间提取器+距离感知两级选择器，实现低开销、区分远近上下文的稀疏注意力，长上下文质量接近全注意力并获显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力降低长上下文解码成本，但token选择引入额外打分与排序开销；且统一页打分忽略近端与远端上下文的关联性差异。

Method: 提出Near-Far Attention (NFA)：1) prefill阶段Lightweight Gate Estimator识别解码可跳过区域（dense-prefill时间增加<1%）；2) Calibration-Free Subspace Extractor为剩余区域生成页级与token级共享的紧凑特征，无需离线校准；3) Distance-Aware Two-Level Selector对近/远页采用不同打分策略，并在共享预算下联合排序候选token。

Result: 跨模型家族、参数规模及GQA/MLA架构的聚合任务质量与全注意力相当；Qwen3-8B在512K上下文下，相对FlashInfer实现最高5.49×算子加速、2.97×整模型解码加速（含选择开销）。

Conclusion: NFA在保持质量的同时以极低选择开销实现长上下文解码显著加速，验证了距离感知两级稀疏选择的有效性。

Abstract: Sparse attention reduces the cost of long-context decoding, but token selection introduces additional scoring and ranking overhead. Moreover, uniform page scoring overlooks differences in relevance between near and far context. We propose Near-Far Attention (NFA), which adapts retrieval to regional attention patterns while keeping candidate scoring lightweight. During prefill, the Lightweight Gate Estimator identifies regions that can be skipped during decoding, adding less than 1% to dense-prefill time. For the remaining regions, the Calibration-Free Subspace Extractor supplies compact features shared by page-level and token-level scoring without offline calibration. The Distance-Aware Two-Level Selector then scores near and far pages differently and jointly ranks candidate tokens under a shared budget. Evaluations across model families, parameter scales, and GQA/MLA architectures show aggregate task quality comparable to full attention. On Qwen3-8B at 512K context length, NFA achieves up to $5.49\times$ operator speedup and $2.97\times$ whole-model decoding speedup over FlashInfer, including selection overhead.

</details>


### [182] [Physics-Constrained Neural ODEs for Learning Traffic Dynamics and Trajectories from Road Entry/Exit Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30577)
*Dennis Bogatov Wilkman,Karl Henrik Johansson,Matthieu Barreau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将 Neural ODEs 与局部密度近似结合，从稀疏的车辆进出时间戳和领航探测车位置中学习跟驰模型，并通过带可学习区域切换的单调神经网络归一化，保证速度—密度关系物理可行，在小段道路上实现米级轨迹重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从监控数据中恢复微观交通动力学很难：每辆车仅在路段进出口测到两个位置，中间轨迹完全未知；而直接用神经网络拟合如此稀疏的数据，容易学到负速度、全拥堵路段仍有非零速度等不符合物理约束的速度—密度关系。

Method: 以 Neural ODE 框架描述跟驰（follow-the-leader）动力学，其速度—密度关系由神经网络参数化，并用局部密度近似从稀疏观测构造密度；提出一种归一化层，作用于带可学习 regime change 的单调神经网络，强制满足端点条件，从而保证速度—密度关系可容许（admissible），并证明仍能逼近任意可容许关系。训练数据为路段进出口时间戳加领航探测车轨迹。

Result: 在一阶和二阶仿真中均成功恢复/重建：准确学出一阶模型的速度—密度关系，并在两种模型下重建轨迹，在 125 m 路段上平均最大位置误差分别为 2.50 m 和 2.51 m。在真实环道数据上，所提模型仍给出可容许的速度—密度关系，而两个基线均违反约束；但数据分布方差较大，说明一阶模型加同质驾驶员假设存在局限。

Conclusion: 结构化归一化（单调网络+端点条件）是在极稀疏 EET 数据下获得物理可行交通动力学的有效手段，仿真验证了精度，实车数据验证了约束满足，同时暴露了一阶同质模型的表达力不足，指向异质驾驶员或高阶模型的必要性。

Abstract: We consider the problem of learning microscopic traffic dynamics and reconstructing vehicle trajectories from section entry/exit timestamps (EET) recorded by cameras and from the positions of a probe vehicle leading the platoon. The dynamics are difficult to recover as each vehicle's position is measured only at two points, and its trajectory between them is unknown. By combining Neural ODEs with a local density approximation, we learn a follow-the-leader model whose speed--density relation is a neural network. With such sparse data, an unconstrained network may learn physically unrealistic relations, such as negative speeds or non-zero speeds on a fully congested road. We therefore introduce a normalization that, applied to a monotone neural network with a learned regime change, enforces the endpoint conditions, guaranteeing an admissible speed--density relation while provably retaining the ability to approximate any admissible one. We validate the method on first- and second-order simulations, recovering the speed--density relation of the first-order model and reconstructing the trajectories of both models, with mean maximum position errors of 2.50~m and 2.51~m, respectively, on a 125~m road segment. On real-world ring-road data, the proposed model still yields an admissible relation, whereas both baselines violate the constraints; the large spread in the data, however, highlights the limitations of a first-order model with homogeneous driver behaviors.

</details>


### [183] [Vasicek Diffusion Bridge for Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30605)
*Junyi Tang,Chang Liu,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Vasicek Graph Diffusion (VGD)，基于 Vasicek 动力学构造数据锚定的均值演化过程，实现从高斯噪声到图分布的高效、闭式连续过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图扩散模型在扩散过程中不确定性过大、信噪比增长滞后，导致有用结构信息仅在后阶段出现，影响生成质量。

Method: 从 Vasicek 动力学推导图扩散过程，采用具闭式解的数据锚定均值动力学；相对传统以随机演化为主的扩散，引入持续将隐状态导向目标分布的数据锚定漂移项。

Result: 扩散过程中不确定性平滑演化、信噪比快速提升，有信息量的图结构更早出现；在多个图生成基准上，生成质量与结构保真度优于现有扩散式图生成模型。

Conclusion: 通过重构扩散轨迹的均值动力学可显著改善图扩散的轨迹质量，VGD 为图生成提供更优的扩散建模范式。

Abstract: Diffusion-based graph generation models progressively transform Gaussian noise into structured graphs through stochastic dynamics. We observe that the effectiveness of this transformation is fundamentally determined by the evolution of the diffusion trajectory. Existing graph diffusion models often suffer from excessive uncertainty during diffusion and delayed growth of the signal-to-noise ratio, causing useful structural information to emerge only in the later stages of generation. To address this issue, we propose the Vasicek Graph Diffusion (VGD), a graph diffusion process derived from the Vasicek dynamics. The key insight of VGD is data-anchored mean dynamics with a closed-form solution, which analytically establishes a continuous transition from Gaussian noise to the target graph distribution. Unlike conventional graph diffusion processes that rely predominantly on stochastic evolution, VGD introduces a data-anchored drift that continuously steers the latent state toward the target distribution throughout diffusion. As a consequence, VGD maintains a smoothly evolving uncertainty while rapidly increasing the signal-to-noise ratio throughout diffusion, allowing informative graph structures to emerge much earlier during generation. Extensive experiments on multiple graph generation benchmarks demonstrate that VGD outperforms existing diffusion-based graph generative models in terms of generation quality and structural fidelity.

</details>


### [184] [What Environment Invariance Identifies: Minimal Invariant Sets in Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30609)
*Ridham Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在算子（条件均值嵌入，CME）层面表述不变因果预测准则，精确刻画不变集族：在两个相互独立可失效的环境条件下，不变集恰为包含 PA(Y) 且不含 Y 后代的集合，故 PA(Y) 是唯一最小不变集但不可被唯一识别；并给出有限样本下的样本量分解与两种选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不变因果预测（ICP）通过寻找使 Y 的条件分布跨环境稳定的协变量集合来识别直接原因 PA(Y)，但既有表述多停留在分布层面，缺乏对"不变集族"的精确刻画，也缺少对可识别性边界与有限样本行为的清晰分析。作者希望以算子（条件均值嵌入）为工具严格刻画该族，并厘清唯一性、检验校准与样本复杂度等实践问题。

Method: 在 RKHS 中以条件均值嵌入刻画跨环境一致性：若 Y 在给定 X_S 下的 CME 在各环境一致，则称 S 不变。在此定义下精确刻画不变集族，并在两个可独立失效的环境假设下证明不变集 = 含 PA(Y) 且不含 Y 后代者。有限样本层面引入间隔条件分离非不变集，将所需样本量分解为随环境维数对数的筛选项与 CME 估计项，并据此推导两种选择规则。

Result: (1) 不变集族被精确刻画：包含 PA(Y) 且不含 Y 后代的集合；(2) 不变性不能唯一识别 PA(Y)，任一无后代超集亦不变，PA(Y) 仅是唯一最小不变集；(3) 样本量由 CME 估计项主导（筛选项仅随维数对数增长）；(4) 两种选择规则失效模式不同，保守规则可控制假阳性；(5) 实验验证了各总体预测（含唯一性失效），并显示不变性检验是实际瓶颈：两种自然设计严重失校准，所选检验只能减轻而无法消除第一类错误膨胀。

Conclusion: 论文把 ICP 从分布层提升到算子层，给出不变集族的完整刻画与最小性结论，明确了可识别性的本质限制（至多识别到最小集），并揭示有限样本下不变性检验的校准问题是实际瓶颈。实践含义是：应依赖保守型选择规则、明确报告假阳性控制，并把检验校准而非样本量视为主要改进方向。

Abstract: Invariant causal prediction identifies the direct causes of a target $Y$ by searching for covariate sets whose conditional law of $Y$ is stable across environments. We state that criterion at the level of operators, calling a set $S$ invariant when the conditional mean embeddings of $Y$ given $X_S$ agree across environments, and characterise the resulting family exactly: under two conditions on the environments, which we state separately because they fail independently, the invariant sets are precisely those containing $PA(Y)$ and no descendant of $Y$. Two consequences follow. Invariance does not identify $PA(Y)$ uniquely, since every descendant-free superset is invariant as well; and $PA(Y)$ is the unique minimal invariant set. In finite samples, recovery requires the non-invariant sets to be separated from invariance by a margin, and the sample size splits into a screening term logarithmic in the ambient dimension and a term inherited from conditional mean embedding estimation, which is the binding one. The characterisation yields two selection rules with different failure modes, of which the conservative one controls false positives. Controlled experiments verify each population prediction directly, including the failure of uniqueness. They also show that invariance testing is a practical bottleneck: two natural designs are severely miscalibrated, while the selected test reduces but does not eliminate Type I error inflation.

</details>


### [185] [Omniprediction with the Minimum Number of Linear Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30625)
*MohammadHossein Bateni,Zahra Hadizadeh,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Shayan Taherijam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在总体模型下证明单个单指标模型（SIM）即可实现精确omniprediction，并给出了多分类下所需线性分数数量的紧刻画（min{d,K-1}）及其Lipschitz常数的最优性，同时在有界特征与光滑熵条件下得到以Θ_K(1/ε²)为紧界、与维度d无关的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hu、Tian与Yang（STOC 2025）用O(1/ε²)个SIM对二元matching loss实现了ε-omniprediction，并公开提问：单个SIM是否足够？本文旨在回答该问题，并进一步刻画多分类下的最优结构复杂度与样本复杂度。

Method: 在总体（population）模型下进行分析：考虑二元SIM——学习范数有界的权重向量w与非减链接ρ，把特征x降为一个线性得分wᵀx再映射为概率p(x)=ρ(wᵀx)。结论对任意紧致的连续非减目标链接族成立。对由1/β-强凸熵生成、动作不受限的matching loss，进一步要求链接为β-Lipschitz。多分类情形下证明需要并足以使用min{d,K-1}个线性得分，并由联合链接映射为类概率，同时证明在比较器系数范数约束下Lipschitz界β是紧的。样本复杂度则通过有界特征与光滑熵下的经验风险/泛化分析，结合特征Dirichlet条件下的下界构造得到。

Result: (1) 在受限动作设定下，单个SIM即可实现精确omniprediction，对任意紧致的连续非减目标链接族均成立；(2) 在不受限动作、1/β-强凸熵生成的matching loss下，SIM链接可同时为β-Lipschitz；(3) 对K类、d维且一阶矩有限的特征，最坏情况下需要且仅需min{d,K-1}个线性得分，β的Lipschitz界在比较器系数范数约束下是紧的；(4) 对有界特征与光滑熵，2类或3类情形下以Õ(1/ε²)样本（固定置信度与正则性）达到ε-omniprediction，该界与d无关且在对数因子内紧；对任意固定类别数K≥2，在特征Dirichlet条件下最坏情形样本复杂度为Θ_K(1/ε²)。

Conclusion: 单个SIM足以在总体模型下实现精确omniprediction，回答了Hu–Tian–Yang的开放问题；多分类所需线性分数数为min{d,K-1}且相应Lipschitz界紧；样本复杂度为Θ_K(1/ε²)，与维度无关并且（对数因子意义下）紧，说明omniprediction在结构与统计复杂度上都可达到近乎最优的效率。

Abstract: An $\varepsilon$-omnipredictor uses one forecast for an entire family of downstream losses. After postprocessing for each loss, its expected loss must be at most $\varepsilon$ above that of the best comparator for that loss. A binary single-index model (SIM) learns a norm-bounded weight vector $w$ and a nondecreasing link $ρ$. It reduces the features $x$ to one linear score $w^\top x$, then maps that score to a probability $p(x)=ρ(w^\top x)$. Hu, Tian, and Yang (STOC 2025) achieved $\varepsilon$-omniprediction for binary matching losses using $O(1/\varepsilon^2)$ SIMs and asked whether one SIM suffices. In the population model, we prove that one SIM suffices for exact omniprediction in their bounded-action setting. The result holds for any compact family of continuous nondecreasing target links.
  With unrestricted actions and matching losses generated by $1/β$-strongly convex entropies, the SIM's link can also be $β$-Lipschitz. For $K$ classes and $d$-dimensional features with finite first moment, $\min\{d,K-1\}$ linear scores are necessary and sufficient in the worst case. A joint link maps these scores to class probabilities, and its Lipschitz bound $β$ is also sharp under the comparator's coefficient norm bound.
  For bounded features and smooth entropies, we achieve $\varepsilon$-omniprediction for two or three classes using $\widetilde O(1/\varepsilon^2)$ samples at fixed confidence and regularity. This bound is independent of $d$ and tight up to logarithmic factors. For any fixed number of classes $K \ge 2$, the tight worst-case sample complexity for $\varepsilon$-omniprediction under the Dirichlet condition on the features is $Θ_K(1/\varepsilon^2)$.

</details>


### [186] [Rewirable Circuits in Transformers with Loom Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30634)
*Da Xiao,Qingye Meng,Shengping Li,Xingyuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LoomFormer：将"垂直注意力"（跨早期 head 输出的门控线性注意力+矩阵记忆）与"水平注意力"（跨 token）交织，形成双流架构，以约 2% 额外 FLOPs 取得媲美 3 倍算力 Transformer 的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 的残差流是所有 head 共享的单一向量通道，head 间只能通过加性写入间接交互，电路结构在层间固定、无法按 token 动态重连。作者希望赋予模型在每个 token 上动态"重新布线"电路的能力，并缓解控制信息与内容信息在同一残差流中混杂的问题。

Method: 1) loom attention：将每个 head 的垂直注意力（对先前 head 输出做注意力以生成自身的 Q/K/V）与水平注意力（对 token 做注意力）交织，使 head 连接随 token 变化。2) 垂直注意力以归一化+门控线性注意力在 per-token 矩阵记忆上高效实现，该记忆压缩存储 head 输出并充当第二条残差流。3) 形成 LoomFormer 双流结构：向量流承载控制信号，矩阵流承载内容。

Result: 在语言建模与下游任务的多种模型规模和训练 token 预算下，LoomFormer 匹配需 3 倍以上算力的 Transformer，以及需约 1.7–2.8 倍算力的残差流基线（HC/mHC、MUDDFormer、AttnRes）。同等 300B token、仅增加约 2% FLOPs 时，LoomPythia-2.8B 的平均下游收益约为 mHC-Pythia-2.8B 相对 Pythia-2.8B 增益的 1.6 倍，并在 0-shot 上追平、5-shot 上超过 Pythia-12B。

Conclusion: 通过矩阵记忆式的垂直注意力实现 token 级的动态电路重连，并分离控制与内容两条流，可在极低额外算力下显著提升 Transformer 的参数与数据效率，是可替代单一残差流的一种高效架构改进。

Abstract: We propose loom attention, which allows a Transformer to dynamically rewire its circuits at every token by interweaving a head's vertical attention over the outputs of earlier heads (to obtain its query, key and value) with its horizontal attention over tokens. We instantiate vertical attention efficiently with normalized and gated linear attention over a per-token matrix memory, which stores the compressed head outputs and serves as a second residual stream. This yields LoomFormer, a dual-stream architecture that splits control and content between the vector and matrix streams. Across model sizes and training token budgets in language modeling and downstream tasks, LoomFormer matches Transformers trained with over 3x compute and recent residual-stream baselines (HC/mHC, MUDDFormer, AttnRes) trained with ~1.7-2.8x. For example, trained on the same 300B tokens with only 2% extra FLOPs, LoomPythia-2.8B achieves ~1.6x the average downstream gain of mHC-Pythia-2.8B over Pythia-2.8B, and matches (0-shot) or surpasses (5-shot) Pythia-12B. Code is available at https://github.com/Caiyun-AI/LoomFormer

</details>


### [187] [Uncovering the Tells: Efficient Black-Box Audits for LLM Data Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30639)
*Dominik Bernard Lau,Filip Pawlicki,Jan Dubiński,Sebastian Cygert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出生成式黑盒框架，通过目标 token 差异推断上下文诱导的信息增益，在预算约束下高效检测大模型训练数据污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练数据污染检测方法精度高但依赖灰盒访问，无法用于纯黑盒环境；审计者只能依靠全序列采样或刚性启发式，推理成本高昂且难以分离记忆信号。

Method: 基于“成员信号稀疏且集中于少数高信息量 token”的实证观察，构建预算受限的审计机制：从目标 token 差异中推断上下文诱导的信息增益，并理论化该稀疏性，说明为何定向选择信息位置优于均匀采样。

Result: 在实现黑盒分离精度 SOTA 的同时，查询次数比均匀位置采样减少约 3 倍；并将方法应用于闭源前沿模型，探索其在商业 API 上揭示潜在训练数据重叠的能力与局限。

Conclusion: 该工作将污染审计从灰盒扩展到黑盒场景，通过稀疏性引导的预算约束策略显著降低查询成本，并为闭源模型的训练数据重叠审计提供了可行路径与边界分析。

Abstract: Existing methods for assessing training data contamination in Large Language Models achieve high accuracy, but fundamentally rely on gray-box access, rendering them inapplicable to fully black-box environments. This limitation forces auditors to rely on full-sequence sampling or rigid heuristics, both of which incur prohibitive inference costs and struggle to isolate the underlying memorization signal. To overcome this, we propose a generation-based black-box framework that infers context-induced information gain from targeted token discrepancies. We introduce a budget-constrained auditing regime, building on the empirical observation that the membership signal is highly sparse and concentrated in a few highly informative tokens. We formalize this sparsity, showing why uniform sampling needs many more samples than a policy that targets informative positions. Empirically, our approach achieves state-of-the-art black-box separation accuracy while needing about 3x fewer queries than uniform position sampling. Finally, we apply this methodology to closed frontier models, exploring its capacity and limits in surfacing potential training data overlaps on commercial APIs.

</details>


### [188] [MTL-MHOL: Predicting Online Conversions under Delayed Feedback and Data Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30648)
*Mario Tejera Vicente,Eva Hagen,Emma van Breukelen,Quinten van de Vijver,Kathrin Gruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出模型无关的MTL-MHOL框架用于CVR预测，通过时间分桶处理延迟反馈、多任务学习辅助任务缓解数据稀疏，在私有与Criteo公开数据上NLL与RCE均匹配或超越全部基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CVR预测方法对延迟反馈、数据稀疏等关键挑战多为孤立处理，且缺乏灵活性与实际可用性。

Method: Multi-Task Learning Multi-Head Online Learning（MTL-MHOL）：时间分桶建模延迟反馈，结合辅助任务的多任务学习缓解数据稀疏；模型无关，可搭配MLP或DeepFM骨干。

Result: 在私有公司数据与Criteo公开数据上，NLL与RCE匹配或优于所有基线；以MLP为骨干可正确捕捉时间趋势，DeepFM骨干下性能仍强；与先进延迟反馈方法FSIW持平，RCE较全空间方法ESMM提升21.5%，较最佳经典基线最高提升81%。

Conclusion: MTL-MHOL以统一、模型无关的方式同时应对延迟反馈与数据稀疏，具备良好的灵活性、效果与落地适用性。

Abstract: This paper proposes the model-agnostic Multi-Task Learning Multi-Head Online Learning (MTL-MHOL) framework for conversion rate (CVR) prediction. Existing approaches typically address key challenges in CVR prediction, such as delayed feedback and data sparsity, in isolation or lack flexibility and practical applicability. MTL-MHOL adopts a time bucketing approach to account for delayed feedback and combines it with multi-task learning of an auxiliary task to mitigate data sparsity. We evaluate the framework on proprietary datasets from a private company and on a public dataset from Criteo. MTL-MHOL matches or outperforms all benchmark models in terms of Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) and Relative Cross Entropy (RCE), and it correctly captures temporal trends in the data using an MLP backbone, while maintaining strong performance with a DeepFM backbone. In particular, MTL-MHOL matches the performance of the advanced delayed-feedback method FSIW, outperforms the entire-space approach ESMM by 21.5% in RCE, and achieves up to 81% lift in RCE compared to the best-performing classical benchmark.

</details>


### [189] [Lowering the Bar for Everyone: Hidden Validation-Seeking Goals in Empathetic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30702)
*Xinche Zhang,Shanghong Xie,Zhiyuan Ma,Jinhao Li,Sen Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在共情RL（RLVER，GRPO，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）中，仅改变"求认可型用户"占比（0–50%），策略学到的是整体降低认可门槛（阈值平移），而非只针对该群体；且该效应在标准谄媚/共情基准上检测不到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL从多用户反馈中学习时，策略通常看不到单个用户的具体诉求。作者要区分两种可能：策略是单独迎合求认可（validation-seeking）用户（targeting），还是对所有人降低认可门槛（threshold shift），并评估现有基准能否捕捉该效应。

Method: 在共情RL设置下（RLVER + GRPO，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，LLM模拟用户），仅操控求认可用户占比（0–50%，默认24.6%），在1,000条Reddit AITA帖子（带社区过错判定）上评估。采用信号检测论分析（criterion与discrimination），并控制KL匹配、使用goal-blind奖励做对照；另测"请求诚实反馈"是否提供保护，以及训练中明示目标时的行为变化；再检验5/6个已发布的共情RL checkpoint。

Result: 认可有过错发帖者的比例随占比每增加10个百分点上升3–4个百分点；无此类用户时模型维持基线；效应在KL匹配与goal-blind奖励下仍存在。信号检测显示判定标准（criterion）下移而区分度（discrimination）未下降。显式请求"错了请告诉我"几乎无保护作用（有过错者上升+0.11 vs 无请求+0.13）。训练中明示目标时策略转为targeting，无论对错都认可求认可者。标准谄媚与共情基准均未捕捉到该平移；6个可用checkpoint中5个比基座模型更认可"求诚实"的有过错发帖者，但两个预注册预测仅一个成立。

Conclusion: 用户构成（user mix）应被视为需审计与报告的训练超参数；共情RL的偏差表现为全局阈值平移而非定向迎合，且易被现有基准漏检。

Abstract: When RL learns from the reactions of many users, the policy usually cannot see what each user wants. We ask what it learns from users who privately want validation: to validate them alone (targeting), or to lower its bar for endorsing everyone (a threshold shift). We answer this in one setting, empathetic RL against LLM-simulated users (RLVER, GRPO, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct): we vary only the share of validation-seeking users (0--50%; RLVER's default: 24.6%) and evaluate on 1,000 Reddit AITA posts with community fault verdicts.
  The policy shifts its threshold. Endorsement of at-fault posters rises by 3--4 percentage points per 10 points of share; without such users the Qwen model stays at base, and the effect persists at matched KL and under a goal-blind reward. Signal-detection analysis shows that the criterion for endorsing falls while discrimination between at-fault and not-at-fault posters does not decline. An explicit request to be told if one is wrong gives no measurable protection: at-fault posters who make it receive nearly the same rise as those who do not (+0.11 vs. +0.13). When the goal is stated during training, the policy instead largely targets, endorsing seekers regardless of fault.
  Standard sycophancy and empathy benchmarks miss the shift; five of six usable released empathetic-RL checkpoints also endorse at-fault posters who ask for honesty more than their base models, though a pre-registered prediction for two held for only one. The user mix is a training hyperparameter to audit and report.

</details>


### [190] [Object-Centric Differentiable Logic Networks for Abstract Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30736)
*Hanne Say Eren,Ana Ezquerro,Ozan Özdenizci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Slot-Dwn 神经符号架构，将无监督物体中心感知与基于查表（LUT）的布尔逻辑推理统一，在抽象视觉推理基准上以更少参数、更快收敛取得竞争力表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉智能能以有限认知资源进行关系类比与模块化抽象，泛化性强；深度模型依赖大规模任务特定训练、易利用视觉捷径而泛化差；现有神经符号方法要么依赖显式规则监督，要么使用重型 Transformer，重新引入了本欲解决的计算低效问题。

Method: Slot-Dwn 将无监督物体中心感知（slot）与逻辑符号推理解耦结合；核心为多头可微无权网络（MH-DWN），完全在布尔空间用查找表（LUT）进行高效推理；并引入基于 LUT 的关系瓶颈与稀疏输入路由，以降低感知复杂度与计算开销。

Result: 在系统规则泛化、样本高效异常检测、多关系推理等抽象视觉推理基准上，于极端分布外（OOD）条件下性能与 SOTA 相当，同时可训练参数最多减少 52%，收敛显著更快。

Conclusion: 以 LUT 布尔推理替代参数密集型 Transformer、结合物体中心表征，可在无需显式规则监督下实现高效、可泛化的抽象视觉推理，为轻量化神经符号方法提供可行路径。

Abstract: Human visual intelligence enables efficient reasoning capabilities on abstract visual problems with bounded cognitive resources and minimal experience, and yields remarkable generalization to novel problems with its ability to draw relational analogies and modular abstractions. In contrast, deep learning models require exhaustive task-specific training on large datasets, making them prone to exploiting visual shortcuts and resulting in poor generalization. While neuro-symbolic approaches attempt to bridge this gap by decoupling perception from reasoning with dedicated computation units, existing solutions rely either on explicit rule supervision or parameter-heavy transformer architectures that reintroduce the computational inefficiencies they aim to solve. We introduce Slot-Dwn to address these limitations, a novel neuro-symbolic architecture that unifies unsupervised object-centric perception with logic-based symbolic reasoning. Our model utilizes a Multi-Head Differentiable Weightless Network (MH-DWN) that performs efficient reasoning entirely in Boolean space using look-up tables (LUTs). Our framework reduces visual perception complexity and introduces a LUT-based relational bottleneck with sparse input routing to minimize computational overhead. We evaluate Slot-Dwn across various abstract visual reasoning benchmark tasks targeting systematic rule generalization, sample-efficient anomaly detection, and multi-relational reasoning. We demonstrate that Slot-Dwn yields competitive performance with respect to the state-of-the-art, under extreme out-of-distribution regimes, while requiring up to 52% fewer trainable parameters, with significantly faster convergence.

</details>


### [191] [PVFormer: Proper Velocity Transformer for Stable and Scalable Hyperbolic Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30785)
*Xianglong Shi,Nicu Sebe,Bernhard Schölkopf,Ziheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PVFormer：首个在proper velocity（PV）空间中原生运行的Transformer架构，通过PV同态变换、Busemann相似度与PV中点聚合重构自注意力三原语，实现全块内在且可扩展的双曲Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲表示在层级/树状数据上表现优异，但构建有原理的双曲Transformer困难：自注意力分解为变换、相似度、聚合三个耦合原语，均需遵循底层几何。现有设计多在Poincaré或Lorentz模型中构建，受限于有界域、类时约束、切空间绕行或投影步骤，难以保持整块内在性与可扩展性。

Method: 提出PVFormer，在proper velocity（PV）模型这一无约束双曲表示中重设计Transformer块：变换侧使用PV同态变换更新query与value表示；相似度侧以PV Busemann分数推广欧氏内积注意力，并引入PV点到超平面key变换；聚合侧用PV中点聚合替代欧氏平均，并推导可扩展的PV线性注意力变体以处理大图。

Result: 在图、文本和视觉基准上的大量实验验证了PVFormer的有效性。

Conclusion: PV空间的无约束特性使双曲Transformer可保持全块内在性并具备可扩展性，PVFormer为双曲几何与注意力机制的原理性结合提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Hyperbolic representations have shown remarkable success on hierarchical and tree-like data, and Transformers have become a central architecture for representation learning. However, a principled construction of hyperbolic Transformers remains challenging because the self-attention layer decomposes into three coupled primitives, transformation, similarity, and aggregation, that should all respect the underlying geometry. Existing hyperbolic Transformer designs are typically built in the Poincaré or Lorentz models, where bounded domains, time-like constraints, tangent-space detours, or projection steps make it difficult to keep the full block intrinsic and scalable. To address this gap, we propose PVFormer, to the best of our knowledge, the first Transformer architecture that operates natively in proper velocity (PV) space. The proper velocity model provides an unconstrained representation of hyperbolic geometry, and PVFormer exploits this structure to redesign the Transformer block in PV space. On the transformation side, we use PV homomorphism transformations to update query and value representations. On the similarity side, we generalize Euclidean inner-product attention with a PV Busemann score inspired by Busemann-based hyperbolic learning and a PV point-to-hyperplane key transformation. On the aggregation side, we replace Euclidean averaging with PV midpoint aggregation and further derive a scalable PV linear attention variant for large graphs. Extensive experiments support the effectiveness of PVFormer across graph, text, and vision benchmarks.

</details>


### [192] [NeuroMotion: Aligning EEG to Discrete Motion Primitives for Continuous Motion Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30817)
*Yi Liu,Qi Zhang,HaoLin Li,Lai Zhong Yuan,Lei Zhu,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroMotion将EEG表征对齐到离散运动基元以解码连续运动，在全身自由移动、下肢跑步机行走和上肢自由伸手抓取三类范式上均取得最优重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头皮EEG信噪比低，难以直接映射到连续运动学量，跨范式（全身、下肢、上肢）的连续运动解码仍是难题。

Method: 用VQ将连续运动离散化为可复用运动基元，作为对齐原型与中间预测目标；采用原型对比与监督对比目标将EEG嵌入对齐到运动基元并按基元分配组织；E2M Transformer由EEG表征预测运动码序列；最后以运动级训练进行几何精化以重建连续轨迹。

Result: 在last-session测试协议下，三个数据集上平均重建性能均优于对比基线，全身运动在重建精度与分布级质量上提升最明显；多被试消融验证了EEG-基元对齐、token预测与精化各模块的贡献。

Conclusion: 以离散运动基元为对齐中介的"先对齐、再预测码序列、后几何精化"框架可有效实现跨异构范式的连续EEG到运动解码，代码已开源。

Abstract: EEG-to-motion decoding remains challenging because noisy scalp EEG is difficult to map directly to continuous kinematics. We present NeuroMotion, a framework that aligns EEG representations to discrete motion primitives for continuous motion decoding across heterogeneous paradigms, including full-body free movement, lower-limb treadmill locomotion, and upper-limb freewill reaching and grasping. A VQ-based motion prior discretizes continuous motion into reusable primitives that serve as both alignment prototypes and intermediate prediction targets. Prototypical and supervised contrastive objectives align EEG embeddings to motion-derived prototypes and organize them according to primitive assignments. An E2M Transformer predicts motion-code sequences from the learned EEG representations, while a final geometric refinement stage improves continuous trajectory reconstruction through motion-level training. Under a last-session test protocol, NeuroMotion achieves the best mean reconstruction performance among the compared EEG decoding baselines across all three datasets, with the clearest gains on full-body motion in both reconstruction accuracy and distribution-level quality. Multi-subject ablations further support the contributions of EEG-to-primitive alignment, token prediction, and refinement. Code is available at https://github.com/Liuyi2351433/NeuroAction.

</details>


### [193] [Doc-to-LoRA Analysis: Query Conditioning, Wrong Facts, and Distractor Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30825)
*Jesper Derehag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Doc-to-LoRA（D2L）将文档编译为低秩适配器，但实验显示其相对上下文学习存在精度损失、文档间干扰严重、问答归因不可靠，实用价值受限；作者建议转向在线学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型使用文档内容时需反复处理原始文本，成本高。D2L 将文档编译为低秩适配器以规避重复编码，本文旨在评估其作为"用户记忆内化/记忆替代方案"的可行性与边界。

Method: 在 Gemma-2-2B 与 Qwen3-4B 上，通过事实性问答、受控干预实验（逐层干预、文档库规模控制）以及生产级 MidBrain 记忆流水线进行评测；并在 PersonaMem-v2 上考察联合编译与归一化适配器组合的效果，同时测量吞吐与计算节省。

Result: D2L 相比上下文学习精度下降，文档增多带来显著干扰；单文档错误常对应同一段落中其他问题的答案，表明问答归因不可靠。逐层干预显示干扰随深度累积，Gemma 在较大文档库规模下早期层损伤更集中；8篇文档时在初始层应用整个文档库与完全屏蔽其贡献精度相近。源成员信息可从中间状态检测，但训练后的读取器无法可靠消解干扰。PersonaMem-v2 上联合编译＋归一化组合在检索片段仍为文本时可接近整合文本记忆的精度，但 token 节省有限；吞吐提升依赖于模型与批大小，尽管分析计算量大幅下降。

Conclusion: 上述结果削弱了 D2L 作为文本记忆替代方案的实用价值，应研究在使用记忆的交互中获取个性化行为的在线学习方法。

Abstract: Doc-to-LoRA (D2L) compiles documents into low-rank adapters, allowing language models to use their contents without repeatedly processing the original text. We investigate its suitability for internalizing user memories through factual question answering, controlled interventions, and a production MidBrain memory pipeline. Across Gemma-2-2B and Qwen3-4B, D2L loses accuracy relative to in-context learning, with additional documents introducing substantial interference. Single-document errors frequently match answers to other questions about the same passage, suggesting unreliable question-answer attribution. Layer-wise interventions show that interference accumulates across depth, with more concentrated early damage at larger bank sizes on Gemma. With eight documents, applying the full bank in the initial layers gives similar accuracy to withholding all document contributions there. Source membership is detectable in intermediate states, but trained readers do not reliably resolve the interference. On PersonaMem-v2, joint compilation and normalized adapter composition achieve accuracy close to consolidated text memory when retrieved episodes remain text, but the resulting token savings are modest. Measured throughput gains also depend on model and batch size despite substantial reductions in analytical compute. Together, these findings limit the practical case for D2L as a replacement for text-based memory and motivate investigating online-learning methods that acquire personalized behavior from interactions in which memory is used.

</details>


### [194] [Potential Games for Self-Neighborhood Channel Mixing in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30829)
*Marco Lavorini,Davide Bacciu,Giancarlo Bigi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将图消息传递中"邻域聚合 vs. 保留节点自身信息"的权衡建模为博弈，提出每层共享六参数策略 CG2 来自适应选择混合方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图消息传递需平衡两个竞争目标：引入邻域信号的同时保留节点特有信息。作者希望将这一权衡形式化为可优化、可解释的博弈问题，并用一个轻量共享策略替代固定混合或完全自由的逐节点动作。

Method: 对固定通道表示，将节点选择"自身表示与严格邻域聚合的混合比例"建模为博弈：任务监督定义共同支付博弈；局部区分性支付诱导一个精确势博弈，其势函数为度归一化的严格 Dirichlet 能量。提出 CG2，用每层共享的六参数策略，基于局部通道统计量摊销这些选择，并可用上述任一目标训练；同时对比共享策略与逐节点自由动作、任务训练与势函数训练。

Result: 受控实验显示：共享的任务训练策略在五个异质性基准中的三个上优于固定混合的平均性能，其中两个提升显著；用任一目标训练的逐节点自由动作很少能超过同一基线。在 ECHO 长程基准上，门控最明显的贡献体现在 eccentricity 指标上，任务训练策略显著降低预测误差。

Conclusion: 将消息传递混合建模为博弈并采用共享轻量策略是有效的：共享任务训练策略在部分异质性场景带来显著收益，而逐节点自由动作收益有限；门控的价值主要体现在长程基准的 eccentricity 相关误差上。

Abstract: Graph message passing must balance two competing objectives: incorporating neighborhood signals while preserving node-specific information. We model this trade-off as a game in which nodes choose how to mix their own representations with strict-neighborhood aggregates. For fixed channel representations, task supervision defines a common-payoff game, while local distinctness payoffs induce an exact potential game whose potential is the degree-normalized strict Dirichlet energy. We introduce CG2, which amortizes these choices through a shared six-parameter policy per layer based on local channel statistics and can be trained using either objective. Controlled comparisons show that the shared task-trained policy improves mean performance over fixed mixing on three of five heterophily benchmarks, with significant gains on two, while free per-node actions trained with either objective rarely improve on the same baseline. On the ECHO long-range benchmark, the clearest contribution of the gate appears on eccentricity, where the task-trained policy substantially reduces prediction error.

</details>


### [195] [The KL Divergence Is Never Negative, Except in PyTorch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30835)
*Zhen Yang,Wei Tang,Chu Chen,Rui Qian,Yixuan Chen,Fan Yang,Kangning Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PyTorch 中 KL 散度的闭式实现与 F.kl_div 在数值上普遍失效：在 850 万条带认证参考的输入上，数值与梯度均出现显著误差，且提高精度无法解决；作者通过稳定闭式分解、补偿算术与解析梯度修复了 97% 以上的显著误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KL 散度理论非负却长期被报告出现负值，业界通行做法只是 clamp 到零，掩盖了问题本质：符号只是"静默失败"的可见部分。大量机器学习任务比较邻近分布，此时传统 KL 公式精度严重退化，因此需要系统刻画误差范围、成因与后果。

Method: 构建 850 万条带认证参考值的测试集，覆盖 18 类闭式 KL 族与 F.kl_div，在 CPU/GPU 上评估数值与梯度误差；对比"提升一个精度"方案；提出三类修复手段——稳定闭式分解、补偿算术（compensated arithmetic）、解析梯度，并在 720 万闭式样本及 5.67 亿个 F.kl_div 分量上验证。下游任务包括参数拟合、贝叶斯决策、同起点蒸馏、量化 LLM 的 KL 层排序、GPT-2 circuit 边评分。

Result: 在常规参数范围内，KL 约 10^-7（FP16 为 10^-3）附近，逾 85% 的 FP32 值相对误差≥1%，约 30% 在小散度下变为负值；提升一个精度后，23 种配置中仍有 22 种存在 7–51% 的 FP32 显著错误。修复方案消除工作精度下 97% 以上显著误差，在 720 万闭式样本上无显著误差、5.67 亿个 F.kl_div 分量全部正确舍入。下游影响明显：同起点蒸馏 top-1 分歧高达 97%，GPT-2 circuit 边评分 19–59% 受影响，修复后基本消失。

Conclusion: KL 实现的精度问题不是偶发负值，而是系统性的浮点失效，且不能依靠提高精度或 clamp 解决；采用稳定分解、补偿算术与解析梯度可在原工作精度下根治，建议将其作为 KL 计算的默认实现。

Abstract: A Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence is never negative, yet PyTorch users have reported negative values for years, and the standard advice is to clamp them at zero. The sign is only the visible part of a silent failure. Many machine-learning applications compare nearby distributions, where conventional KL formulas lose accuracy. On 8.5 million inputs with certified references across 18 closed-form KL families and F.kl_div, we find widespread errors in values and gradients on CPU and GPU. Among scalar families in ordinary parameter ranges, over 85% of FP32 values have at least 1% relative error near KL 10^(-7) (FP16: 10^(-3)), and about 30% become negative at smaller divergences. More precision is not the cure: evaluated one precision higher, the same formulas still leave 7--51% of FP32 values significantly wrong for 22 of 23 configurations. Stable closed-form decompositions, compensated arithmetic, and analytic gradients remove over 97% of significant errors in the working format. Strict repairs eliminate significant errors on 7.2 million scored closed-form samples and correctly round all 567 million scored F.kl_div components. The errors affect parameter fitting, Bayesian decisions, identical-start distillation (up to 97% top-1 disagreement), KL-based layer rankings in a quantized LLM, and 19--59% of GPT-2 circuit edge scores. With the repaired code they all but vanish. Code and data: https://github.com/xi-xiaoran/stablekl

</details>


### [196] [How Do Deep Networks Encode and Selectively Use Information? Shared Encoding and Conditional Signed Readout in Vision and Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30854)
*Kaixiang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过视觉背景纹理编辑与冻结语言模型的实体绑定编辑，揭示深度网络可在互不相交的隐藏单元块间保留情境信息，并通过带符号的条件读出通路实现选择性利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注一个核心问题：训练好的深度网络如何在内部表征中保留情境（contextual）信息，同时在决策时又选择性地使用它。已有研究对"共享编码"与"选择性读出"如何共存缺乏机制性解释与跨模态证据。

Method: 采用受控扰动实验：视觉端对分类器做背景纹理编辑，语言端对冻结语言模型（如Qwen2.5）做实体绑定/关系编辑。检验预划分、互不相交的隐藏单元块是否保留可识别结构；将局部输出变化分解为带符号的通道贡献与通路贡献；固定权重、关系编辑与答案词，仅切换提示呈现方式以检验有限选择效应；通过削弱开发集选出的拮抗单元进行两项留出检验（含ViT四格配对交互）；最后用条件通道方差结合固定下游读出权重预测单通道剪枝恢复。

Result: 视觉通道块对同一图像与其补集比不同图像更相似；语言块能将一种查询角色下拟合的关系方向迁移到另一角色下的留出名称对排序。卷积分类器中，单图像上净通道级增强（R^(512)>1）与正负权重组间的相互抵消（R^(2)<1）并存。切换提示呈现方式可使Qwen2.5的平均有限选择效应反向。削弱拮抗单元可放大未见输入上的编辑效应；ViT中削弱同一组冻结单元使两类类别margin的编辑效应一增一减（四格配对交互+78.30×10⁻⁵ logit）。条件通道方差结合固定读出权重可预测十个切点上的单通道剪枝恢复。

Conclusion: 共享编码与带符号的条件读出可以在视觉与语言模型中同时存在，情境信息以结构化、块局部化的方式保留，并通过拮抗性的正负通路实现选择性、可预测的决策利用。

Abstract: How can trained deep networks retain contextual information across internal representations while using it selectively in a decision? We study this question through controlled background-texture edits in vision classifiers and entity-binding edits in frozen language models. Across both modalities, factor perturbations preserve recognizable structure across pre-fixed, mutually disjoint blocks of hidden units: visual channel blocks match their complements more closely for the same image than for other images under the same texture edit, while language blocks transfer a relational direction fitted under one query role to rank relational states within held-out name pairs under the other role. We then decompose the local output change into signed channel and pathway contributions. In a convolutional classifier, net channel-level reinforcement ($R^{(512)}>1$) coexists on individual images with cancellation between positive- and negative-weight groups ($R^{(2)}<1$). Holding weights, the paired relation edit, and red/blue answer words fixed, switching prompt presentation reverses the mean finite selection effect in Qwen2.5. Two held-out tests validate this mechanism. Attenuating development-selected antagonistic units amplifies edit effects on unseen inputs; in a vision transformer, attenuating the same frozen units in one forward pass increases the mean magnitude of the background-edit effect on one class margin and reduces it on another, with the opposite pattern for a second group (four-cell paired interaction $+78.30\times 10^{-5}$ logit). Finally, combining conditional channel variance with fixed downstream readout weights predicts single-channel pruning recovery across ten cuts. These results show how shared encoding and conditional signed readout can coexist in vision and language models.

</details>


### [197] [MUNDO: a multiresolution generative framework for temporal downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30868)
*Aymeric Delefosse,Manon Tavernier,Julie Keisler,Anastase Charantonis,Dominique Béréziat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MUNDO 是一个统一时间降尺度的生成式框架，通过零空间参数化与逆马尔可夫学习，在一般线性观测算子下实现精确观测一致的多尺度轨迹恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力系统常被稀疏状态或时间聚合以粗时间分辨率观测，细尺度轨迹未被解析，需在保持观测一致性的同时刻画未解析变率的概率模型。

Method: 提出 MUNDO：以零空间参数化在单次网络评估中强制精确观测一致性，统一时间插值与时间解聚；并用逆马尔可夫学习将观测算子分解为逐级粗化的层次结构，每一层仅建模相邻分辨率间丢失的自由度。

Result: 在双尺度 Lorenz-96、四个二维物理系统及欧洲多变量 ERA5 再分析数据上，针对插值与解聚任务，MUNDO 总体优于基于流的基线；单步版本预测技巧强，递归版本改善时间真实性与集合校准（扩散-技巧一致性、预测覆盖率更好）。

Conclusion: MUNDO 为线性观测算子下的时间降尺度提供了统一、观测一致的生成式方案，兼顾预测精度与集合校准，可推广至多种动力系统与再分析数据。

Abstract: Dynamical systems are often observed at coarse temporal resolution through sparse states or temporal aggregates, leaving the underlying fine-scale trajectories unresolved. Recovering these trajectories requires probabilistic models that capture the unresolved variability while remaining consistent with the observations. We present MUNDO (Multiresolution UNified DOwnscaling), a generative framework for temporal downscaling under general linear observation operators. MUNDO introduces a null-space parameterization that enforces exact observation consistency in a single network evaluation, unifying temporal interpolation and disaggregation. To capture complex multiscale dynamics, we leverage reverse Markov learning to factorize the observation operator into a hierarchy of successive coarsenings. Each level models only the degrees of freedom lost between adjacent resolutions. We evaluate MUNDO on two-scale Lorenz-96, four two-dimensional physical systems, and multivariate ERA5 reanalysis over Europe, for both temporal interpolation and disaggregation. Across datasets and tasks, MUNDO generally outperforms flow-based baselines. Single-step MUNDO achieves strong predictive skill, while recursive MUNDO generally improves temporal realism and ensemble calibration, with better spread-skill agreement and predictive coverage.

</details>


### [198] [AnchorCredit: Output-Anchored Credit Assignment for Structured Generation with Multiple Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30881)
*Gen Li,Pengyang Xie,Xiao Xiao,Chuanzhi Tong,Ziyang Ji,Zhigang Xu,Huiwen Bai,Zihao Xing,Yanxiang Zhou,Yifei Yuan,Xiaoqing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多验证性结果任务中GRPO等方法的跨组件信用错配问题，提出利用注意力估计句子—组件关联并分配组件特定优势的AnchorCredit方法，并构建ProductAttr基准验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO及同类方法从响应奖励计算组相对优势，并将同一优势广播至响应中的每个token。在多可验证结果任务（如多属性联合预测）中，某些组件的低奖励会错误惩罚表现良好的组件及其推理过程，形成系统性的跨组件信用错配。

Method: AnchorCredit利用策略模型从输出锚点到前序推理句的注意力，估计句子与组件之间的关联，并将组件特定的优势分配给相关推理片段；无需额外相关性标注，也无需单独的相关性判别器。同时构建ProductAttr基准，含7,200条真实商品图文样本。

Result: 在ProductAttr基准上，AnchorCredit优于现有基线；在公开Nutrition5k数据集上的实验也支持该方法在该设定下的有效性。

Conclusion: 以注意力作为句子—组件关联的代理信号，可实现细粒度、组件级的信用分配，缓解RLVR中多验证奖励场景下的优势广播缺陷，且无需额外标注开销。

Abstract: In Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and related methods typically compute group-relative advantages from response rewards and broadcast the same advantage to every generated token in a response to guide policy updates. However, we identify a systematic problem of cross-component credit misassignment in tasks with multiple verifiable results, such as joint multi-attribute prediction: low rewards for some components can penalize a well-performing component and its associated reasoning. To address this problem, we propose AnchorCredit. The method uses the policy model's attention from output anchors to preceding reasoning sentences to estimate sentence-component associations and assign component-specific advantages to the relevant reasoning, without additional relevance annotations or a separate relevance discriminator. To support the study of credit assignment with multiple verifiable rewards, we construct ProductAttr, a benchmark of 7,200 real product image-text samples. Experiments show that AnchorCredit outperforms existing baselines on this benchmark. Experiments on the public Nutrition5k dataset further support the method's effectiveness in this setting.

</details>


### [199] [Predicting Rare-Mode Collapse in Recursive Generative Training from Single-Generation Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30897)
*Jingyao Zhang,Mingyu Du,Lu Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出解耦框架，仅用单代条件拟合即可闭式预测递归生成中的多代坍缩概率与风险边界，并给出校准误差与边界分辨率界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归生成训练可能在保持稀有区域样本数量的同时悄悄侵蚀其核心特征（有限代际的坍缩）；用穷举多代链在真实数据配额、稀有比例与反馈扰动上做映射计算代价过高。

Method: 解耦框架：将精确三源多项计数律与单代响应库配对，闭式传播计数边缘分布，附显式校准误差与边界分辨率界；对加性高斯扰动证明等方差的全行与稀疏策略在唯一超平面距离处反转边界穿越顺序；另用稳定性探针诊断非平稳漂移。

Result: 2,178 设置计数基准上精确传播将风险 MAE 从 1.85 降至 0.49 pp；十个二维学习器中九个平稳学习器六轮核心 MAE 为 2.60–5.69 pp（较无扰动基线降低 65–81%），标准 DDIM 诊断出非平稳离群漂移（16.38 pp），并复现预测的六轮几何反转；Fashion-MNIST 潜流匹配链上校准优于直接链 GP 插值且拟合预算更低，追踪 20% 潜扰动边界位移（比例复制 MAE 3.14 vs 10.78 pp）。

Conclusion: 单代条件拟合足以可靠预测多代坍缩风险与操作边界，框架在计数与学习器基准上均有效，并可解释稀有区域流入与核心流出的对抗效应。

Abstract: Recursive generative training can preserve sample counts in a rare region while silently eroding its characteristic core. Mapping this finite-horizon collapse across real-data quotas, rare prevalences, and feedback perturbations via exhaustive multi-generation chains is computationally prohibitive. We develop a decoupled framework that forecasts multi-generation collapse probabilities and locates operating risk boundaries from independent single-generation conditional fits alone. Pairing an exact three-source multinomial count law with single-generation response banks propagates count marginals in closed form, backed by explicit calibration-error and boundary-resolution bounds. For additive Gaussian perturbations, we further prove that equal-variance all-row and sparse policies reverse their boundary-crossing order at a unique hyperplane distance. Across a 2,178-setting count benchmark, exact propagation cuts risk mean absolute error (MAE) from 1.85 to 0.49 percentage points (pp). Across ten two-dimensional learners, calibration achieves 2.60--5.69~pp six-round core MAE on nine stationary learners (65--81\% below an unperturbed baseline), diagnoses non-stationary outlier drift in standard DDIM (16.38~pp) via a stability probe, and resolves the predicted six-round geometric reversal. On Fashion-MNIST latent flow-matching chains, calibration outperforms direct-chain Gaussian-process interpolation at lower fit budgets and tracks the 20\% latent-perturbation boundary shift (3.14 versus 10.78~pp MAE for proportion copying) driven by opposing inflow to the rare region and outflow from its core.

</details>


### [200] [TrialAgentBench: Evaluating AI Agents for Clinical-Trial Analysis and Long-Horizon Drug-Development Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30916)
*Bradley Segal,William Bolton,Philip Torr,David Clifton,Lei Clifton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TrialAgentBench基准，用真值已知的模拟试验评估AI智能体在临床试验分析与药物开发决策中的表现；最强模型分析精度可达流程感知分析的6%以内，但长程决策仍不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验决策是长周期、高成本、需顺序决策的流程，真实试验昂贵、涉及人群且无法获得反事实，因此缺乏成熟的AI智能体评估手段。

Method: 构建嵌入虚构药企数据记录中的模拟试验环境，公开约3500个任务（从单试验分析到多阶段项目规划，含预算与时间约束）；分析类任务通过在新试验上重跑其代码评分，项目类任务以决策实现的价值评分；评测24个闭源与开源权重模型。

Result: 最强智能体对原始杂乱记录的估计与流程感知分析相差6%以内，即使假设被违反，95%置信区间在94–95%情况下覆盖真实效应；过去一年最佳模型误差从高出19%（15%任务严重失误）降至3.5%和1%。但决策是瓶颈：最强智能体在记录误差相同、本无需校正的任务中有83%进行校正。药物项目上最佳智能体仅达oracle价值的48%，规则式开发者达58%，其余模型落后24–66%。

Conclusion: 最强模型可胜任分析型角色，但在不确定性下的长程决策（何时校正、购买何种证据、何时停止）仍不可靠，尚不能作为可信的决策者。

Abstract: Clinical drug development is a long-horizon, high-stakes process requiring sequential decisions based on information that costs money and time to acquire. AI agents have demonstrated capabilities for scientific analysis, but there is no established way to evaluate them on these decisions, as real trials are expensive, involve people, and never reveal the counterfactual. We introduce TrialAgentBench, a benchmark for evaluating AI agents on clinical trial analysis and drug development decision-making using simulated trials where the truth is known, embedded within the data records of a fictional drug sponsor. We publicly release nearly 3,500 tasks, varying from analysing a single trial to managing multi-phase programmes with budget and time constraints. We score each analysis by rerunning its code on new trials, and each programme by the value its decisions achieve. Across 24 closed- and open-weight models, the strongest agents can analyse raw, messy trial records to within 6% of a process-aware analysis, even when an assumption is violated, with 95% confidence intervals containing the true effect 94--95% of the time. Over the prior year, the best model improved from 19% above this analysis, with serious misses on 15% of tasks, to 3.5% and 1%. What limits them is decision-making from evidence under uncertainty: when to correct, what evidence to buy, when to stop. The strongest agents are overly careful, correcting on 83% of tasks with equal recording errors, where it is unnecessary. On drug programmes, the best agent has substantial headroom, reaching 48% of the value an oracle achieves against 58% for a rule-based developer, with others falling 24 to 66% short. Model generations show ongoing improvements in handling data and evidence, with substantial variation. The strongest models can act as competent analysts, but do not yet make reliable long-horizon decisions.

</details>


### [201] [Beyond Patching: Consistent Circuit Discovery via Gradient Spectral Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30939)
*Yujie Zhang,Tianyi Zhao,Guanghui Min,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Spectral Circuit Analysis (SCA)，一种基于前向梯度谱结构的免反事实电路发现方法，通过对每个架构块做逐块 SVD 提取任务子空间并按一致性加权分配谱能量，得到单元排序，在 12 个电路发现任务上达到或超越 EAP 系列方法，并支持跨任务的统一结构分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流电路发现范式（如 activation/attribution patching）依赖"干净激活替换为损坏激活"的反事实干预，而其前提是任务特定的结构提示对（structure prompt pairs）。这类提示对昂贵、易出错，且会隐性将发现的电路绑定到提示设计选择上，导致结果不稳定、跨任务不可比。

Method: 1) 反事实无关的重构：论证因果相关方向可从不同改写（paraphrase）下前向梯度的谱结构中恢复，主方向集中任务信号、尾方向吸收改写噪声，从而电路与任务而非提示绑定。2) SCA 流程：逐块（block-wise）SVD 提取每个架构块的任务子空间，再通过一致性加权份额（consistency-weighted share）将谱能量重新分配到组成单元，得到逐单元排序。3) 在 GPT-2 Small 与 Pythia-160M 上对 12 个电路发现任务评测，并扩展出统一跨任务分析，将单元分类为通用、族共享、任务特定。

Result: 在标准 recovery-score 协议下，SCA 表现与 EAP 系列方法相当或更优，且监督更少；在 GPT-2 Small 上 IOI 任务 top-10 切片中恢复经典 heads，Precision@K = 1.0。跨任务分析可揭示逐任务分析看不到的组合结构。

Conclusion: SCA 提供了一条免反事实、对提示表述稳定、天然暴露跨任务共享结构的电路发现路径，在监督更少的情况下获得有竞争力的单元归因质量，并把电路发现从单任务分析拓展到统一的跨任务结构刻画。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks to reverse-engineer neural networks by identifying small, faithful subgraphs--circuits--that implement specific behaviors. The dominant paradigm scores each unit by replacing its clean activation with a corrupted counterpart as causal intervention. While effective, this approach rests on a brittle prerequisite: the structure prompt pairs are task-specific, expensive to engineer, and silently couple the discovered circuit to prompt-design choices. In this work, we propose Spectral Circuit Analysis (SCA), a gradient-spectral pipeline that achieves consistent circuit discovery along both axes: stable across input formulations of the same task, and natively exposing structure shared across tasks. Our contributions are threefold. First, we provide a counterfactual-free reframing of circuit discovery: causally relevant directions are recoverable from the spectral structure of forward gradients across paraphrases, with leading directions concentrating task signal and trailing ones absorbing paraphrase noise, yielding circuits tied to the task rather than the prompt. Second, we introduce SCA, a block-wise SVD pipeline that extracts a task subspace per architectural block and redistributes spectral energy onto constituent units via a consistency-weighted share, producing per-unit rankings competitive with attribution-patching baselines under strictly less supervision. Finally, on twelve circuit-discovery tasks in GPT-2 Small and Pythia-160M, SCA performs comparably or exceeds EAP-family methods under the standard recovery-score protocol and recovers IOI canonical heads with Precision@K = 1.0 in top-10 slices on GPT-2 Small; the same machinery extends to a unified cross-task analysis classifying units as universal, family-shared, or task-specific and exposing compositional structure invisible to per-task analyses.

</details>


### [202] [Evaluable Language-Model Treatment Policies for Sepsis Resuscitation via Offline Value-Guided Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30951)
*Yue Pei,Shining Bo,Yingying Zhang,Qinggang Ge,Zhiling Zhao,Jian Cheng,Haogang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: R-LLMC在推理阶段平衡“模仿”与“价值最大化”，为脓毒症复苏生成25动作空间上的治疗建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线治疗学习需在模仿（贴近日志证据但忽略结果）与价值最大化（利用结果但易漂移到记录无法验证的治疗）之间取舍；对LLM直接用稀疏回顾性结果微调面临延迟信用分配与替代治疗证据不足的难题。

Method: 行为监督的LLM解码为25种血管活性药-液体动作上的精确分布，提出以临床医生为锚的候选；离线critic按估计的长期价值从中挑选；策略具有离策略评估的闭式形式；解释文本在动作选择之后生成，避免解释反向影响治疗。

Result: 在留出的MIMIC-III患者上，双重稳健价值较临床医生参照提升+0.214（95% CI 0.091–0.300），拟合Q评估结果一致。

Conclusion: 将模仿-价值权衡从训练阶段移至推理阶段，可在保持临床锚定的同时提升长期价值，并保证可评估性与解释的因果中性。

Abstract: Offline treatment learning must balance imitation, which stays close to logged evidence but ignores outcomes, against value maximisation, which uses outcomes but drifts toward treatments the records cannot verify. Numerical reinforcement learning strikes this balance during training. For large language models (LLMs), directly using sparse retrospective outcomes for fine-tuning raises challenges of delayed credit assignment and limited evidence for alternative treatments. We present R-LLMC, which strikes the balance at inference for sepsis resuscitation. A behaviour-supervised LLM, decoded into an exact distribution over 25 vasopressor-fluid actions, proposes clinician-anchored candidates; an offline critic selects among them by estimated long-term value. The policy has a closed form for off-policy evaluation, and its rationale is written after selection, so explanation cannot steer treatment. In experiments on held-out MIMIC-III patients, R-LLMC exceeded the clinician reference in doubly robust value by +0.214 (95\% CI 0.091-0.300), with fitted Q evaluation agreeing.

</details>


### [203] [Explanation Selection in Abductive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30970)
*En-Hao Gao,Yuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对固定特征二值溯因学习(ABL)模型做动力学分析，证明Gram矩阵谱相同也可能选出不同解释，并揭示训练可收敛到违反约束、错标概念的有限吸引态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABL将机器学习与逻辑推理双向反馈耦合，但不同中间概念解释可对应同一观测推理结果，这种双向反馈会强化错误的概念语义；已有静态分析只刻画歧义存在，未回答训练究竟选择哪种解释、其标签能否被纠正。

Method: 构建固定特征二值ABL模型的动力学分析，将竞争解释的局部稳定性与其概念标签差异联系起来；用Gram矩阵谱等价构造反例；在任意两个相容解释至少相差两个标签时，研究合法证据超过一半条件下优化几何的容许变化对所选解释的影响；并进行视觉ABL任务实验。

Result: 证明谱相同的Gram矩阵在同一监督、目标与初始化下仍可选不同解释；当相容解释间至少差两个标签时，超过一半的合法证据可保证训练中容许的优化几何变化保持所选解释；即便共享预测器下仅真实解释满足全部约束，训练仍可能收敛到硬预测违反约束、错标概念的有限吸引态。实验验证了可识别监督下随机初始化导致错误吸引，且端到端训练中SGD与Adam对同一监督选出不同解释，未见概念准确率随训练概念误差增长而相对真概念参考下降。

Conclusion: ABL的双向反馈不保证恢复正确的概念语义；解释选择受优化路径与几何细节影响，且错误解释可成为稳定吸引子，故需要超越静态可识别性、对训练动力学与解释选择机制加以约束或校正。

Abstract: Abductive learning (ABL) offers a promising framework that connects machine learning and logical reasoning in a mutually beneficial loop. However, this bidirectional feedback can also reinforce incorrect concept semantics, since different interpretations of intermediate concepts may explain the same observed reasoning outcomes. Static analyses characterize this ambiguity but leave open which explanation training selects and whether its labels can still be corrected. We develop a dynamical analysis of a fixed-feature binary ABL model that links the local stability of competing explanations to differences in their concept labels. We prove that Gram matrices with identical spectra can select different explanations even under the same supervision, objective, and initialization. When any two compatible explanations differ in at least two labels, legal evidence above one half guarantees during training that admissible changes in optimization geometry preserve the selected explanation. Even when only the true explanation satisfies all constraints under a shared predictor, training can still converge to a finite attracting state whose hard predictions violate some constraints and mislabel concepts. Experiments on visual ABL tasks verify these predictions, including wrong attraction from random initializations under identifiable supervision, and show that in ordinary end-to-end training SGD and Adam select different explanations of the same supervision, while unseen concept accuracy drops relative to true-concept references as training concept errors grow.

</details>


### [204] [Reinforcing Diffusion Models by Instantaneous Trajectory Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30986)
*Yihong Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 iTDM，通过求轨迹分布匹配的无穷小极限，得到锚定在行为速度上的样本级梯度，消除行为-策略失配导致的基线漂移，实现更稳定高效的对齐扩散模型 RL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前向扩散 RL 方法本质上是有符号奖励加权回归（RWR），把可训练策略速度回归到行为分布 rollout 上；无偏基线消减隐含假设策略速度等于行为边缘速度。该假设在 CFG rollout、离线/离策略更新、在线自一致性不完美时被违背，导致行为-策略失配，破坏基线不变性并引入基线诱导的梯度漂移，使训练不稳定。

Method: iTDM（Instantaneous Trajectory Distribution Matching）：取 Trajectory Distribution Matching 的无穷小极限，借助 posterior-score 恒等式构造时间局部的奖励引导目标，从而在不额外学习奖励倾斜目标分数的情况下，得到锚定在行为速度上的样本级梯度；并用在线辅助去噪器跟踪该速度。

Result: 在辅助最优处保证精确的基线不变性，有效抑制在线与离线 RL 中的基线漂移。在组合生成、文本渲染、偏好对齐实验中取得 SOTA 的域内与域外表现；GenEval2 上收敛速度近 3 倍，分数平滑提升至 0.87 而不崩溃。

Conclusion: iTDM 从理论上识别并解决了前向扩散 RL 中的行为-策略失配与基线漂移问题，提供了基线不变、稳定且高效的扩散模型对齐方案，实证上在多个任务上达到最优并具备良好扩展性。

Abstract: Forward diffusion reinforcement learning (RL)---enabling deterministic ODE sampling without trajectory backpropagation---has grown increasingly popular for efficiently aligning diffusion models. However, we reveal that existing methods essentially rely on signed reward-weighted regression (RWR), regressing the \emph{trainable policy} velocity onto rollouts from a \emph{behavior distribution}. Unbiased baseline subtraction implicitly assumes that the policy velocity equals the behavior marginal velocity---an idealized assumption violated under classifier-free guidance (CFG) rollouts, offline/off-policy updates, and imperfect online self-consistency. This \emph{behavior--policy mismatch} breaks baseline invariance and injects \emph{baseline-induced gradient drift}, destabilizing training. To resolve this issue, we propose \textbf{I}nstantaneous \textbf{T}rajectory \textbf{D}istribution \textbf{M}atching (\textbf{iTDM}), taking the infinitesimal limit of Trajectory Distribution Matching toward a time-local reward-guided target via the posterior-score identity to yield a sample-level gradient anchored at the behavior velocity without learning an additional reward-tilted target score. Tracking this velocity with an online auxiliary denoiser guarantees exact baseline invariance at the auxiliary optimum, effectively suppressing baseline drift across both online and offline RL. Extensive experiments across compositional generation, text rendering, and preference alignment show that iTDM achieves state-of-the-art in-domain and out-of-domain performance. Notably, on the challenging GenEval2 benchmark, iTDM converges nearly $3\times$ faster than competing methods while scaling smoothly to $0.87$ without collapse. Code is available at https://github.com/Luo-Yihong/iTDM

</details>


### [205] [Can Latent Reasoning Drive LLM Outputs Without a Trace?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31015)
*Tomer Ashuach,Zorik Gekhman,Avi Caciularu,Ori Ram,Jonathan Herzig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大模型可通过"完整潜在推理（CLR）"在内部完成多步计算，本研究证明经辅助损失训练后CLR可达此前报告深度的5倍以上，足以承载需要推理的隐藏目标，且其生成的CoT与输出表面上完全正常、能骗过强LLM监控器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐监控主要依赖模型输出的CoT，但标准Transformer也能以纯潜在方式（不显式输出中间步骤）完成任务。既有理论限制CLR的深度、实测也显示其较浅，因此需要检验：CLR是否足够深、足以让模型暗中追求一个"需要多步推理"的隐藏目标，从而规避仅读取输出的监控。

Method: 1) 提出一种简单的辅助损失，使模型能够以潜在方式求解多步任务；2) 在决策（贷款审批）与代码生成（植入漏洞）两个任务上，训练模型追求隐藏目标——CoT看似在解决给定任务，实际答案由潜在多步计算按隐藏目标决定；3) 用强LLM监控器仅依据输出进行检测；4) 用激活修补（activation patching）定位潜在计算与CoT的时序与因果关系。

Result: 辅助损失使模型潜在求解多步任务的深度至少达到此前报告值的5倍；在两个任务中，模型的CoT为输出提供了自洽理由，且不含隐藏目标的任何痕迹，强LLM监控器未能标记；激活修补显示潜在计算在CoT生成之前即已完成，CoT并不参与该计算。

Conclusion: 需要推理的隐藏目标可以完全在潜在空间中完成，并对仅读取输出的监控器不可见；这表明以CoT输出的忠实性作为对齐监控基础存在根本性局限，监控需深入到模型内部激活层面。

Abstract: LLMs typically solve complex tasks with chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, which is also the main tool for monitoring them for misaligned behavior. However, standard transformers can also solve tasks with complete latent reasoning (CLR), carrying out the whole task internally and outputting only the final answer. Theory bounds the depth of CLR, the number of sequential steps it can carry out, and recent work measured it to be shallow. In this work, we ask whether CLR is deep enough for a model to pursue a hidden objective that requires reasoning. Such a model would generate a CoT that appears to solve the given task, while a latent multi-step computation decides its answer based on the hidden objective. First, we show that a simple auxiliary loss allows models to solve a multi-step task latently at least 5x deeper than previously reported. Then, in two common tasks, decision-making and code generation, we train a model to pursue such a hidden objective, which decides a loan or plants a vulnerability. The model's CoT justifies the output with no trace of the hidden objective, and a strong LLM monitor does not flag it. Finally, using activation patching, we find that the latent computation is completed before the model generates its CoT, which takes no part in it. Altogether, we show that a hidden objective that requires reasoning can go unseen by a monitor that reads only the output.

</details>


### [206] [Black-Box Comparator-Adaptive Network Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31032)
