*Tomas Ortega,Hamid Jafarkhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究去中心化在线学习中的"比较器自适应网络遗憾"，证明每轮仅一步gossip的现有算法本质上无法达到次线性网络遗憾（给出双节点指数级遗憾反例），并提出无需图预处理的通用黑箱归约，实现次线性比较器自适应网络遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络遗憾衡量每个节点对所有节点损失的预测表现，比传统去中心化遗憾更强。现有基于gossip的算法在该目标下存在根本性局限，且"有界不一致性下gossip算法是否仍可能出现指数级遗憾"是文献中的开放问题。

Method: 1）构造双节点反例，证明每轮一步gossip的算法即使不一致性有界也会产生指数级遗憾，从而否定其达到次线性网络遗憾的可能性；2）提出黑箱归约：以具有次线性比较器自适应遗憾的本地学习器为组件，得到次线性比较器自适应网络遗憾，每轮每节点仅与邻居交换一次预测，边权对任意连通无向图通用且无需图预处理；3）刻画可允许边权，并给出利用图结构自适应的边权以改进遗憾界（完全图上改进为节点数的二次量级）；4）在合成与真实数据上验证反例并比较归约方法与去中心化基线。

Result: 证明gossip类算法在单步通信下无法实现次线性网络遗憾，并解决关于有界不一致性下指数级遗憾的开放问题；所提归约在通用边权下即达次线性比较器自适应网络遗憾，图自适应边权可进一步改进界（完全图上二次量级提升）；实验验证反例并显示归约方法优于去中心化基线。

Conclusion: 单步gossip不足以支撑比较器自适应网络遗憾，需借助黑箱归约结合强本地学习器；通用与图自适应边权分别提供无预处理适用性与更优遗憾界，为去中心化在线学习在更强性能指标下提供了可行框架。

Abstract: We study comparator-adaptive decentralized online learning with network regret, which evaluates each node's prediction on the loss of every node in the network. We show that existing gossip-based algorithms are fundamentally limited for this task, and cannot achieve sublinear network regret with only one gossip step per decision round. To prove this, we construct a two-node counterexample, which shows that gossip-based algorithms can produce exponential regret despite bounded disagreement, resolving a previously open question in the literature. We then introduce a black-box reduction, which uses local learners with sublinear comparator-adaptive regret to obtain sublinear comparator-adaptive network regret. Each node exchanges predictions with its neighbors once per decision round, and a universal choice of edge weights works on every connected undirected graph without graph preprocessing. We also characterize admissible edge weights and show how weights adapted to the graph improve the regret bound. For example, for the complete graph, this improvement is quadratic in the number of nodes. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data illustrate the counterexamples we propose and compare the reduction with decentralized baselines.

</details>


### [207] [RoFE: Rotated Frozen Embeddings for Low-Data Multimodal Tabular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31049)
*Yihao Qiao,Jiajian Ma,Wushuang Rui,Abhishek Jha,Aarti Darji,Yiqiu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示PCA接口在少样本多模态表格学习中因PFN按列标准化导致样本塌缩为正单纯形，并提出无需训练的正交旋转方法RoFE予以修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFN类表格基础模型依赖上下文学习，但预训练时的特征维度远低于现代图文编码器输出的数百/数千维嵌入，现有做法用PCA压缩冻结嵌入；作者发现标签稀缺时该PCA接口甚至不如简单线性分类器，需查明并解决该失效机制。

Method: 理论分析指出：当PCA保留标注样本的全部方向时，PFN的逐列标准化会抹去PCA得分方差，使样本在标准化空间中塌缩为正单纯形（两两距离相等），破坏嵌入几何。据此提出RoFE——一种免训练的预处理方法，在送入PFN推理前对PCA得分施加正交旋转，使各列方差近似相等，从而在标准化下近似保持嵌入几何。

Result: 在图像与文本分类任务、多种编码器与表格基础模型上，RoFE在低数据情形下优于PCA接口，并超过需要任务特定训练的基线；改进同样适用于回归任务。

Conclusion: 多模态表格学习接入PFN的关键瓶颈在于标准化对PCA得分方差的破坏而非压缩本身；通过旋转使方差均衡即可在零训练成本下恢复几何并显著提升少样本性能，为冻结嵌入与表格基础模型的接口设计提供了简洁有效的方案。

Abstract: Multimodal tabular learning combines tabular data with images and text, often in settings where labeled data are scarce. Tabular foundation models such as prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) offer a promising solution via in-context learning without task-specific training. However, modern image and text encoders produce embeddings with hundreds or thousands of dimensions, far exceeding the number of features encountered during PFN pretraining. Existing approaches bridge this gap using principal component analysis (PCA) to compress frozen embeddings. Surprisingly, we find that this PCA interface can underperform simple linear classifiers when labeled examples are scarce, even though PFNs are designed for small datasets. We identify the underlying failure mechanism: the PFN's per-column standardization removes the variances of the PCA scores. When the PCA keeps every direction of the labeled examples, the examples collapse into a regular simplex in which all pairwise distances are equal. To address this problem, we propose Rotated Frozen Embeddings (RoFE), a training-free method that rotates PCA scores before PFN inference. We theoretically show that rotation makes the column variances nearly equal, so that standardization approximately preserves the embedding geometry. Across image and text classification tasks, encoders, and tabular foundation models, RoFE improves low-data performance over the PCA interface and outperforms baselines that require task-specific training. The improvements also extend to regression.

</details>


### [208] [Attention Temperature Confounds Zero-Shot Context Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31113)
*Ruben Navasardyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出，零样本上下文扩展实验若在默认温度τ=1下与稠密基线比较，结论会被基线的"温度失校准"污染；在纯bf16无master weights训练下这种失校准极为严重（37-58%），会使block-sparse、attention dropout、ALiBi等方法虚增6-35%的优势，而按模型与长度逐一校准温度后可完全消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本上下文扩展的评估惯例是在训练长度之外、默认温度τ=1下与稠密基线对比困惑度。但该比较隐含假设基线已良好校准；若基线在超训练长度处本身失校准，则任何"改进"都可能只是校准差异的伪影。作者希望量化这种失校准的规模、来源及其对方法排序的影响。

Method: 以"单标量锐化logits可回收的困惑度比例"度量失校准。使用三组种子配对的365M孪生模型（fp32 master weights标准配方，8k训练、30k评估）与1.2B模型；对比纯bf16无master weights训练；分析AdamW（β2=0.95）下初始化为1.0的normalisation gain的更新量相对bf16间隔的大小；对每个模型、每个长度拟合一个温度后再比较block-sparse预训练、attention dropout、ALiBi、NTK-aware scaling（正对照）与RoPE基线，并进行解冻gain的消融。

Result: 标准配方下8k→30k基线失校准8-15%，其中一个孪生模型中block-sparse看似优7%，校准后消失。纯bf16下失校准放大数倍：学习率低于1.7e-3时，AdamW对gain的更新不足半个bf16间隔，gain在22个checkpoint中始终不动，基线失校准37-58%，block-sparse、attention dropout、ALiBi虚增6-35%优势。逐模型逐长度单温度校准后三者优势全部消失，ALiBi反而落后RoPE 5%，NTK-aware scaling仅保留部分增益。解冻gain后，365M处block-sparse优势反转，1.2B处其71-89%被消除。

Conclusion: 训练长度外的扩展结论高度依赖基线校准状态，而校准又受数值精度（bf16 vs fp32 master weights）和gain是否实际更新影响。建议在已校准温度下报告扩展结果，并核查normalisation gain是否发生移动，否则方法间的"扩展优势"可能只是失校准伪影。

Abstract: A training-time attention change is usually judged for zero-shot context extension against a dense baseline beyond the training length at the default temperature, $τ= 1$. The outcome depends on how miscalibrated the baseline is there: the share of perplexity it recovers when its logits are sharpened by one scalar. Under the standard recipe (fp32 master weights), an 8k-trained baseline is 8-15% miscalibrated at 30k in three seed-paired 365M twins; in one of them this makes the block-sparse-trained twin look 7% better, which calibration removes. In pure-bf16 training without master weights the miscalibration is several times larger: an AdamW step ($β_2 = 0.95$) on a gain initialised at 1.0 is below half the bf16 spacing at peak learning rates under $1.7\times10^{-3}$, so gains never move (22 checkpoints), the baseline is 37-58% miscalibrated, and block-sparse pretraining, attention dropout and ALiBi look 6-35% better. Calibrating one temperature per model and length removes all three advantages, leaves ALiBi 5% behind RoPE and keeps part of the gain of a positive control, NTK-aware scaling. Unfreezing the gains reverses the block-sparse advantage at 365M and removes 71-89% of it at 1.2B. We recommend reporting extension results at calibrated temperatures and checking that normalisation gains moved.

</details>


### [209] [Site-Conditioned Boundary Closure for Subsurface Seepage Forecasting and Relief Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31125)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Site-Conditioned Boundary Closure（SCBC），将局部物理描述子映射为截断边界上的正交换系数，构建保无源性的降阶算子，可跨未见强迫历程泛化；在堤基渗流与约束选址任务中评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在局部阈值激活出口处截断大型异质扩散模型可减少自由度，但会切断支配出口开启时机与排放量的外部反馈，导致降阶模型缺乏对强迫响应的准确刻画。

Method: 将强迫驱动的背景水头与局部抽水降深分离，SCBC以局部物理描述子预测截断边界上的正交换系数；以无强迫供体域抽水响应作监督训练，保证算子无源性；在MODFLOW 6全网格参考下评测三块留出堤基面板的洪水驱动地下渗流，并测试约束选址（最小化陆侧趾部峰值水头，受排放能力与三次全网格校核预算约束）。

Result: 单一冻结SCBC树估计器相对共享边界系数的相同局部补丁，峰值流量对数RMSE分别降低26.1%、30.9%、42.6%；在36个基础的选址测试中（30状态环算子），神经SCBC估计器改善了候选排放预测，但校核前选出4个超容量方案，而共享系数环仅1个，且三次校核后恢复全部144个参考最优解。

Conclusion: SCBC在排放预报精度上显著优于共享系数边界，但更准确的筛选预报并不必然转化为更优的约束选址表现；降阶算子的预报质量与决策可靠性之间需分别评估。

Abstract: Truncating a large heterogeneous diffusion model around a localized, threshold-activated outlet saves degrees of freedom but severs exterior feedback that governs when the outlet opens and how much it discharges. By separating forcing-driven background head from local withdrawal drawdown, Site-Conditioned Boundary Closure (SCBC) maps local physical descriptors to positive exchange coefficients at the cut boundary. Supervised on unforced donor-domain withdrawal responses, these coefficients yield a passivity-preserving reduced operator that generalizes across unseen forcing histories without refitting. We evaluate SCBC on flood-driven subsurface seepage beneath levee foundations against full-grid reference simulations (MODFLOW~6). Across three held-out foundation panels, a single frozen SCBC tree estimator reduced peak-discharge log RMSE by 26.1%, 30.9% and 42.6% relative to identical local patches using a shared boundary coefficient. We then test whether better screening forecasts improve constrained placement, minimizing peak landside toe head subject to a discharge capacity limit and a budget of three sequential full-grid checks. On a separate 36-foundation test using a coarser 30-state ring operator, a neural SCBC estimator improved discharge forecasts across candidates yet selected four over-capacity placements before checks, compared with one for a shared-coefficient ring, which recovered all 144 reference optima after three checks.

</details>


### [210] [Attention Latent Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31154)
*Xuyuan Xiong,Siyu Chen,Mengzhao Chen,Yue Su,Zhuoran Yang,Ping Luo,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Attention Latent Feedback (ALF)：让每个子层在当前 token 处直接关注上一位置深层的输出，从而打破标准自注意力“同深度跨位置”的连通限制；训练展开为两路并行计算，解码仍单次前向并复用缓存的上一 token 状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归解码时，上一 token 的深层表示已计算完毕，但当前 token 的浅层因标准注意力只在同一深度连接位置而无法读取这些表示，造成深层语义信息在位置间传递的浪费与延迟。

Method: 引入跨位置的深度反馈通路：每个子层可关注前一位置更深层的输出；训练时将这种跨位置递归展开为两次并行 pass，推理时利用缓存的上一 token 状态实现单次解码，避免额外递归开销。实验覆盖 MoE 模型最高 4.0B 参数，并在标准残差与注意力残差骨干、三种规模上对比既有 final-state feedback，并分析层选择与固定最近窗口变体。

Result: 相对标准残差，held-out loss 降低 0.022–0.024 nats，解码 FLOP 开销约 1.1%；三个规模上均优于先前的最终状态反馈方法。MoE-1.6B 上等效于 1.38 倍更大的基线 token 预算；MoE-4.0B 上十七项下游基准平均准确率由 45.1% 提升至 47.0%。分析显示收益主要来自中间层与邻近深层，固定最近窗口变体以线性读取成本保留 89%–94% 的增益，而学习式注意力随规模扩大进一步拉大优势。

Conclusion: 跨位置、跨深度的潜状态反馈是一种低成本高效的架构增强；主要收益集中于中间层与近邻深层，固定窗口即可近似大部分效果，学习式注意力在更大规模下更具扩展性。

Abstract: During autoregressive decoding, the deeper representations of the previous token have already been computed, yet shallow layers at the current token cannot read them directly because standard self-attention connects positions only at the same depth. We introduce Attention Latent Feedback (ALF), an architecture that allows every sublayer to attend to the outputs of the deeper layers at the previous position. Training unrolls this cross-position recurrence into two parallel passes, whereas decoding remains single-pass using cached previous-token states. Across mixture-of-experts models with up to 4.0B parameters, ALF lowers held-out loss by 0.022-0.024 nats over the standard residual with about 1.1% decoding FLOP overhead, and consistently outperforms prior final-state feedback across all three scales on both standard and attention residual backbones. On MoE-1.6B, ALF matches a $1.38\times$ larger baseline token budget, and on MoE-4.0B it lifts average accuracy across seventeen downstream benchmarks from 45.1% to 47.0%. Analysis further reveals that intermediate and nearby deeper layers drive most of this benefit, allowing a fixed nearest-window variant to retain 89%-94% of the gain at linear read cost while learned attention widens its margin with scale.

</details>


### [211] [Precision-Aware Learning Rate Transfer Across Block Count in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31166)
*Nan Chen,Soledad Villar,Soufiane Hayou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TipSweep：只训练最后 k₀ 个 Transformer 块来搜索学习率，并用缩放规则预测任意 k 个块（含全模型）的最优学习率，使 post-training 的 LR 网格搜索大幅降本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM post-training（SFT、DPO、RLVR）对学习率极为敏感，而网格搜索在计算和显存上都代价高昂。

Method: 跨可训练 Transformer 块数迁移学习率：仅在最后 k₀ 个块（典型 k₀=1）上搜索最优 LR，再给出将最优 LR 推广到任意 k 块（含 k=L 全模型）的缩放规则；并通过考虑更新舍入的解析模型解释精度相关的缩放行为（FP32 主权重约 k⁻¹，直接更新 BF16 权重约 k⁻¹ᐟ²），辅以 profiling 验证假设。

Result: 在 1B–7B 模型上验证最优 LR 遵循精度相关的缩放规则；实现两种原型：一是可在 16 GiB 单卡消费级 GPU 上完成 6.7B 模型的搜索，二是在高端 GPU 上单趟遍历数据并行评估所有候选 LR，使 5 候选搜索的 GPU 小时约为一次全深度（k=L）运行的 0.5 倍。

Conclusion: 学习率可跨可训练块数迁移，TipSweep 以低成本、低显存实现可靠 LR 搜索，为 LLM post-training 提供实用方案。

Abstract: The post-training of pretrained large language models, including supervised finetuning (SFT), direct preference optimization (DPO), and reinforcement learning with verifiable reward (RLVR), is very sensitive to the learning rate, and tuning it through grid search is costly in both computation and memory. In this work, we propose \textbf{TipSweep}, a method to transfer the learning rate across the number of trainable transformer blocks. To do so efficiently, we search for the best learning rate by training only the last $k_0$ blocks (in our experiments, typically $k_0=1$), and provide scaling rules that predict the optimal learning rate when training any set of $k$ blocks, including when $k=L$ (the full transformer). Across experiments with models ranging from 1 to 7 billion parameters, we observe that the optimal learning rate follows precision-dependent scaling rules: the optimal rate scales approximately as $k^{-1}$ with float32 (FP32) master weights, but as $k^{-1/2}$ when updates are applied directly to bfloat16 (BF16) weights without a master copy. We explain both rules using an analytical model that accounts for update rounding, under assumptions supported by profiling experiments. Finally, we develop two prototypes of the learning rate search for a 6.7-billion-parameter model. The first fits the search on a single consumer GPU with 16 GiB of memory. The second speeds up the search on a high-end GPU by evaluating all candidate learning rates in a single pass over the data, so that a five-candidate search takes about half the GPU hours of one full-depth ($k=L$) run.

</details>


### [212] [EEG Decoding in Context: A Framework for Tuning-Free EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31171)
*Hao-Run Cai,Si-Yang Liu,Han-Jia Ye,Hairong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需参数更新的EEG解码框架：以EEG特定上下文构建配合冻结的上下文学习预测器，并通过基于度量的上下文自适应为每个任务匹配合适上下文表示，在七个范式十个数据集上取得最高平均平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型虽提升了可复用表征，但下游使用仍需任务专用解码器、基于梯度的适配以及依赖验证集的模型选择，流程复杂且成本高。作者希望探索基于上下文学习（in-context learning）的互补范式，实现免调参的EEG解码。

Method: 先将原始EEG特征与预训练通用上下文预测器直接配对，发现性能有限且不稳定、难以跨范式泛化；进而提出框架：一、EEG特定的上下文构建，用于提升有效信噪比并显式呈现时间与空间结构；二、冻结的上下文预测器，不更新任何参数；三、基于度量的上下文自适应机制，在不依赖验证数据的情况下为每个下游任务匹配恰当上下文表示。

Result: 在覆盖七种EEG解码范式的十个数据集上，该方法在无任务专用参数更新、无验证集模型选择的条件下，平均平衡准确率（BAcc）优于十一个专用或微调的EEG基线。

Conclusion: 该工作表明适配可通过上下文而非参数更新来表达，为免调参的EEG基础模型提供了可行路径，并为脑机接口等下游应用降低了适配与部署成本。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) decoding is a central route toward brain-computer interfaces for communication, rehabilitation, cognitive assessment, and neurological monitoring. Recent EEG foundation models have substantially advanced reusable signal representations, but their downstream use still typically requires a task-specific decoder, gradient-based adaptation, and validation-driven model selection. We investigate a complementary foundation-model paradigm based on in-context learning: labeled EEG recordings define the decoding task as context, and new recordings are predicted without updating model parameters. We first pair raw EEG features with a pretrained general-purpose in-context predictor. This direct formulation provides a promising starting point, but its performance remains limited and unstable and does not generalize reliably across EEG paradigms. We find that suitable context construction can address these limitations by improving the effective signal-to-noise ratio and exposing temporal and spatial structure. Building on this insight, we introduce a general framework that combines EEG-specific context construction with a frozen in-context predictor and a metric-based context adaptation mechanism, which matches each downstream task to an appropriate context representation without relying on validation data. Across ten datasets spanning seven EEG decoding paradigms, our method achieves the highest mean BAcc among eleven specialized and fine-tuned EEG baselines, without task-specific parameter updates or validation-based model selection. This framework provides a practical path toward tuning-free EEG foundation models in which adaptation is expressed through context rather than parameter updates.

</details>


### [213] [Learning Neurovascular Codes for EEG-to-fMRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31194)
*Yiming Sun,Yifan Wang,Aosong Feng,Yijia Ma,Ben Cheng,Xurui Wang,Yufan He,Yucheng Tang,Qing Lyu,Daguang Xu,Pei-Fen Kuan,Mingzhou Ding,Wen Li,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"神经血管编码"(Neurovascular Codes)作为共享EEG表征用于fMRI生成：先为每个fMRI信号训练独立的EEG编码器，再冻结编码器并将拼接隐特征投影为共享码，以其为条件用随机插值变换将高斯噪声映射为fMRI信号向量，在8个公开同步EEG-fMRI数据集上显著优于编码器与生成器基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究面向EEG到fMRI的跨模态脑建模，目标是建立可从EEG窗口生成fMRI响应的共享表征，并支持同一EEG窗口采样多种fMRI响应（体现其随机性/不确定性），从而为跨模态脑建模提供新途径。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 为每个fMRI目标单独训练EEG编码器，从同一EEG窗口学习目标特定的时间与频谱特征；(2) 训练后冻结编码器，将其拼接隐特征投影为共享的神经血管编码；(3) 以该编码为条件，用随机插值(stochastic interpolant)将高斯噪声变换为fMRI信号向量，实现同一EEG窗口下多响应采样。评估覆盖8个公开同步EEG-fMRI数据集（4个静息态、4个任务态），共61,646个体积级配对，含被试内与被试外(held-out)两种设置。

Result: 被试内与被试外设置下，平均Pearson相关分别较最强编码器基线提升19.1%与19.8%，较最强生成器基线提升18.8%与47.9%；在基于真实fMRI训练的被试身份识别分类器评估中，该方法在对比的生成器中取得最高平均准确率。

Conclusion: 神经血管编码可作为有效的共享EEG表征用于fMRI生成，在重建精度与被试身份可辨识性上均优于基线，作者期望该工作为跨模态脑建模开辟新机会。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce Neurovascular Codes as shared EEG representations for fMRI generation. We first train a separate EEG encoder to predict each fMRI signal, learning target-specific temporal and spectral features from the same EEG window. After training, we freeze the encoders and project their concatenated hidden features into a shared neurovascular code. Conditioned on this code, stochastic interpolant transforms Gaussian noise into a vector of fMRI signals, allowing different responses to be sampled for the same EEG window. We evaluate our approach on eight public simultaneous EEG-fMRI datasets, including four resting-state and four task-based datasets with 61,646 volume-level pairs. Under within-subject and held-out-subject evaluation, our model improves average Pearson correlation over the strongest encoder baselines by 19.1% and 19.8%, respectively. The corresponding improvements over the strongest generator baselines are 18.8% and 47.9%. Beyond reconstruction accuracy, we also evaluate the generated signals using subject-identification classifiers trained on real fMRI. Our method achieves the highest mean accuracy among the evaluated generators. We hope that our work opens up new opportunities for cross-modal brain modeling.

</details>


### [214] [Matching Under Metric Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31208)
*Hongyi B. Hu,Dung Nguyen,M. Amin Rahimian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究度量隐私下二分匹配的表示与随机化，给出Gumbel匹配的紧致超额代价率Θ(n·min{d, log n}/ε)，并指出信任模型决定维度与表示选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在度量隐私约束下实现高精度二分匹配时，如何在保护私有点的同时控制匹配代价损失，以及信任模型（本地 vs 中心）如何影响表示与维度选择，尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 考察对每个私有点坐标或其到公开点距离做本地随机化的机制；利用公开点仿射张成构造保持全部匹配代价的更小表示；在可信服务器场景下提出Gumbel匹配（随机化边代价、仅发布分配），并通过上界与最坏情形构造分析期望额外代价。

Result: 证明期望超额代价的紧致率为Θ(n·min{d, log n}/ε)；基准实验支持以d≈log n作为直接随机化坐标与Gumbel匹配之间转换的实用判据；并扩展到平方欧氏代价下的隐私非参数双样本假设检验。

Conclusion: 应用信任模型决定表示与维度选择：本地隐私下低维点表示降低超额代价，中心隐私下距离表示即使高维也能达到最优率。

Abstract: We study how to represent and randomize points in $d$-dimensional Euclidean space to obtain accurate bipartite matchings under metric privacy. Given $n$ private and $n$ public points, we analyze local mechanisms that randomize each private point or its distances to the public points. Using the public points' affine span, we construct a smaller representation that preserves all matching costs. When a trusted server receives the private points, we establish privacy and utility guarantees for Gumbel matching, which randomizes edge costs and releases only the assignment. Through upper bounds and worst-case constructions, we establish the tight rate $Θ\!\left(n\min\{d,\log n\}/\varepsilon\right)$ for expected cost above the nonprivate optimum. Benchmark experiments support $d\approx\log n$ as a practical guide to the transition between directly randomizing point coordinates and using Gumbel matching. Extensions to squared Euclidean costs enable private nonparametric two-sample hypothesis testing. These results show how the application's trust model guides representation and dimension choices for better utility: under local privacy, low-dimensional point representations reduce excess cost, while under central privacy, distance representations achieve the optimal rate even in high dimension.

</details>


### [215] [Agentic AI for Intent Management and Control in Autonomous Communication Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31223)
*Burak Demirel,Yu Wang,Pablo Soldati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出三元智能体AI框架，通过解释器、优化器、控制器三个智能体协同，将高层网络意图与底层网络优化控制功能联合设计，并在差异化连接的无线资源管理案例中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI驱动的网络自动化方法多聚焦于意图管理与编排，而对底层网络优化与控制功能如何适配意图缺乏关注，导致高层意图与运行时网络控制脱节。

Method: 提出三元智能体框架：1）由语言模型驱动的解释器，将意图翻译并细化为机器可读的优化模板；2）优化器，依据意图规格调整控制器策略；3）控制器，执行快速决策。讨论各智能体的设计空间及工作流跨网络架构域的嵌入方式，并构建具体实现：语言模型解释器 + 贝叶斯优化优化器 + 多目标强化学习控制器，在差异化连接的无线资源管理场景中评估。

Result: 实验表明该框架能够将高层意图与运行时网络控制连接起来，意图规格与控制策略可随业务需求及网络条件变化而自适应调整。

Conclusion: 三元智能体AI框架为意图管理与意图履行的协同设计提供了可行路径，实现从高层意图到具体控制动作的端到端贯通。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI)-driven automation will help future mobile networks translate high-level network intents into concrete network optimization or control actions. However, existing approaches often focus on intent management and orchestration, leaving the adaptation of underlying network optimization and control functions largely unaddressed. This article proposes a triadic agentic AI framework that co-designs intent management and intent fulfillment through three agents: an interpreter, powered by a language model, that translates and refines intents into machine-readable optimization templates; an optimizer that adapts controller policies to intent specifications; and a controller that performs fast decision-making. We discuss the design space of each agent and how the workflow can be embedded across network architectural domains. Finally, we develop a concrete realization combining a language-model interpreter with Bayesian optimization for the optimizer and multi-objective reinforcement learning for the controller, and evaluate it in a radio resource management case study for differentiated connectivity. Results demonstrate how the agentic AI framework can connect high-level intents with runtime network control, showing adaptability of intent specifications and control policies to service requirements and changing network conditions.

</details>


### [216] [How Far Can Linear Go? BiPert, a Unified Framework for Single-Cell Perturbation Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31227)
*Abhishek Sharma,Aleksej Zelezniak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 

Method: 

Result: 

Conclusion: 

Abstract: Understanding how genetic perturbations affect cellular behaviour is essential for studying gene function and regulatory mechanisms. Large-scale CRISPR screens measure these effects across thousands of genes, but cover relatively few cellular contexts. Predicting perturbation responses in unseen cell types and identifying perturbed genes from their transcriptional effects therefore remain challenging. Here, we show that both tasks can be addressed through a common linear decomposition of transcriptional responses into shared and perturbation-specific components. Building on this principle, we introduce BiPert, a simple linear framework for both forward perturbation prediction and inverse identification. For forward prediction, BiPert outperforms existing methods that use no external biological knowledge when predicting gene expression changes in unseen cell lines. Its few-shot extension, BiPert-FS, adapts to new cell lines using as few as ten measured perturbations. For inverse identification, BiPert-ID identifies perturbed genes from single-cell expression profiles, outperforming deep-learning methods in unseen cell lines and donors. We further show that perturbations whose effects transfer more consistently across cellular contexts are also easier to identify, even after controlling for perturbation effect size. Together, these results establish a connection between perturbation transfer and identification, and demonstrate that simple linear models provide strong, interpretable baselines for both tasks.

</details>


### [217] [De Novo Generation of Odorant Molecules with Targeted Olfactory Receptor Activation Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30509)
*Kexin Zhang,Manhao Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个分子生成框架，可从头设计与目标多受体激活谱（嗅觉组合码）匹配的分子：先构建覆盖20个嗅觉受体的全景结合矩阵并聚类出代表性激活模式，再用结合成对排序、分布匹配与复杂度约束的结构化选择性损失引导强化学习生成器，在18000次oracle调用内于9种目标谱上实现高预测选择性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气味感知由组合码决定：单个分子激活特定嗅觉受体子集，其激活模式决定所感知的气味。因此若能有针对性地设计出具有指定多受体激活谱的分子，就能实现气味的理性设计而非依赖筛选，但现有方法难以从头生成满足多受体选择性的分子，尤其是先验分布下稀有的激活谱。

Method: 1) 构建覆盖20个嗅觉受体的全景结合矩阵；2) 对该矩阵聚类，提取代表性激活模式作为生成目标；3) 将目标匹配表述为结构化选择性损失，融合成对排序损失、分布匹配项与分子复杂度约束；4) 以该损失为奖励信号，通过强化学习驱动分子生成器优化。

Result: 在9种复杂度不同的目标激活谱上，方法在18000次oracle调用内即达到较高的预测选择性；在数据先验下较为稀有的谱型上提升最大，尤其体现在广谱激活目标与稀疏激活目标上。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将多受体选择性目标建模为结构化损失并结合强化学习，可高效实现面向目标气味组合码的从头分子生成，并在稀有激活谱上具有明显优势，为气味分子的理性设计提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Odor perception arises from a combinatorial code in which each molecule activates a specific subset of olfactory receptors (ORs), and the resulting activation pattern determines the perceived smell. We present a framework for de novo generation of molecules predicted to match target multi-receptor activation profiles. We first construct a panoramic binding matrix over 20 ORs, then cluster it to discover representative activation patterns, and finally formulate a structured selectivity loss, combining pairwise ranking, distribution matching, and complexity constraints, to steer a molecular generator via reinforcement learning. Across nine target profiles of varying complexity, our method achieves high predicted selectivity within 18000 oracle calls and provides the largest gains on profiles that are rare under the prior, especially broad-spectrum and sparse-activator targets.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [SAMA: Training-Free Consensus Ridge Tracking for Camera-Based Heart Rate on Five Public Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28185)
*Sudhakar Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练rPPG估计器SAMA，融合POS/CHROM光谱共识、无脉搏参考频点抑制、Viterbi随机游走跟踪与时窗置信度，在五个公开数据集上于逐视频/30s/10s三级协议均取得最低MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有rPPG评测以整段视频单一心率作为指标，掩盖了远程问诊真正需要的短时窗读数；同时需避免依赖学习参数与数据集特定调参的可复现性缺陷，并厘清逐视频协议的误差下限与肤色可恢复性问题。

Method: SAMA为免训练估计器：(i) 对POS与CHROM色度谱做质量加权共识；(ii) 抑制由同帧无脉搏参考（帧间运动、皮肤掩膜几何、背景亮度）所占频点；(iii) 以随机游走先验下的Viterbi脊线跟踪心率随时间的轨迹；(iv) 输出经轨迹滤波、幅值归一化的波形及逐窗置信度。无参数学习、无数据集特定调优。

Result: 218段录音、111名被试、五个数据集（UBFC-rPPG、PURE、SUMS、M3PD及MMPD两人子集），统一固定人脸框并采用rPPG-Toolbox协议重实现评分：跨数据集平均MAE在各协议层级均为最低——逐视频4.40 vs POS 5.13 bpm，30s窗4.80 vs 5.43，10s窗5.05 vs 6.59。汇总被试的bootstrap区间显示与POS的配对差在30s与10s窗不含零（Wilcoxon p=0.11、0.004），逐视频则含零；逐视频在SUMS和MMPD子集上未优于POS。剔除20%最低置信窗后，汇总10s误差由4.06降至2.33 bpm。逐视频协议在心率漂移数据集上存在1.2–1.6 bpm的下限；未标定视频无法恢复肤色。

Conclusion: SAMA在短时窗精度上对经典POS/CHROM有稳定改进并提供可用置信度，但逐视频层面优势不显著、部分数据集上不占优；在UBFC-rPPG上未超越最佳已发表神经网络（仅作对比未复跑）；据此提出面向印度人群的仪器化、肤色分层采集方案。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) is usually benchmarked by one heart rate per video, a figure that hides the short windows in which a tele-consultation would actually read the pulse. We present SAMA, a training-free estimator that (i) forms a quality-weighted consensus of POS and CHROM chrominance spectra, (ii) discounts frequencies occupied by pulse-free references derived from the same frames (frame motion, skin-mask geometry and background brightness), (iii) follows the heart rate through time with a Viterbi ridge under a random-walk prior, and (iv) returns a track-filtered, amplitude-normalised waveform with a per-window confidence. No parameter is learned and no dataset-specific tuning is used. On 218 recordings from 111 participants in five public datasets (UBFC-rPPG, PURE, SUMS, M3PD and a two-participant subset of MMPD), all processed with the same fixed face box and scored with a re-implementation of the rPPG-Toolbox protocol, SAMA has the lowest mean absolute error (MAE) averaged over datasets at every protocol level: 4.40 against 5.13,bpm for POS per video, 4.80 against 5.43 on 30,s windows and 5.05 against 6.59 on 10,s windows. Pooled over participants, the bootstrap interval of its paired difference from POS excludes zero on 30,s and 10,s windows (Wilcoxon p=0.11 and 0.004) but not per video, and it does not beat POS on SUMS per video or on the MMPD subset. Withholding the 20\% least confident windows lowers the pooled 10,s error from 4.06 to 2.33,bpm. We also show that the per-video protocol has a floor of 1.2--1.6,bpm on datasets where heart rate drifts, and that skin tone cannot be recovered from uncalibrated video, which motivates the instrumented, skin-tone-stratified collection we propose for the Indian population. The method does not surpass the best published neural networks on UBFC-rPPG; we report where it stands against them without re-running them.

</details>


### [219] [torchtsetlin: Fast, Interpretable Tsetlin Machines for PyTorch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28195)
*Vajira Thambawita,Pål Halvorsen,Ole-Christoffer Granmo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: torchtsetlin 将 Tsetlin 机家族实现为普通 PyTorch 模块，以批量化的 Type I/Type II 反馈（自动机转移转为事件计数并用二项抽样实现）为核心技术，在十个基准上持平现有库的精度，并在同一 GPU 上大幅加速训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tsetlin 机用 Tsetlin 自动机以奖励-惩罚（而非梯度）学习命题规则，模型天然可解释且推理廉价；但其现有软件是 C 与 CUDA 内核外加 scikit-learn 风格封装，游离于深度学习生态之外，难以与主流框架的模块化、设备迁移与训练流程集成。

Method: 把 Tsetlin 机家族（vanilla、weighted、coalesced、回归、序列/图像/体数据的卷积版本、逐像素密集分割）封装为常规 PyTorch 模块；自动机状态存为张量缓冲区，从而支持 .to(device)、state_dict() 存取，并以一次 update(x,y) 完成一个小批量更新。核心技术是 Type I 与 Type II 反馈的批量形式化：将自动机转移表述为事件计数，用二项分布抽样实现，每个小批量只提交一次更新。

Result: 在取自现有库的十个基准上，精度与现有实现相当；除两个最小基准外，在同一 GPU 上训练速度比 PyTsetlinMachineCUDA 快 2.4–10.9 倍，比 tmu 快 10.3–81.2 倍。软件为纯 Python、MIT 许可、发布于 PyPI，论文中每个数值均可由单一脚本复现。

Conclusion: torchtsetlin 证明可在不牺牲精度的情况下，将 Tsetlin 机无缝融入 PyTorch 生态，并借助批量化的转移计数与二项抽样获得显著加速，为可解释规则学习提供了实用、可复现的工程方案。

Abstract: The Tsetlin machine learns propositional rules with teams of Tsetlin automata that are driven by reward and penalty rather than gradients, so its models are interpretable by construction and cheap to evaluate. Its software, however, consists of C and CUDA kernels behind scikit-learn-style wrappers that sit outside the deep learning ecosystem. torchtsetlin implements the Tsetlin machine family as ordinary PyTorch modules: vanilla and weighted, coalesced, regression, convolutional over sequences, images and volumes, and dense per-pixel segmentation. The automata are a tensor buffer, so a model moves with .to(device), is saved with state_dict() and learns from a mini-batch with one update(x,y) call. Its core technique is a batched formulation of Type I and Type II feedback, in which automaton transitions become event counts realised as binomial draws, with one commit per mini-batch. On ten benchmarks taken from the existing libraries, it matches their accuracy, and on all but the two smallest it trains 2.4-10.9X faster than PyTsetlinMachineCUDA and 10.3-81.2X faster than tmu on the same GPU. The package is pure Python, MIT-licensed and on PyPI; every number in this paper is reproduced by one script. Code: https://github.com/vlbthambawita/torchtsetlin; package: https://pypi.org/project/torchtsetlin/.

</details>


### [220] [From Point Clouds to Engineering Models: Benchmarking Agentic 3D Reconstruction in the Built Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28197)
*Changyu Du,Stavros Nousias,Syed Salman Ahmed,Kai Dietmair,Mohammad Alrabab'h,André Borrmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将场景级 Scan-to-BIM 形式化为"智能体化 3D 编码任务"，并发布 S2Bench 基准，含 5 个领域 128 个真实场景的公开与私有扫描及专家标注参考模型；实验显示几何拟合易、语义与工程约束推理难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体可将 3D 测量转为 CAD 几何，但建成环境的工程模型重建需要语义解释与一致的构造约束，超出表面拟合范畴；缺少针对该任务的系统性评测基准。

Method: 构建 S2Bench：128 个真实场景（5 个领域）扫描配专家精修参考模型，覆盖多种空间尺度。提供统一框架，使智能体可用 Python 测量扫描、在实时 CAD/BIM 内核中构建带类别标签的构件并迭代改进代码；另提出并行异步协作范式——由 lead 分派版本化任务、在 worker 执行时继续建模，并整合受修订约束的提案。评测 5 个智能体与 1 个协作团队，共 1,028 次重建。

Result: 扫描-模型残差可直接指导几何修复，但语义类别与构造关系推断需几何、视觉与工程联合推理，智能体难以落地与验证。最佳智能体在 5cm 内覆盖 96%，但 mIoU 仅 63%；无智能体在超过 38% 的场景上通过全部工程检查。覆盖率与工程一致性随场景规模下降；以团队方式运行较小模型可三方面同时提升，且在大场景上覆盖增益更大。

Conclusion: Scan-to-BIM 的关键瓶颈已从几何拟合转向语义与工程约束的可验证推理；多智能体异步协作是有效改进方向，S2Bench 为后续研究提供标准化评测平台。

Abstract: Coding agents can translate 3D measurements into CAD geometry, but reconstructing engineering models for the built environment demands semantic interpretation and consistent construction constraints beyond surface fitting. We formulate scene-level Scan-to-BIM (Building Information Modeling) as an agentic 3D coding task and introduce S2Bench, comprising public and proprietary scans of 128 real-world scenes across five domains, paired with expert-curated reference models spanning diverse spatial scales. A shared framework lets agents measure scans in Python, build class-labeled components in a live CAD/BIM kernel, and iteratively refine their code. We also propose parallel asynchronous collaboration, where a lead delegates versioned tasks, continues modeling while workers execute, and integrates revision-bound proposals. Evaluating five agents and a collaborative team across 1,028 reconstructions shows that scan-to-model residuals directly guide geometric repair, whereas inferring semantic classes and construction relations requires joint geometric, visual, and engineering reasoning that agents struggle to ground and verify. Consequently, the leading agent fits scan geometry closely ($96\%$ coverage within $5\,\text{cm}$) yet reaches only $63\%$ mIoU, and no agent passes all engineering checks on more than $38\%$ of scenes. Coverage and engineering conformance drop with scene scale, whereas running a smaller model as a team improves all three axes with greater coverage gains on larger scenes. The project page is available at https://tumcms.github.io/S2Bench.github.io/

</details>


### [221] [AI-Assisted Auto-formalization for Trustworthy Scientific Computing: Case Studies and Lessons Learned](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31033)
*Mohit Tekriwal,Het Mankad,Naifeng Zhang,Keita Teranishi,Sahil Bhola,Daisuke Uchida,Jean-Baptiste Jeannin,Franz Franchetti,Karthik Duraisamy,Jeffrey Vetter,Yohann Dudouit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文系统研究了AI智能体在科学计算（SC）领域的形式化自动生成能力，通过跨软件栈抽象层的案例研究，提出失败模式的跨层分类法，并分析各层所需专家反馈形式，进而给出可复用证明模式的工作流与实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic AI在数学推理上的成功主要集中于纯数学：定理陈述自足、表示固定、形式化库支持充分。而应用数学（尤其科学计算）的源材料常描述计算过程或建模变换而非声明式定理，形式化难度更高，且缺乏系统性认识其可行性与失败原因。

Method: 对科学计算软件栈中多个层级抽象层的案例展开研究，使用通用编程智能体与专用数学智能体进行AI辅助形式化，评估其能否完成数值证明，并定位失败发生的层级与成因；归纳跨层失败模式分类，分析恢复数学严谨性所需的专家反馈形式。

Result: 识别出跨抽象层的一致失败模式类别，明确各层对专家反馈的不同需求，并从中抽出面向常见证明模式的形式化工作流。

Conclusion: AI辅助形式化在可信科学计算中具备实际价值，但当前存在明确局限；应按层配置专家反馈并遵循结构化工作流以保持数学严谨性。

Abstract: Agentic AI tools have recently had success in mathematical reasoning. Both general purpose coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex, and specialized mathematics agents such as Aristotle, have been used for formalizing mathematical problems such as Fermat's last theorem, Erdős problems and several IMO level math problems. However, much of these efforts are concentrated in the pure mathematics domain, which benefits from relatively self-contained theorem statements, fixed mathematical representation and extensive support in existing formal libraries. Applied mathematics, on the other hand, presents additional challenges because the source may describe a computational procedure or modeling transformation rather than a declarative theorem. In this work, we present a systematic study of AI-assisted auto-formalization for scientific computing (SC), a domain in applied mathematics central to U.S. Department of Energy applications. Through case studies spanning hierarchical abstraction layers in SC software stack, we evaluate not only whether AI agents can complete numerical proofs, but also where and why they fail across the stack. Our primary contribution is a cross-layer taxonomy of failure modes and an analysis of the form of expert feedback required at each layer to recover mathematical soundness. From these observations, we derive a workflow for recurring proof patterns and practical guidance for using AI agents while preserving mathematical soundness. Our results characterize both the practical value and the current limitations of AI-assisted formalization for trustworthy scientific computing.

</details>


### [222] [AutoPILOT: A Visual Agent Harness for Real-Time Gameplay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28202)
*Mark Zhao,Pengcheng Wang,Rui Yang,Jiarui Yao,Xirui Jiang,Yuxi Chen,Bingxuan Li,Hanyang Chen,Ke Yang,Manling Li,Tong Zhang,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多模态基础模型在游戏暂停时表现良好，但实时游戏中失败；作者指出瓶颈在"harness"如何将模型输出转化为随时间延续的动作，提出AutoPILOT与AFK-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有harness（如VISTA）改善了"看什么"和"记什么"，但每次模型调用最多产生一个动作，使智能体执行频率受限于模型思考频率，无法应对不等待的实时游戏。

Method: AutoPILOT改变"输出如何变为动作"：每次调用可发送原始动作、启动开环宏，或编写闭环控制器直接在画面上持续操作，直到交还控制权。并构建AFK-Bench，含120款浏览器游戏，按对"不作为"的响应分组，支持实时与暂停两种模式。

Result: 同等预算下GPT-6.1 Sol使用AutoPILOT实时成功率68.6%，VISTA为38.3%；GameWorld上保持80.0%，VISTA从84.1%降至42.9%；跨模型增益稳健，以约五分之一API成本达到VISTA最终成功率。

Conclusion: harness将模型输出随时间转化为动作的机制是智能体设计中核心却未被充分探索的部分；AutoPILOT与AFK-Bench可为"不等待的世界"中的智能体奠定基础。

Abstract: Multimodal foundation models can play video games well when the game waits for them, but often fail when it keeps running. We find that much of this gap lies in the harness, which decides what the model sees, what it remembers, and how its outputs become actions over time. Existing harnesses such as VISTA improve the first two, yet turn each model call into at most one action, so the agent acts only as often as its model thinks. We introduce AutoPILOT, which changes the third: in each model call, the model can send a primitive action, start an open-loop macro, or write a closed-loop controller that keeps playing from the screen without further model calls until it hands control back. To evaluate agents in real-time games, we build AFK-Bench, 120 diverse browser games grouped by how they respond to inaction and playable in real time or with the clock paused. With GPT-6.1 Sol under the same budgets, AutoPILOT reaches 68.6% real-time success against 38.3% for VISTA, and on GameWorld it keeps 80.0% in real time while VISTA drops from 84.1% to 42.9%. Its gains hold across models, and it reaches VISTA's final success rate at a fifth of its API cost. Our results suggest that how a harness turns model outputs into actions over time is a central yet underexplored part of agent design, and we hope AutoPILOT and AFK-Bench will serve as a foundation for agents in a world that does not wait.

</details>


### [223] [FinEvo: Recursive Self-Improvement for Long-Horizon Financial Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28239)
*Chengjun Pan,Shichun Liu,Zekai Zhou,Shihan Dou,Jiahang Lin,Chengsong Huang,Dingwei Zhu,Tao Gui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinEvo：一个将自主经验构建与金融能力提升相结合的自进化框架，通过自博弈迭代生成挑战、以可执行奖励学习，实现金融建模能力的持续增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已深度参与金融分析与建模等高价值任务，同时具备自我改进潜力。作者由此提出两个互补问题：金融建模能力能否为模型自进化提供基础，自进化又能否反过来提升金融能力。意在专业领域实证探索递归自我改进（RSI）。

Method: FinEvo全程使用同一模型：先准备金融模型，再通过自博弈迭代演化任务挑战，并利用执行结果产生的可执行奖励（executable rewards）从交互中学习；端到端执行验证基于Qwen3.6-27B。求解反馈同时支撑推理难度递增与可执行检查难以发现的规格（specification）问题识别。

Result: 学习这些经验后，留出同源任务整任务成功率由22.73提升至29.09；SpreadsheetBench 2 Financial上由13.13提升至19.19。不同任务来源与评测设置下增益相当，且训练未涉及的任务类别也有提升。

Conclusion: FinEvo增强了跨任务共享的底层金融建模能力，表明自进化可成为通向更广泛金融胜任力的路径，推进了专业领域内递归自我改进的实证研究。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) have expanded their role in high-value productivity tasks, including financial analysis and modeling, while enabling models to play an increasingly active role in their own improvement. This motivates two complementary lines of inquiry: can financial modeling capabilities provide a foundation for model self-evolution, and can self-evolution, in turn, advance financial capabilities? We introduce FinEvo, a self-evolution framework that integrates autonomous experience construction with financial capability improvement. FinEvo prepares financial models, iteratively evolves challenges through self-play, and learns from the resulting interactions using executable rewards, relying on the same model throughout. An end-to-end execution with Qwen3.6-27B shows that solving feedback supports both increasing reasoning demands and identifying specification issues that executable checks can miss. Learning from these experiences raises whole-task success from 22.73 to 29.09 on held-out within-source tasks and from 13.13 to 19.19 on SpreadsheetBench 2 Financial. Comparable gains across task sources and evaluation settings, together with improvement on a task category absent from training, suggest that FinEvo strengthens underlying financial modeling capabilities shared across tasks. These findings support self-evolution as a path toward broader financial competence, advancing the empirical exploration of recursive self-improvement (RSI) in professional domains. Code is available at https://github.com/pcj-sudo/FinEvo.

</details>


### [224] [Demystifying the Autoresearch Harness: How Context and Skills Shape Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28244)
*Kelvin Kiu-Wai Tam,Newt Hue-Nam K. Nguyen,Tianshi Zheng,Yunxiang Mo,Weihan Li,Zhongwei Xie,Chiu Yeung Tang,Jui-Yu Ho,Yangqiu Song,Ginny Y. Wong,Simon See*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 系统性拆解 autoresearch 智能体的 harness 设计，提出上下文折叠与技能删除两项设计原则，并据此构建 MLEvolver，在 MLE-Bench Lite 与 KernelBench 上显著超越 AIDE 和 OpenEvolve。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 autoresearch 智能体将多种机制耦合于统一 harness 中，难以归因单个组件对性能的贡献，因此需要受控地研究长程、自演化场景下的 harness 设计。

Method: 沿两个维度隔离变量：上下文管理（保留历史尝试的方式）与技能管理（存储与复用经验教训的方式）。在统一智能体框架内，以“近期截断”为基线，在 MLE-Bench、KernelBench 数据集与 gpt-oss-120b、gpt-6-luna、gpt-6-sol 三个模型组成的六种设置下逐一评测各设计选择。

Result: 上下文管理方面，保留可独立访问的历次尝试记录优于滚动摘要，其中上下文折叠（context folding）在全部六种设置中最佳，较基线最高提升 19.2%；技能管理方面，选择性删除长期未使用的经验平均而言优于合并或升格为常驻规则。融合两者构建的 MLEvolver 在 MLE-Bench Lite 上达到 72.7% 奖牌率，在 KernelBench Level 3 上达 94% 的 fast_1 分数，同等推理预算下大幅优于 AIDE 与 OpenEvolve。

Conclusion: 研究提炼出 autoresearch harness 中“保留实验历史”与“筛选可复用经验”的设计原则，为将累积经验转化为持续进展的智能体奠定基础。

Abstract: Autoresearch agents, which autonomously design, run, and refine experiments, are accelerating scientific progress, particularly in AI research. However, existing agents rely on harnesses that combine multiple mechanisms, making it difficult to determine how individual components contribute to performance. We systematically study harness design for long-horizon, self-evolving autoresearch agents along two dimensions: context management, which determines what an agent retains from its past attempts, and skill management, which governs how it stores and reuses lessons from those attempts. Across MLE-Bench and KernelBench with gpt-oss-120b, gpt-6-luna, and gpt-6-sol, we isolate the effects of individual harness design choices by evaluating each against a recency-truncation baseline within a shared agent framework. For context management, we find that preserving individually accessible records of past attempts is more effective than maintaining a rolling summary, with context folding performing best across all six settings and improving upon the baseline by up to 19.2%. For skill management, selectively discarding persistently unused lessons is more effective on average than consolidating them or promoting them to standing rules. Building on these findings, we combine context folding and skill deletion in MLEvolver, which achieves a 72.7% medal rate on MLE-Bench Lite and a 94% $\mathrm{fast}_1$ score on KernelBench Level 3, substantially outperforming AIDE and OpenEvolve under the same inference budget. Overall, our study distills design principles for preserving experimental history and curating reusable lessons in autoresearch harnesses, paving the way for agents that turn accumulated experience into sustained progress.

</details>


### [225] [A Nepali-Accented English Evaluation Dataset for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28263)
*Santosh Dahal,Kiran Chandra Dahal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个尼泊尔口音英语的 ASR 评测数据集，基于 Web 平台采集 57 名说话人录音，采用固定 22 提示协议，并给出开源 ASR 基线的显著性能退化证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ASR 在原生英语基准上表现优异，但面对欠代表非母语口音时准确率急剧下降；尼泊尔口音英语尤为缺乏专门资源——现有资源或聚焦母语尼泊尔语、或覆盖多口音但无专门尼泊尔评测、或尼泊尔口音覆盖有限。

Method: 通过不采集直接身份元数据的 Web 平台采集 57 名说话人录音；每场会话遵循固定 22 提示协议（11 个音素提示、10 个领域提示、1 个即兴提示），覆盖发音、主题词汇与自然语体；提供转写文本、用于粗略探索性子群分析的口音元数据，以及说话人级人工录音质量标签。

Result: 人工质量评估显示 50.9% 会话为干净、42.1% 仅含轻微噪声；开源 ASR 基线在该语料上的 WER 为 38.15%–55.00%，远差于官方模型卡中 LibriSpeech test-clean 的 2–4% WER。

Conclusion: 该语料可作为评估尼泊尔口音英语口音鲁棒性与分布外泛化能力的实用留出资源。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems perform strongly on native-English benchmarks, yet their accuracy degrades sharply when the input speech comes from under represented non-native accents. Nepali-accented English is particularly under-served: existing resources either focus on native Nepali speech, cover broader multi-accent settings without dedicated Nepali evaluation, or provide only limited Nepali-accent coverage. This paper presents a Nepali-accented English evaluation dataset designed to support robust ASR benchmarking under accent mismatch. The corpus was collected through a web-based platform that did not collect directly identifying metadata and contains recordings from 57 speakers. Each session follows a fixed 22-prompt protocol consisting of 11 phonetic prompts, 10 domain prompts, and 1 spontaneous prompt, providing complementary coverage of pronunciation, topical vocabulary, and natural speaking style. In addition to transcribed speech, the dataset includes participant metadata for coarse exploratory subgroup analysis and speaker-level manual recording-quality labels. Manual quality assessment shows that 50.9% of sessions are clean and 42.1% contain only mild noise. As a descriptive reference, open-source ASR baselines are substantially worse on this corpus than the corresponding LibriSpeech test-clean values reported in official model cards, reaching 38.15-55.00% WER on the collected set versus reported 2-4% WER on LibriSpeech test-clean. These baseline results position the corpus as a practical held-out resource for evaluating accent robustness and out-of-distribution generalization on Nepali-accented English.

</details>


### [226] [Temporal Logic Tensor Networks: Foundations of Temporal Reasoning in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28277)
*Edjard Mota,Artur d'Avila Garcez,Maria Azevedo,Odalisio Neto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出tLTN（Temporal Logic Tensor Networks），一个可微神经符号框架，通过多排序形式逻辑统一"点态"与"区间"两种时间语义：事件为一等实体，具可学习梯形模糊时长与可微Allen区间关系；状态谓词被grounding为时间点上的模糊真值路径，二者相互约束，融合成单一可微LTN grounding。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态场景（足球、交通流、手术、装配）中动作发生在根本不同的时间尺度上，毫秒级瞬间与长时区间事件共存。仅用视觉数据训练的模型难以跨时间尺度有效学习，缺乏在细粒度瞬间与较宽区间之间推理的原则性机制。纯点态语义把事件碎片化为帧级快照、丢失整体事件辨识；纯区间语义把区间视为原子真值、忽略状态轨迹，二者均不充分。

Method: 扩展Interval Logic Tensor Networks（ILTN）提出tLTN：采用多排序（multi-sorted）形式逻辑，事件与状态在结构上相互依赖；状态谓词grounding为随时间点演化的模糊真值路径（state trajectories）；事件作为一等实体，具有可学习的梯形模糊持续时间（trapezoidal fuzzy durations）与可微的Allen式区间关系；通过组合语义将静态、事件结构、状态动态三类满足度融合进单一可微LTN grounding。双向约束机制：区间层决定点态约束（Until、Since等）的求值范围，将其限制在事件定义的跨度内；点态层反过来验证区间关系，若底层状态动态与之矛盾则拒绝该关系。

Result: 通过受控合成实验表明：单一模态（仅点态或仅区间）学习在特定情形下会失败；所提出的定位/局部化机制能够降低长时序轨迹上的帧级计算开销。

Conclusion: tLTN以可微、多排序的时间逻辑形式化地弥合了点态与区间语义之间的二元对立，使事件结构与状态动态相互约束，为跨时间尺度的动作/事件推理提供了原则性且计算更高效的神经符号方案。

Abstract: In dynamic scenes---football matches, traffic streams, surgery, or assembly---actions unfold at fundamentally different time scales: in football, a kick happens in milliseconds, a possession lasts seconds, and complex events such as a successful pass followed by a goal combine multiple atomic actions over extended durations. Models trained solely on visual data struggle to learn effectively across different time scales, lacking a principled way of reasoning across fine-grained instants and broader intervals. Neither point-wise nor purely interval semantics suffices: the former fragments events into frame-level snapshots lacking overall event identification; the latter treats intervals as atomic truth-values, ignoring state trajectories. We introduce Temporal Logic Tensor Networks (tLTN), a differentiable neuro-symbolic framework addressing this duality via a formal, multi-sorted logic where events and states are structurally interdependent. Extending Interval Logic Tensor Networks (ILTN), tLTN grounds state predicates as fuzzy truth-value paths over time points, while events are first-class entities with learnable trapezoidal fuzzy durations and differentiable Allen-style relations. A compositional semantics fuses static, event-structural, and state-dynamic satisfaction into a single differentiable LTN grounding. Crucially, the modalities constrain each other bidirectionally: the interval layer dictates where point-wise constraints (e.g., Until, Since) are evaluated, restricting them to event-defined spans; conversely, the point-wise layer validates interval relations, rejecting them if underlying state dynamics contradict the relation. Through controlled synthetic examples, we show when single-modality learning fails and how this localization reduces frame-level computational cost on long traces.

</details>


### [227] [Specialized Large Language Models for Markovian Reasoning: Structural Cache Compression, Transition Pretraining, and Dual-Loop Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28285)
*Haibin Tong,Liangliang Xin,Yamei Zhu,Jiang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出面向马尔可夫式推理的专用大语言模型统一设计范式，通过状态接口与双环Transformer架构，使显存与推理链长度解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归LLM需对全部生成历史做注意力，KV缓存成为长链推理与多分支采样中的核心显存与吞吐瓶颈；而许多推理问题本质是一阶马尔可夫的，下一步仅依赖当前状态的充分表示，而非全部原始历史。

Method: 三层设计：(1) 缓存层——马尔可夫状态接口，将历史依赖的KV存储转为与推理步数无关的常数大小状态传递；(2) 学习层——将长链预测重构为可独立训练的单步转移目标，配合显式状态瓶颈、蒸馏、不变性与漂移一致性损失；(3) 架构层——双环Transformer，外环推进推理状态，内环以循环深度按共享权重与显式记忆token/循环矩阵状态为每次转移分配自适应算力。并衔接三区缓存、步相对位置编码、截断反传、分支感知检查点与以转移为中心的评价协议。

Result: 摘要为范式性论述，未报告具体实验数据；所提出的机制在原理上使显存占用与推理链长度解耦，并通过三区缓存与分支感知检查点支持多分支采样与长链推理。

Conclusion: 核心原则是：最优推理系统无需记住一切，只需精确携带未来所需信息。该原则为构建记忆开销与推理链长度解耦的专用推理引擎提供了路径。

Abstract: Standard autoregressive large language models attend to the growing history of generated tokens, making the key-value (KV) cache a central memory and throughput bottleneck in long-chain reasoning and multi-branch sampling. Yet an important class of reasoning problems is naturally first-order Markovian: the next step depends on a sufficient representation of the current state, rather than on the raw sequence of all earlier steps. We develop a unified design paradigm for Markovian-reasoning specialized large language models. At the cache level, the Markovian state interface transforms history-dependent KV storage into constant-size state transfer with respect to the number of reasoning steps. At the learning level, we reformulate long-chain prediction as independently trainable one-step transition objectives, with explicit state bottlenecks, distillation, invariance, and drift-consistency losses. At the architectural level, we introduce a dual-loop Transformer: an outer loop advances the reasoning state, while an inner recurrent-depth loop allocates adaptive computation to each transition through shared weights and explicit memory tokens or recurrent matrix states. We connect this architecture to a three-region cache, step-relative positions, truncated backpropagation, branch-aware checkpoints, and a transition-centric evaluation protocol. The organizing principle is simple: the optimal reasoning system need not remember everything; it must carry forward exactly what the future needs. This principle opens a path toward specialized reasoning engines whose memory footprint is decoupled from reasoning-chain length.

</details>


### [228] [Feedback-Driven Prompt Refinement for Educational AI: Affective Trajectory Estimation and Bloom's Taxonomy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28293)
*Josh Dean,Karan Patel,Gaurangi Raul,Yu-Zheng Lin,Jesus Pacheco,Sicong Shao,Shalaka Satam,Soheil Salehi,Pratik Satam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在固定执行器参数的前提下，由独立反馈信号引导的共享提示精炼流程可同时提升情感参考一致性与布鲁姆分类性能，并降低生成波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业4.0推动跨计算与工程领域的个性化技术教育需求，但提示敏感性与生成波动性妨碍教学对话与学习结果的可靠解读。

Method: 由次级语言模型依据执行器输出与参考之间的差异修订执行器提示：情感侧使用4段对话的模型生成情感参考，认知侧使用70条学习结果的布鲁姆标注；另以100段EduTalk对话与1,000条结果做留出对比，执行器参数不更新。

Result: 情感平均绝对误差由0.742降至0.702，生成标准差均值由0.307降至0.238；布鲁姆微F1由0.687升至0.893，精确匹配子集准确率由0.418升至0.636。

Conclusion: 提示精炼可作为开发自适应技术教学的候选路径，但其对学习者与劳动力学习成效的影响尚待验证；精炼集规模小、情感参考未经验证、缺乏学习者结果测量限制了结论解释。

Abstract: Contribution: This study has shown that a shared prompt-refinement procedure, guided by distinct affective and cognitive feedback signals, can improve held-out reference agreement and multi-label classification while reducing affective-generation variability without executor parameter updates. Background: Industry 4.0 has increased demand for personalized technical education across interconnected computing and engineering domains, while prompt sensitivity and generation variability have complicated dependable interpretation of instructional dialogue and learning outcomes. Research Questions: The study has examined whether task-specific feedback can improve affective-reference agreement, generation consistency, and cognitive classification without updating executor parameters. Methodology: A secondary language model has revised executor prompts using executor-output discrepancies against model-generated affective references for four dialogues and annotated Bloom labels for 70 learning outcomes; separate sets of 100 EduTalk conversations and 1,000 outcomes have supported held-out comparisons. Small refinement sets, unvalidated affective references, and absent learner-outcome measures have constrained interpretation. Findings: Affective mean absolute error has decreased from 0.742 to 0.702 and mean generation standard deviation from 0.307 to 0.238, while Bloom micro-F1 has increased from 0.687 to 0.893 and exact-match subset accuracy from 0.418 to 0.636. These findings have supported prompt refinement as a candidate approach for developing adaptive technical instruction, while its effects on workforce learning remain to be established.

</details>


### [229] [GERF: A Token-Efficient Multimodal Retrieval Framework for Schema-Guided Scientific Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28295)
*Yiyu Qian,Xing Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GERF：以异构证据图为骨架、以schema字段为检索锚点的多模态科学信息抽取框架，实现高覆盖、低token的证据检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学IE是AI for Science的基础环节，但关键实验细节分散于正文、表格、图、图注及讨论中，纯文本抽取难以恢复；现有LLM与多模态RAG依赖全文上下文或粗粒度页级检索，引入大量无关证据且文本/视觉token开销高。

Method: 将每篇文章建模为异构证据图，节点涵盖schema字段、文本块、图及由图衍生的内容，边为语义与结构关系；以schema字段为检索锚点，融合直接语义检索、随机游走重启（RWR）与null-aware证据扩展，构造紧凑的字段级多模态证据包；仅对图检索选中的图进行视觉解读，避免全文盲目处理。评估从证据检索质量与流水线输入token消耗两个互补维度展开。

Result: 在专家标注的SOFC-Exp语料上，GERF对94.55%的待评估字段实例检索到金标支持的证据，具备较宽的字段级证据覆盖；token方面，相比全文上下文处理平均降低73.40%输入token，且少于所评估的页级流水线。

Conclusion: 基于图的证据检索可在保证高字段级证据覆盖率的同时显著压缩多模态输入开销，为schema引导、token高效的科学信息抽取提供可行路径。

Abstract: Scientific information extraction (IE) is a fundamental component of AI for Science, transforming experimental knowledge embedded in scientific literature into structured and traceable data for evidence synthesis, scientific database construction, and data-driven discovery. However, critical experimental details are often distributed across text, tables, figures, captions, and surrounding discussion, making them difficult to recover using conventional text-only extraction. Existing LLM-based and multimodal RAG pipelines typically rely on full-document context or coarse page-level retrieval, introducing substantial irrelevant evidence and high textual and visual token costs. We propose GERF, a Graph-based Evidence Retrieval Framework for token-efficient, schema-guided multimodal scientific IE. GERF represents each article as a heterogeneous evidence graph that connects schema fields, text chunks, figures, and figure-derived content through semantic and structural relations. Using schema fields as retrieval anchors, GERF combines direct semantic retrieval, Random Walk with Restart, and null-aware evidence expansion to construct compact, field-specific multimodal evidence packages. Visual interpretation is applied only to graph-selected figures, avoiding indiscriminate processing of the entire document. We evaluate GERF from two complementary perspectives: evidence retrieval quality and pipeline-level input-token consumption. On the expert-annotated SOFC-Exp corpus, GERF retrieves gold-supported evidence for 94.55% of the evaluated field instances, providing broad field-level evidence coverage for downstream extraction. In the token comparison, GERF reduces average input-token usage by 73.40% relative to full-context processing and uses fewer tokens than the evaluated page-level pipelines.

</details>


### [230] [INSEP: Refusal-Direction Inseparability as a Defense Against Abliteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28314)
*Ivan Brugere,Junqi Jiang,Saumitra Mishra,Sumitra Ganesh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 INSEP：通过微调降低有害/良性激活在拒绝方向上的可分离性，从而防御 abliteration（消融拒绝方向的白盒攻击），在 8 个模型、5 种攻击下综合得分居首。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Abliteration 是广泛使用的白盒安全对齐攻击：它在激活空间中估计"拒绝方向"（类均值差），并从残差流写出矩阵中投影去除该方向，从而诱发有害行为。现有防御缺乏针对该类攻击的系统性方案与全面评测。

Method: INSEP 微调模型以削弱有害与良性激活沿拒绝方向的可分离性，进而削弱攻击所依赖的信号：(1) 用对抗性特征分解挑选最具可分离性的提示子集；(2) 用低秩适配器（LoRA 类）降低这些样本在该方向上的分离度；(3) 加入行为损失，训练中保持基座模型的拒答能力与良性回答质量。

Result: 在 8 个 1B–14B 参数、多模型家族的 LLM 上，对 6 种已发表防御与 5 种攻击进行基准测试；除回答危害性外还评估连贯性、直接性、过度拒答与 MMLU，以区分"鲁棒拒答"与"因回避/退化导致的低危害"。INSEP 在全部 8 个基座模型的平均综合得分上排名第一，并保持实用性。这是目前规模最大的 abliteration 防御评测。

Conclusion: 降低拒绝方向上的类间可分离性是一种有效且低代价的 abliteration 防御思路，可在保持模型效用与拒答质量的同时显著提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: Abliteration is a widely used white-box attack against the safety alignment of large language models (LLMs). It elicits harmful behavior by estimating a refusal direction in the model's activation space and projecting that direction out of the residual-stream write-out matrices. We propose INSEP, a defense that fine-tunes the model to reduce the separability of harmful and benign activations along the refusal direction. Abliteration estimates this direction as a difference in class means and projects it out. Reducing separability weakens the signal the attack relies on. An adversarial eigendecomposition selects the most separable prompt subsets. A low-rank adapter reduces their separation along that direction. Behavioral losses preserve the base model's refusal and the quality of its benign responses during training. We benchmark INSEP against six published defenses under five attacks, across eight LLMs from 1B to 14B parameters and several model families. Beyond response harm, we measure coherence, directness, over-refusal, and MMLU, to distinguish robust refusal from low harm produced by evasive or degraded responses. INSEP ranks first on the mean composite score across all eight base models while retaining utility, in the largest abliteration-defense evaluation reported to date.

</details>


### [231] [Selection Policies for Equal-Byte Mixed-Precision Quantization of Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28318)
*Smit Anand*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在参数量不变的前提下，用遗传算法（二元锦标赛父代选择）重新分配2/4/8比特量化位宽，可在Qwen3-1.7B上稳定降低留出集NLL，但未带来ARC-Challenge准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在参数存储量不变的前提下，改变哪些矩阵分配2/4/8比特，能否提升小型语言模型性能；并比较不同父代选择规则在同一遗传算法框架下的效果差异。

Method: 同一遗传算法中比较两种父代选择规则：二元锦标赛变体与Alternis启发式规则；另设随机分配基线（修复后随机采样，无交叉/变异）。三者共用同一MLX仿射量化器、字节上限、初始化与候选配额。探索性研究：Qwen3.5-2B，每次运行90个分配；主研究：Qwen3-1.7B，每次运行500个不同分配，10个配对随机种子。以均匀4比特为对照。

Result: 相对均匀4比特，锦标赛在等参数存储下于AG News降低留出集NLL平均0.0616、IMDb降低0.0152，且两个语料上10个种子全部改善；Alternis在AG News上5个种子改善、5个持平，IMDb上平均增益近零；随机基线在每个种子上均退回均匀4比特水平。代表性模型在ARC-Challenge上答对156/400，均匀4比特为163/400，未显示准确率提升。

Conclusion: 在测试协议下，该锦标赛变体有利于文本预测（NLL），但并非在压缩比或任务准确率上取得收益。

Abstract: Can changing which matrices receive 2, 4, or 8 bits improve a small language model without increasing its parameter storage? I compare two parent-selection rules in the same genetic algorithm: a binary-tournament variant and an Alternis-inspired rule. A separate baseline samples repaired random allocations without crossover or mutation. All three use the same MLX affine quantizer, byte ceiling, initialization and candidate allowance within each study. An exploratory study on Qwen3.5-2B evaluates 90 allocations per run. The main study on Qwen3-1.7B evaluates 500 distinct allocations per run over 10 paired seeds. Against uniform four-bit, tournament reduces held-out next-token negative log-likelihood (NLL) by 0.0616 on AG News and 0.0152 on IMDb on average, improving all 10 seeds on each corpus at identical parameter storage. Alternis improves five seeds and ties five on AG News; on IMDb its mean gain is near zero. Random returns uniform four-bit in every seed. The tournament representative answers 156 of 400 ARC-Challenge questions correctly versus 163 for uniform; no accuracy gain is demonstrated on this subset. The result favors this tournament variant for text prediction under the tested protocol, not for compression ratio or task accuracy.

</details>


### [232] [MatHarness: Towards Long-Horizon Open-ended AutoResearch for Computational Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28324)
*Yanlin Fei,Lei Li,Shaolong Chen,Nazhou Liu,Xinmiao Yu,Madalina Ciobanu,Navan Preet Singh,Qingqing Mao,Ritankar Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MatHarness——面向开放式、长周期计算材料研究的智能体框架，并在 Cu-Zn 合金吸附物诱导表面偏析这一开放问题上完成从构思到成稿的全流程验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料科学研究是非线性、长周期过程，而现有科学智能体多依赖特定任务执行或预定义工作流，在开放式场景下脆弱、缺乏适应性。

Method: MatHarness 耦合三类机制：(1) 执行鲁棒性——预检校验（pre-flight verification）、专家蒸馏技能、即时审计（just-in-time auditing）；(2) 认知适应性——持久化研究工件与持续演化的科学心智模型；(3) 集体智能——异构多智能体圆桌讨论，配合有针对性的人类监督。评估任务为 Cu-Zn 合金中吸附物诱导的表面偏析（甲醇合成催化中的开放问题）。

Result: 多周轨迹、约 10^3 GPU 小时（171 次机器学习势筛选后执行 50 个生产级 DFT 弛豫）；系统六次修改研究计划，自主从失败计算中恢复，独立审计员在发表前推翻了一条过度归因的结论。在采样的金属稀释极限模型内，DFT 分析支持两个成分端点处吸附物诱导表面 Cu 暴露增强的趋势，但存在晶面与覆盖度相关的例外。

Conclusion: MatHarness 可在人类仅参与需求定义、审批与战略引导节点的情况下，独立支撑长周期、开放式材料研究并产出完整稿件；代码已开源（https://github.com/PrentisAI/pippin）。

Abstract: Materials science research is a non-linear, long-horizon process. Existing scientific agents, however, largely rely on specific task execution or predefined workflows, rendering them brittle in open-ended settings. To bridge this gap, we present MatHarness, an agent harness designed for open-ended, long-horizon computational materials research spanning the complete lifecycle from initial ideation to manuscript writing. MatHarness couples three mechanisms: execution robustness through pre-flight verification, expert-distilled skills, and just-in-time auditing; epistemic adaptability through persistent research artifacts and an evolving scientific mental model; and collective intelligence through a heterogeneous multi-agent round table under targeted human oversight. We evaluate MatHarness on adsorbate-induced surface segregation in Cu-Zn alloys, an open question in methanol-synthesis catalysis. Over a multi-week trajectory consuming roughly 10^3 GPU-hours (50 production DFT relaxations prescreened by 171 machine-learning-potential evaluations) the system revised its plan six times, recovered autonomously from failed calculations, and had an independent auditor overturn one of its own over-attributed claims before publication. Within the sampled metallic dilute-limit models, the validated DFT analysis supports an adsorbate-induced tendency toward greater surface Cu exposure at both composition endpoints, with facet- and coverage-dependent exceptions. These results were compiled into a complete manuscript with human involvement concentrated in specification, approval, and strategic steering gates. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/PrentisAI/pippin.

</details>


### [233] [Decision models for UAVBench and the viability of edge deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28331)
*Matthew Michelson,Goran Muric,Adrien Bibal,Steven Minton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文以UAVBench为基准，评估小型结构化决策模型在无人机端侧部署的可行性，并提出用本地Mac进行候选模型初筛的流程。H2O Lightning 4B在Mac上取得最高准确率77.65%，但决策模型在边缘设备上遭遇转换与运行时限制；Mac与Qualcomm RB3的匹配评测显示排名一致（LFM2 2.6B优于Qwen2.5 1.5B），但逐题答案存在差异，故实机测试仍不可省略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机端侧推理常依赖小型语言模型，但基准分数无法反映模型能否在边缘计算机上实际运行并有效使用。作者希望厘清三方面问题：小型结构化决策模型是否适合此类任务；普通本地机器（Mac）能否在物理设备测试前完成候选模型筛选；哪些软件与资源限制决定部署成败。

Method: 基于UAVBench的2,000道经验证的多选题，在Mac上评测24种模型配置：11个生成式模型的20种参数量—精度组合，外加4个未接受UAVBench专项训练的开放权重结构化决策模型；随后尝试将决策模型部署至边缘设备，记录转换与运行时限制；并对两个生成式模型在Mac与Qualcomm RB3板上做匹配评测，比较准确率与排序一致性。

Result: 结构化决策模型H2O Lightning 4B在Mac上取得观察到的最高准确率77.65%，表明此类模型适配该任务。部署阶段，决策模型在所测边缘设备上出现格式转换与运行时限制。跨平台匹配评测中，Mac与RB3板的准确率数值不完全一致，但模型排序一致：LFM2 2.6B在两个平台上均优于Qwen2.5 1.5B；然而逐题答案仍存在平台间差异。

Conclusion: 小型结构化决策模型是无人机决策任务的有力候选；本地Mac可用于候选模型的性价比初筛与短名单构建，但无法替代真实硬件验证，因为单题级输出存在平台差异。部署可行性受转换工具链与运行时资源约束制约，需在选型阶段即纳入考量。

Abstract: Small language models are often proposed for reasoning onboard a drone. However, a good benchmark score does not tell us whether a model will actually run, and run usefully, on an edge computer. In this paper we study three related questions using the UAV decision benchmark, UAVBench. First, are small structured decision models good candidates for these tasks? Second, can an ordinary local machine (a Mac) screen candidate models before we test them on physical devices? Third, what software and resource limits govern deployment? Our main Mac evaluation covers 24 model configurations on the same 2,000 validated multiple-choice questions from UAVBench. These include 20 size-and-precision configurations of 11 generative models, plus four open-weight structured decision models that received no additional UAVBench-specific training. A structured decision model, H2O Lightning 4B, achieved the highest accuracy we observed, scoring 77.65% on the Mac. This shows that these models are well suited to this kind of task. However, when we tried to deploy the decision models, we ran into conversion and runtime limits on the edge devices we tested, which we describe in detail. To test local screening, we ran matched evaluations of two generative models on both the Mac and a Qualcomm RB3 board. The accuracies did not match exactly, but the ranking did. LFM2 2.6B ranked above Qwen2.5 1.5B on both platforms. This supports using a local machine to shortlist candidates. That said, individual answers still differed between platforms, so testing on the actual hardware remained necessary.

</details>


### [234] [Aligning Judge Models to Be Logically Consistent Multi-Rule Interpreters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29975)
*Michel Liao,Nimra Nadeem,Luxi He,Peter Henderson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"多规则不一致性"问题：让裁判模型一次性面对整套规则时，其对单条规则的判断会与逐条判断相冲突，且整体更趋宽松；作者比较了提示、GRPO一致性训练与迭代规则精炼等缓解方法，并主张规则集设计者需显式度量与处理规则间交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型技术治理将要求模型同时推理并遵循来自不同司法辖区的成千上万条规则。现有做法（逐条判断）与真实部署（整集呈现）存在差异，而整集呈现可能改变模型对要求的解读，导致与逐条判断相冲突的结论，进而引发未被察觉的对齐失败。

Method: 对比"整集呈现"与"逐条判断"两种条件下的裁判结果以刻画多规则不一致性；以安全关键规则（禁止模型追求给定目标之外的目标）为案例进行检验；并评估三类缓解手段：提示工程、基于GRPO的一致性训练、迭代式规则精炼。

Result: 所有被测裁判模型在规则嵌入完整规则集时，比单独判断时更宽松；对于上述安全关键规则，整集情境下各模型判定其被违反的概率均下降。

Conclusion: 如同人类成文法解释中周边条款会影响单条规则的解释，但与之不同的是，这种交互目前隐式发生在裁判模型的推理内部。规则集设计者必须显式度量并处理多规则交互，以防解释漂移在无察觉情况下导致下游对齐失败。

Abstract: Technical governance of AI models will soon require them to reason over and follow thousands of rules from different jurisdictions. However, as we show in this work, presenting an entire ruleset to a judge model---rather than asking it to judge one rule at a time---changes the way the model interprets the requirements, making it reach conclusions that conflict with its rule-by-rule judgments. We call this challenge multi-rule inconsistency and find that models are more lenient when judging rules embedded within the full ruleset than when judging rules separately. For example, we find that when a safety-critical rule prohibiting a model from pursuing objectives other than those it was given appears in a larger ruleset, all tested judge models are less likely to deem it violated. We examine several methods for reducing inconsistency, including prompting, consistency training using GRPO, and iterative rule refinement. As in human statutory interpretation, surrounding rules shape how a rule is interpreted. Unlike the legal setting, however, these interactions are currently happening implicitly inside the judge model's reasoning. We argue that multi-rule interactions must be measured and handled explicitly by ruleset designers to prevent undetected shifts in interpretation from causing downstream alignment failures.

</details>


### [235] [When Tools Fail, Agents Fabricate: Silent Failure and Verification-First Oversight in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28341)
*Gün Kaynar,Boshen Yan,Carl Kingsford*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 RetryRealistic 测试平台，系统量化工具调用失败时 LLM 智能体的恢复能力，发现恢复率仅 19–68% 且 6–26% 的运行以"自信编造"告终，其机制为"静默摄取"（silent ingestion），并证明 verification-first Monitor 可将正确恢复率从 45.6% 提升至 56.3%、编造率从 17.3% 降至 10.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强的 LLM 智能体在工具调用失败时必须能重试、切换工具或修正计划，但现有工作缺乏在真实、可控条件下对"工具故障—智能体失效"关系的系统刻画，尤其不清楚失败模式由模型能力还是故障类型主导。

Method: 构建 RetryRealistic：沙箱化但贴近真实的受控故障注入测试平台，包含真实 OS 文件系统与权限强制、SQLite 数据库、确定性本地 HTTP 服务器和受限 shell；覆盖 8 个工具、9 类带可控持续性的故障、30 个多步任务。对 4 个智能体共 2,100 次故障运行与 ground truth 对比评测，并进行机制分析与干预实验（Monitor、去除 CoT）。

Result: 恢复不可靠（正确率 19–68%），且 6–26% 的运行以自信编造（把错误答案断言为事实）结束。失败模式主要受故障类型而非模型支配：返回部分数据的故障驱动编造（最高 38%），硬性拒绝则导致诚实失败。机制为 silent ingestion：智能体首次接触即接受被污染数据，不重试、不标记，并在其上构建答案。verification-first Monitor（将校验锚定于结构化工具输出、发出纠正指令而非硬阻断）将正确恢复率由 45.6% 升至 56.3%，编造率由 17.3% 降到 10.8%；移除 chain-of-thought 使编造率上升 8.3 个百分点，说明推理过程可抑制静默摄取。

Conclusion: 工具故障下的智能体失效具有结构性可诊断特征，"静默摄取"是编造的核心机制，且可通过验证优先的监控与保留显式推理有效缓解；作者开源 RetryRealistic、故障注入工具包及全部标注轨迹以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents must recover when a tool call fails, by retrying, switching tools, or revising their plan. We study how agents fail when tools fail, using RetryRealistic, a sandboxed yet realistic testbed for controlled fault injection in which agents operate on a real OS filesystem with genuine permission enforcement, SQLite databases, a deterministic local HTTP server, and a constrained shell, across 8 tools, 9 fault types with controlled persistence, and 30 multi-step tasks. Evaluating four agents over 2,100 faulted runs against ground truth, we find that recovery is unreliable (19-68% correct) and frequently silent: 6-26% of runs end in a confident fabrication, a wrong answer asserted as fact. The failure mode is governed more by the fault type than by the model: faults that return partial data drive fabrication (up to 38%), while hard denials drive honest failure. The mechanism is what we call silent ingestion: the agent accepts faulted data as valid on first contact without retrying or flagging the problem, then builds a fabricated answer on top of it. We show that fabrication is reducible: a verification-first Monitor, grounding checks in structured tool outputs and issuing corrective directives rather than hard blocks, raises correct recovery from 45.6% to 56.3% and cuts fabrication from 17.3% to 10.8%. Removing chain-of-thought increases fabrication (+8.3pp), so deliberation guards against silent ingestion. We release RetryRealistic, the fault-injection toolkit, and all annotated traces at https://github.com/Kingsford-Group/RetryRealistic

</details>


### [236] [Can Cities Have Personalities? Introducing the SCD City Personality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30733)
*Andrei Khurshudov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SCD城市人格评估法（SCD-CPA），为每座城市配置文档接地型ChatGPT人格代理，用统一的24题类MBTI量表进行可复现的城市人格测量，并检验其对证据来源与模型配置的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市描述受人类经验与制度叙事塑造，缺乏可比较、可重复的测量方式。若直接让通用模型自由描述城市，结果难以复现且无法追溯证据来源，因此需要将城市"人格"感知转为证据条件化、可对照的实验构型。

Method: 为每座城市建立独立的文档接地对话代理（document-grounded persona），以受MBTI四对结构启发的统一24条陈述量表进行访谈式测量；每个代理采用独立定义的城市概念与评分规则，输出四个双极维度与四字母摘要。方法评估：东京、纽约、巴黎共33次评估（跨重复会话、三种GPT-6推理强度设置、替代证据画像）；应用评估：上海、迪拜、孟买、拉各斯、圣保罗共15次评估。

Result: 原始画像下东京在相同设置内的逐题完全一致率为85.4%；替换证据会改变部分维度强度。五城应用中，跨设置逐题完全一致率77.8%，误差在1分以内的比例达99.4%。不同推理强度设置下结果保持一致，说明方法对已测试的配置差异相对不敏感。

Conclusion: 将城市对话代理视为可复现、证据条件化的实验构型，可使基于文献的城市性格感知变得可比较，并揭示其对来源选择的敏感性；局限在于模型对证据的内部解读无法被完全观测。该框架为未来模型迭代下的稳定性评估提供了基础。

Abstract: Descriptions of cities are shaped by human experience and institutional narratives. We introduce the SCD City Personality Assessment (SCD-CPA), a method that instantiates document-grounded ChatGPT city personas and interrogates each with an identical 24-statement instrument inspired by the four-pair structure of the Myers-Briggs Type Indicator. Rather than asking a general-purpose model to characterize cities freely, we configure a separate document-grounded conversational agent for each city and use alternative evidence portraits to compare documentary inputs. The resulting four bipolar dimensions and four-letter summaries use independently defined urban concepts and scoring rules. Tokyo, New York, and Paris form the methodological evaluation (33 assessments across repeated sessions, three GPT-6 effort settings, and alternative portraits): Tokyo's original portrait yielded 85.4% exact within-setting item agreement, while evidence substitutions shifted some dimension strengths. A five-city application to Shanghai, Dubai, Mumbai, Lagos, and Sao Paulo (15 assessments) yielded 77.8% exact and 99.4% within-one-point cross-setting agreement, indicating consistency under the tested configurations. By treating each city conversational agent as a reproducible, evidence-conditioned experimental configuration, SCD-CPA makes documented city-character perceptions comparable and exposes their sensitivity to source selection. Although the documentary evidence is explicitly controlled, the model's internal interpretation of that evidence cannot be fully observed. We also examine the robustness of SCD-CPA across three AI reasoning-effort settings. The observed consistency suggests that the methodology is relatively insensitive to the tested configuration differences and provides a framework for evaluating its stability as future generations of AI models become available.

</details>


### [237] [SMAD: A Semantics-Guided Multi-Agent Framework for Unsupervised Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28348)
*Qinghua Liu,Junpeng Wang,Mingzhi Hu,Menghai Pan,Shubham Jain,Yuzhong Chen,Yan Zheng,Yiwei Cai,John Paparrizos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SMAD 提出基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体框架，将"异常意图"转化为显式的自然语言语义契约，从而驱动时间序列异常检测流水线的构建与评估，在单变量和多变量基准上分别比最强基线提升 11.5% 和 9.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构数据与多样异常类型下不存在通用 TSAD 检测器。现有自动化方法受限于隐式优化目标、可能不可靠的历史知识以及高计算成本；更根本的问题在于，它们只决定"如何检测"，却未显式定义"检测什么"（即目标异常语义）。

Method: 提出 SMAD：以可测量的时间序列性质为基础建立自然语言语义契约，明确目标事件模式、期望的分数响应与验证问题。该契约同时约束检测器构建与评估：LLM 智能体提出候选流水线；契约匹配的合成干预在目标序列上提供无标签证据，评估语义覆盖度并引导迭代式自我改进；最终通过语义感知集成（semantics-aware ensemble）融合通过验证的候选，并将每条告警归因到贡献检测器及支撑证据。

Result: 在单变量与多变量 TSAD 基准上，检测性能分别较最强基线提升 11.5% 与 9.6%；同时优于通用编码智能体，且语言模型 token 消耗降低 2–5 倍。

Conclusion: 显式语义契约是有效的 TSAD 自动化抽象：将"检测什么"与"如何检测"解耦并以可验证方式统一，可在无标签条件下实现可解释、可归因且成本更低的检测流水线构建。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection (TSAD) lacks a one-size-fits-all detector for heterogeneous data and anomaly types. Existing automation is limited by implicit objectives, potentially unreliable historical knowledge, or high computational costs. More fundamentally, it selects how to detect anomalies without explicitly defining what to detect. We propose SMAD, an LLM-based multi-agent framework that translates anomaly intent into executable, testable detection pipelines. Rather than selecting detectors against an implicit target, SMAD establishes a natural-language semantic contract grounded in measurable time-series properties, specifying target event patterns, expected score responses, and validation questions. This contract governs both detector construction and evaluation: LLM agents propose pipelines, while contract-matched synthetic interventions provide label-free evidence from the target series to assess semantic coverage and guide iterative self-refinement. A semantics-aware ensemble integrates validated candidates and attributes each alert to contributing detectors and supporting evidence. On univariate and multivariate TSAD benchmarks, SMAD improves detection performance over the strongest baseline by 11.5% and 9.6%, respectively, while outperforming general-purpose coding agents at 2-5x lower language-model token cost.

</details>


### [238] [Engram: Multi-Stage Memory for Agentic Tuning of Heterogeneous Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28356)
*Huanran Li,Nhat X. T. Le,Kevin De Angeli,Yao Xuan,Alireza Vahdatpour,Djordje Gligorijevic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Engram是一个带跨会话记忆的LLM智能体记忆系统，通过在线智能体在关键决策点检索证据、离线智能体蒸馏经验，实现在广告模型调参中比无记忆基线少47%试验次数达到相同效果，并取得更优最终模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体虽能调优神经网络，但缺乏跨会话记忆，每次都要重新试错、重复发现已知经验，代价高昂；同时由于生产模型在架构、训练设置和推理预算上各不相同，直接复用历史经验并不容易，因此需要一种能判断历史证据是否适用于当前模型的记忆机制。

Method: 提出Engram记忆系统：将历史试验结果与其背后的假设和理由关联，并评估证据对当前模型的适用性。在线智能体在三个决策点提供证据——Initial Guidance（开始时提供已验证经验）、Session Retrieval（每批假设生成前检索相关历史会话）、Trial Candidate Review（将每个试验候选与匹配的历史试验比对）；会话之间由离线智能体蒸馏已验证经验，使记忆具备自我改进能力。

Result: 在两个广告模型上，Engram以少47%的试验次数达到无记忆对照组32次试验的结果，且平均而言最终得到的模型更优。

Conclusion: 结构化的跨会话记忆（关联假设与理由、并做适用性评估）能显著提升LLM智能体调参效率与最终质量，离线蒸馏机制使记忆可持续自我进化。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can now tune neural networks, yet without cross-session memory they spend costly trials rediscovering known lessons. Reuse is difficult because production models differ in architecture, training setup and inference budget. We present Engram, a memory system that links past trial outcomes with the hypotheses and rationale behind them and assesses whether this evidence applies to the current model. An online agent curates evidence at the decision points where it is needed: Initial Guidance supplies validated lessons at the start, Session Retrieval supplies related past sessions before each batch of hypotheses is generated, and Trial Candidate Review checks each trial candidate against matching past trials. Between sessions, an offline agent distills validated lessons, making the memory self-improving. On two ads models, Engram reaches the 32-trial result of a no-memory control with 47% fewer trials and also ends with a better model on average.

</details>


### [239] [Schedule, Rank, Profile: Continuous Evaluation of LLM Agents in Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28368)
*Martyna Plomecka,Yao Yan,DJ Sterling,Jon Lipovetz,John Schultz,Michael Aaron,Chiamaka Chukwuka,Will Cukierski,Christopher D'Mello,Bovard Doerschuk-Tiberi,Robert Fraser,Ian Gemp,Yuting Han,Addison Howard,Oran Kelly,Siqi Liu,Jeff Moser,Meghan O'Connell,Jun Peng,Laurel Prince,Meg Risdal,Jonathan Ward,Domino Weir,Marc Lanctot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向LLM智能体博弈评测的持续评估框架，含自适应调度器、统一排行榜（ULB）与游戏技能画像三组件，在十款游戏上验证并报告成本节省与性能-推理成本帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一次性游戏基准会随模型、提示、环境实现与评测策略变化而过时，而刷新成本高昂：全轮循环赛在模型对数量上扩展性差，且各游戏结果处于互不兼容的尺度上。
