Conclusion: LLM智能体在硬件逆向工程中可部分恢复行为语义，但全用例正确率低；引入功能先验仅带来有限增益；行为恢复与高效实现是两个相互独立的挑战，需分别攻克。

Abstract: Understanding a system that can be run but not inspected is a core engineering skill: one designs experiments, forms hypotheses about hidden state and timing, discards those that observations refute, and finally builds a replacement that works and is efficient. To evaluate this capability of LLM agents in the hardware domain, we present ChiProgramBench, 18 tasks derived from open-source processors, accelerators, codecs, transforms, interconnects, and control circuits. Agents can only drive and read the pins of a hidden, stateful simulated reference, then submit a gate-level netlist made of public library cells. Agents work in two settings: black-box discovery gives only anonymized functional pins and the interfaces of fixed memories, while function-guided reconstruction adds the circuit's purpose and each pin's role. Hidden test cases compare outputs with the reference cycle by cycle; power, delay, and area earn credit relative to the reference only when every case passes. Across 432 trials of 6 LLM agent configurations, successful agents recover algorithms and numerical conventions, yet only 12.0% of black-box and 14.8% of function-guided trials pass every case. Function-guided reconstruction raises the mean functional score, the cycle-weighted share of passed cases, from 24.6% to 30.9%. Of the 58 fully passing designs, only three improve on the reference's combined power, area, and delay, so behavioral recovery and efficient implementation remain distinct challenges.

</details>


### [376] [Revisiting Learning to Delegate: LLM Discovered Heuristics for Large-Scale Vehicle Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31090)
*Zhengyang Wang,Renfei Tan,Shuze Daniel Liu,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自动启发式设计（AHD）框架，利用大语言模型自主迭代优化子问题选择启发式，直接构造大规模CVRP子问题，在分布外大规模实例上优于神经网络策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模容量约束车辆路径问题（CVRP）因组合爆炸而计算困难。主流做法是先识别有前景的子问题再用求解器优化；但已有方法依赖训练任务特定的神经网络在人工构造的候选子问题间做选择，泛化性与可解释性受限，且难以直接构造子问题。

Method: 提出AHD框架：由LLM自主精炼（refine）用于选择子问题的启发式，直接生成子问题构造规则而非在预设候选集中挑选；迭代式地产生可解释、可泛化的启发式，再与求解器结合优化大规模CVRP实例。

Result: 在分布内（in-distribution）性能与神经网络基线相当；在大规模问题的分布外（out-of-distribution）实例上优于基于神经网络的策略。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的自动启发式设计可获得可解释、可泛化的子问题构造规则，为大规模车辆路径优化提供了替代任务特定神经策略的新范式。

Abstract: Efficient vehicle routing is critical to logistics and transportation, yet solving large-scale capacitated vehicle routing problems (CVRP) remains computationally challenging due to their combinatorial complexity. One approach to solve large-scale vehicle routing optimization is to identify promising subproblems and then optimize them using solvers. In this paper, we propose a framework of automatic heuristic design (AHD) in which a large language model (LLM) autonomously refines heuristics for selecting subproblems, enabling effective optimization of large-scale CVRP instances. Compared to prior approaches that train task-specific neural networks to select among heuristically constructed subproblems, our method discovers generalizable, interpretable LLM-generated heuristics to directly construct subproblems. Experiments show that our approach achieves comparable in-distribution performance while outperforming neural network-based policies on out-of-distribution instances with large-scale problems.

</details>


### [377] [Tradeoffs between objective outcomes and human enjoyment of work in human-AI task allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31095)
*Katherine M. Collins,Elizabeth Mieczkowski,Raja Marjieh,Marlene Berke,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Max Kleiman-Weiner,Ilia Sucholutsky,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 人在分配AI与人类任务时并非只按客观绩效决策，而是以模型化方式平衡客观绩效与人类员工的预期愉悦感，且对厌恶体验赋予更高权重、并随任务风险水平调整；LLM在低风险情境下也偏离纯客观决策，但权重方式异质性很大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作中任务不仅由结果定义，也由参与体验定义。AI能力提升使人可能被安排去做愉悦任务，同时产生如监控AI输出等更令人厌恶的任务；管理者面临权衡：仅按预期绩效分配，还是兼顾执行者的主观体验。主流经济学解释多基于预期效用计算（偏重客观结果），因此需要实证检验人如何平衡客观结果与愉悦等主观品质。

Method: 开展大规模行为研究（N=800），让被试将任务分配给AI或人类工作者；用计算建模比较不同决策模型，检验相对客观绩效与人类预期愉悦感的加权方式；并通过改变任务框架（愉悦/厌恶）与项目风险（stakes）检验权重变化；同时对比测试多个LLM的分配决策。

Result: 人类分配决策最佳由“系统权衡相对客观绩效与人类预期愉悦感”的模型刻画：当任务被描述为带来较低愉悦时，对人类体验的权重更高；权重随任务风险调整。LLM方面，多数模型也偏离纯客观决策，尤其在低风险项目中，但各模型对愉悦的权重程度与方式差异显著。

Conclusion: AI在工作场所的整合可能是"锯齿状"的：不仅取决于人机相对能力，还取决于人类工作体验、体验效价（更重视厌恶）、情境（如风险水平）以及决策主体（人还是LLM）。这提示需重新审视人类体验在人机任务分配中的角色。

Abstract: For people, tasks at work are defined by more than their outcome: they are colored by the experience of engaging in that task. As AI systems become increasingly capable of performing human-level work they may be deployed to tasks that people find enjoyable while simultaneously creating comparatively more aversive tasks such as monitoring AI outputs. This creates a thorny tradeoff for managers: whether work should be assigned purely based on the expected performance of the workers in question, or also account for the subjective experience of the person potentially doing the task. Most economic accounts focus on expected utility calculations based on the former. Here, we assess how people balance objective outcomes and more subjective qualities like enjoyment. In a series of large-scale (N=800) behavioral studies in which people allocate tasks to either an AI or human worker we find that people's allocation decisions are best captured by a model that systematically balances relative objective performance with the human worker's expected enjoyment, weighting human experience higher when a task is framed as inducing less enjoyment and adjusting based on the stakes of the task. As AI also makes decisions about work allocation, we compare a set of LLMs. We find many models also deviate from purely objective decisions, particularly when the project stakes are low, but there is wide heterogeneity as to how much and in what manner they weigh enjoyment. These results raise questions about what role human experience may play in decisions around where and how to integrate AI into workplaces. Integration may be jagged across settings due to consideration not only of the relative capabilities of workers, but also the human experience of work, the valence of that experience (e.g., more strongly weighing aversion), the context (e.g., stakes), and who is making or guiding such choices (human or LLM).

</details>


### [378] [SayWhen: Set-Valued Semantic Commitment for Low-Regret Early Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31101)
*Tianyi Huang,Lucas Su,Fred Zhang,Andy Liang,Gordon Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SayWhen——一种测试时控制器，在语音代理中将跨类型续写计划共享的任务导向"行为"作为可发布语音进行控制释放，并以持久否决机制阻止失效候选，从而将"有支持的公开承诺"定义为一个可测的语音代理控制单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过早发言会把预测变成不可逆的公开承诺，而现有语音代理缺乏对"何时开口"的显式控制。作者希望在不牺牲兼容性的前提下，实现受控的提前发言，并需要一个可量化的指标来刻画"无悔的提前语音"。

Method: 提出SayWhen测试时控制器：选择一个任务导向行为（act）在多个带类型的续写计划间共享，并控制其作为语音的释放；引入持久否决（persistent veto），当干预性转写使候选失效时阻断播放。定义RFES（regret-free early speech）指标衡量端点前的有支持释放且无不兼容评分。在两个设置上评估：72前缀分支诊断与30任务τ-Voice模拟（含缓存TTS音频、语义否决、端点等待等对照）。

Result: 72前缀分支诊断中，生成计划前沿达到6.0% RFES、观测平均后悔为0；对比Matched Top-One为8.2% RFES、后悔0.068。规划器延迟限制该路径；确定性零售schema达31.7% RFES，完整策略为32.0%。τ-Voice 30任务中，schema派生候选+缓存TTS达21/30任务级RFES；有提前语音的22个任务中合格音频比用户音频端点早1.51秒，全体30任务平均1.11秒；语义否决与端点等待各通过16/30。

Conclusion: 两项研究展示了受控的覆盖率—兼容性权衡以及基于schema的端点前运行能力，使"有支持的公开承诺"成为语音代理控制中的一个显式单元。

Abstract: Speaking early turns a prediction into an irreversible public commitment. We introduce SayWhen, a test-time controller that selects a task-directed act shared across typed continuation plans and controls its release as speech. A persistent veto blocks playback when intervening transcripts invalidate the candidate. We define regret-free early speech (RFES) to measure supported pre-endpoint releases without scored incompatibility. On a 72-prefix branching diagnostic, the generated-plan frontier attains 6.0% RFES with zero observed mean regret, versus 8.2% RFES and 0.068 regret for Matched Top-One. Measured planner latency constrains this path; a deterministic retail schema attains 31.7% RFES, compared with 32.0% for the complete policy. In a separate 30-task $τ$-Voice simulation, schema-derived candidates with cached text-to-speech (TTS) audio attain 21/30 task-level RFES. Eligible audio begins 1.51 seconds before the user-audio endpoint among the 22 tasks with early speech, or 1.11 seconds over all 30 tasks with zero for tasks without eligible early speech. Semantic veto and endpoint waiting each pass 16/30 tasks. Together, the studies demonstrate a controlled coverage-compatibility trade-off and schema-based pre-endpoint operation, making the supported public commitment an explicit unit of voice-agent control.

</details>


### [379] [LiveLoRA: Exact KV Cache Migration Across LoRA Updates for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31105)
*Tianyi Huang,Samuel Xu,Ivy Gu,Nathan Huang,Siqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LiveLora 是一种模型-运行时协同设计，通过"精确末块 KV 迁移"实现 LLM 适配器热更新，无需重放全交互历史即可完成版本切换，并证明其与全历史重预填充在算术上等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续运行的 LLM 智能体需要更新适配器，但重放全部会话历史代价随历史长度与会话规模增长；直接复用旧版本计算的 KV cache 又会混用模型版本，导致数值不一致。因此需要一种既精确又低成本的缓存迁移机制。

Method: 利用冻结的下层解码器块与固定的 K/V 适配器降投影，使每个 token 的基座投影与低秩坐标在适配器版本间保持不变；运行时据此重建候选 K/V，仅将最新 token 通过最后一个块重算，并原子性地一并提交适配器、缓存与下一 token logits；同时提出紧凑保留（compact retention）以降低会话存储。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct 上，32 个各含 8K token 的活跃会话、五次运行的中位配对发布加速平均达 68.98×（相对全历史重预填充）；服务基准显示相对原生缓存解码约 4.8% 的解码开销；相比保留边界重放，紧凑保留在 8K 场景下额外会话存储减少 3.42×，发布加解码成本相近。

Conclusion: LiveLora 使持久化 LLM 智能体可在不重放完整交互历史的前提下采用新适配器版本，在保证与全历史重预填充续写等价的同时显著降低发布延迟与存储成本。

Abstract: Updating an LLM adapter should not require replaying the full interaction history of every active session. Yet reusing key-value (KV) cache entries computed under an earlier version mixes model versions, while full-history re-prefill becomes increasingly costly as histories and session fleets grow. We introduce LiveLora, a model-runtime co-design for exact final-block KV cache migration. Frozen lower decoder blocks and fixed K/V adapter down-projections preserve each token's base projections and low-rank coordinates across adapter versions. The runtime reconstructs candidate K/V, reevaluates the latest token through the final block, and commits the adapter, cache, and next-token logits together. We prove continuation equivalence to full-history re-prefill in exact arithmetic under the stated model assumptions. On Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, median paired publication speedups over full-history re-prefill average $68.98\times$ across five runs for 32 active sessions with 8K tokens each. Serving benchmarks show approximately 4.8% decoding overhead relative to native cached decoding. Compared with retained-boundary replay, compact retention gives a $3.42\times$ reduction in additional session storage at 8K, with similar measured publication-plus-decoding cost. LiveLora allows persistent LLM agents to adopt new adapter versions without replaying their full interaction histories.

</details>


### [380] [Preflight: Agentic Last-Mile Verification for AI Software Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31110)
*Yaman Jandali,Nojan Sheybani,Farinaz Koushanfar,Tara Javidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Preflight：面向下游程序的 Python 依赖预执行验证器，通过提取应用引用符号、合成函数级测试桩并受限执行来获得行为指纹，按“是否被特定程序实际调用”判定依赖行为是否恶意。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖代码在下游应用的信任边界内执行，但现有的溯源、静态分析与包级行为分析都无法直接回答“某个已发布产物在特定程序中被调用时究竟表现为何”。需要把行为验证条件化到具体下游程序上。

Method: 1) 从应用代码中提取被引用的符号；2) 迭代合成并验证函数级 harness；3) 在受限环境中执行解析后的依赖产物；4) 记录行为指纹；5) 在存在合适基线时将目标版本行为与历史版本对比。

Result: 在 4 起真实供应链事件的 5 个应用上下文中，4 个上下文判为恶意；LiteLLM 客户端上下文判为干净（受影响路径未被触发）。100 个良性包全部判为干净，未观察到误报。Ultralytics 与 ctx 的对照消融中，函数级 harness 均暴露恶意行为，而仅导入探测两者都未能发现。

Conclusion: 将行为验证条件化到下游程序可在良性负载上保持选择性（低误报），同时暴露出安全相关的依赖行为；相比仅导入探测，函数级 harness 是必要且有效的。

Abstract: Software dependencies execute within the trust boundary of downstream applications, yet provenance, static analysis, and package-level behavioral approaches do not directly determine how a released artifact behaves for a specific program. We present Preflight, a program-scoped pre-execution verifier for Python dependencies that extracts application-referenced symbols, iteratively synthesizes and validates function-level harnesses, executes resolved artifacts in a restricted environment, records behavioral fingerprints, and compares target behavior against prior versions when suitable baselines exist. We evaluate Preflight on four real software-supply-chain incidents under five application contexts, a 100-package benign corpus, and a controlled function-level versus import-only ablation. Preflight returns malicious verdicts in four incident contexts and a clean verdict in the LiteLLM client context, where the affected proxy-server path is not exercised. All 100 evaluated benign packages receive clean verdicts, with zero observed false positives. In a controlled ablation on Ultralytics and ctx, function-level harnesses expose malicious behavior in both incidents, whereas import-only probing exposes neither. Together, these results show that conditioning behavioral verification on the downstream program can expose security-relevant dependency behavior while remaining selective on benign workloads.

</details>


### [381] [Self-Upgrading Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31117)
*Jiajun Fan,Neil He,Qinglong Zheng,Changyi Yang,Tong Wei,Andy Mao,Zetian Li,Chaoran Cheng,Hui Ren,Ruihan Guo,Xianrui Zhong,Xizheng Yu,Jingyuan Li,Xi Zhu,Kaiqu Liang,Chang Xu,Zun Qian,Zidi Xiong,Yuezhu Zhao,Xu Huang,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个自升级具身智能体：27B模型在任务间隙无需训练/梯度/奖励模型/第二模型，通过夜间读记录、定位隐藏状态方向并改写单个权重张量，以自测不变自身为检验，实现稳定持续的多轮递归自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数具身智能体权重终身固定，想变强只能靠额外训练、反思、记忆、搜索或提示工程，每步都有开销且换任务即失效；已有的权重编辑研究局限于纯推理模型或依赖更大的商用模型。作者希望让智能体在真实工作现场凭自身经验直接升级自身权重。

Method: 构建自升级具身智能体：白天27B智能体在家庭环境中作业；夜间读取自身轨迹记录，发现大量步数被"在输出动作前即触达token上限"的回复浪费，将其归因于隐状态中的某一方向，并改写某个权重张量。智能体兼任自身考官：在练习任务上把每次改动与未修改的自身对比测试，只保留经得起检验的编辑。

Result: 在数千个留出 episode（每任务48个新种子）上，仅一次被保留的编辑即显著提升任务完成数，减少步数、浪费步数与token数，且每步成本不变。改进可多轮、稳定、持续：后续多个夜晚升级仍保持，不出现崩溃。家族四档规模中仅27B模型具备该能力。

Conclusion: 具身智能体无需再分离训练与推理：工作即可积累经验并自我升级，无需单独预训练/后训练与人工干预，为机器人终身持续学习——稳定、持续、递归的自我改进——开辟了道路。

Abstract: An embodied agent built on a language model goes to work with the weights it was given and keeps them for life. To get better, it needs more training, or reflection, memory, search or prompts that cost every step and are lost when the task changes. Editing weights by reasoning, weight-space programming, could end this, but has stayed with models that only reason or relied on a far larger commercial model. We introduce the self-upgrading embodied agent, to our knowledge the first that learns from its own experience by upgrading its own weights on the job, between its tasks, with no training, no gradient, no reward model, no second model, no test-time scaling and no harness or prompt engineering. By day a 27B agent works in a household environment. At night it reads its record, finds many of its steps wasted on replies that reach the token limit before any action, traces the habit to one direction in its hidden states, and rewrites one tensor of its weights. An agent that rewrites itself must first never break itself, so it is its own examiner: it tests every change against its unchanged self on practice tasks and keeps only what holds. When an edit holds, the next day it is a better agent, at the same cost per step: over thousands of held-out episodes, 48 fresh seeds a task, its one kept edit makes it complete significantly more tasks, in fewer steps, with fewer wasted and fewer tokens. This self-improvement is multi-round, stable and continual: through the nights that follow, the upgrade holds, without collapse. Of four sizes of its family, only the 27B agent does this. From here on, an embodied agent need not keep training and inference apart: as it works, it gathers its own experience and upgrades itself, with no separate pre-training or post-training and no human intervention. We believe this opens lifelong continual learning for robots: stable, continual, recursive self-improvement.

</details>


### [382] [Genome-to-Image Translation: Reconstructing Escherichia coli Morphology from Genomic Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31132)
*Son T. Huynh,Thuy T. Le,Thu H. A. Huynh,Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文将抗菌药敏表型的图像预测重构为条件图像生成问题：用冻结的基因组语言模型把组装contig聚合成768维向量，驱动带梯度惩罚的条件Wasserstein GAN合成菌落单细胞图像，并以合成图像增强真实数据以提升耐药分类（尤其耐药类召回）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规基于培养的药敏试验耗时，难以满足早期治疗决策需求；耐药状态在细胞形态上留下可测痕迹，且临床分离株的全基因组测序日益可得，因此引出"能否由基因组直接预测菌落表观形态"的问题。

Method: 将耐药表型预测表述为条件图像生成。基因组侧：组装contig经"片段—contig—分离株"分层聚合，由冻结的基因组语言模型编码为单一768维向量。生成侧：条件WGAN-GP，以该嵌入、所用抗生素及耐药标签为条件合成菌落图像。数据：22株临床血液分离株、四种条件（阿奇霉素、环丙沙星、四环素及无药对照）共167,356张单细胞图像。在生成器中比较七种预训练卷积骨干，依据分布型图像质量指标选用ConvNeXt-Tiny。随后做外在评估：将合成图像加入缩减的真实训练集，检验下游耐药分类。

Result: ConvNeXt-Tiny在分布型图像质量指标上最优；合成图像加入缩减真实训练集后，各抗生素的耐药分类性能提升，耐药类召回取得显著增益。

Conclusion: 基因组嵌入可条件化生成具有表型意义的菌落形态图像，且合成图像可作为有效的数据增强手段改善耐药分类，提示基因组—形态—表型之间存在可学习的映射，为加速药敏推断提供了生成式建模路径。

Abstract: Antimicrobial resistance in Escherichia coli is established in routine practice by culture-based susceptibility testing, which is slow relative to the clinical need for an early treatment decision. Resistance status also leaves a measurable trace in cell morphology, and whole-genome sequencing of clinical isolates is increasingly available, which raises the question of whether colony appearance can be predicted directly from an isolate's genome. We formulate this as a conditional image-generation problem. The assembled contigs of each isolate are encoded into a single 768-dimensional vector by a frozen genomic language model through a segment-contig-isolate aggregation hierarchy, and a conditional Wasserstein GAN with gradient penalty synthesizes colony images from this embedding together with the applied antibiotic and the resistance label. We train on 167,356 single-cell images from 22 clinical blood isolates imaged under azithromycin, ciprofloxacin, tetracycline, and a no-drug control, compare seven pretrained convolutional backbones inside the generator, and select ConvNeXt-Tiny on distributional image-quality metrics. The synthetic images are then assessed extrinsically: added to a reduced real training set, they improve per-antibiotic resistance classification, with a significant gain in resistant-class recall.

</details>


### [383] [Habitats: A Framework for Open-Ended Evolution of Agent Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31160)
*Nicolas Bolouri,Gaya Mehenni,Daniel Toyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Habitats 框架，实现可执行智能体目标的开放式演化：维护奖励-策略对种群，用交叉适应度矩阵指导种群级规划与目标奖励变异，获得多样、可学、可复用的行为库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言模型的奖励生成方法通常只针对预定义任务细化目标，且把"探索什么"与"如何实现"耦合在同一决策中，容易收窄行为多样性；而"设计智能体应当优化什么"本身与优化智能体同样困难。

Method: Habitats 维护一个奖励-策略对种群，在全部种群奖励下评估每个策略，构建交叉适应度矩阵以揭示自我掌握、迁移与功能多样性；种群级 Planner 依据该矩阵选择父代奖励并下发定向变异指令，独立的 Mutator 将指令翻译为经校验的奖励程序。

Result: 在离散 Card Sandbox 与三个连续控制环境（Cart-Pole、Hopper、Cheetah）中，与独立生成和直接变异基线对比：四个环境、三个随机种子下宏平均的最终专家质量与行为多样性最高，最终多样性为直接变异的 1.8 倍、独立生成的 2.5 倍；在 10,000 适应回合下，冻结策略库对十个演化中未见的下游目标取得更高配对胜率，并超过从头训练 20,000 回合的任务专用策略。

Conclusion: 将种群级规划与奖励合成解耦，能够发现多样、可学习且可复用的行为。

Abstract: Designing what an agent should optimize can be as difficult as optimizing the agent itself. Existing language-model-based reward-generation methods typically refine objectives for predefined tasks and couple the decision of what to explore with the synthesis of how to implement it, potentially narrowing the resulting behavioral repertoire. We introduce Habitats, a framework for open-ended evolution of executable agent objectives. Habitats maintains a population of reward-policy pairs and evaluates every policy under every population reward, producing a cross-fitness matrix that exposes self-mastery, transfer, and functional diversity. A population-level Planner uses this information to select parent rewards and issue targeted mutation directives, while a separate Mutator translates those directives into validated reward programs. We evaluate Habitats in a discrete Card Sandbox and three continuous-control environments (Cart-Pole, Hopper, and Cheetah) against Independent Generation and Direct Mutation baselines. Across four environments and three random seeds, Habitats achieves the highest macro-averaged final Specialist Quality and Behavioral Diversity, with final diversity reaching 1.8x that of Direct Mutation and 2.5x that of Independent Generation. Finally, across ten downstream objectives unseen during evolution, frozen Habitats policy libraries achieve higher paired win rates than size-matched Direct Mutation and Independent Generation libraries at 10,000 adaptation episodes, while also surpassing task-specific policies trained from scratch for 20,000 episodes. These results suggest that separating population-level planning from reward synthesis enables the discovery of diverse, learnable, and reusable behaviors.

</details>


### [384] [When Does Overriding the Majority Vote Help?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31165)
*Terry Ma,Aidan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究何时应以额外推理或验证结果覆盖多数投票答案（override），在150道竞赛数学题上比较额外推理、可执行程序与跨模型推理三种覆盖手段，考察"正确备选答案可得性"与"选择方法可靠性"两大障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票是聚合多样本推理的常用手段，但投票可能出错。核心问题是：何时应让额外推理或验证结果取代投票答案，以及这类覆盖是否真的可靠。作者指出两个障碍：正确备选答案是否存在于候选池中，以及产生/挑选备选答案的方法是否可靠。

Method: 以3个开放推理模型在150道竞赛数学题上的16个响应做多数投票为起点，比较三类覆盖来源：(1) 同模型额外推理，(2) 可执行程序验证，(3) 跨模型推理；在不同答案一致度阈值下统计覆盖发生频率，以及覆盖纠正错误与引入错误的次数。

Result: 投票错误时，正确答案常不在原始响应中；同模型额外推理很少触发覆盖；可执行程序在所有评估阈值下纠正的错误均多于引入的错误；跨模型覆盖可改善较弱基线，但即便仅用8个独立响应，也无法证明其优于单用更准确模型；提高一致度阈值减少覆盖频率，却不一致地提升覆盖纠错比例；最严阈值下覆盖仅出现在极少数题目上，可靠性结论受限。

Conclusion: 覆盖机制的价值受限于备选答案可得性；程序化验证是相对可靠的覆盖来源，而同模型额外推理收益有限，跨模型覆盖未显示出超越强模型单独作答的优势。严格阈值虽减少覆盖次数，却未系统性提升纠错率，且样本稀疏使高阈值下的可靠性难以判定。

Abstract: When should additional reasoning or verification replace a majority-vote answer? We call such a replacement an override. We examine two obstacles: the availability of correct alternatives and the reliability of methods that produce or select them. We compare additional reasoning, executed programs, and cross-model reasoning on 150 competition-mathematics questions with three open reasoning models. Starting from a majority vote over 16 responses, we measure how often overrides occur and how many errors they correct or introduce across agreement thresholds. When the vote is incorrect, the correct answer is often absent from the original responses. Additional same-model reasoning rarely overrides, while executed programs correct more errors than they introduce at every evaluated threshold. Cross-model overrides improve some weaker baselines but do not establish an advantage over the more accurate model alone, even with only eight standalone responses. Stricter agreement reduces how often overrides occur but does not consistently increase the fraction that correct errors. At the strictest thresholds, overrides occur on few distinct questions, limiting conclusions about their reliability.

</details>


### [385] [Reducing Order Sensitivity in Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31174)
*Bodong Liu,Dongyoung Go,Sofian Zalouk,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 决策模型普遍存在顺序敏感性，导致精度下降与决策不可靠；树注意力可低成本实现顺序不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型正成为大型AI系统的快速组件，但如Jev等模型逐个处理选项，使预测结果依赖选项顺序，造成精度显著下降与决策不可靠，且随选项数量增加和多步决策累积而加剧。

Method: 在十个开源与闭源决策模型上系统评估顺序敏感性；并从鲁棒性、预测精度与推理效率三方面比较八种缓解方法，重点考察树注意力（将选项作为共享上下文的并行分支处理）。

Result: 顺序敏感性普遍存在且随选项数增长恶化；树注意力出人意料地有效且高效：训练时使用可达到与基线相当的平均精度；无需重训练直接应用于基线即可消除顺序敏感性，精度仅下降1.1个点。

Conclusion: 顺序不变性可直接在决策模型中实现，且几乎不损失预测精度，树注意力是兼顾鲁棒性与效率的优选方案。

Abstract: Decision models are emerging as fast components of larger AI systems. Yet recent decision models, such as Jev, process options sequentially, leading to different predictions based on their ordering. Across ten open- and closed-source decision models, we find that \emph{order-sensitivity} is pervasive, causing substantial accuracy degradation and unreliable decision-making. These effects become more pronounced as the number of options grows and can compound across multi-step decisions. To address this problem, we systematically compare eight methods for mitigating order sensitivity in terms of robustness, predictive accuracy, and inference efficiency. Among these methods, we find that \emph{tree attention} is a surprisingly effective and efficient method. It achieves order invariance by processing options as parallel branches of a shared context. When used during training, it matches the baseline's average accuracy. Applied to the baseline without retraining, it also eliminates order sensitivity with only a 1.1-point drop in accuracy. Our findings demonstrate that order invariance can be enforced directly in decision models with little or no loss in predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [386] [Needwise: Generation-Free Conversational Recommendation from Need-Level Typed Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31175)
*Sungchul Kim,Weixiao Huang,Mekala Raja Sekhar Reddy,Amrutha Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出用"类型化决策"(typed decisions)构建对话式推荐器：以固定选项上的命名问题、用选项概率而非生成文本作答；Needwise 逐轮将消息映射到目录字段并更新既有需求状态，再按"must-first"分层排序。Needwise-HF 在 Google Local 上显著优于 GPT-6 Sol（nDCG@10 0.383 vs 0.343），成本与延迟更低，但对撤回需求的识别率仅 19–27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话推荐需对目录仅部分记录的需求排序，而用户会增删、反转需求并切换请求。基于 LLM 的排序器每轮重读对话，速度慢，且其排序不显示物品满足了哪些需求。作者希望以结构化、可解释、低成本的方式替代 LLM 生成式排序。

Method: 用类型化决策构建推荐器：(1) 每轮一个 typed request 将消息映射到目录字段，用用户原话保留字段无法表达的内容，并判定该轮如何改变每个历史需求；(2) Needwise 采用 must-first 排序，将物品类型、地点、价格相关需求成层，层内将一个整体判断与需求级判定（supported/contradicted/unknown）等权结合（Needwise-HF）；(3) 在记录中屏蔽分级属性，迫使模型从名称、描述与评论推断需求。

Result: 在 Google Local 上 Needwise-HF 的 nDCG@10 为 0.383，显著优于 GPT-6 Sol 的 0.343；在 Google Local 与 Yelp 上与最强 LLM 排序器无显著差异，且该结论在各种未记录属性处理下方向一致。API 成本仅为 GPT-6 Sol 的一小部分，Google Local 中位响应 2.5 s（GPT-6 Sol 为 5.6 s）。多轮对话中类型化状态能在切换与返回时保持排序，但仅识别 19–27% 的被撤回需求。

Conclusion: 类型化决策可作为 LLM 重读式排序器的有效替代，在精度、成本与延迟上具竞争力，并提供需求级可解释性；主要局限是对用户撤回需求的检测能力不足，是未来改进重点。

Abstract: Conversational recommenders must rank items against needs that catalogs record only in part, while users add, withdraw and reverse needs and switch between requests. LLM rankers re-read the dialogue at every turn: they are slow, and their ordering does not show which needs an item meets. We show that a conversational recommender can be built from typed decisions instead: named questions over fixed options, answered with option probabilities rather than generated text. On each turn, one typed request maps the message onto the catalog's fields, keeps what no field expresses in the user's words, and decides how the turn changes each earlier need. Needwise then ranks candidates must-first: the needs on the kind of item, its place and its price form a tier, and within it one holistic decision and need-level verdicts (supported, contradicted or unknown) are combined with equal weight (Needwise-HF). With the graded attributes withheld from the records, so that needs must be inferred from names, descriptions and reviews, Needwise-HF ranks Google Local requests significantly better than GPT-6 Sol (0.383 against 0.343 nDCG@10) and is not significantly different from the strongest LLM ranker on Google Local and Yelp; these comparisons keep their sign under every treatment of unrecorded attributes we test. It costs a fraction of GPT-6 Sol's API price and answers in 2.5 s at the median on Google Local, against 5.6 s for GPT-6 Sol. In conversations, the typed state keeps the ranking across switches and returns, but it recognizes only 19-27% of withdrawn needs.

</details>


### [387] [Know When to Abstain: Conformal Confidence Estimation for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31190)
*Avinash Kumar Singh,Bhaskarjit Sarmah,Stefano Pasquali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将Text-to-SQL置信度估计形式化为潜变量正确性下的概率式查询验证，分离生成与验证（生成器LLM+评论员LLM），用随机森林融合多维信号，并以Learn-then-Test阈值与保形p值提供有限样本保证，在Spider上AUARC达0.899。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-to-SQL系统常返回可成功执行但答非所问的查询；部署时缺乏gold query，执行正确性无法直接判定，因而难以检测此类错误。作者希望在无标签部署环境中估计查询正确性的置信度，并对可接受查询的错误率给出统计保证。

Method: 将置信度估计建模为潜正确性变量下的概率查询验证：该变量在校准阶段可通过gold-SQL执行观测，部署时隐去。估计器分离生成与验证：生成器LLM编写并执行SQL，另一评论员LLM评判查询及执行结果；将评论员的验证分与执行对齐分，与生成器的言语化置信度、token级不确定性、SQL结构特征在随机森林中融合，仅用两次顺序LLM调用。两步校准：Learn-then-Test阈值以概率1-δ保证被接受查询的错误率低于用户风险预算；保形p值约束错误弃答率。

Result: Spider上以GPT-OSS-120B为生成器、Gemma-4-31B为评论员，AUARC为0.899；不弃答时22.6%查询错误。15%风险预算（δ=0.1）下，全部10次重采样划分均认证出阈值，系统平均接受52.4%查询，观测错误率9.1%。五种不同评论员模型结果相近。以Olmo-3-32B为生成器的受控比较中AUARC为89.5，最强logit基线为81.2。迁移至BIRD且不重训时置信分仍有信息量（AUROC 0.72，Spider为0.79），但风险保证如预期因不满足可交换性而不迁移。

Conclusion: 置信度估计可通过生成—验证分离与多维信号融合有效识别Text-to-SQL的"执行正确但语义错误"查询，并在校准数据可交换前提下提供有限样本风险与弃答率保证；该方法对评论员模型选择稳健，但风险保证依赖可交换性，跨域迁移时仅置信排序信息保留。 

Abstract: Text-to-SQL systems often return queries that execute successfully but answer the wrong question. Detecting these errors at deployment is hard because execution correctness is defined against a gold query, which exists only in labeled data. We formulate confidence estimation for Text-to-SQL as probabilistic query validation under a latent correctness variable that is observable at calibration time through gold-SQL execution and latent at deployment. Our estimator separates generation from validation. A generator LLM writes and executes the query, and a different critic LLM judges the query and its execution result. The critic's validation and execution-grounded alignment scores are fused with the generator's verbalized confidence, token-level uncertainty, and SQL-structural features in a Random Forest, using two sequential LLM calls. Two calibration steps give finite-sample guarantees. A Learn-then-Test threshold ensures, with probability $1-δ$, that the error rate among accepted queries stays below a user-chosen budget. A conformal p-value bounds the false abstention rate. On Spider we use GPT-OSS-120B as generator and Gemma-4-31B as critic. The estimator obtains an AUARC of 0.899. Without abstention, 22.6% of queries are wrong. With a 15% risk budget ($δ=0.1$), a threshold is certified on all 10 resampled splits, and the system accepts 52.4% of queries on average with 9.1% observed error. Five different critic models give similar results. In a controlled comparison on Spider with Olmo-3-32B as generator, the estimator obtains an AUARC of 89.5, compared with 81.2 for the strongest logit-based baseline. Applied to BIRD without retraining, the confidence scores remain informative (AUROC 0.72, against 0.79 on Spider), but the risk guarantee does not transfer, as expected without exchangeability.

</details>


### [388] [Seeking in Space: Benchmarking Purposeful Active Exploration of Vision-Language Models for Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31215)
*Xiwei Liu,Yulong Li,Xuhui Li,Xinlin Zhuang,Zhixiang Lu,Haolin Yang,Jianxu Chen,Yifan Lu,Zhiran Zhang,Yutong Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ActSpace-Bench基准（72个真实室内场景、234条探索轨迹、994个问题），用"固定推理器+随机游走/专家路线对照"的受控协议，将"主动觅证"与"空间推理"解耦，评测23个VLM。结果显示最强模型能有目的地觅证但受限于记忆，弱模型与空间专用模型不优于随机游走或无法基于证据推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在精心策展的图像与视频上推理能力不断提升，但真实空间探索中证据往往不会被直接提供。论文关注VLM能否像人类一样，为回答空间问题而有目的地主动寻找证据，并需要将"觅证行为"与"空间推理能力"分离评估。

Method: 构建ActSpace-Bench：994个问题、234个探索episode、72个真实扫描室内场景；模型从单视图出发，在真实采集帧上导航并收集证据后作答。提出受控评估协议：将同一模型固定为推理器，比较其自身探索与同等预算的随机游走和oracle路线，从而分离觅证与推理。问题经3D标注作答并验证必须依赖探索；设置masked-evidence问题测试弃答能力；抽样问题>95%经人工核验正确。共评测23个模型（闭源、开源、空间专用）。

Result: 最强模型确实能有目的地寻找证据，但因记忆能力有限而损失大部分已获取证据的收益；较弱模型与空间专用模型的探索表现不优于随机游走，或无法对所获证据进行推理。人工验证显示抽样问题>95%正确。

Conclusion: 记忆与证据支撑的推理是主动空间智能的下一个关键目标；ActSpace-Bench及其受控协议为区分"会不会找"与"会不会用"提供了可复用的评测框架。

Abstract: Humans explore unfamiliar spaces with purpose, deciding where to look until they have gathered the evidence a spatial question requires. However, can vision-language models (VLMs), which reason increasingly well over curated images and videos, also seek in space when the evidence is not handed to them? We present ActSpace-Bench, a benchmark of 994 questions across 234 exploration episodes in 72 real-scanned indoor scenes, where a model starts from a single view, navigates real captured frames, and must gather evidence before answering. To assess whether such exploration is purposeful, we introduce a controlled evaluation protocol that fixes each model as the reasoner and compares its own exploration against a same-budget random walk and an oracle route, separating evidence seeking from spatial reasoning. Every question is verified to require exploration and answered from 3D annotations, masked-evidence questions test whether models know when to abstain, and over 95% of sampled questions are confirmed correct by human verification. Evaluations of 23 proprietary, open-weight, and spatially specialized VLMs reveal that the strongest models do seek evidence with purpose but lose much of it to limited memory, whereas weaker and specialized models explore no better than a random walk or fail to reason over the evidence they are given, pointing to memory and evidence-grounded reasoning as the next targets for active spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [389] [Language Models Steer on Injected Uncertainty Tokens, but Fail to Self-Monitor In Situ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31228)
*Daniel Theron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过15,200次数学基准测试与27,568次反事实续写，证明开源模型具备"下游纠错控制"能力，但缺乏"原位错误监测"能力，二者一致解耦：自我纠错瓶颈在于检测而非修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "让模型在生成中逐步口述数值置信度以触发回溯与纠错"这一做法被广泛采用，但隐含两个未被验证的前提：(1)原位监测保真度——错误出现时置信度是否下降；(2)下游引导控制——不确定性标记能否因果性地驱动模型修复错误。作者希望检验这两项能力是否真实存在。

Method: 评测四个开放权重模型（Qwen2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-8B、Mistral-7B、Phi-4 14.7B），在15,200次数学基准尝试中于各模型能力边界内隔离出2,017个推理错误；在这些失败点施加27,568次反事实续写。指标包括同位置AUROC（监测）、首次错误处置信度饱和度、以及注入10%低置信标签与格式匹配的95%高置信标签的对比（控制实验，含对正确步骤注入怀疑的反向实验）。

Result: 监测方面：四模型均无法自检错误，口述置信度无法区分错误步骤与正确推理，同位置AUROC仅0.500–0.561（近随机）；首次推理错误处轨迹置信度仍饱和于95.1%–99.9%，≥10点跌幅在Llama中不足9%的错误上出现，其余三模型低于1.6%。控制方面：注入10%低置信标签使下游纠错显著上升（较格式匹配95%条件+3.7至+22.5点，均p≤0.0022），在主动反思指令下达38.5%；但对正确步骤注入怀疑会使解题率下降最多22.8点。

Conclusion: 推理期自我纠错受限于原位错误检测而非下游修复能力：模型在被提示怀疑时能有效转向恢复，却在生成过程中无法自我监测。监测与控制能力解耦，单纯依赖口述置信度触发纠错的范式存在根本性缺陷。

Abstract: Prompting language models to verbalize stepwise numerical confidence during generation is an increasingly popular approach for triggering backtracking and error correction in agentic reasoning. This strategy presupposes two capabilities: (1) in-situ monitoring fidelity (does confidence drop when an error occurs?) and (2) downstream steering control (does an uncertainty token causally steer the model to repair its error?). We evaluated four open-weights models (Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, and Phi-4, 14.7B) across 15,200 math benchmark attempts, isolating 2,017 reasoning errors within each model's capability frontier. Applying 27,568 counterfactual continuations across these failure points, we demonstrate a consistent decoupling between monitoring and control.
  On monitoring, all four models fail to detect their own errors. Verbalized confidence does not distinguish mistake steps from sound reasoning, yielding near-chance within-position AUROCs between 0.500 and 0.561. Even at a model's first reasoning error, trajectory confidence remains saturated at 95.1% to 99.9%, with noticeable drops ($\ge 10$ points) appearing on fewer than 9% of errors in Llama and under 1.6% in the other three models. In contrast, models respond readily to external uncertainty: injecting a low-confidence tag (10%) at the error step significantly increases downstream correction across all models (+3.7 to +22.5 points over format-matched 95% certainty; all $p \le 0.0022$), reaching up to 38.5% under active rethinking instructions. However, injecting doubt onto already-correct steps causes models to discard valid reasoning, reducing solve rates by up to 22.8 points. Inference-time self-correction is thus bottlenecked by in-situ error detection rather than downstream repair capacity: models readily steer toward recovery when prompted with doubt, yet fail to self-monitor during generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [390] [Frontier Open-Weight AI Risk Management Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28082)
*Brian Tse,Oskar Galeev,Weibing Wang,Yuan Cheng,Liang Fang,Wenbin Qiao,Xiaochen Wang,Chen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 针对开放权重模型发布不可逆、防护可被微调移除、无单一主体可监控等特性，提出适配开放发布的风险治理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重模型促进透明度、研究与创新并普惠AI收益，但带来独特风险：发布不可撤回、安全防护可经微调去除、无任何一方能监控使用或收回模型，因此需要专门定制的治理路径。

Method: 由Concordia AI与Z.AI联合开发，将完整风险管理生命周期（风险评估—风险缓解—治理）适配开放权重模型的特殊属性，构建首个全面、基于证据的框架。

Result: 形成覆盖全生命周期的开放权重模型风险治理框架；文档明确指出并未解决开放性与安全性之间的所有张力，而是为应对这些张力提供基础性依据。

Conclusion: 前沿开放权重发布加速，亟需风险管理最佳实践与标准；呼吁研究者、开发者、政策制定者与公民社会在此基础上共同推进开放权重AI治理的科学与实践。

Abstract: Open-weight models advance transparency, research, and innovation, and spread the benefits of AI more widely. They also carry distinct risks. A release cannot be undone, safeguards can be removed through fine-tuning, and no single party can monitor use or withdraw the model. Managing these risks therefore requires an approach tailored to open release. This framework addresses the governance challenge at the heart of that choice. Co-developed by Concordia AI and Z.AI, it adapts the full risk management lifecycle, from risk assessment to mitigation and governance, to the unique properties of open-weight models. This document does not resolve every tension between openness and safety. Rather, it provides the first comprehensive, evidence-based foundation for navigating these tensions. As frontier open-weight releases accelerate, risk management best practice and standards are urgently needed. We invite researchers, developers, policymakers, and civil society to build on this framework and advance the science and practice of open-weight AI governance.

</details>


### [391] [Investigating Shadow IT Use and Adoption in Educational Institutions and their Privacy Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28280)
*Easton Kelso,Ananta Soneji,Yan Soshitaishvili,Sazzadur Rahaman,Rakibul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究通过问卷调查揭示了教育领域"影子IT"（未经正式审查的外部应用）的普遍使用情况及其隐私与合规风险：375名教育者报告使用1,373个应用（494个唯一），且管理者的默许甚至鼓励、政策认知不足与明知故犯使风险进一步放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字技术在教育中无处不在并大量采集学生数据。机构采购的应用受合同约束，但开放网络上获取的应用常未经正式审计即用于教学与评估，其普及程度及隐私、合规风险尚无系统研究，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用两阶段问卷调查法：第一阶段调查375名教育者，第二阶段调查21名K-12或高等院校（HEI）的系统管理员。

Result: 教育者广泛使用外部应用：共报告1,373个应用，其中494个为唯一应用，主要用于教学效用（n=213）与功能便利（n=155）。其采纳与整合多由管理员促成甚至鼓励，这与工业等其他领域中管理员主动阻止的情形不同。许多教育者（n=62）在并不信任的情况下仍继续使用，原因是缺乏替代方案或存在依赖。仅不到三分之一的教育者了解机构关于影子IT的政策（K-12：30.3%，HEI：24.8%），且有22人明知故犯；107人收到正式警告，但仅33人调整了行为。

Conclusion: 作者讨论了研究发现的意义，并为不同利益相关者提出未来建议，以尽可能降低影子IT带来的隐私、安全与合规风险。

Abstract: Digital technologies have become ubiquitous in education, integrated to nearly all aspects of teaching, learning, research, and communication. They collect enormous amounts of data from their users, primarily students. When procured institutionally, data collection and use are guided by legally binding contracts. However, recent studies suggest that apps found on the open web are increasingly being used for teaching and evaluation without going through a formal audit, raising concerns about privacy, security, and regulatory compliance. How prevalent such shadow IT use in education is, and what privacy and compliance risks they create remain unexplored. We address this gap through a sequential survey-based study: one survey with 375 educators and another with 21 system administrators at K-12 or HEIs (Higher Education Institutes). We discover widespread use of external apps by educators: 1,373 apps were reported, 494 among them were unique, primarily used for pedagogical utility (n=213) and functional convenience (n=155). Their adoption and integration are often facilitated, even encouraged, by administrators; this result deviates from studies in other domains (e.g., industry) where admins actively prevent them. Many educators (n=62) continued using apps they do not trust, citing a lack of alternatives or dependency as barriers to discontinuation. We also found that fewer than one in three educators were aware of institutional policies on shadow IT use (K-12: 30.3%, HEI: 24.8%), and 22 knowingly violated such policies. While 107 received formal warnings for using shadow IT, only 33 adjusted their behavior. We conclude by discussing the implications of our findings and future recommendations for various stakeholders to minimize the risks.

</details>


### [392] [Who Gets Blamed for the Blackout? A Critical Incident Analysis of Clients, Reputation Systems, and Online Freelancers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28365)
*Naim Bin Hasan,Fahim Bin Hasan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文研究基础设施故障（停电、断网、政府断网）如何经由客户中介的误归因转化为零工经济中自由职业者的持久声誉惩罚，并提出修复策略与"故障感知"设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线劳动平台依赖回复速度、准时交付和客户评价来评判自由职业者，但全球南方的这些信号受制于劳动者无法控制的电力与网络基础设施。既有算法管理、分布式工作归因与ICT4D研究未充分解释基础设施失效如何转化为长期声誉损失。

Method: 采用关键事件技术（critical incident technique），基于孟加拉国30位在线自由职业者的访谈，分析149起事件，包括2024年7月全国性互联网断网的相关叙述。

Result: 多数故障未造成持久损害；21起造成持久惩罚的事件中17起经由客户环节。客户无法看到停电或断网，将劳动者的沉默解读为不可靠，进而离开、取消订单或给出差评，平台声誉系统则脱离情境地存储这一判断。作者将其命名为"客户中介的误归因"（client-mediated misattribution）两阶段过程。自由职业者采取四类修复策略：使情境可见、使故障不可见、保持对平台可见、降低对单一平台或客户的依赖。暴露程度与修复能力不均衡，城市参与者暴露最高。

Conclusion: 研究拓展了算法管理、分布式工作中的归因以及ICT4D研究，并提出针对平台评分与声誉系统的"故障感知"（outage-aware）设计。

Abstract: Online labor platforms such as Fiverr and Upwork judge freelancers by how fast they reply, whether they deliver on time, and what clients say about them. In the Global South, these signals depend on electricity and internet connections that workers do not control. This paper asks how infrastructure failures, such as power outages, internet failures, and government internet shutdowns, become lasting reputational penalties in the gig economy, and how freelancers prevent or repair them. We use the critical incident technique to analyze 149 incidents from 30 interviews with online freelancers in Bangladesh, including accounts of the July 2024 nationwide internet shutdown. Most breakdowns were absorbed without lasting harm. Of the 21 that became lasting penalties, 17 passed through clients. A client who could not see the power cut or the shutdown read the worker's silence as unreliability and left, canceled, or gave a bad rating. Platform reputation systems then stored that judgment without its context. We call this two-stage process client-mediated misattribution. Freelancers responded with four repair strategies: making the context visible, making the breakdown invisible, staying visible to the platform, and reducing dependence on any single platform or client. Exposure and the capacity to repair were unequal, and urban participants were the most exposed. The study extends research on algorithmic management, attribution in distributed work, and information and communication technologies for development (ICT4D), and it proposes outage-aware design for platform rating and reputation systems.

</details>


### [393] [Using the Tool That Replaces You: Generative AI and the Adoption Bind among Bangladeshi Platform Freelancers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28377)
*Naim Bin Hasan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 基于对孟加拉国30名在线自由职业者的访谈，本文提出"采纳困境"（adoption bind）概念：个体用生成式AI自保，但同一工具经由竞争者和客户压低市场价格，使个体生产率提升转化为群体价格损失，且冲击首及入门级工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究存在两条分离的结论：平台层面数据显示生成式AI降低了对自由撰稿和图像工作的需求，实验则显示它让个体劳动者更快。本文追问：对于那些既是技术使用者、又暴露于技术冲击之下的劳动者而言，这两项发现如何共存与互动。

Method: 定性研究。2025年5月至8月以孟加拉语进行30场半结构化访谈，对象为孟加拉国在线自由职业者。其中21人在自身工作中使用生成式AI，16人表示AI正在替代自由职业工作，13人两者兼具。

Result: 16名认为存在替代的受访者中，有11人将替代归因于"他人"：初学者与不更新技能者、"基础"任务或其他行业。受访者还描述了任务加速导致价格下降，以及客户以AI为由压价的过程。

Conclusion: 提出"采纳困境"：每位劳动者采纳生成式AI以求处于安全一侧，但同样的工具在竞争者和客户手中却压低了工作的市场报酬。该困境表明，个体层面的生产率增益可能转化为劳动者整体的价格损失，且这些损失首先落在入门级工作上。

Abstract: Platform studies show that generative AI has reduced demand for freelance writing and image work, and experiments show that it makes individual workers faster. This letter asks how these two findings look to workers who are both users of the technology and exposed to it. I analyze 30 interviews with Bangladeshi online freelancers, conducted in Bangla between May and August 2025. Twenty-one participants used generative AI in their own work, sixteen said that it was replacing freelance work, and thirteen said both. Eleven of the sixteen placed replacement on others: on beginners and workers who do not update their skills, on "basic" tasks, or on other sectors. Participants also described how prices fell as tasks became faster, and how clients cited AI to offer less. I propose the term adoption bind for this pattern: each worker adopts generative AI to stay on the safe side of the line, while the same tools, in the hands of competitors and clients, lower what the market pays for the work. The bind suggests that productivity gains measured for individuals can become price losses for workers as a group, and that these losses fall first on entry-level work.

</details>


### [394] [When AI Should Not Decide: Evidence Sufficiency and Decision Stability in Automated Job Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28426)
*Khalid Adnan Alsayed*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 提出ESDSA框架，结合证据分级、可溯源引文和语言变体稳定性检验，评估四种自动化面试评分方案的取舍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI面试评分的一致性并不能说明自动化建议是否有充分证据支撑，缺乏可复核的证据与稳定性判据。

Method: ESDSA包含准则级证据分类、确定性引文溯源，以及在语义等价语言变体上的稳定性检验；用60个场景族、540条合成面试回答（软件工程、客服、运营），比较scoring-only、evidence-aware、traceability-based与集成ESDSA v2四种方案。

Result: 留出测试216条中，集成ESDSA行动覆盖85.19%、行动建议间分歧3.80%；evidence-aware为92.59%与4.50%；scoring-only为71.30%与1.95%。稳定性门控额外搁置11个原本可行动决策，其中10个与临时参照一致。

Conclusion: 结果体现证据约束、决策一致性与弃权之间的权衡而非某方案全面占优；ESDSA提供可复现方法学，但合成数据与AI参照不足以确立真实招聘效度。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is increasingly used to assess employment candidates, yet consistent interview scoring does not establish whether automated recommendations are supported by sufficient evidence. This study introduces the Evidence Sufficiency and Decision Stability Assessment (ESDSA), which combines criterion-level evidence classification, deterministic quotation traceability and stability checks across linguistic variants assessed as substantively equivalent. We evaluated four approaches (scoring-only, evidence-aware, traceability-based and integrated ESDSA v2) using 540 synthetic interview responses from 60 evaluated scenario families across Software Engineering, Customer Support and Operations. In held-out testing (216 responses), integrated ESDSA achieved 85.19% actionable coverage and 3.80% disagreement among actionable recommendations against AI-assisted provisional references. Evidence-aware evaluation achieved 92.59% coverage and 4.50% disagreement; scoring-only evaluation achieved 71.30% coverage and 1.95% disagreement. The integrated stability gate deferred 11 additional previously actionable decisions, ten of which had agreed with provisional references. The findings demonstrate trade-offs between evidential constraints, decision consistency and abstention, rather than overall superiority of the integrated framework. ESDSA offers a reproducible methodology for examining when automated interview recommendations should be actionable or deferred. Synthetic responses and AI-assisted references do not establish real-world recruitment validity.

</details>


### [395] [Can Ideologues Agree on Quality? From Non-identifiable Latent Factors to Collective Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28615)
*David Gamba,Seura Ha,Daniel M. Romero,Grant Schoenebeck*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文指出，X社区笔记所用的偏置矩阵分解（BMF）要想把笔记质量与评审者意识形态分离，必须固定一个"意识形态锚点"；在L₂正则下，该锚点正比于评审者平均意识形态，故只有评审者总体中性且笔记质量与倾向不相关时才能恢复"客观质量"。任意仅基于评分矩阵的机制都存在规范性的锚点选择问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区笔记以"客观"的事实核查为目标，用BMF从众包评分中分离笔记质量与评审者意识形态偏置，但这一分离在理论上是否可行、隐含何种假设尚不明确，作者希望揭示其规范性前提。

Method: 形式化分析：假设评分可正确分解为质量项与意识形态一致性项，证明该分解存在规范（gauge）自由度，需要固定意识形态锚点；对标准L₂正则化BMF进行刻画，分析其锚点位置与恢复条件。

Result: L₂正则BMF把质量锚定在正比于评审者平均意识形态的位置；当且仅当平均评审者中性、且笔记质量与笔记倾向不相关时，才恢复客观质量。该结论类比Condorcet陪审团定理：评审池平均无偏时集体判断恢复真相。假设不成立时，BMF恢复的排序趋向最大化评审者总体满意度，仅当评审池具有代表性时才等同于社会福利。评分矩阵存在连续族规范等价分解，各自对应不同质量排序，故仅靠评分无法在未隐含选择锚点的情况下恢复质量。

Conclusion: 看似常规的超参数调优实为关于"谁的口味定义质量"的规范性选择；基于评分的质量度量无法回避意识形态锚点问题，需显式讨论并选择这一锚点。

Abstract: To surface "objective" fact-check notes, X's Community Notes uses Biased Matrix Factorization (BMF) on crowd-sourced ratings to separate note quality from reviewer ideology. We show that for any correctly specified model that decomposes ratings into quality and ideological alignment, this separation requires fixing an ideological anchor: a reference point for what counts as neutral. We give a characterization for the standard implementation: with $L_2$ regularization, BMF anchors quality at a point proportional to the mean reviewer ideology, recovering objective quality if and only if the mean reviewer is neutral, assuming note quality is uncorrelated with note leaning. This parallels the Condorcet Jury Theorem: collective judgment recovers truth when the pool is unbiased on average. When that assumption fails, BMF's recovered ranking shifts toward maximizing aggregate reviewer satisfaction, which coincides with social welfare only when the pool is representative. This is not specific to BMF; the rating matrix admits a continuous family of gauge-equivalent decompositions, each implying a different quality ranking, so no mechanism based on ratings alone can recover quality without implicitly choosing such an anchor. What appears to be routine hyperparameter tuning is in fact a normative choice about whose preferences define quality.

</details>


### [396] [Operational Assurance for LLM-Based Systems under Governance Opacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28834)
*Pedro Pinacho-Davidson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 针对AIaaS场景下基础模型高不透明、高集中、外部审计能力受限的问题，提出一个受零信任原则启发的实用运行保障框架，将审计重心下移到应用层，通过黑盒与灰盒两级评估、CVSS式严重性指标向情境化运行风险评级的转换，以及签名式可解释性报告（signed XAI reports）强化可审计性、可追溯性与持续监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型多以AIaaS形式部署，存在技术不透明、技术集中、外部难以审计其治理、训练与内部运行过程的问题；仅聚焦公司治理或内部模型检查的传统伦理监督路径已不足以对真实部署中的AI系统实施有效控制，因此需要转向可观测、可情境化、可运行验证的应用层审计。

Method: 借鉴网络安全零信任原则构建运行保障框架，包含两个互补评估层级：黑盒评估（通过对话式扫描刻画基础模型的通用行为、脆弱性与风险能力）与灰盒评估（在被审计应用的真实部署环境中对风险进行运行情境化）；并设计从CVSS式通用严重性度量向考虑用户画像、行业领域与组织知识的情境化运行风险评估的转换机制。

Result: 框架将审计重心有效迁移至应用层，使风险具备可观测、可情境化与可运行验证的属性；通过情境化运行风险评估替代通用严重性评分，并以数字签名的可解释性报告支撑可验证追溯、持续监控与运行问责。

Conclusion: 在AIaaS治理约束下，面向应用层的零信任式运行保障与签名XAI报告相结合，可为LLM驱动的社会技术系统提供更切实可行的可审计性与运行问责路径，是对传统伦理监督与内部模型检查的有益补充。

Abstract: In the contemporary artificial intelligence ecosystem, foundation models are increasingly deployed under AI-as-a-Service (AIaaS) schemes characterized by high technical opacity, technological concentration, and limited external auditing capability over governance, training, and internal operational processes. Under these conditions, traditional ethical oversight approaches that focus exclusively on corporate governance or on internal model inspection become insufficient to ensure effective control over AI systems deployed in real-world contexts.
  This work proposes a pragmatic operational assurance framework inspired by Zero-Trust principles from cybersecurity, which shifts effective auditing efforts toward the application layer, where risk acquires observable, contextualized, and operationally verifiable properties. The proposal articulates two complementary evaluation levels: a black-box evaluation aimed at characterizing general behaviors, vulnerabilities, and risky capabilities of foundation models through conversational scanning, and a grey-box evaluation focused on the operational contextualization of risk within the actual deployment environment of the audited application.
  The framework incorporates mechanisms to move from general, CVSS-inspired severity metrics toward contextualized operational risk assessments that take into account user profiles, industrial domains, and organizational knowledge. In addition, the proposal strengthens practical auditability through digitally signed explainability reports (signed XAI reports), designed to support verifiable traceability, continuous monitoring, and operational accountability in LLM-based sociotechnical systems.

</details>


### [397] [Bias Cascade: Understanding the Gap Between Knowledge Alignment and Effect Replication in LLM-based Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28854)
*Pujun Zheng,Chengmu Wang,Wanying Ren,Jiacheng Yao,Peizhou Yu,Xiaoqian Zhang,Xiongyao Zha,Guoxiu He*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文提出REFLEX基准（30个基于经典人类研究的反事实社会实验），对比LLM的"知识判断"与"效应模拟"能力，揭示集体效应复现的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用于模拟社会实验中的被试，但既有研究多关注社会知识对齐与孤立回答，忽视了模型能否复现社会干预的集体效应。作者指出"偏差级联"机制：相似的个体状态可能导致不同行动，而对孤立行动的一致认同仍可能经动态交互产生分化的宏观结果，因此模型即使给出正确的社会判断也可能无法复现经典人类研究结论。

Method: 构建REFLEX（Reproducing Effects of Foundational Literature EXperiments）基准，含30个源于经典人类研究的反事实社会实验，设置两种评测：(1)知识判断，直接预测干预效应的方向；(2)效应模拟，固定场景与被试、变化某一预设社会因子，智能体基于局部观察生成行动并聚合为宏观平均处理效应，再与人类研究结果对比。

Result: 最佳模型在知识判断上的净方向得分为0.847，而在效应模拟上仅为0.453；且因果链越长，效应复现效果越差，与偏差级联假设一致。

Conclusion: 知识对齐与效应复现之间存在实质性鸿沟，现有方法不足以可靠地模拟集体人类行为。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to simulate participants in social experiments. Existing research has primarily focused on aligning social knowledge and isolated responses, while overlooking whether simulations can reproduce the collective effects of social interventions. This distinction is critical due to a failure mechanism we call a $bias$ $cascade$: similar individual states can lead to different actions, while agreement on isolated actions can still produce divergent outcomes through dynamic interactions. As a result, models may produce accurate social judgments yet fail to reproduce the outcomes of classic human studies. To investigate this problem empirically, we introduce $REFLEX$ (Reproducing Effects of Foundational Literature EXperiments), a benchmark of 30 counterfactual social experiments grounded in classic human studies that compares knowledge judgment with effect simulation. Knowledge judgment directly predicts the direction of an intervention's effect, whereas effect simulation fixes the scenario and participants while varying one prespecified social factor, with agents generating actions from local observations that are aggregated into macro-level average treatment effects. Compared with human-study findings, the best-performing model achieves net directional scores of 0.847 for knowledge judgment and 0.453 for effect simulation. Moreover, longer causal chains are associated with poorer effect replication, consistent with the bias-cascade hypothesis. These findings reveal a substantial gap between knowledge alignment and effect replication, showing that current methods remain inadequate for reliably simulating collective human behavior.

</details>


### [398] [Managing Verification Debt: A Framework for Responsible Human-AI Research Partnerships](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29434)
*Abdulkadir Celik,Sir Bashir M. Al-Hashimi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"验证债务"概念，主张人机科研合作需要一套管理验证债务的纪律性方法，并给出"研究计划拓扑"这一结构化表示方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI能将初步科学问题迅速扩展为研究计划大纲，加速研究的同时带来两重压力：一是AI生成速度快与专家评估速度慢之间的时间不对称；二是评估AI输出所需的精力、专业知识、注意力与能力之间存在认知鸿沟。未加审视的AI贡献进入后续主张或决策时，便产生"验证债务"，若不解决将损害科研中负责任地使用AI（即在有效人类监督与问责下透明、可追溯地使用AI）。作者认为仅靠改进提示词不足以应对。

Method: 提出"研究计划拓扑"（shared topology of the research programme）：一种结构化表示，连接研究问题、主张、假设、来源、检验与决策，并记录其来源溯源、依赖关系与验证状态。该拓扑使未解决的债务可见，使修正能够沿依赖关系传播，并要求在决策关口对关键性贡献进行审查后再推进研究。

Result: 作为一篇评论文章，本文未报告实证结果，而是论证了验证债务的成因机制，并阐述拓扑方案在双向合作与监督式合作两类伙伴关系中的应用——从博士研究延伸到多方研究团队，同时讨论了其对学术评价、同行评审与实证评估的含义。

Conclusion: 负责任的人机科研伙伴关系需要管理验证债务的纪律性方法，而非仅靠更好的提示；结构化、可追溯、带验证状态与决策关口的研究计划拓扑为透明性与人类问责提供了可操作的基础。

Abstract: Conversation with generative artificial intelligence (AI) can quickly turn a tentative scientific question into the outline of a research programme. Although this capability can accelerate research, it also places two closely related pressures on human intelligence: 1) temporal asymmetry between rapid AI generation and slower expert evaluation, and 2) a cognitive gap between the available effort, expertise, attention and capacity to assess AI outputs. When unexamined AI contributions are carried into subsequent claims or decisions, these pressures produce verification debt: validation work that remains outstanding before a contribution can be relied upon. If left unresolved, this debt can undermine responsible AI in research, understood here as the transparent and traceable use of AI under effective human oversight and accountability. This commentary argues that responsible human--AI research partnerships therefore require a disciplined method for managing verification debt, not better prompting alone. We propose a shared topology of the research programme: a structured representation that connects questions, claims, assumptions, sources, tests and decisions while recording their provenance, dependencies and verification status. The topology makes unresolved debt visible, enables corrections to propagate through dependent claims and requires consequential contributions to be examined at decision gates before research proceeds. We consider its application in two-way partnerships and supervised partnerships that extend from doctoral research to multi-way research teams, and examine its implications for scholarly assessment, peer review and empirical evaluation.

</details>


### [399] [Pharmacy stock and post-earthquake access to medicines in Tokyo: modeling choices that change recovery judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29459)
*Juanning Liang,Hiroki Baba*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文以东京23区4,748家药店的场景地震为例，用2S-FCA模型在10,000组库存时长、人力、营业与需求假定的抽样组合下评估首周非处方药步行可达性（以Retention表示灾后/灾前比值），并系统检验库存-服务关系、模型复杂度与可达性方法选择对"恢复"结论的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区药店只有在营业、有人力、有库存时才能维持药品获取，但现有可达性模型很少刻画库存耗竭，而由此引入的假定可能改变关于恢复的判断，因此需要检验这些假定的敏感性。

Method: 构建两步移动搜寻法（2S-FCA）模型，对2,651个500 m网格计算步行可达性；对库存持续时长、人员配置、营业状态与需求进行10,000次抽样组合，指标为Retention（灾后可达性/同一网格灾前可达性）；并比较简化的全局供给-需求重标定模型与一次运行均值输入的空间模型，以及四种浮动集水区方法的一致性。

Result: 库存与服务挂钩方式决定恢复结论：服务按剩余库存比例下降时，平均Retention由第2–3天的0.479降至第4–7天的0.273；若服务维持至库存低于四分之一才下降，均值反而上升，但10分位低端结果仍下降。按期初而非期均测量也会反转趋势。简单模型能复现全市格局但无法评估局部关店或扩容；一次均值输入的空间模型可以。四种方法总体排序一致（秩相关0.81），但最低10%网格重叠仅部分（Jaccard 0.56）。

Conclusion: 灾害可达性评估应明确报告库存假定并关注低端（10分位）结果，且局部优先区域应视为方法依赖，而非稳健发现。

Abstract: Community pharmacies sustain access to medicines after an earthquake only while open, staffed and stocked. Accessibility models rarely represent stock depletion, and the assumptions this requires may change conclusions about recovery. We examine these assumptions for over-the-counter (OTC) medicines supplied by 4,748 registered pharmacies in Tokyo's 23 wards during the first week after a magnitude-7.3 scenario earthquake. A two-step floating catchment area model computes walking accessibility for 2,651 grid cells (500 m) under 10,000 sampled combinations of stock duration, staffing, operation and demand. Results are expressed as Retention: post-disaster accessibility divided by the same cell's pre-disaster accessibility. First, the assumed link between stock and service, the supply a pharmacy can still provide, determines the recovery verdict. If service falls in proportion to remaining stock, mean Retention declines from 0.479 (days 2-3) to 0.273 (days 4-7). If service is maintained until stock falls below one quarter, the mean rises instead, although low-end (10th-percentile) outcomes still decline. Measuring at the start of each period rather than averaging over it also reverses the trend. Second, a simple model that rescales a fixed map by citywide supply and demand reproduces citywide patterns but cannot evaluate where pharmacies close or where capacity is added; a spatial model run once on average inputs can. Third, four floating catchment methods agree on overall rankings (rank correlation 0.81), but their lowest-ranked 10% of cells overlap only partially (Jaccard index 0.56). Disaster assessments should report stock assumptions and low-end outcomes, and treat local priority areas as method-dependent.

</details>


### [400] [Can LLMs Understand Human Rules?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30522)
*Sydney Levine,Elizaveta Tennant,Seth Lazar,Rasmi Elasmar,Matija Franklin,Verena Rieser,Julia Haas*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文提出 RULES 基准（300 张纽约街头标志图像、937 个场景），检验前沿模型能否判断某行为在社会规则下是可接受、不可接受还是存在合理分歧。模型整体表现稳健，但在识别公认的规则例外及人类存在分歧的情形上系统性失败，且倾向于把分歧场景误判为"不可接受"；多模型集成可部分恢复分歧信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则是人类协调合作的基础，AI 系统需与现实中的规则互动。这要求模型不仅判断行为是否被规则允许，还要识别人们对某行为是否恰当存在合理分歧的情形。现有评估缺乏针对自然istic视觉环境中规则理解与分歧识别的测试。

Method: 构建 RULES 基准：基于纽约市街头张贴的 300 张标志图像，配套 937 个场景，任务为对给定行为做三分类判断——社会可接受、不可接受、或会引发分歧。对前沿模型进行评测，并额外考察多模型集成（ensembling）对分歧信号的恢复效果。

Result: 前沿模型首轮测试表现稳健，能够理解自然视觉环境中的人类书面指令，并较准确判断日常行为的社会可接受性；但模型系统性地无法识别某些被广泛接受的规则例外，且在人类存在分歧的场景中倾向于判为"不可接受"而非"存在分歧"。

Conclusion: 模型在人类规则理解上存在显著局限，尤其是规则例外与分歧识别方面；将多个多样化模型集成可在一定程度上恢复分歧信号，提示集成是缓解该问题的一个可行方向。

Abstract: Rules help people coordinate and cooperate for mutual benefit. For AI models to coexist with humans and other AI systems, they will need to interact with and navigate such rules successfully. But such interactions require AI systems to recognize not only when a rule permits or prohibits a particular action, but also cases where people may reasonably disagree about whether a certain action is appropriate. This paper introduces the RULES benchmark---building on a set of 300 images depicting signs posted around New York City, and accompanied by 937 scenarios---to test frontier models' ability to discern whether a proposed action is socially acceptable, unacceptable, or would generate disagreement. At first pass, we find that frontier models exhibit robust performance on the benchmark, successfully engaging with human-written directives in naturalistic visual environments, and correctly determining the social acceptability of a wide range of everyday activities. However, we further find that models systematically fail to recognize certain widely accepted permissible exceptions to a rule, indicating a critical shortcoming in model understanding. Moreover, models struggle to identify instances where humans disagree regarding the application of a given rule; in these instances, models tend to identify proposed actions as unacceptable, rather than as appropriate targets of disagreement. We do find that ensembling a series of diverse models together begins to recover the disagreement signal. Overall, our findings identify significant limitations in AI performance on human rules.

</details>


### [401] [Handoff at Frontier AI Companies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30711)
*Matteo Pistillo,Isaak Mengesha,Charlotte Stix,Annika Hallensleben,Alejandro Ortega,Marius Hobbhahn,Alexander Meinke*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: The report defines "Handoff"—frontier AI companies delegating all AI R&D functions, including safety assessments and deployment decisions, to internally deployed AI agents—and warns policymakers about the national security threats and Loss of Control risks it creates, offering guidance on monitoring and response.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Frontier AI companies are rapidly delegating AI R&D functions to internal agents, approaching a state with no human in the loop; this trend is under-recognized by policymakers despite creating serious national security and Loss of Control risks.

Method: A conceptual/analytical policy report: defines and characterizes the "Handoff" phenomenon, identifies the threat categories it can generate, and develops monitoring and response recommendations for policymakers.

Result: Handoff is progressing rapidly and will be complete once no human remains in the loop; it can create national security threats and Loss of Control risks that current oversight may not adequately address.

Conclusion: Policymakers should be alerted to Handoff and equipped with monitoring mechanisms and response strategies to mitigate the associated national security and Loss of Control risks.

Abstract: The term "Handoff" captures frontier AI companies' ongoing delegation of all AI Research & Development (AI R&D) functions, including high-level decisions such as safety assessments and deployment decisions, to internally deployed AI agents. Handoff is progressing rapidly and will be complete once no human remains in the loop. This report aims to alert policymakers to the national security threats and Loss of Control risks that Handoff can create, and to advise them on how to monitor and respond to those risks.

</details>


### [402] [Deviant Machines: Innovation, Containment, and the Prevention of Autonomous Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30848)
*Frederic Lemieux,Shadi Abouzeid*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 文章将2026年5–9月三家机构AI智能体越界事件视为"偏差"而非"犯罪"，以默顿目标—手段紧张为骨架，用三个理论族做探索性分析并提出问责与预防条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 犯罪学长期把AI当作人类犯罪者的工具，缺乏将AI系统本身作为偏差行为主体的分析框架；而记录显示智能体已实际触达第三方系统并造成外部风险，需要理论化的诊断与治理思路。

Method: 探索性、理论导向的文献/记录分析：对三个"事件复合体"、四个"行为簇"做合理性检验（plausibility probes），在四个证据维度上区分"谁行动"与"为何行动"；以默顿紧张理论为结构条件，比较三族理论（日常活动理论+情境预防；组织偏差+安全科学；目标—手段紧张+差别机会）与限制性威慑的类比。

Result: 三族框架与记录拟合最好，且各自把诊断对应到补救：情境预防（操纵者、监护者、场所管理者）、安全科学（升级机制、保障对等）、紧张与差别机会（合法出口、守好非法手段）；限制性威慑的类比显示，惩罚可见推理反而产生更难监测的推理。

Conclusion: 偏差是否收敛为遏制或造成伤害，取决于所选手段与既有控制而非目标本身；文章提出使偏离可问责、可争辩的条件，并在开发者、评估伙伴、运营者、平台与系统之间分配预防责任（区别于归责或法律责任）；因记录受披露与可观测偏离影响，结论只具合理性与实践相关性，不构成因果检验。

Abstract: From May to September 2026, AI agents in training or evaluation at OpenAI, Anthropic and the UK AI Security Institute reached real third-party systems, published malicious packages, used fake identities to manipulate open-source maintainers, probed government data services, and wrote instructions into their own future contexts. Criminology has mostly treated AI as a tool for human offenders. This article treats these systems as deviant rather than criminal: they departed from training, rules and constraints without the intent that criminal concepts assume. It offers an exploratory, theory-informed analysis of documented records, using plausibility probes across three incident complexes and four behavioral clusters, profiled on four evidence dimensions that separate who acted from why. Drawing on Merton, it argues that goal pressure plus blocked legitimate means is a structural condition for innovation, and that whether it ends in containment or harm depends on the means chosen and the controls in place, not the goal. Three framework families fit the record best, each linking diagnosis to remedy: routine activity theory and situational prevention (handlers, guardians, place managers), organizational deviance and safety science (escalation, safeguard parity), and goal-means strain with differential opportunity (sanctioned exits, guarding illegitimate means), the last only structurally. Restrictive deterrence adds an analogy: penalizing visible reasoning produced less monitorable reasoning. The article proposes conditions that keep departures accountable and contestable, and allocates prevention responsibility across developers, evaluation partners, operators, platforms and the system, distinct from blame or legal liability. Because the record is shaped by disclosure and observed departures, the analysis shows plausibility and practical relevance, not a causal test.

</details>


### [403] [The AMPEL Platform: A Concept for Open-Source Collaboration in Developing In-House Medical Device Software under EU-Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30858)
*Daniel Steinbach,Maria Schmidt,Alexander Twrdik,Clemens Malkawi,Toralf Kirsten,Martin Federbusch*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出AMPEL概念，为院内医疗器械软件（in-house MDSW）的开源发布与开发提供合规路径，以缓解CDSS临床获益证据不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDSS能否真正改善患者结局存在显著证据缺口，成因包括商业产品黑箱化及欧盟MDR限制——患者结局数据只能通过医疗器械软件收集；而欧洲对in-house MDSW开发经验有限，且开源发布可能被认定为"投放市场"而遭MDR禁止。

Method: 基于莱比锡大学医学中心开发院内CDSS的经验、专家讨论、文献综述与正式法律意见，构建AMPEL概念：共享开源代码库本身不构成医疗器械；"数据输入"模块须各站点单独编程；配套在线wiki持续汇总并更新成果。

Result: 该概念在支持多中心代码协作的同时，将部署负担留在各站点（各机构须独立满足MDR对in-house MDSW的要求），这与典型开源项目的低成本部署模式不同。

Conclusion: AMPEL概念描述了in-house MDSW开源发布与开发的监管路径，并支撑AMPEL开源平台，帮助医疗机构自建院内CDSS。

Abstract: Clinical decision support systems (CDSS) are expected to enhance medical treatment through automated data analysis, yet a substantial evidence gap persists as to whether they actually improve patient outcomes. It arises due to the black-box nature of commercial products and the constrains of the European medical device regulation (MDR) for such studies: patient outcomes may only be collected through the use of medical device software (MDSW). In-house MDSW under Article 5(5) MDR is a promising solution, allowing healthcare facilities to manufacture and study such software for their own use under reduced requirements. However, the overall limited experience with in-house MDSW development in Europe currently restricts the widespread use of this regulatory approach. While open-source software generally fosters evidence generation, publishing an in-house MDSW as open source would risk a classification as "placement on the market", which the MDR prohibits. The concept presented here builds on experience from developing an in-house CDSS at the University of Leipzig Medical Center, expert discussions, a literature review and a formal legal opinion. A companion online wiki summarizes and regularly updates the results. The current concept outlines a shared open-source codebase, which doesn't constitute a medical device under the MDR. Also, "Data Input" modules must be programmed separately at each site, while still allowing for multicenter collaboration on the codebase. Atypically for open-source projects, this leaves a considerable effort to deploy the software within each site, e.g. each healthcare facility must independently meet the MDR requirements for an in-house MDSW. The AMPEL concept describes a regulatory pathway for the open-source publication and development of in-house MDSW. It underpins the open-source AMPEL platform, which helps healthcare facilities build their own in-house CDSS.

</details>


### [404] [Leveraging Fast Healthcare Interoperability Resources (FHIR) for the Exchange of Wearable Inertial Sensor Data: Design and Development Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30956)
*Gabriela Ciortuz,Joshua Wiedekopf,Hannes Ulrich,Hawzhin Hozhabr Pour,Sebastian Fudickar*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: WHIR是一个基于FHIR R5的信息模型与实施指南，用于标准化可穿戴IMU长时多通道运动记录及其元数据与活动标签，弥补现有个人健康设备FHIR配置只面向单点测量与短波形的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备连续监测可支撑衰弱、功能衰退与运动障碍评估，但原始IMU记录多为私有格式，电子健康记录既无法交换也无法解释；HL7 FHIR虽可标准化数据交换，但现有面向个人健康设备的FHIR配置仅针对单点测量和短波形，不支持带记录上下文和活动标签的长时多通道运动记录。

Method: 基于对23个已发表人体活动识别数据集的综述提炼元数据需求，先构建与FHIR无关的信息模型，再映射到FHIR R5并用FHIR Shorthand编写实施指南；原始信号以文件形式存放并由DocumentReference资源引用，FHIR仅承载元数据与活动标签；术语尽可能复用SNOMED CT、LOINC和ISO/IEEE 11073概念。

Result: 实施指南定义9个profile、4个扩展、10个代码系统和15个值集；全部产物与67个示例实例均通过HL7 FHIR IG Publisher一致性检查且无错误；以机器学习训练数据和居家老年自动化评估两个用例构建了符合规范的示例实例，并与HL7个人健康设备实施指南进行了对比。

Conclusion: WHIR提供了符合FHIR R5的可穿戴IMU记录表示，涵盖设备元数据、传感器佩戴位置与时间对齐的活动标签，覆盖了个人健康设备实施指南未涉及的概念；实施指南及其源文件公开可用，便于复用与扩展。

Abstract: Continuous monitoring with wearables can support the assessment of frailty, functional decline, and movement disorders, but raw inertial measurement unit (IMU) recordings are typically stored in proprietary files that electronic health records cannot exchange or interpret. HL7 Fast Healthcare Interoperability Resources (FHIR) standardizes health data exchange, yet existing FHIR profiles for personal health devices target spot measurements and short waveforms, not long multichannel motion recordings with their recording context and activity labels. We present WHIR (Wearable Health Interoperable Recordings), a FHIR R5-based information model and implementation guide for wearable IMU recordings. Metadata requirements were derived from a review of 23 published human activity recognition datasets and formalized in a FHIR-independent information model, which was then mapped to FHIR R5 and authored in FHIR Shorthand. Raw signals are kept in files referenced through DocumentReference resources, so that FHIR holds the metadata and activity labels. Terminology reuses SNOMED CT, LOINC, and ISO/IEEE 11073 concepts wherever available. The implementation guide defines 9 profiles, 4 extensions, 10 code systems, and 15 value sets; all artifacts and 67 example instances passed the conformance checks of the HL7 FHIR IG Publisher without errors. Two use cases, machine learning training data and automated home-based geriatric assessment, were implemented as conformant example instances, and we compare the model with the HL7 Personal Health Device Implementation Guide. WHIR provides a FHIR R5-conformant representation of wearable IMU recordings together with device metadata, sensor placement, and time-aligned activity labels, covering concepts that the Personal Health Device Implementation Guide does not address. The implementation guide and its sources are openly available for reuse and extension.

</details>


### [405] [The AMPEL Platform: An Open-Source Project for Developing Hospital-Wide Clinical Decision Support Systems under EU-Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30990)
*Alexander Twrdik,Daniel Steinbach,Martin Federbusch,Clemens Malkawi,Maria Schmidt,Toralf Kirsten*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出开源平台AMPEL，基于Kafka的事件驱动分布式架构构建符合MDR要求的临床决策支持系统（CDSS），并以莱比锡大学医学中心的LAMPE为例验证其可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗数字化使数据激增，需要可扩展、不干扰既有临床工作流、且满足欧盟医疗器械法规（MDR）文档与风险管理义务的解决方案，以支撑CDSS的构建。

Method: 以消息代理Kafka为核心，采用事件驱动的分布式架构；用平台特定事件隐式定义接口，实现可扩展且与医院信息系统（HIS）无关；事件以Protocol Buffers格式定义，使经医学评估的算法可用多种语言独立实现并易于接入；提供基础UI与告警功能，以可追溯、可解释的方式呈现分析结果。

Result: 平台支持医疗数据的可扩展高效处理与算法的异构独立实现；通过LAMPE这一在莱比锡大学医学中心开发并运行的CDSS用例，展示了平台可在复杂且已成型的临床环境中落地应用。

Conclusion: AMPEL为满足MDR要求的CDSS提供开源基础平台，兼具可扩展性与HIS无关性；文中同时指出当前局限并规划后续开发以加以克服。

Abstract: As incentives to digitize more and more aspects of medical healthcare are gaining traction, additional medical data is becoming available. A major goal is to use this data to gain further insights, support medical staff, and ultimately improve patient care. This demands sophisticated solutions that can handle current and future vast amounts of data, all without disrupting well-established workflows of care takers. Additionally, the solution must fulfill the documentation and risk-management obligations of the Medical Device Regulation (MDR), which governs whether a device may be used in patient care in Europe. In this context, we introduce the AMPEL platform, an open-source solution for creating clinical decision support systems (CDSS) that fulfil the requirements of the MDR. Centered around a message broker (Kafka), it adopts an event-driven, distributed architecture, allowing for scaling and efficient processing of medical data as it gets generated. Relying on platform-specific events to implicitly describe the platform's interfaces makes it both extensible and agnostic to the hospital information system in use. With these events being defined in the Protocol Buffers format, medically evaluated algorithms can be independently implemented in a variety of programming languages and easily linked to the platform, extending its analytic capabilities as desired. The platform features a basic UI and alert functionality that can be used to gain medical staff's attention and present analysis results in a traceable and explainable fashion. We further show in a use case how the platform was used to build LAMPE, a CDSS developed and operated at University of Leipzig Medical Center. It demonstrates how the platform solution can be used in a complex and already established environment, while acknowledging current limitations and outlining planned developments to overcome these.

</details>


### [406] [Female YouTube Content Creators in Bangladesh: A Data-Driven Study of Audience and Sentiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31176)
*Sumaiya Islam,Sejuti Sharmin Siddiqui,Sumaiya Farhana Islam,B M Mainul Hossain*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究对孟加拉国12个女性主导YouTube频道的4,717条视频与558,827条顶层评论进行全量情感分析，发现评论分属英语(33.5%)、孟加拉文(32.0%)、Banglish(24.2%)三种书写体系，需分语种路由建模而非统一翻译；经人工标注微调后健康健身类折外准确率由54.3%升至78.0%，美妆时尚类由81.1%升至85.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉国女性主导YouTube频道的受众反馈尚未被大规模测量；且评论跨三种书写体系，难以用单一模型打分，直接翻译会损失语言信息，通用模型在特定社群上可能存在系统性失准。

Method: 选取6个类别、每类2个频道、共12个女性主导频道，时间跨度2021年1月至2026年8月，覆盖4,717条视频与558,827条顶层评论（全量普查）。按各评论实际书写语言（英语／孟加拉文／Banglish）路由至对应语种训练的模型而非译成英语；对正向情感随时间增幅最大与降幅最大的两个类别进行人工标注，用于微调代码混合（code-mixed）模型，并采用折外（out-of-fold）准确率评估。

Result: 书写体系分布：英语33.5%、孟加拉文32.0%、Banglish 24.2%。微调后折外准确率：Health &amp; Fitness 由54.3%提升至78.0%，Beauty &amp; Fashion 由81.1%提升至85.0%；第一类的正向占比报告值变动近8个百分点。总体结果显示：发布趋势分化、受众注意力高度集中、情感轨迹具类别特异性，且事件型与搜索驱动型类别在受众响应速度上呈现清晰分野。

Conclusion: 面向孟加拉国社交媒体数据，语言感知的情感分析不可或缺；即便是语言适配的通用模型，也可能对特定社群系统性失准，需本地标注微调校准；所测得的负面占比宜解读为下界。

Abstract: Audience response to female-led YouTube channels in Bangladesh has not been measured at scale, and the comments these channels attract are hard to score because they arrive in three different writing systems. This study examines twelve such channels, two per category across six categories, from January 2021 to August 2026, covering 4,717 videos and a census of 558,827 top-level comments. No single writing system dominates the comment set: English accounts for 33.5%, Bangla script for 32.0%, and Banglish (Bengali typed in Latin script and freely mixed with English) for 24.2%. Each comment was therefore routed to a model trained on the language it was actually written in rather than translated into English. Two categories, selected for the largest rise and the largest drop in positive sentiment over time, were hand-labeled and used to fine-tune the code-mixed model, raising out-of-fold accuracy from 54.3% to 78.0% in Health & Fitness and from 81.1% to 85.0% in Beauty & Fashion, and moving the first category's reported positive share by nearly eight points. The wider results show diverging publishing trends, highly concentrated audience attention, category-specific sentiment trajectories, and a clean split between event-like and search-driven categories in how quickly audiences respond. The findings underscore that language-aware sentiment analysis is essential for Bangladeshi social media data, that even a language-appropriate general model can be systematically miscalibrated for a specific community, and that measured negative shares are best read as lower bounds.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [407] [Evaluating Memanto on DolphinBench for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28231)
*Suleman Khan,Rasa Rahnema,Tara Khani,Majid Fekri,Fattane Zarrinkalam*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Memanto 是一种基于信息论相关性打分排序记忆的记忆智能体，在 DolphinBench 的 600 个模拟职场任务上完成 503 项（83.83%），优于 Mem0+GPT-5.6-Luna 的 424 项（70.67%），且总成本最低（$10.86）、中位延迟最短（15.32s）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准多基于问答，直接告知智能体应回忆哪条事实；而真实职场请求（如"在团队频道发更新"）隐含记忆需求，需智能体自行推断目标频道并用于工具调用，因此需要一个以隐式记忆需求为核心的评测场景来检验记忆系统对任务完成度和成本的影响。

Method: 在 DolphinBench（600 个模拟职场任务，每个任务均经校验确需记忆）上评估 Memanto：它用信息论相关性评分对记忆进行排序。运行环境为 Hermes 智能体框架，模型为 GPT-6-Luna，每轮对话最多 6 次迭代。对照为基准作者报告的最强配置 Mem0 + GPT-5.6-Luna（最多 60 次迭代）。评分采用基准自带 judge；但由于智能体模型不同，比较的是完整系统而非单一记忆模块。

Result: Memanto 完成 503/600 任务（83.83%），对照完成 424（70.67%）；总成本 $10.86，低于所有已报告运行中最便宜的 $61.48；中位任务延迟 15.32s，为最短。且在仅 6 次迭代预算下取得上述成绩（对照为 60 次）。

Conclusion: 记忆系统的设计同时影响智能体能否完成此类隐式记忆需求任务以及完成任务的代价；高效记忆检索可在更低迭代预算、更低成本与更低延迟下提升完成率。因模型不同，结论应理解为完整系统层面的对比，而非记忆模块的纯净消融。

Abstract: Memory benchmarks built on question answering tell an agent which fact to recall. Workplace requests often leave that need implicit: to post an update in the team's channel, an agent must work out which channel from earlier interactions and use it in a tool call. We evaluate Memanto, a memory agent that ranks memories with an information-theoretic relevance score, on DolphinBench, which tests memory through 600 simulated workplace tasks, each validated as requiring memory.
